To train it, we first construct the Action and Video Mismatch Preference Dataset (AVM-Pref) with 506.3k pairs providing complementary supervision: action-mismatch pairs supervise AF, while video-mismatch pairs supervise VQ and expose the model to generated videos. We then pretrain an action-conditioned predictor on real videos to learn forward dynamics before jointly training it with a patch-weighted score head on AVM-Pref to transfer these dynamics to AF assessment on generated videos. Evaluation on AVM-Eval reveals the limited reliability of IDMs on generated videos, while downstream RL experiments show that ACWM-RM outperforms reward baselines in both criteria.

</details>


### [36] [Towards Effective and Transferable Physical Camouflage against Multi-View BEV-based 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32230)
*Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Daojing He,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BEVCA，面向多视角BEV 3D感知的对抗伪装生成框架，通过BEV特征对抗损失结合多视角神经渲染实现可迁移、可物理部署的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角BEV 3D感知模型虽性能优越，但其对抗伪装鲁棒性研究不足；现有伪装攻击多针对单视角2D检测器，难以适配多视角3D感知场景。

Method: BEVCA框架：将基于BEV特征的对抗损失与多视角神经渲染模块集成，生成可在不同模型与任务间迁移的对抗伪装纹理。

Result: 在数字与物理两种设置下的大量实验表明，BEVCA优于现有最先进基线，并在真实世界条件下表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BEVCA填补了多视角BEV 3D感知对抗伪装研究的空白，验证了该类模型在真实环境中的安全脆弱性，并提供公开代码。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving systems widely adopt multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) based 3D perception models due to their superior performance. Despite their success, the robustness of these models against adversarial camouflage attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing camouflage attacks primarily target single-view 2D detectors, limiting their effectiveness in multi-view 3D perception settings. To address this gap, we propose BEVCA, a novel adversarial camouflage generation framework tailored for multi-view BEV-based 3D perception. Our framework integrates a BEV-feature-based adversarial loss with a multi-view neural rendering module, enabling effective and transferable camouflage attacks across different models and tasks. Extensive experiments in both digital and physical settings demonstrate that BEVCA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and exhibits strong robustness under real-world conditions. Our code is available at https://github.com/WAVE-AT-IDEA/BEVCA.

</details>


### [37] [TileShare: Training-Free Acceleration of Overlapping Tiled ViT Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32233)
*Xiangzhong Ye,Zhongshen Li,Shen You,Ziyang Meng,Liang Xu,Junkai Ji,Qiuzhen Lin,Xiangtao Li,Ka-Chun Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 固定窗口ViT解码器以重叠瓦片方式部署，重叠带token被多次重复计算。TileShare保留各瓦片的attention，其余算子在每个token上只算一次，并按释放权重将重叠token的逐瓦片结果融合为共享状态；仅前若干层保留逐瓦片计算。无需训练即可加速1.38–1.51x（FP8下最高1.96x），质量与原解码器等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定窗口ViT解码器在部署分辨率下按重叠瓦片运行，重叠带内每个token都被多个完整解码器实例处理；在2.4B参数的MiniMax-H3视频解码器中，约一半的逐token计算被重复。直接去掉瓦片不可行，因为attention、全局token与坐标都与瓦片绑定。

Method: 保留每个被释放瓦片的attention，其余所有操作对每个token仅计算一次；被释放计算只在一种步骤处改变：用释放权重把重叠token的逐瓦片结果融合为单一共享状态。通过干预实验定位融合带来的质量损失主要发生在attention读取共享状态的浅层，因此这些层仍保留逐瓦片计算。无需训练；每模型的保留层数只测量一次（解码器无需标签）。可选地，用两GPU小时自蒸馏让MiniMax-H3共享所有层；另可训练一个坐标场（网络冻结），使全共享的CLIP开放词表分割达到与滑窗相当。

Result: 无需训练保留六层逐瓦片时，在两种GPU上比批处理+编译的瓦片化快1.38–1.51x（FP8最高1.96x），在DAVIS视频上与发布解码器在PSNR、LPIPS、DISTS上等价；在设置预先冻结的未见UVG视频上，PSNR与DISTS持平并提升LPIPS。迁移到另外两个解码器并在未见数据上再次验证，MAGI-1上PSNR与DISTS等价。ViTok-v2图像自编码器在2K–4K下PSNR持平、LPIPS与DISTS提升，解码快1.5–1.8x。报告了14个编解码器的保留层数；自蒸馏后MiniMax-H3可共享全部层且PSNR无损；坐标场使全共享的CLIP开放词表分割在七个基准上与滑窗持平。

Conclusion: 瓦片化ViT解码器的重复计算主要集中于融合语义而非全部算子；只保留浅层逐瓦片attention即可在免训练条件下消除近半冗余计算并保持质量，且方法可跨解码器、图像自编码器与分割任务迁移，自蒸馏与坐标场训练可进一步消除剩余逐瓦片依赖。

Abstract: Fixed-window Vision Transformer (ViT) decoders run at deployment resolution as overlapping tiles: every token in an overlap band is processed by several full decoder instances. In the 2.4B-parameter MiniMax-H3 video decoder, about half of all per-token computation is duplicated. Removing the tiles fails because attention, global tokens and coordinates are bound to them. TileShare keeps each released tile's attention and computes every other operation once per token. The released computation changes at one kind of step only: an overlap token's per-tile results are blended into one shared state with the released weights. Interventions show that this blend costs quality where attention reads the shared states, mainly in the first few layers, which TileShare therefore keeps per tile. Without training, keeping six layers per tile decodes 1.38-1.51x faster than batched and compiled tiling on two GPU types (up to 1.96x with FP8) and is equivalent to the released decoder in PSNR, LPIPS and DISTS on DAVIS videos. On unseen UVG videos with the setting frozen beforehand, it matches PSNR and DISTS and improves LPIPS. TileShare carries over to two further decoders and is again confirmed on unseen data. It is equivalent in PSNR and DISTS on MAGI-1. On the ViTok-v2 image autoencoder at 2K-4K, it matches overlapping tiling in PSNR, improves LPIPS and DISTS and decodes 1.5-1.8x faster. How many layers stay per tile is measured once per model (for decoders, without labels); we report it for 14 decoders and encoders. Optionally, two GPU-hours of self-distillation let MiniMax-H3 share every layer without a loss in PSNR, and a coordinate field trained while the network stays frozen brings fully shared CLIP-based open-vocabulary segmentation to parity with sliding windows on seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [38] [Easy-3Dfy: Object-Level Geometric Composition for Single-Image 3D Scene Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32250)
*Ziang Zhang,Wenyou Yi,Zehan Wang,Jiachen Xu,Xintong Han,Chunchao Guo,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Easy-3Dfy，一种将物体生成与场景布局估计解耦的组合式单图 3D 场景构建流程，通过基于 RPF 的对齐器建立物体局部网格与部分场景点图之间的几何对应以恢复布局，在多个几何指标上优于 SAM-3Dfy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法（如 SAM-3Dfy）在细粒度场景几何与资产品质上不足；而专用 3D 物体生成器擅长精细几何、3D 基础模型具备强泛化空间理解能力，但两者未被有效结合。

Method: 组合式工作流：解耦物体生成与场景布局估计，让专用模型各自发挥优势；引入基于 RPF 的对齐器，在物体局部网格与部分场景点图间建立几何对应，恢复物体布局并完成场景合成；无需完整场景级监督。

Result: 在 Scene-Maker 与 3D-FUTURE200 上，多个几何指标一致优于 SAM-3Dfy。

Conclusion: 显式几何组合能有效结合高质量物体生成与强空间理解，且该流程可独立受益于 3D 物体生成与基础模型的各自进展。

Abstract: Single-image 3D scene construction is a fundamental problem in 3D vision. Recent methods such as SAM-3Dfy enable end-to-end reconstruction of 3D assets and their scene layouts, but still struggle with fine-grained scene geometry and asset quality. Meanwhile, specialized 3D object generators provide detailed object geometry, while 3D foundation models offer strong and generalizable spatial understanding. We propose Easy-3Dfy, a compositional workflow that decouples object generation from scene layout estimation, allowing specialized models to fully exploit their respective strengths. An RPF-based aligner then bridges these capabilities by establishing geometric correspondence between object-local meshes and partial scene pointmaps, recovering object layout for scene composition. Without requiring complete scene-level supervision, the proposed workflow consistently outperforms SAM-3Dfy across multiple geometric metrics on Scene-Maker and 3D-FUTURE200. Our results demonstrate that explicit geometric composition can effectively combine high-quality object generation with strong spatial understanding, while enabling the workflow to benefit independently from advances in both 3D object generation and foundation models.

</details>


### [39] [Representation Learning via Composite Subspace Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32256)
*José Afonso,Mário Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出复合子空间流匹配（CSFM），将表示学习视为对确定性退化课程（光谱、颜色、光照子空间）的方差最小化反演，用硬时间风格截断约束轨迹方差，使容量受限编码器忽略瞬态风格伪影、编码持久内容，在判别迁移与生成合成间取得兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习存在权衡：联合嵌入架构通过激进丢弃类内方差获得判别特征，但不可逆地损失空间与重建信息；生成模型保留空间细节，却因优化被像素变化主导而难以分离语义。作者希望弥合这一分歧。

Method: CSFM不采用非结构化各向同性噪声破坏数据，而是在光谱、颜色、光照子空间上构造确定性退化课程，学习其方差最小化反演；通过硬时间风格截断限制轨迹方差，驱动容量受限编码器忽略瞬态风格伪影、保留持久内容。在卷积与ViT架构上评估。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、STL-10上，CSFM取得有竞争力的判别迁移性，同时保持生成合成能力；避免了非生成方法的坍缩病理，形成平滑潜空间，在线性探测与少样本分类上优于标准最优传输流，并支持连贯合成与轨迹插值。

Conclusion: 引导生成流的物理动力学可作为一种可扩展范式，学习同时具备线性可分性与生成可逆性的表示。

Abstract: Distilling semantic meaning from raw image data remains a challenge in machine learning. Current self-supervised paradigms face a trade-off: joint-embedding architectures produce relevant features by aggressively discarding intra-class variance, irreversibly spatial and reconstruction. Conversely, generative models preserve spatial details but struggle to isolate semantics, as their optimization is dominated by pixel variations. To bridge this divide, we propose composite subspace flow matching (CSFM). Rather than corrupting data with unstructured isotropic noise, CSFM formulates representation learning as the variance-minimizing inversion of a deterministic degradation curriculum across spectral, color, and illumination subspaces. By bounding trajectory variance with hard temporal style truncation, a capacity-constrained encoder is driven to ignore transient style artifacts and encode persistent content. Empirical evaluations across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and STL-10 show that CSFM yields competitive discriminative transferability while preserving generative synthesis across both convolutional and vision transformer architectures. By avoiding the collapse pathologies of non-generative methods, CSFM forms a smooth latent space that outperforms standard optimal transport flows on linear probing and few-shot classification, while enabling coherent synthesis and trajectory interpolation. These results confirm that steering the physical dynamics of generative flows offers a scalable paradigm for learning representations that are simultaneously linearly separable and generatively invertible.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Pattern Matching in 3D MLLMs: Part-Level Functional Reasoning for Generalizable Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32262)
*Weihong Li,Hao Jiang,Yaohua Zha,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PFIR，将"功能推理"从标注装饰改为识别依据（几何→功能→身份），在部件级数据与双目标GRPO下训练3D MLLM，使零样本识别提升且在几何歧义样本上增益集中2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D多模态大模型（3D MLLMs）依赖模式匹配识别物体，当物体几何相似、需借助功能推理（如dresser与wardrobe）消歧时会失败；作者用逐样本的几何混淆分数（GCS）量化该问题：ModelNet40中最歧义的20%样本准确率仅33.7%，最不歧义20%达84.0%。同时指出数据中存在"功能作为标注"（function-as-annotation）模式，即功能语言只装饰已知身份而非支撑推理。

Method: 提出PFIR（Part-level Functional Inference for Recognition），反转信息流：基于PartNeXt部件分割构建数据管线，生成507K QA对，功能链式推理为"几何→功能→身份"；采用渐进式课程学习，最终以双目标GRPO训练——严格功能推理目标与面向Objaverse多样分布的描述性 grounding 目标。两个RL目标产生近乎正交的任务向量（cos=0.056），通过Task Arithmetic进行组合。评估采用严格双OOD协议（Objaverse→ModelNet40；XYZRGB→仅XYZ）。

Result: 零样本准确率从60.5%提升至66.7%；增益在几何歧义样本上集中程度高2.6倍，验证了假设：反转信息流使提升集中于最歧义样本。

Conclusion: 部件级功能推理是通向更具泛化能力的3D识别的有效路径；GCS可作为诊断几何混淆的诊断指标，双目标RL的正交任务向量组合为多能力融合提供可行方案。

Abstract: 3D MLLMs recognize objects via pattern matching, which fails when geometrically similar objects require functional reasoning to disambiguate (e.g., dresser vs. wardrobe). Our per-sample Geometric Confusion Score (GCS) exposes this failure: baseline accuracy is 33.7% on ModelNet40's most ambiguous 20% versus 84.0% on its least ambiguous 20%. We identify a function-as-annotation pattern, in which functional language decorates a known identity instead of supporting inference, and hypothesize that reversing this information flow should concentrate improvements on the most ambiguous samples. PFIR (Part-level Functional Inference for Recognition) reverses that flow. Built on PartNeXt part segmentation, its data pipeline produces 507K QA pairs with functional CoTs (geometry $\to$ function $\to$ identity), and its progressive curriculum culminates in dual-objective GRPO: strict functional reasoning and descriptive grounding for Objaverse's diverse distribution. The two RL objectives yield nearly orthogonal task vectors ($\cos = 0.056$), enabling composition via Task Arithmetic. Under a strict dual OOD protocol (Objaverse $\to$ ModelNet40; XYZRGB $\to$ XYZ-only), PFIR raises zero-shot accuracy from 60.5% to 66.7%, with gains concentrated $2.6\times$ more on geometrically ambiguous samples---supporting part-level functional reasoning as an effective route to more generalizable 3D recognition. Code: https://github.com/this233/pfir-3d.

</details>


### [41] [LapaFM: A Laparoscopic Foundation Model via Multi-Stage Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32263)
*Xiaobo Zhu,Shiwei Wu,Yu An,Yuechen Tao,He Sun,Song Zhang,Jianbo Wang,Xiaojing Wang,Junwei Liu,Wei Guo,Yanfei Shao,Xuan Zhao,Jiangwei Xiao,Wenbo Niu,Bo Wei,Yuhan Wang,Gang Hu,Fei Liu,Kunshan He,Chongwei Chi,Jianqiang Tang,Kang Zhang,Jie Tian,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出腹腔镜手术基础模型LapaFM，基于2万余分钟手术视频与多阶段持续学习策略训练，支持多任务并跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中腹腔镜视频的AI分析有助于提升手术决策与操作精度，但有效的手术辅助要求模型能泛化到多种术中任务，现有任务专用模型和通用基础模型难以兼顾。

Method: 使用多阶段持续学习策略（缓解灾难性遗忘与任务间干扰）在超20,000分钟腹腔镜手术视频上训练基础模型，学习多模态视觉表征，支持去烟、手术阶段识别、器械与息肉分割、病变识别等任务；在13个回顾性队列与2个前瞻性队列（来自不同成像设备）上评估。

Result: 在跨数据集评估中持续优于任务专用模型与现有基础模型，并可泛化至胃肠内镜任务（息肉检测与分类）；恢复超过90%的烟雾遮挡帧，在淋巴结转移预测与前瞻性息肉分类上达到临床医生水平或更优。

Conclusion: 多阶段持续学习构建的腹腔镜基础模型可实现多任务统一建模与跨设备、跨领域泛化，展现作为手术辅助基础模型的潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-based analysis of intraoperative laparoscopic videos has the potential to improve surgical decision-making and precision. However, effective surgical assistance requires models that can generalize across diverse intraoperative tasks. Here we present LapaFM, a foundation model for laparoscopic surgery trained on more than 20,000 minutes of surgical videos using a multi-stage continual learning strategy that mitigates catastrophic forgetting and inter-task interference. LapaFM learns multimodal visual representations and supports multiple tasks, including smoke removal, surgical phase recognition, instrument and polyp segmentation, and lesion identification. The model was evaluated on 13 retrospective and two prospective cohorts collected from different imaging devices. LapaFM consistently outperformed task-specific models and existing foundation models across datasets, and also generalized to gastrointestinal endoscopy tasks, including polyp detection and classification. Notably, it recovered over 90% of smoke-obscured frames and achieved clinician-level or superior performance in lymph node metastasis prediction and prospective polyp classification.

</details>


### [42] [Hyperparameter-Free Boundary-Interior t-vMF Dice Loss for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32266)
*Hinako Mitsuoka,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STB-tvMF Dice损失，按边界/内部区域分别由上一轮平均余弦相似度自定形状参数，无需调参，在Synapse/U-Net上DSC达70.75%（对比63.03%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割存在类别不平衡与解剖边界附近误差两大难题，需要针对性的损失函数。自适应t-vMF Dice虽能在训练中调整相似度函数，但需额外超参数调优。

Method: 提出STB-tvMF Dice：将边界区域与内部区域分开处理，各自依据前一轮epoch中预测-目标平均余弦相似度确定t-vMF函数的形状参数，并在当前epoch内固定；该规则使相似度函数在观测均值处的斜率最大，从而无需基于验证集的参数搜索。

Result: 在三个数据集与多种网络架构上验证有效；Synapse配U-Net时平均DSC为70.75%，而Adaptive t-vMF Dice为63.03%。

Conclusion: 区域自适应、免调参的边界感知t-vMF损失能有效缓解类别不平衡与边界误差问题，性能显著优于需调参的自适应版本。

Abstract: Class imbalance and errors near anatomical boundaries remain challenges in medical image segmentation, motivating loss functions that account for these difficulties. Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss adjusts the similarity function during training but requires hyperparameter tuning. We propose Self-Tuned Boundary-Aware t-vMF Dice Loss (STB-tvMF Dice), which adapts the function separately for boundary and interior regions without tuning shape-adaptation parameters. Each region's shape parameter of t-vMF function is determined from its mean prediction-target cosine similarity in the preceding epoch and remains fixed throughout the current epoch. This rule maximizes the slope of the similarity function at the observed mean similarity, providing a principled basis for adaptation without validation-based parameter selection. Experiments on three datasets and architectures demonstrated its effectiveness. On Synapse with U-Net, STB-tvMF Dice achieved a mean DSC of 70.75%, compared with 63.03% for Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss. Our code will be available soon.

</details>


### [43] [RVG-Bench: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Driven Audio-Visual Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32268)
*Yue Feng,Boshen Xu,Zihao Yue,Xiaokun Liu,Miao Yang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RVG-Bench，一个推理驱动的音视频时序定位基准，要求模型先理解查询意图再定位片段；含1255个视频、1890个查询-区间对、八类推理，22个模型评测中最强仅49.6 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TVG基准多以描述视觉内容的文本查询定位片段，而真实查询更需先理解用户意图（找视觉证据、事件原因、回忆片段等），此类查询被严重忽视。

Method: 构建RVG-Bench：每条查询须经由对意图的推理才能确定目标片段；精细构建并严格校验数据质量以确保模型真正理解音视频内容；覆盖八种推理类型；评测22个模型，并开展oracle-grounding探针与测试时扩展等控制性分析。

Result: 基准含1,255个视频与1,890个查询-区间对；最强模型Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash仅达49.6 mIoU。

Conclusion: 现有模型在推理驱动音视频时序定位上仍显著不足，论文系统揭示其失败位置与原因，并为后续模型发展提供洞见。

Abstract: Existing temporal video grounding (TVG) benchmarks primarily focus on localizing target video segments according to descriptive text queries of the visual content. However, real-world queries are more diverse and often require the model to first understand the user's intent before localization, such as finding visual evidence, identifying the cause of an event, or recalling a remembered moment, yet such queries remain largely overlooked. To mitigate this gap, we introduce RVG-Bench, a benchmark for reasoning-driven audio-visual temporal grounding, where every query is constructed such that the target segment can only be identified through reasoning about the query's underlying intent. We carefully construct the benchmark and rigorously verify its data quality to ensure models truly comprehend the audio-visual content. RVG-Bench comprises 1,255 videos and 1,890 query-interval pairs across eight reasoning types. We evaluate 22 models and find that the strongest, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, achieves only 49.6 mIoU. Through a series of controlled analyses, including oracle-grounding probes and test-time scaling experiments, we systematically examine where and why current models struggle, providing insights for future model development.

</details>


### [44] [RootDown: Deploying and Benchmarking Models for Beet-Root Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32275)
*Gerardus Croonen,Andreas Trondl,Julia Simon,Daniel Steininger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出端到端的图像检测流水线，用于装载输送带上部分遮挡甜菜的检测，构建专用基准，并利用SAM 3基础模型自动生成边界框标注，比较自动标注与人工标注训练的模型，同时在边缘设备上评测精度、推理时间与功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收获及储藏后甜菜质量检测对保持含糖量和加工效率至关重要，装载输送带为逐颗自动视觉检测提供机会；但甜菜数量大、相互重叠遮挡严重，导致标注与检测困难。

Method: 构建专用基准数据集；提出基于SAM 3基础模型的自动标注策略生成边界框；对比人工标注与自动生成标签训练的检测模型；并对多种推理配置（含边缘设备部署）在精度、推理时间与功耗约束下进行基准测试。

Result: 在严重遮挡与标注固有歧义条件下，基础模型生成的标签可替代相当比例的人工标注，以适度精度损失换取标注工作量的降低与数据量的扩大；搭载Hailo加速器的Raspberry Pi 5实现高性能、低功耗推理。

Conclusion: 自动标注可有效缓解遮挡场景下的人工标注负担，且轻量边缘硬件组合具备移动户外部署的实用可行性，为甜菜自动化质量检测奠定基础。

Abstract: Quality inspection of sugar beets after harvest and storage is critical to preserve sugar content and ensure optimal processing efficiency. The transfer of harvested beets to field clamps or trucks provides an opportunity to visually inspect individual beets automatically on loader conveyors. However, this is a challenging task as the large number of beets causes partial overlap and occlusion, complicating both annotation and detection. In this work, we propose an end-to-end image-based pipeline for detecting partially occluded sugar beets in loading scenarios as a first step toward automated quality inspection. We introduce a dedicated benchmark and present an automatic annotation strategy that leverages the SAM 3 foundation model to generate bounding boxes. We compare models trained on manually annotated and automatically generated labels. Additionally, we investigate deployment considerations by benchmarking multiple inference configurations, including execution on an edge device, under realistic operational constraints regarding precision, inference time and power consumption. Our results show that, despite substantial occlusion and inherent annotation ambiguity, foundation-model-based labels can substitute for a substantial share of manual annotation, trading labeling effort for data volume at a modest accuracy cost. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a Raspberry Pi 5 equipped with a Hailo accelerator enables high-performance, low-power inference, making it a practical solution for mobile outdoor deployment.

</details>


### [45] [Polyp-VSSD: A Pre-trained Transformer with Local-Global Joint VSSD Decoding for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32282)
*Xiaobo Zhu,Shiwei Wu,Yu An,Yuechen Tao,He Sun,Song Zhang,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Polyp-VSSD，一种基于预训练Transformer骨干的局部-全局联合解码框架，用于高精度结直肠息肉分割，在多个公开内镜数据集上Dice与IoU显著优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠息肉早期检测对预防结直肠癌至关重要，但现有内镜分割方法表征能力有限，且全局与局部上下文信息融合不充分。

Method: 构建于预训练Transformer骨干之上，利用大规模视觉预训练特征的强表达能力，设计局部-全局联合解码框架，将全局语义建模与局部细粒度重建相统一。

Result: 在多个公开内镜数据集上Dice与IoU显著超越SOTA；EndoScene数据集上mIoU 0.903、mDice 0.946，体现鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 为智能内镜图像分析提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Early detection of colorectal polyps is essential for preventing colorectal cancer, making accurate polyp segmentation a key component in computer-aided diagnosis systems. However, existing endoscopic segmentation methods often exhibit limited representational capacity and insufficient integration of global and local contextual information. To address these limitations, we propose Polyp-VSSD, a novel local-global joint decoding framework built upon a pre-trained Transformer backbone for high-precision polyp segmentation. Polyp-VSSD leverages the strong feature expressiveness of large-scale visual pre-training and unifies global semantic modeling with local fine-grained reconstruction to enhance segmentation fidelity. Extensive experiments on multiple public endoscopic datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both Dice and IoU. On the EndoScene dataset, Polyp-VSSD achieves mIoU and mDice scores of 0.903 and 0.946, respectively, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization capability. This work presents an effective and scalable solution for intelligent endoscopic image analysis.

</details>


### [46] [ReLEAP: Upcycling Vision Datasets through Cross-Task Unification and Semi-Supervised Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32289)
*Julia Simon,Daniel Steininger,Andreas Trondl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReLEAP——一个可扩展的 Python 框架，通过数据源抽象、标注的跨任务系统化复用以及标签到共享层级分类体系的映射，统一异构图像数据集；在精准农业中以 15 个多样化数据集的整合为例，并进一步实现基于学习的“标注上循环（annotation upcycling）”，验证了统一数据访问与标注再利用能显著扩大可复用训练数据与标注池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度、环境多变条件下的视觉任务依赖大量标注数据，而现有数据集在标注格式、标签定义与目标任务上存在差异，阻碍了数据复用与整合，导致数据稀缺与异构领域难以获得充分利用。

Method: 提出 ReLEAP 框架，核心为三点：(1) 数据源抽象（data-source abstraction）；(2) 标注在不同学习任务间的系统化复用；(3) 将标签映射至共享层级分类体系（shared hierarchical taxonomy）。以此统一 15 个覆盖多种植物物种与标注类型的精准农业数据集，并在此基础上开展跨数据集与微调实验，研究经统一数据训练的领域专用模型与基础模型在无监督、弱监督、半监督设定下自动生成高层级标注的效果，以及数据集特性对其影响。

Result: 统一数据访问与标注上循环显著扩展了可复用的训练数据与标注池，同时提升了数据透明度；实验还揭示了数据集特性对自动标注生成有效性的影响。框架与训练模型以学术用途发布。

Conclusion: ReLEAP 为异构数据集提供统一、可扩展的整合基础，使现有数据得到更可持续、资源高效的利用，支持数据稀缺与异构领域中的开放协作研究。

Abstract: Developing robust vision models requires large amounts of annotated data, especially for tasks involving fine-grained class distinctions under variable environmental conditions. Even in domains with numerous existing datasets, differences in annotation formats, label definitions and targeted learning tasks hinder data reuse and integration. To address this challenge, we introduce ReLEAP, a scalable Python framework for unifying heterogeneous image datasets through data-source abstraction, systematic reuse of annotations across learning tasks, and mapping of labels to a shared hierarchical taxonomy. We demonstrate its applicability in precision agriculture by integrating fifteen diverse datasets covering a broad range of plant species and annotation types. Beyond unification, ReLEAP enables systematic recycling of existing data and provides a robust foundation for learning-based annotation upcycling. Leveraging this foundation, we conduct extensive cross-dataset and fine-tuning experiments to investigate how domain-specific models trained on unified data and foundation models can be used to automatically generate higher-level annotations under unsupervised, weakly supervised and semi-supervised settings, and how dataset characteristics influence their effectiveness. Our results show that unified data access and annotation upcycling substantially expand the pool of reusable training data and annotations while improving data transparency, enabling more sustainable and resource-efficient use of existing datasets. We release the framework and trained models for academic use to support accessible and collaborative research, particularly in data-scarce and heterogeneous domains.

</details>


### [47] [Complementing DINO Features with Image Structure for Part Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32293)
*Samyak Rawlekar,Nikhil Chowdary Paleti,Amey Gupta,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Part-DINO：一种免训练框架，将低层图像分割得到的边界与 DINO 语义特征结合，实现结构连贯的部件发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督 ViT（如 DINO）能编码丰富语义，支持免训练的物体分割与部件发现，但现有部件发现方法产生的部件边界不精确。作者将其归因于 DINO 训练所用的刚性 patch 网格：跨越边界的 patch 混合了多个部件的像素。实验（超过 2.6 万个前景 patch）表明 DINO 基于不变性的训练并未消除这种像素级混合，边界重叠 patch 的特征熵更高、分配置信度更低，从而模糊了预测的部件边界。

Method: 不做代价高昂的 DINO 重训练，而是引入 Part-DINO：从低层图像分割中获取边界（提供 DINO 特征缺失的几何信息），用这些边界将图像划分为区域，在每个区域内对 DINO 特征进行池化，再对池化特征聚类以发现部件。整个流程无需任何训练。

Result: 在四个标准基准（CUB、CelebA、PartImageNet-OOD、PartImageNet-Seg）上，Part-DINO 无需训练即产生结构连贯的部件分配，优于免训练基线，并与弱监督和无监督方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DINO 部件的边界误差源于 patch 网格的几何错位而非语义不足；用边界检测器的几何信息弥补 DINO 特征的短板即可显著改善部件发现，无需重训练模型。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) like DINO encode rich semantics, enabling training-free segmentation of objects from the background and discovery of their parts via feature clustering. This paper focuses on part discovery: partitioning a foreground object into semantic parts. Existing approaches produce imprecise part boundaries. We trace this to the rigid ViT patch grid used in training DINO, where patches straddling boundaries contain pixels from multiple parts. While DINO's invariance-based training could absorb this pixel-level mixing into clean features, experiments with over 26K foreground patches show it does not: boundary-overlapping patches produce features with higher entropy and lower assignment confidence, blurring predicted part boundaries. Rather than make the massive effort of retraining DINO over geometrically coherent regions, an easier approach is to take boundaries directly from a boundary detector, which supplies the geometric information DINO's features lack. Motivated by this, we introduce Part-DINO, a training-free framework that combines boundaries from low-level image segmentation with DINO's semantic features. We use the boundaries to partition the image into regions, pool DINO features within each region, and group them to discover parts. Without any training, Part-DINO produces structurally coherent part assignments, outperforming training-free baselines across four standard benchmarks (CUB, CelebA, PartImageNet-OOD, PartImageNet-Seg) and remaining competitive with weakly-supervised and unsupervised methods.

</details>


### [48] [OlmoEarth v1.3: Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32294)
*Gabriel Tseng,Favyen Bastani,Patrick Alan Johnson,Yawen Zhang,Piper Wolters,Ando Shah,Hadrien Sablon,Joseph Redmon,Heather DeHaven,Rachel Ratner,Sasha Levy,Jordan Steward,Katrina Midgley,Andrew Howe,Hunter Pitelka,Ted Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OlmoEarth v1.3模型，将多模态、多时相遥感数据压缩为64或128维嵌入向量，并在更低训练成本下取得SOTA嵌入与强编码器骨干性能，同时发布全球年度10m嵌入数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感数据具有多模态、多时相特性，难以高效利用；已有模型（v1、v1.2）训练成本高，缺乏可直接使用的统一、紧凑嵌入表征。

Method: 训练新模型OlmoEarth v1.3，将多模态、多时相遥感输入编码为单一64或128维向量；将其作为编码器骨干与v1、v1.2对比评估，并基于该模型计算全球、年度化的10m分辨率嵌入。

Result: 嵌入质量达到SOTA；作为编码器骨干与v1、v1.2相当，训练GPU小时分别仅需其18%和53%；产出全球年度10m嵌入并按OlmoEarth artifact license发布。

Conclusion: OlmoEarth v1.3在嵌入质量与训练效率上实现兼顾，可作为高效遥感编码器骨干，并支撑大规模全球年度10m嵌入数据的公开开放。

Abstract: We make some OlmoEarth embeddings. To do this, we train a new model: OlmoEarth v1.3. OlmoEarth v1.3 is designed to condense multimodal, multitemporal remote sensing data into a single digestible 64 or 128-dimensional vector. OlmoEarth v1.3 produces state-of-the-art embeddings. In addition, OlmoEarth v1.3 is a strong encoder backbone -- competitive with OlmoEarth v1 and v1.2 -- while requiring 18% and 53% of the GPU hours to train, respectively. We use OlmoEarth v1.3 to compute global, annualized 10m embeddings, which we release under the OlmoEarth artifact license.

</details>


### [49] [GeoLEON-CLIP: Graph-Anchored Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Geographic Region Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32311)
*Radoslaw Malek,Szymon Soltysiak,Aleksandra Kawala-Sterniuk,Piotr Szymanski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoLEON-CLIP，一个以OpenStreetMap图嵌入为语义锚点的三模态（图-影像-文本）对比框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理区域本质上是多模态的（卫星影像的视觉形态、文本的可解释语义、地图结构的城市语境），但现有地理空间对比方法多只对齐图文或图-位置对，结构化地图数据利用不足。

Method: 对每个H3网格区域对齐三个视图：OSM派生的图嵌入、卫星影像裁片、文本描述。使用冻结的预训练编码器（LEON图嵌入作为视觉与语言之间的语义锚点），通过可训练的残差投影头连接，并以对称三模态对比目标训练。另提出链式跨模态召回R@A@B，以图模态为中间路由，在匹配候选预算下与直接检索对比。

Result: 图-影像与图-文本对齐效果强，图-影像检索在混合分辨率候选池中取得最佳跨模态性能；R@1@1下image→graph→text与text→graph→image分别比直接图文检索高4.8和4.4个点；下游探针提升呈任务依赖，Airbnb与北京房价任务提升最大。

Conclusion: OSM图结构可作为锚点，统一地图、影像与语言的多模态表征。

Abstract: Geographic regions are multimodal: satellite imagery captures visual morphology, text conveys human-interpretable semantics, and map structures describe urban context. Existing geospatial contrastive methods typically align image-text or image-location pairs, underusing structured map data. We introduce GeoLEON-CLIP, a graph-anchored contrastive framework that aligns three views of each H3-indexed region: an OpenStreetMap-derived graph embedding, a satellite image crop, and a textual description. Frozen pretrained LEON graph embeddings provide a semantic anchor between vision and language. Frozen graph, image, and text encoders are connected by trainable residual projection heads and trained with a symmetric tri-modal contrastive objective. Experiments show strong graph-image and graph-text alignment; graph-image retrieval achieves the best cross-modal performance in a mixed-resolution pool. To evaluate the anchoring hypothesis, we propose chained cross-modal recall $\mathrm{R}@A@B$, which routes retrieval through the graph modality and compares it with direct retrieval under a matched candidate budget. At $\mathrm{R}@1@1$, image$\rightarrow$graph$\rightarrow$text and text$\rightarrow$graph$\rightarrow$image outperform direct image-text retrieval by 4.8 and 4.4 points, respectively, despite using a single intermediate cell. Downstream probes show task-dependent gains, with the largest improvements on Airbnb and Beijing housing. These results indicate that OpenStreetMap graph structure can anchor multimodal representations combining maps, imagery, and language.

</details>


### [50] [Organizing Temporal Evidence in Hyperbolic Space for Video Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32317)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Jiaxuan Lin,Gang Hua,Le Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双曲空间框架HTEO，将时序表征映射到Poincaré球，通过径向证据组织与正交角度精修来组织查询感知的时序证据，再投影回切空间以残差方式注入Transformer解码器，提升视频时刻检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索方法在欧氏空间做视频-文本对齐，表征处于平坦几何中。而准确定位需要跨多种grounding状态（目标区域、边界过渡、上下文干扰）组织查询相关证据，这些状态具有不同的时序结构与相关性模式，欧氏表征缺乏显式几何来联合刻画这些关系，故需从几何视角重新组织结构化时序证据。

Method: HTEO框架：将时序表征映射到Poincaré球；Radial Evidence Organization (REO) 调整时序token的径向位置以编码证据间结构差异；Orthogonal Angular Refinement (OAR) 通过正交旋转增强查询条件语义对齐；组织后的表征投影回欧氏切空间，经轻量残差记忆更新注入解码器（保留原始跨模态记忆）；并引入径向结构正则与角度相关性监督分别指导证据组织与语义对齐。

Result: 多个基准上的大量实验与分析表明，HTEO提升了时序定位性能，并对时序证据提供了可解释的几何组织。

Conclusion: 双曲几何可作为组织查询感知时序证据的有效归纳偏置，在保持标准Transformer解码器结构的同时融入几何线索，兼顾性能提升与可解释性。

Abstract: Video moment retrieval aims to localize a query-relevant temporal segment in an untrimmed video. Existing methods typically perform video-text alignment in Euclidean space, where representations are embedded in a flat geometry. However, accurate moment localization requires organizing query-relevant evidence across multiple grounding states, where target regions, boundary transitions, and contextual distractors exhibit distinct temporal structures and relevance patterns. Such Euclidean representations lack an explicit geometry to jointly capture these relationships, motivating us to explore structured temporal evidence organization from a geometric perspective. Specifically, we propose Hyperbolic Temporal Evidence Organization (HTEO), a geometric framework that organizes query-aware temporal memory in hyperbolic space. HTEO maps temporal representations into a Poincaré ball and organizes temporal evidence through Radial Evidence Organization (REO) and Orthogonal Angular Refinement (OAR). REO adjusts temporal tokens' radial positions to encode the structural differences among temporal evidence, while OAR enhances query-conditioned semantic alignment through orthogonal rotation. The organized representation is then projected back to the Euclidean tangent space and injected into the decoder through a lightweight residual memory update, enabling hyperbolic geometric cues to be incorporated into standard Transformer decoders while preserving the original cross-modal memory. Furthermore, we introduce radial structure regularization and angular relevance supervision to explicitly guide temporal evidence organization and query-conditioned semantic alignment, respectively. Extensive experiments and analyses across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HTEO improves temporal localization while providing an interpretable geometric organization of temporal evidence.

</details>


### [51] [DeformStruct: Deformable Structure Modeling for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32321)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Yonghao Dong,Jie Wang,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeformStruct——一种实例级可变形结构建模框架，用于点监督时序动作定位（PTAL），通过结构锚点挖掘、可变形部件对齐与关系结构组织三阶段建模动作实例内部结构，在四个数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTAL仅用单个时间戳标注，监督稀疏；现有方法主要依赖分类响应推断动作区域，未充分建模每个实例内部多个动作相关部件的结构组织。

Method: 三阶段渐进式构建实例结构：（1）Structure Anchor Mining从标注点与动作响应区域挖掘多个锚点，作为实例结构节点；（2）Deformable Part Alignment通过形变感知匹配将潜在结构部件与锚点关联，使各部件时间支撑可适应实例差异；（3）Relational Structural Organization显式建模对齐部件间的相对时间关系，形成刻画部件组织方式的关系表征。

Result: 在THUMOS14、ActivityNet-v1.3、GTEA与BEOID上实验，点监督时序定位性能一致提升，验证了可变形结构建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式的实例级可变形结构建模可有效弥补点监督信息不足，提升PTAL定位精度。

Abstract: Point-supervised Temporal Action Localization (PTAL) aims to localize action instances using only a single annotated timestamp for each instance. Such sparse supervision offers limited guidance for modeling the structural organization of an action instance. Existing PTAL methods primarily infer action regions from classification responses, leaving the structural organization of multiple action-related parts within each instance insufficiently modeled. To address this problem, we propose DeformStruct, an instance-level deformable structure modeling framework for PTAL. DeformStruct progressively constructs the structure of each action instance through three stages. First, Structural Anchor Mining discovers multiple anchors from annotated points and action-responsive regions, providing structural nodes grounded in the current instance. Second, Deformable Part Alignment associates latent structural parts with these anchors through deformation-aware matching, allowing their temporal supports to adapt to instance-dependent variations. Third, Relational Structural Organization explicitly models the relative temporal relations among the aligned parts, forming a relational representation that characterizes how different action-related parts are organized together. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14, ActivityNet-v1.3, GTEA, and BEOID demonstrate consistent improvements in point-supervised temporal localization and validate the effectiveness of deformable structural modeling.

</details>


### [52] [4DMemory: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Memory with Harness-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32332)
*Yanhao Jia,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Weigao Sun,Feida Zhu,Pengkun Jiao,Sihan Yang,Ming Ma,Steven Hoi,Erik Cambria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LongVideo 数据集与 4DMemoryBench 基准，用于评测长时程时空推理，并构建 4DMemory Harness 集成四类技能、22+ 专用工具模型与模型专属 RAG 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能跨小时至数年构建持久、细粒度的时空记忆并据此推理与预测长时程事件；而现有基准集中于短/中上下文任务，多数基础模型难以在长时程上保持细粒度信息。

Method: 构建约 900 小时视频、5000+ 人工复核问题的 LongVideo 数据集，覆盖视觉、文本与时空推理及多样上下文长度；提出 4DMemoryBench 基准；设计 4DMemory Harness，融合四项互补技能、22+ 专用工具模型与模型特定检索增强生成系统。

Result: 评测 13 个领先模型：独立基础模型在长时程任务上表现不佳；4DMemory Harness 使全部模型提升超过 50%；相比以往基准，同一批模型在 4DMemoryBench 上性能下降逾 10%。

Conclusion: 4DMemory 有望推动具备长时程复杂任务处理能力的时空智能体发展。

Abstract: Humans efficiently process multimodal streams spanning hours to years, building persistent, fine-grained spatiotemporal memories that support reasoning about and predicting long-horizon events. In contrast, existing benchmarks focus on short- and medium-context tasks, and most foundation models struggle to preserve fine-grained information over long horizons. To evaluate and improve long-horizon spatiotemporal reasoning, we introduce the LongVideo dataset and the 4DMemoryBench. LongVideo comprises about 900 hours of video and over 5,000 manually reviewed questions covering visual, textual, and spatiotemporal reasoning across diverse context lengths. We further develop the 4DMemory Harness, which integrates four complementary skills, 22+ specialized tool models, and a model-specific retrieval-augmented generation system. Evaluating 13 leading models, we find that standalone foundation models struggle with long-horizon tasks, while the 4DMemory Harness improves all of them by over 50\%. Compared to previous benchmark, the same models exhibit a performance drop of more than 10\% on 4DMemoryBench. We hope 4DMemory will facilitate the development of spatiotemporal agents capable of addressing complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Search-Based Testing of Modality Sensitivity in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32360)
*Oliver Weißl,Eduardo Breitembach,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出黑盒测试框架MoST，通过多目标搜索比较图像、文本与联合扰动，刻画视觉语言模型（VLM）的模态敏感性，并在四个VLM的目标定位任务上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM鲁棒性评估往往对图像和文本分别扰动，或仅使用预设的扰动强度，难以判断哪一模态更易受影响，也无法揭示跨模态的小幅联合扰动是否会导致模型失效。

Method: MoST为黑盒测试框架，采用多目标搜索来选取并组合扰动、调节扰动严重程度，在追求预测性能下降的同时限制图像与提示的改动量，从而在图像单独、文本单独与联合扰动三种设置下对比模态敏感性。

Result: 在ImageNet及驾驶图像上的目标定位任务中评测四个VLM：部分模型以文本扰动为主导，另一些则对两种模态均敏感；在若干配置中，联合扰动以更小的单模态改动量达到或超过单模态扰动的效果。人类研究中，60–76%的评估认为扰动输入仍可理解，且人类定位精度高于对应VLM。

Conclusion: 仅分别评估各模态会遗漏可致失效的扰动组合；模态敏感性相较单一聚合失败率能更细致地刻画VLM鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process images and text jointly, yet robustness evaluations often perturb these inputs separately or assess only predefined corruption levels. Such evaluations provide limited evidence of which modality is more susceptible to perturbations and whether smaller changes across both modalities can jointly expose failures. We present MoST, a black-box testing framework that characterizes modality sensitivity by comparing image-only, text-only, and joint perturbations. MoST uses multi-objective search to select and combine perturbations and adjust their severity, seeking prediction degradation while limiting changes to images and prompts. We evaluate MoST on four VLMs performing object localization on ImageNet and driving images. The models exhibit distinct sensitivities: textual perturbations dominate for some, whereas others are susceptible across both modalities. In several configurations, joint perturbations match or exceed the effectiveness of single-modality perturbations with smaller modifications to each input. In a human study, 60-76% of assessments across models judge the perturbed inputs sufficiently understandable to perform the task, and human localization is more accurate than that of the corresponding VLMs. These results show that evaluating modalities separately can overlook failure-inducing combinations and that modality sensitivity provides a more detailed account of VLM robustness than aggregate failure rates alone.

</details>


### [54] [A Regularity Condition Under Which a CPAB Transformation is a Diffeomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32369)
*Amit Aflalo,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇技术注记，指出CPAB变换本质上是双Lipschitz同胚而非必然的微分同胚，并给出保证其为微分同胚的充分正则性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CPAB论文（Freifeld et al., 2015, 2017）存在数学上的疏漏：将CPAB变换默认视为微分同胚，但实际上它们只保证是双Lipschitz同胚，变换及其逆仅几乎处处可微，未必处处可微。作者希望弥补这一遗漏。

Method: 识别CPAB变换可微性可能失效的机制，提出一组充分正则性条件以防止该失效，并在1D和2D情形下分析这些条件的可实施性；对常用镶嵌结构，在标准采样假设下证明其以概率一成立，并推断3D应有类似性质。

Result: 证明在1D和2D中该正则性条件易于施加，且对常用镶嵌结构在标准采样假设下以概率一满足；同时说明3D可能具有类似行为。

Conclusion: CPAB变换的微分同胚性需要额外的正则性条件保障；本文给出可实施且概率一成立的条件，修正了原文献的理论表述，使CPAB在保持表达力的同时具备严格的微分同胚保证。

Abstract: Continuous Piecewise-Affine Based (CPAB) transformations, introduced by Freifeld et al. (2015, 2017), are flows obtained by integrating continuous piecewise-affine (CPA) velocity fields. These expressive transformations have proven useful in applications including nonlinear temporal warping and trainable activation functions in 1D, as well as nonrigid spatial deformations in 2D and 3D. While computational costs were not an issue in 1D, they originally limited the practical expressiveness of CPAB transformations in higher dimensions, especially in 3D. Our recent work (Aflalo et al., 2026) removed this bottleneck, enabling efficient optimization with much finer tessellations and substantially greater expressiveness. There is, however, a mathematical subtlety that was overlooked in the original CPAB papers (Freifeld et al., 2015, 2017). CPAB transformations are always bi-Lipschitz homeomorphisms, implying that both the transformations and their inverses are differentiable almost everywhere. They are not, however, necessarily diffeomorphisms. This note remedies the omission by identifying sufficient regularity conditions under which a CPAB transformation is guaranteed to be a diffeomorphism. We explain how differentiability can fail and how the proposed conditions prevent this failure. In 1D and 2D, we show that these conditions can be easily enforced and, for commonly used tessellations, hold with probability one under standard sampling assumptions. We expect analogous behavior in 3D.

</details>


### [55] [Past as State, Present as Attention: Persistent-State Blockwise Flow Matching for Long-Horizon Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32373)
*Yi Liu,Xiangyue Zhang,Jianfang Li,Jia Ma,Yisheng He,Jianqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PASPA提出"过去即状态、现在即注意力"(Past-As-State, Present-as-Attention)原则，用块间持久状态压缩缓慢变化的历史风格、用块内双向自注意力建模快速变化的局部动态，在块式flow matching框架下实现长时程语音驱动动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程共语动作生成需兼顾时间连贯性与风格一致性。已有方法分两类：仅以近期少量帧作种子条件，会逐渐漂移；或保留越来越长的显式历史，维护代价高且在小数据上难学习。作者将问题归因于过去与现在的不对称性——过去承载缓慢变化的风格、应被紧凑总结，当前段具有快速变化的局部动态、需细粒度建模。

Method: 基于上述不对称性原则，提出PASPA。具体在三个层面实现：(1) 以块间持久状态(inter-block persistent state)总结历史，以块内双向自注意力(intra-block bidirectional self-attention)建模当前段；(2) 实例化于块式flow matching框架，采用多块监督以适应可变长度历史训练；(3) 采用prefill-decode状态缓存实现高效rollout，并使用混合无分类器引导(hybrid CFG)增强历史与语音条件。

Result: 在公开基准上，PASPA取得state-of-the-art的FGD表现，并在长序列生成中保持动作风格一致。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分"历史风格压缩"与"当前局部细粒度建模"，PASPA以紧凑状态替代长显式历史，兼顾了长时程连贯性、风格保持与推理效率，为长序列共语动作生成提供了有效的建模范式。

Abstract: Co-speech motion generation remains challenging over long horizons due to the need for temporal coherence and consistent style. Existing methods typically generate long motions segment by segment, either conditioning on a short seed of recent frames, which gradually drifts, or retaining ever-longer explicit histories, which are costly to maintain and hard to learn from limited data. We trace these limitations to a key asymmetry between past and present: past motion carries slowly varying style that should be compactly summarized, whereas the current segment exhibits fast-changing local dynamics that require fine-grained modeling. This asymmetry motivates a simple Past-As-State, Present-as-Attention principle for long-horizon co-speech motion generation. Following this principle, we propose PASPA, which summarizes past history with an inter-block persistent state and models the current segment with intra-block bidirectional self-attention. We instantiate PASPA within a blockwise flow matching framework, with multi-block supervision for effective training over variable histories, prefill-decode state caching for efficient rollout, and hybrid classifier-free guidance for enhanced history and speech conditioning. Extensive public-benchmark experiments show that PASPA achieves state-of-the-art FGD performance and preserves motion style across long-sequence generation.

</details>


### [56] [EigenGradCAM with AugSmooth: Enhancing Explainability and Precision in Multi-Stage Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32374)
*Arsham Azam,Sagar Ashraf,Tayyaba Farhat,Rasikh Ali,Sheeraz Akram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的深度学习框架，用于基于2D轴位MRI的四分类阿尔茨海默病分期，融合特征向量化Grad-CAM与梯度平滑提升显著性定位，并用WGAN-GP缓解类别不平衡，取得97.2%测试准确率，热图聚焦海马与内嗅皮层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD为进行性神经退行性疾病，早期准确诊断至关重要；但基于MRI的深度学习模型普遍缺乏可解释性，限制了临床应用，且数据存在类别不平衡（中度痴呆样本不足）。

Method: 1) 采用2D轴位MRI进行四分类AD分期；2) 提出基于特征向量的Grad-CAM，对梯度加权卷积层施加PCA分解，并在分解前向显著性权重注入高斯噪声，结合梯度平滑以稳定热图、改善定位；3) 使用WGAN-GP生成合成样本以平衡四阶段训练分布；4) 在Best Alzheimer MRI Dataset上训练自定义CNN。

Result: 测试准确率97.2%，平衡准确率97.0%，MCC为0.944，各类别ROC曲线下面积0.96–0.99；生成热图突出海马与内嗅皮层等AD生物标志物相关区域。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持强分类性能的同时提升了模型可解释性，但尚需进一步的临床验证。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a progressive neurological disorder associated with memory loss, cognitive decline, and behavioral disturbances. Early and accurate diagnosis is essential, yet MRI-based deep learning models often lack interpretability, limiting their clinical use. This study presents an explainable deep learning approach for four-class AD staging using 2D axial MRI images. The method combines eigenvector-based Grad-CAM with gradient smoothing to improve saliency localization and heatmap stability. Principal component analysis is applied to gradient-weighted convolutional layers, while Gaussian noise is added to saliency weights before decomposition. To address class imbalance in the Moderate Dementia category, Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) generates synthetic samples to balance the four-stage training distribution. Custom convolutional neural networks are trained on the Best Alzheimer MRI Dataset, achieving 97.2% test accuracy, 97.0% balanced accuracy, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.944. The area under the ROC curve ranges from 0.96 to 0.99 across classes. Generated heatmaps highlight the hippocampus and entorhinal cortex, regions associated with Alzheimer's disease biomarkers. These findings suggest that the proposed approach combines strong classification performance with improved model interpretability, although further clinical validation is needed.

</details>


### [57] [Sam-Correction: Fully Adaptive Label Noise Reduction for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32377)
*Takuro Shimaya,Masahiro Saiko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SAM-Correction，利用基础模型 SAM 以全自适应方式纠正医学图像分割中的噪声 GT 掩码，无需人工准备干净标签，实现精准降噪并训练出高性能分割模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师标签质量严重影响机器学习结果，而医学图像分割因标注一致性困难常产生含噪真值掩码。已有方法虽能净化噪声 GT，但依赖多位专家提供的干净掩码，往往难以获得。

Method: 利用分割一切模型（SAM）作为基础模型，以另一个同步训练的分割网络输出作为提示（prompt），逐步迭代地缓解标签噪声，全自适应地纠正噪声 GT 掩码，无需人工准备干净标注。

Result: 在源自真实人工标注的多类标签噪声实验中，SAM-C 能精确降噪，并训练出高性能的分割模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为完全缺乏干净标签情形下的鲁棒模型训练开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The quality of the teacher label has a dramatic impact on the outcome of the machine learning process. Medical image segmentation often suffers from a noisy ground truth (GT) mask due to difficulties in consistent annotation. Although a recent study has proposed a promising method to curate the noisy GT mask, this requires additional well-curated GT masks that are not always accessible without the aid of multiple medical experts. As an alternative, we propose SAM-Correction, which utilizes a foundation model to correct the noisy GT mask in a fully adaptive manner instead of manually preparing well-curated masks. In our approach, we progressively mitigate label noise via the segment anything model (SAM), where prompts are given from another segmentation network that we train concurrently. Experiments with various types of label noise derived from actual human annotations demonstrate that SAM-C achieves precise noise reduction and high-performing segmentation models trained thereby. Our method paves the way for robust model training even when clean labels are entirely unavailable.

</details>


### [58] [UniWorld: A Unified World Model for Real-Time Visual World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32381)
*Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Shuaiwei Wang,Zixuan Duan,Haibin Huang,Feng Xu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld 是一个统一的连续视觉世界模拟框架，在共享视频生成模型中耦合深度轨迹与参考外观，通过时间线对齐的潜在状态实现精确历史保持与实时吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视觉世界模拟需要同时满足两个要求：演化中的场景几何与时间上持久的外观。现有方法难以在同一模型中兼顾几何一致性与外观持久性，也难以在长时程上无损保留历史。

Method: 核心是在同一时间线上维护视觉与几何历史。具体包含三个机制：(1) 噪声对齐的重叠续接，将已生成历史置于指定采样噪声水平、仅提交新生成的潜变量；(2) 滚动深度潜状态，增量读取新可用几何并在每个采样窗口内固定条件；(3) 因此实现潜历史的精确保持，并将活跃深度状态限制在窗口大小内。同一生成器支持估计、原生与按需模拟器深度；缓存与增量执行路径输出逐位一致。

Result: 在多样场景类别上评估了连续生成、成对历史干预与长时程行为；外观变化与几何控制分支可在不改动已提交历史的前提下通过同一接口操作；稳态 DiT 执行路径达到实时生成吞吐。

Conclusion: UniWorld 以统一的视频生成模型实现了几何与外观的历史一致性、精确的潜历史保持以及实时效率，适用于连续视觉世界模拟。

Abstract: Continuous visual world simulation requires coupling evolving scene geometry with temporally persistent appearance. We present UniWorld, a unified framework that integrates depth trajectories and reference appearance within a shared video generation model. The central design maintains visual and geometric histories on a common timeline. Noise-aligned overlapping continuation places generated history at the prescribed sampling noise level and commits only newly generated latents. Rolling depth latent state preserves the corresponding geometric controls, incrementally reads newly available geometry, and keeps the conditioning fixed within each sampling window. Together, these mechanisms ensure exact preservation of latent history while bounding the active depth state by the window size. We evaluate continuous generation, paired history interventions, and long-horizon behavior across diverse scene categories. The same generator supports estimated, native, and on-demand simulator depth, while cached and incremental execution paths produce bitwise-identical outputs. Appearance changes and geometric control branches operate through the same interface without modifying committed history. The measured steady-state DiT execution path achieves real-time generation throughput, demonstrating the efficiency of UniWorld for continuous visual world simulation.

</details>


### [59] [PASTA: Prompt-Aware Annealed Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32390)
*Ren Ajiki,Ryugo Morita,Stanislav Frolov,Brian Bernhard Moser,Ko Watanabe,Riku Takahashi,Andreas Dengel,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PASTA是一种免训练扩散采样框架，通过掩码引导的共享潜变量轨迹融合全提示与上下文提示，并以退火调度抑制语义泄漏，从而严格保留图像中的预留区域并保持视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实视觉设计需要在图像中为标题、logo或UI元素预留干净区域。现有布局感知文生图方法难以严格保持预留区域，常引入多余内容，干扰后续编辑；而通过操控注意力或噪声级干预抑制条件的方法又会牺牲视觉连贯性，导致区域扁平或风格不一致。

Method: 提出PASTA（Prompt-Aware Shared-Latent Training-Free Annealed Diffusion Sampling）：在共享潜变量轨迹上运行，通过掩码引导采样融合全提示与上下文提示的引导；并采用退火调度，在去噪早期抑制语义泄漏，在后期逐步恢复视觉一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，PASTA在提升预留区域保真度的同时，保持了整体图像的真实感与感知一致性。

Conclusion: PASTA以无需训练的方式实现了严格的空间布局约束，克服了注意力操控与噪声干预方法在视觉连贯性上的缺陷，为面向设计的可控文生图提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While Diffusion models produce high-quality images, real-world visual design requires intentionally preserving clean regions for headlines, logos, or UI elements. Existing layout-aware text-to-image methods struggle to strictly preserve reserved regions, often introducing unwanted content that complicates downstream editing. Existing approaches either manipulate attention to displace content or suppress conditioning via noise-level interventions. However, these methods sacrifice visual coherence, producing flat regions or noticeable style inconsistencies between reserved and non-reserved areas. To address this limitation, we propose a Prompt-Aware Shared-Latent Training-Free Annealed Diffusion Sampling (PASTA), a training-free framework that enforces reserved-region constraints by selectively applying semantic guidance to object-allowed regions during sampling. PASTA operates on a shared latent trajectory, where full-prompt and context-prompt guidance are fused through mask-guided sampling. An annealed schedule suppresses semantic leakage during early denoising stages while gradually restoring visual consistency in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PASTA improves reserved-region fidelity while preserving overall image realism and perceptual consistency.

</details>


### [60] [Interactive image diagnosis using test-time backpropagation from the auxiliary task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32396)
*Takuro Shimaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务梯度辅助（MGA）方法，无需额外训练即可为多任务CAD模型引入交互性，放射科医生可对辅助任务预测给出反馈，从而获得改进的诊断建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务CAD系统通过辅助任务预测可解释特征来增强诊断的可解释性，但当辅助任务预测偏离放射科医生预期时，医生往往不再参考CAD意见，导致CAD知识未被充分利用。

Method: 提出MGA方法，利用多任务模型的梯度信息将放射科医生对辅助任务的反馈传导至诊断预测，实现交互式修正且无需重新训练；在乳腺X线摄影与CT两个数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在多种辅助任务反馈下，MGA均能提升诊断准确率；与仅使用前向传播的现有方法相比，MGA更稳健，且二者可结合以进一步提升准确率。

Conclusion: MGA为将人工智能集成到影像诊断中提供了有前景的交互式途径。

Abstract: Multi-task deep learning has been developed to ensure explainability in computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) systems. These systems simultaneously predict interpretable characteristics of findings in auxiliary tasks to help radiologists assess the reliability of the diagnostic prediction. However, the CAD's opinion would not be referred to when the auxiliary task prediction deviates from the radiologist's expectation. To further leverage CAD's knowledge in the above situation, we proposed a multi-task gradient assist (MGA) method that brings interactivity to multi-task models without additional training. MGA allows radiologists to give feedback to the CAD and ask for additional diagnostic suggestions. Using two datasets of mammography and CT, we show that MGA successfully improves diagnostic accuracy upon feedback on various auxiliary tasks. Compared to an existing method using forward propagation only, MGA performs more robustly and can be combined to further improve accuracy. Our method paves a promising way for integrating artificial intelligence into image diagnosis.

</details>


### [61] [An Error Budget for Shuffled Multimodal Windows in the 3rd WEAR Challenge: Stacking, Count Constraints and Seam Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32398)
*Soheil Kooklan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文对WEAR第3届挑战赛（四名未见参与者、每1秒窗口需给活动标签、窗口打乱、每窗口含15个VideoMAEv2帧嵌入与单随机肢体加速度）提出一个截止后构建的系统，做经验性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战设定下窗口被随机打乱、只有单肢体加速度且参与者未见过，需在离线转导条件下把被打乱的窗口重新组织并分配活动标签；作者希望量化各组件（堆叠、计数约束、接缝距离）的实际贡献并给出诚实的性能估计。

Method: 窗口分类器堆叠，特征为在参与者级视频近邻图上的平均特征与已打分的后继候选；均值场式解码器对每名参与者的所有窗口联合标注，并施加每个活动出现一次的软计数约束；后继链接模型额外测试了二次接缝距离；解码器设置与混合权重在折内选定，误标片段排除出训练；采用参与者级bootstrap区间与非嵌套堆叠协议。

Result: 堆叠相对第一阶段融合提升0.051 macro F1，计数约束提升0.011（95%区间0.006–0.017）；二次接缝距离在约720个候选中将真实同肢体后继排第一的概率为83.9%，但加入链接模型后macro F1变化为-0.0001（-0.0006–0.0005）。模拟得分0.936（每个活动做一次的参与者为0.940），提交配置0.938（0.943），参与者个体得分0.80–0.97；用真实分段平均最终一元分数的oracle达0.973，剩余错误中79%涉及null类。私榜上选中提交为0.9231（公榜0.9254，边界部分在公榜上调），后续用模拟选边界的提交为0.9247（公榜0.9253）。

Conclusion: 堆叠与计数约束是主要增益来源，接缝距离虽有判别力但对最终指标几乎无贡献；非嵌套堆叠与转导使用未标注特征使结果不构成完全归纳式估计，且错误集中在null类。

Abstract: The 3rd WEAR Dataset Challenge asks for an activity label for every one-second window of four unseen participants. Each window has 15 VideoMAEv2 frame embeddings, the accelerometer signal of one random limb, and the windows are shuffled. This report is an empirical analysis of one system built after the challenge deadline. Window classifiers are stacked with features averaged over per-participant graphs of video neighbours and scored successor candidates, and a mean-field style decoder with a soft count constraint per activity labels all windows of a participant together. The setting is offline and transductive, and the count constraint relies on each activity being performed once. In a participant-wise simulation with participant-level bootstrap intervals, stacking added 0.051 macro F1 over stage-1 fusion and the count constraint 0.011 (95% interval 0.006 to 0.017). A quadratic seam distance ranks the true same-limb successor first among about 720 candidates in 83.9% of cases, but adding it to the link model changed macro F1 by -0.0001 (-0.0006 to 0.0005). With the decoder settings and the mixing weight chosen inside the folds and the mislabelled spans left out of training, the system scores 0.936 in simulation (0.940 for participants who performed each activity once); the submitted configuration scores 0.938 (0.943). Per-participant scores range from 0.80 to 0.97. These results come from a non-nested stacking protocol that uses unlabelled features transductively, so they are not a fully inductive estimate. An oracle that averages the final unary scores over the true segments reaches 0.973, and 79% of the remaining errors involve the null class. On the private leaderboard, which no choice used, the selected Kaggle submission scored 0.9231 (public 0.9254, with bounds partly tuned there), and a later one with bounds chosen in simulation scored 0.9247 (public 0.9253).

</details>


### [62] [TIE: Training-free CLIP Embedding Guidance for Compositional Text-to-Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32407)
*Sujung Hong,Tae Eun Choi,Youngjun Jun,Chanyong Yoon,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练、推理阶段的文本嵌入更新方法 TIE，通过熵感知奇异值放大缓解概念丢失、基于 CLIP 注意力分数的插值-外推绑定缓解概念混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I 扩散模型仍难以满足提示词条件，出现概念丢失（对象或属性缺失）与概念混淆（属性错绑、多对象融合），根源在于 CLIP 文本编码器对细粒度语义刻画能力有限，已有方法在多对象多属性提示下仍失效。

Method: 在嵌入层面做分析并据此设计推理时方案：(1) 发现后提及的概念嵌入熵更高、表征更分散，易导致概念丢失；(2) 首个 CLIP 注意力层编码了对象-属性的绑定强度。TIE 仅通过一次文本嵌入更新实现：以熵感知的奇异值放大抑制概念丢失，以基于 CLIP 注意力分数的插值-外推绑定修正概念混淆，全程无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，TIE 在多概念场景下提升了语义保真度，且采样开销极小。

Conclusion: 嵌入层分析可有效诊断并缓解 CLIP 类 T2I 模型的概念丢失与混淆，单次文本嵌入更新即可在无训练条件下显著改善提示词对齐。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have advanced notably, but still fail to satisfy prompt conditions, resulting in attribute mismatches and missing objects. These errors normally fall into two categories: concept loss, when an object or attribute (i.e., concept) goes missing, and concept confusion, when an attribute is assigned to the wrong object or multiple objects blend together. Recent studies suggest that these challenges are caused by the limited capacity of CLIP text encoder to capture fine-grained semantic details. Although several methods have been proposed, concept loss and concept confusion persist in recent T2I models when handling multi-object, multi-attribute prompts. In this paper, we conduct an embedding-level analysis to develop a training-free, inference-time solution for concept loss and concept confusion across CLIP-based T2I models. We find that (1) concepts mentioned later exhibit higher embedding entropy, reflecting less concentrated representations that are more often associated with concept loss, and (2) the first CLIP attention layer captures the strength of binding between each object and its attribute. Guided by our findings, we introduce TIE, a method that improves semantic alignment through a single text-embedding update. TIE addresses concept loss via entropy-aware singular value amplification and resolves concept confusion through interpolation-extrapolation binding based on CLIP attention scores, all in a training-free manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIE enhances semantic fidelity in multi-concept scenarios with minimal sampling overhead. The project page is available at https://hong-sujung.github.io/TIE/.

</details>


### [63] [AlphaAnything: Extending Pretrained RGB Generators to RGBA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32413)
*Qijie Wang,Zhixin Xu,Zhe Li,Haowei Zhu,Honghao Qiao,Bo Peng,Dawei Li,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AlphaAnything，一个轻量框架，将预训练 RGB 生成器直接在其原始潜空间中扩展为 RGBA 生成，无需 RGBA 重建预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RGBA 生成方法通过重建学习透明度专用表示，需额外训练阶段，且可能改变从预训练模型继承的 RGB 解码函数，破坏原有生成能力。

Method: 在生成 transformer 中插入 LoRA 模块，并在冻结的 RGB VAE 解码器上附加 alpha 分支；采用单向 RGB→alpha 特征共享，保证任意固定潜变量下 RGB 解码与预训练解码器完全一致；同时提出 Endpoint-Aware Alpha Loss（EA Loss），从合成所用的前景-背景互补权重推导不透明度监督，支持精确端点与连续半透明并聚焦局部 alpha 误差。

Result: 在多种预训练 RGB 骨干上，文本到 RGBA 与图像到 RGBA 任务均取得有竞争力的生成质量；受控消融实验验证了共享潜空间训练设计与 EA Loss 的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在原始潜空间内直接扩展预训练 RGB 生成器并配合 EA Loss，可在无需重建预训练的前提下实现高质量 RGBA 生成，同时完整保留预训练 RGB 解码行为。

Abstract: Existing RGBA generation methods commonly learn a transparency-specific representation through reconstruction, introducing an additional training stage and potentially altering the RGB decoding function inherited from pretrained models. We present AlphaAnything, a lightweight framework that extends pretrained RGB generators to RGBA directly in their original latent space, without RGBA reconstruction pretraining. AlphaAnything inserts LoRA modules into the generative transformer and attaches an alpha branch to the frozen RGB VAE decoder. Unidirectional RGB-to-alpha feature sharing allows opacity supervision to shape the generative latent while guaranteeing that, for any fixed latent, RGB decoding remains identical to that of the pretrained decoder. We further introduce Endpoint-Aware Alpha Loss (EA Loss), which derives opacity supervision from the complementary foreground--background weights used in compositing. It supports exact opacity endpoints and continuous partial transparency while emphasizing localized alpha errors. Experiments on text-to-RGBA and image-to-RGBA tasks across multiple pretrained RGB backbones demonstrate competitive generation quality, and controlled ablations validate the shared-latent training design and EA Loss.

</details>


### [64] [Cycle-of-Thought: Self-Evaluative Iterative Reasoning for Table Visual Question Answering in Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32423)
*Somraj Gautam,Gaurav Harit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、推理期自纠错框架 Self-RefineVQA，让单个 VLM 循环扮演生成器、评论者与修正者，在无需标注数据和模型更新的情况下提升表格 VQA 准确率，对 2B–7B 小模型增益尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在表格等结构化内容上的推理不稳定，常出现空间错位与逻辑幻觉。现有改进方法依赖架构修改或昂贵的监督微调（SFT），需要大规模专家标注数据和大量算力，门槛过高。

Method: 受人类修改行为启发，提出训练无关、推理时自纠错框架：单一 VLM 迭代充当生成器（generator）、评论者（critic）和修正者（refiner），通过提示引导的重新评估构成闭环自反馈，纠正常见推理错误；无需额外标注数据或模型参数更新。

Result: 在表格 VQA 基准上，单语与跨语言理解均取得一致的准确率提升；小模型（2B–7B）提升尤其明显，可媲美更大规模模型。复杂度分析表明推理成本随迭代线性增长，但省去标注瓶颈，数据效率更高、累计成本更低。

Conclusion: 基于提示的自我改进是一种可扩展、易获取的范式，可用于 VLM 可靠的表格推理。该方法无需训练与标注，降低了进入门槛，但代价是推理期计算开销线性增加。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved Visual Question Answering (VQA) performance, yet their reasoning over structured content such as tables remains inconsistent, often plagued by spatial misalignment and logical hallucinations. Existing approaches for improving table VQA typically rely on architectural modifications or expensive supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which impose high barriers to entry due to the need for large-scale, expert-annotated datasets and significant computational resources. In this work, we propose Self-RefineVQA, a training-free, inference-time self-correction framework that improves table VQA performance without additional labeled data or model updates. Inspired by human revision behavior, our method enables a single VLM to iteratively act as a generator, critic, and refiner, forming a closed self-feedback loop that corrects common reasoning errors through prompt-guided re-evaluation. Experiments on tabular VQA benchmarks demonstrate that Self-RefineVQA yields consistent accuracy gains in monolingual and cross-lingual understanding, with a particularly significant boost for small models, enabling lightweight models (e.g., 2B-7B parameters) to rival the performance of significantly larger counterparts. Furthermore, we provide a comparative complexity analysis demonstrating that while inference cost increases linearly, our framework offers superior data efficiency and a lower cumulative cost by eliminating the prohibitive annotation bottleneck of human labeling required for fine-tuning. Our results highlight the potential of prompt-based self-improvement as a scalable, accessible paradigm for reliable table reasoning in VLMs.

</details>


### [65] [Tempo-Zero: Recursive Self-Play in Executable Worlds for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32433)
*Jingmin Zhu,Yanyan Zou,Xiuyu Li,James Bailey,Dinh Phung,Tao Yu,Yunyun Hou,Jun Liu,Jun Zhou,Sirui Wang,Nan Duan,Jiachen Liu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tempo-Zero自改进框架，用可执行环境生成视频并提供精确时间标注，通过执行验证的自博弈统一Questioner与Solver，无需监督微调或外部视频数据即在12个真实视频基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理需要理解事件的时间演化，但带精确时间标注的视频代价高昂；现有视频自博弈方法只能在固定录制视频集上训练，无法改变事件动态，且依赖多数投票等含噪伪标签，易放大模型的共性错误。

Method: 构建"可执行环境—执行验证自博弈—环境演化"闭环：执行程序渲染出携带每个问题正确答案选项与时间区间的视频，从而提供真值监督；单一策略同时扮演Questioner与Solver，提问并以带时间戳的证据行作答，训练信号为执行验证的奖励而非模型共识；Solver表现驱动任务与难度课程，策略选择规则变更以丰富后续轮次事件动态。

Result: 仅用简单程序训练、无需监督微调与外部视频数据，在多个模型规模下，于12个真实视频基准上平均优于基座模型与近期自博弈基线，时间定位任务提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 以可执行环境替代录制数据，可从根源上消除伪标签噪声并提供可验证的时间监督，环境演化保证训练分布持续扩展，为视频时间推理的无标注自改进提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Video reasoning requires understanding how events unfold over time, yet videos with accurate temporal annotations are expensive to collect. Self-play offers an alternative by letting a model generate and solve its own tasks without manual annotation. However, existing video self-play methods train on fixed sets of recorded videos, so they cannot alter the underlying event dynamics and rely on noisy pseudo-labels derived from the model's own outputs, such as majority voting, that risk reinforcing shared model errors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Tempo-Zero, a self-improvement framework that closes a loop of executable environments, execution-verified self-play, and environment evolution, where executing a program renders a video with the ground-truth option and interval of every question. A single policy plays both Questioner and Solver, proposing questions and answering them with timestamped evidence lines, and is trained on execution-grounded rewards instead of model consensus. The Solver's performance steers a curriculum over tasks and difficulty levels, and the policy selects rule changes that enrich the event dynamics of later rounds. Despite being trained solely on simple programs, without supervised fine-tuning or external video data which prior methods typically require, Tempo-Zero outperforms the base models and recent self-play baselines on average across twelve real-world video benchmarks at different model scales, with particularly strong gains on temporal grounding tasks. Project page: https://alchemist-jo.github.io/tempo-zero/

</details>


### [66] [PROVE: Probabilistic Visual Grounding via Patch-Level Evidence for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32440)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Tianru Zhang,Li Ju,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROVE：冻结CLIP补丁特征上后验式证据适配器，单次前向输出定位图、不确定性图与目标缺失分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP类视觉定位每个图像-查询对仅输出单一确定性相关图，无法反映定位是否可靠或指称对象是否存在。

Method: 将定位建模为逐补丁隶属度，把冻结CLIP补丁特征映射为每补丁Beta分布（正/负证据累积），不改动骨干；在软补丁覆盖目标上用证据目标训练，并引入先验拉回目标使缺失样本保持接近无信息先验，以"空洞度"作缺失信号；推理时闭式得到后验均值定位图、空间空洞图、图像级缺失分数。

Result: 在指称表达式基准上定位性能与SOTA CLIP定位方法相当；不确定性可追踪定位误差并支持弃权下的选择性预测；图像级分数可检测指称对象完全缺失。

Conclusion: 证据式补丁隶属建模为冻结视觉-语言定位系统提供简单、可即插即用的概率化验证机制。

Abstract: Visual grounding with vision-language models such as CLIP typically produces a single deterministic relevance map for an image-query pair, without indicating whether the localisation is reliable or whether the referent is present at all. We introduce \textbf{PROVE} (\textbf{Pro}babilistic \textbf{V}isual Grounding via Patch-Level \textbf{E}vidence), a post-hoc evidential adapter that maps frozen CLIP patch features to per-patch Beta distributions without modifying the backbone. PROVE casts grounding as per-patch membership: each patch accumulates positive and negative evidence for whether it belongs to the referent, so a query may match zero, one, or many patches. We train PROVE with an evidential objective on soft patch-coverage targets, and define a prior-pull objective that keeps absent-referent examples near an uninformative prior, so that vacuity serves as an absence signal without a dedicated presence classifier. During inference, a single forward pass yields, in closed form, a posterior-mean grounding map, a spatial vacuity map, and an image-level absence score. We evaluate PROVE on referring-expression benchmarks: it is competitive in localisation with state-of-the-art CLIP grounding methods, its uncertainty tracks localisation error and supports selective prediction under abstention, and its image-level score detects when the referent is absent altogether. Our evidential patch-membership modelling offers a simple, drop-in mechanism for probabilistic verification in frozen vision-language grounding systems.

</details>


### [67] [Learning Fine-Grained Visual Classification with Text as Privileged Information via Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32442)
*Aishwarya Venkataramanan,Christian Ickler,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FG-LUPI：训练阶段用文本属性作为特权信息构建多模态教师，再通过预测/图块/注意力三级蒸馏把知识迁移到纯图像学生模型，在六个细粒度基准上同时提升准确率与置信度校准（ECE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉分类依赖细微局部差异的辨别；文本属性描述可提供显式语义监督，但测试时通常不可得，无法直接用于部署，因此需要一种"训练可用、推理不用"的知识利用方式。

Method: 基于 LUPI 框架：教师端以门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）融合图像图块与文本属性，使语义属性对视觉表征进行条件化调制；学生端为纯图像模型，通过多级蒸馏（预测级、图块表征级、注意力级）对齐教师的全局预测、图块表示与注意力模式。

Result: 在多个 FGVC 基准上相比强 ViT 基线取得一致的准确率提升；在全部六个基准上 ECE 显示预测校准更优。

Conclusion: 文本属性监督不仅提升细粒度识别性能，还改善模型置信度可靠性，同时保留纯图像推理的高效性与实用性。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual classification (FGVC) requires distinguishing between highly similar categories by focusing on subtle, localized visual differences. Recent multimodal methods have demonstrated that textual attribute descriptions can provide valuable semantic supervision by explicitly describing discriminative object characteristics. However, these descriptions are typically unavailable at test time, limiting their direct use in deployment. We propose FG-LUPI, a Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI)-based framework that leverages attribute descriptions during training. We train a multimodal teacher that integrates image patches and textual attributes via gated cross-attention, allowing semantic attributes to condition visual representations. We distill this knowledge into an image-only student using multi-level distillation at the prediction, patch, and attention levels, aligning global predictions, patch representations, and attention patterns with the teacher. This encourages the student to inherit attention structure learned from multimodal supervision while maintaining image-only inference. Experiments on multiple FGVC benchmarks show consistent improvements in classification accuracy over strong ViT-based baselines. Furthermore, evaluation using Expected Calibration Error (ECE) shows that FG-LUPI also produces better-calibrated predictions across all six benchmarks. These results suggest that textual attribute supervision can benefit not only fine-grained recognition performance but also the reliability of model confidence, while preserving the efficiency and practicality of image-only inference.

</details>


### [68] [AutoMIL: Automated Multiple Instance Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32452)
*Jonas Waibel,Frank Kramer,Dominik Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出开源框架 AutoMIL，将自动机器学习（AutoML）思想引入全切片图像（WSI）多示例学习（MIL）分类流程，自动化预处理、训练与评估等环节；在默认设置下于 UBC-OCEAN、PCam 及 TCGA-NSCLC 子集分别取得 0.94、0.99、0.93–0.96 的 ROC-AUC，与现有专用流程相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建 WSI 多示例学习分类流程涉及大量相互依赖的设计决策，且需同时具备病理学与机器学习专业知识，门槛高、复现成本大，缺乏易用的自动化基线。

Method: 基于 AutoML 原则设计开源框架 AutoMIL，自动化 MIL 流程中的预处理、训练与评估等关键步骤，并集成集成学习（ensembling）策略；通过默认设置下的基准评测与已有专用流程进行对比。

Result: 默认设置下 ROC-AUC 为 UBC-OCEAN 0.94、PCam 0.99、TCGA-NSCLC 子集 0.93–0.96，与相关工作专用流程性能相当；集成方法主要在类别不平衡数据上带来提升。

Conclusion: 自动化流程需适配数据集特性（如不平衡程度）；AutoMIL 为无需手工配置的 WSI 分类提供了可及的开源基线，降低了 MIL 流程构建门槛。

Abstract: Developing multiple instance learning pipelines for the classification of whole-slide images involves many interdependent design decisions and expertise in both pathology and machine learning. We present AutoMIL, an open-source framework that applies principles from the field of automated machine learning to tackle this task by automating integral pipeline steps, such as preprocessing, training and evaluation. In a benchmark evaluation with default settings, AutoMIL reached ROC-AUC scores of 0.94 on UBC-OCEAN, 0.99 on PCam and 0.93-0.96 on a TCGA-NSCLC subset, comparable to specialised pipelines in related work. Ensembling mainly improved performance on imbalanced data, showing that an automated pipeline must adapt to dataset characteristics. AutoMIL thus provides an accessible baseline for whole-slide image classification without manual pipeline configuration.

</details>


### [69] [MDMR-Bench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multi-Reference Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32459)
*Bowen Zheng,Jintao Lin,Changheng Yi,Haoran Yang,Binxiao Huang,Chenyang Lei,Rui Liu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MDMR-Bench，一个面向多参考图像生成的多维度基准，涵盖实体融合/属性迁移、单图多元素提取、信息图操作和视觉推理密集型生成四类能力，并引入基于清单的细粒度评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参考图像生成基准评估维度有限，无法全面反映该任务的多样挑战，缺乏对不同能力设定与失败模式的系统分析。

Method: 构建围绕代表性能力设置组织的多子集基准 MDMR-Bench；提出基于清单（checklist）的评估协议，将生成质量分解为全局级与原子级的细粒度查询，替代单一整体打分；并对开源与闭源模型进行评测。

Result: 对开源和专有模型的基准测试表明，MDMR-Bench 能在不同挑战维度上给出细粒度的性能洞察，并揭示模型的失败模式。

Conclusion: MDMR-Bench 提供了更具诊断性的多参考图像生成评估框架，数据集与代码已公开，有助于系统分析模型能力与不足。

Abstract: Image generation models have rapidly advanced, with many models now supporting generation conditioned on multiple reference images and textual instructions. Such models enable a wide range of applications, including entity fusion and attribute transfer across images. However, existing benchmarks for multi-reference image generation rely on limited evaluation dimensions and fail to capture the diverse challenges of the task. To address this limitation, we propose MDMR-Bench, a Multi-Dimensional benchmark for Multi-Reference image generation. MDMR-Bench is organized around representative capability settings and consists of multiple evaluation subsets spanning diverse dimensions of multi-reference image generation, including (1) common entity fusion and attribute transfer, (2) multi-element extraction from a single image, (3) infographic manipulation, and (4) visual reasoning-intensive generation. This design enables systematic analysis of model capabilities and failure modes across different challenge dimensions. Furthermore, to achieve more reliable and interpretable evaluation, we introduce a checklist-based evaluation protocol that decomposes generation quality into fine-grained evaluation aspects. Instead of relying solely on holistic scoring, our framework uses structured checklists composed of both global-level and atomic-level queries, enabling more diagnostic assessment of model performance. By benchmarking both open-source and proprietary models, we demonstrate that MDMR-Bench provides fine-grained insights into model performance across different dimensions. The dataset and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [PathJev: Decision-Native Vision-Language Modelling for Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32478)
*Cristian Tommasino,Antonio M. Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出决策原生模型PathJev-P（MedGemma-1.5-4B + LoRA + 决策头），在隐式病理数据上直接输出校准的候选分布；准确率优于生成式同骨干模型、CONCH与通用医学决策模型，但校准优势依赖于生成式模型概率的读取方式，且线性探针校准更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向决策的视觉-语言模型在病理图像中很少能给出用户所关心的候选集上的校准概率；作者希望用"决策原生"架构（输入中写入问题与运行时选定的候选集，单次前向由小决策头输出归一化分布）来解决该问题。

Method: 以MedGemma-1.5-4B为骨干，加LoRA适配器与决策头构建PathJev-P；训练数据为三个公开数据集的40,000个图像块状态，专家标签被转化为带器官特异的候选集及"none of these"选项的类型化问题；与同骨干同数据同选择规则的生成式模型、零样本病理模型CONCH、通用医学决策模型对比，3个随机种子、预先固定分析方案，测试集与骨干训练数据无记录重叠。

Result: 平衡准确率0.541，对比生成式模型0.492、CONCH 0.345、通用医学决策模型0.213；对生成式模型的优势来自两个训练中未出现的器官。校准方面：采用既往工作的候选对数似然求和读法时PathJev-P ECE更低（0.204 vs 0.279）；改用长度归一化读法（事后检验）后两者平均ECE相近（0.201），且生成式模型在未见器官上校准更好，PathJev-P仍过度自信。病理基础模型上的监督线性探针在所有设定下校准最佳。全切片定性演示中，PathJev-P对无监督组织簇给出带弃权的标注，并在仅使用部分图块的情况下区分癌与其他组织。

Conclusion: 决策原生架构在准确率上具优势，但其校准优势对生成式基线概率的读取方式敏感，且并未在未见器官与校准上全面胜出；简单线性探针在校准上更强，提示决策原生模型的校准仍需改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models used for decisions in histopathology rarely return calibrated probabilities over the candidates a user cares about. Decision-native models write a question and a candidate set chosen at run time into the input and return a normalised distribution from a small decision head in one forward pass. We build PathJev-P, a decision-native model for histopathology: MedGemma-1.5-4B with LoRA adapters and a decision head, trained on 40,000 patch states from three public collections whose expert labels are turned into typed questions with organ-specific candidate sets and a "none of these" option. In a comparison over 3 seeds with an analysis plan fixed before testing, on test sets without documented overlap with the training data of the backbone, PathJev-P is more accurate than a generative model trained with the same backbone, data and selection rule, the zero-shot pathology model CONCH and a general medical decision model (balanced accuracy 0.541 against 0.492, 0.345 and 0.213); its advantage over the generative model comes from two organs absent from training. The calibration advantage depends on how the generative model's probabilities are read out. With the summed candidate log-likelihood used in previous work, PathJev-P has the lower calibration error (ECE 0.204 against 0.279); with a length-normalised read-out of the same generative model, examined post hoc, the two have similar mean calibration error (0.201) and the generative model is better calibrated on the unseen organs, where PathJev-P remains over-confident. A supervised linear probe on a pathology foundation model is better calibrated in every setting. In a qualitative whole-slide demonstration, PathJev-P labels unsupervised tissue clusters with abstention and separates carcinoma from other tissue while running the model on a fraction of the tiles.

</details>


### [71] [PCFBench: How Far Can Large Vision-Language Models Go in Physics-aware Photonic Inverse Design?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32485)
*Shengchao Chen,Ting Shu,Sufen Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个评测视觉语言模型（VLM）在光子晶体光纤（PCF）逆向设计全流程中感知-推理-决策能力的综合基准PCFBench，揭示出"感知-设计鸿沟"与多智能体误差级联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCF设计长期依赖人工专家，需将几何直觉与波导物理深度耦合，属专家密集型流程；VLM有望实现自动化设计，但其究竟能真正从显微图像中进行光子物理推理，还是仅识别视觉模式，尚缺乏系统评估手段。

Method: 构建PCFBench基准：基于18类结构多样的光纤家族、超过22万条FDTD仿真样本，设计26项任务，划分为认知需求递增的六大类别（几何感知、物理理解、文本生成、多模态推理、逆向设计决策、代码生成）；既对单个VLM在孤立任务上评测，也对多智能体系统（MAS）在从感知到设计的完整串行流水线上评测。

Result: 发现显著的"感知-设计鸿沟"：现有模型能以中等成功率识别光纤几何结构，却始终无法将视觉理解转化为物理感知的设计动作；同时流水线式智能体级联导致明显误差传播，上游感知错误在下游逐级放大为设计失败。
