Conclusion: 模型规模扩展与朴素智能体串联均不足以解决问题，闭环设计需要显式的物理 grounding；PCFBench为AI辅助光子工程提供了严谨、多维度的评测试验台。

Abstract: Photonic crystal fibers (PCFs) underpin critical applications in telecommunications, sensing, and high-power laser delivery, yet their design remains a manual, expert-intensive process that couples geometric intuition with deep waveguide physics. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising path toward automating PCF design, but can they truly reason about photonic physics from microscopy images, or do they merely recognize visual patterns? We introduce PCFBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLMs on the perception, reasoning, and decision steps of the photonic inverse-design loop. PCFBench spans 26 tasks organized into six categories of increasing cognitive demand (Geometry Perception, Physics Understanding, Text Generation, Multi-modal Reasoning, Inverse-Design Decision, and Code Generation), built from over 220K FDTD simulation samples across 18 structurally diverse fiber families. We evaluate both individual VLMs on isolated tasks and multi-agent systems (MAS) on the full sequential pipeline from perception to design. Our results reveal a striking perception-design gap: current models can identify fiber geometry with moderate success, yet consistently fail to translate that visual understanding into physics-aware design actions. Moreover, cascading agents through the pipeline exposes significant error propagation, where upstream perception mistakes compound into downstream design failures. These findings suggest that neither model scaling nor naive agent chaining suffices, and that closing the loop demands explicit physical grounding. PCFBench provides the community with a rigorous, multi-dimensional testbed to measure progress toward AI-assisted photonic engineering. The project is available at https://pcfbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [72] [Efficient Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection via Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32486)
*Khaled Waleed Dawoud,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAAF框架通过双信号自适应采样、阈值门控级联架构和信号感知加权融合，实现高效零样本视频异常检测，在多个基准上达到零样本SOTA并以2倍以上吞吐量提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）用于视频异常检测时，其语义推理的计算开销与真实视频流的规模不匹配，存在根本性约束，需在保持检测性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Method: 提出SAAF框架，含三项创新：(1) 语义嵌入空间中的双信号自适应采样器——delta信号用帧间余弦距离捕捉突发事件，drift信号用偏离滚动时间上下文的程度检测静态和渐变异常，二者OR门控结合自适应EMA阈值，无需训练数据；(2) 阈值门控级联架构，先做轻量帧级评分，仅在置信度足够时才调用昂贵的视频级时序分析；(3) 信号感知加权融合机制，根据触发帧选择的信号动态调整分数组合。

Result: 在四个基准上，SAAF以显著更低成本取得有竞争力精度。在免训练零样本方法中，于XD-Violence (92.07%)、MSAD (86.70%)、ShanghaiTech (80.54%) AUC-ROC上新SOTA，其中XD-Violence和MSAD还超越有监督基线；在XD-Violence、UCF-Crime、MSAD上分别维持44.95、48.61、41.06 FPS，相较此前最佳零样本方法实现2.43×、2.63×、2.22×吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: SAAF有效解决了VLM视频异常检测的计算瓶颈，在零样本条件下兼顾高精度与高吞吐，证明了自适应信号采样与级联门控机制在效率-精度权衡上的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models for video anomaly detection confronts a fundamental constraint. Their semantic reasoning demands computational budgets incompatible with the scale of real-world video streams. We present Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF), a framework that achieves efficient zero-shot anomaly detection through three key innovations. First, we introduce a dual-signal adaptive sampler operating in semantic embedding space: a delta signal captures sudden events via frame-to-frame cosine distance, while a drift signal detects static and gradual anomalies through deviation from rolling temporal context. Their OR-gated combination with adaptive EMA-based thresholding provides comprehensive anomaly coverage without training data. Second, we propose a threshold-gated cascaded architecture where lightweight frame-level scoring precedes expensive video-level temporal analysis, invoking it only when confidence warrants additional context. Third, we design a signal-aware weighted fusion mechanism that dynamically adapts score combination based on which signal triggered frame selection. Across four benchmarks, SAAF delivers competitive accuracy at substantially lower cost. Among training-free zero-shot methods, SAAF sets a new state of the art on XD-Violence (92.07%), MSAD (86.70%), and ShanghaiTech (80.54%) AUC-ROC, with XD-Violence and MSAD also surpassing supervised baselines. SAAF sustains 44.95, 48.61, and 41.06 FPS on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and MSAD, a 2.43$\times$, 2.63$\times$, and 2.22$\times$ throughput improvement over the prior best zero-shot method. Code: https://github.com/kdawoud91/SAAF

</details>


### [73] [L0-Norm-Based CycleGAN for Unpaired Brain CT-to-MR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32491)
*Yunxiao Zhang,Shouqiang Du,Zexian Xu,Chuanlong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文用平滑有界的函数近似L0范数构造循环一致性损失，替代CycleGAN中标准L1范数惩罚，以提升非配对CT→MR合成的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT与MR对脑部成像提供互补信息，但难以同时采集。CycleGAN可实现非配对CT-to-MR合成，然而标准L1范数循环一致性惩罚随残差幅值线性增长，使网络偏向关注大残差，对小残差不够敏感。

Method: 引入基于L0范数的循环一致性项，并用一个平滑且有界的函数近似L0范数；该近似既提高对小残差的敏感性，又有效抑制大残差的影响。与仅循环一致性惩罚不同（L1范数）的基线模型在其他设置完全相同条件下对比，训练集102幅、测试集9幅图像。

Result: 相比L1基线：MSE由0.03464降至0.02746（降低20.7%）；平均PSNR由14.77 dB升至15.80 dB；平均SSIM由0.5407升至0.5689。此外最大MSE更低，最小PSNR与最小SSIM均更高。

Conclusion: 在该数据集上，修改循环一致性惩罚形式可提升重建精度；L0范数近似的循环一致性损失优于L1范数基线，且在最差样本指标上同样更稳健。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance (MR) imaging provide complementary information for the brain, but acquiring both modalities is not always possible. The cycle-consistent adversarial network (CycleGAN) enables unpaired CT-to-MR synthesis, but because the standard L1-norm cycle?consistency penalty grows linearly with the residual magnitude, the network tends to focus on large residuals. This paper introduces an L0-norm-based cycle?consistency term, in which the L0-norm is approximated by a smooth and bounded function. This approximation not only increases sensitivity to small residuals but also effectively suppresses the influence of large residuals. The proposed model and an otherwise identical L1-norm baseline were trained on 102 images and evaluated on 9 test images. The proposed model achieves a lower mean squared error (0.02746 versus 0.03464, a 20.7% reduction), a higher mean peak signal-to-noise ratio (15.80 versus 14.77 dB), and a higher mean structural similarity index (0.5689 versus 0.5407). It also has a lower maximum MSE and higher minimum PSNR and SSIM than the baseline. These results indicate that modifying the cycle-consistency penalty can improve reconstruction accuracy on this dataset.

</details>


### [74] [K-CLUE: A Segmentation-to-Clustering Framework for Ki67 Cell Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32493)
*Ana Beatriz Vieira,Sara P. Oliveira,Diana Montezuma,João Fraga,Jaime S. Cardoso,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结预训练视觉表征在无标注Ki67细胞分型中的作用，提出基于K-CLUE框架（核分割+预训练编码器+PCA+k-means）的方案，发现原始主成分聚类对编码器选择高度敏感（F1波动44点），而对保留主成分做白化可将跨编码器差异压缩至1.00个F1点；端到端结果在MIST与BCData上优于DeepLIIF和IHCScoreGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌Ki67免疫组化图像的定量通常依赖按数据集分别训练的有监督分类器，缺乏标注时难以迁移。作者希望探究冻结预训练表征能否支撑无需标注、无需任务特定训练的Ki67细胞分型，并厘清其成功或失败的条件。

Method: 采用K-CLUE框架：先分割单个细胞核，用预训练编码器表示每个核，经主成分分析（PCA）降维后，以k-means聚类区分Ki67阳性与阴性核，全程无任务特定训练与人工标注。实验比较七种编码器，并将K-CLUE与DeepLIIF、IHCScoreGAN进行端到端对比；同时分析嵌入空间的方差结构，检验白化与手工颜色表征的效果。

Result: 在MIST匹配核上，七种编码器的Ki67阳性F1相差达44点，但Ki67状态在每种表征上均线性可分（有监督探针AUC>0.98）。聚类成功与否取决于DAB相关变异是否占据方差主方向；对保留主成分白化后跨编码器F1差距降至1.00点。手工颜色表征同样具竞争力但需显式选择颜色空间。端到端K-CLUE在MIST上阳性/阴性F1为85.00%/81.91%，在BCData上为79.59%/81.16%，两个数据集均优于DeepLIIF与IHCScoreGAN。

Conclusion: 预训练表征可在无需标注的条件下支持Ki67分型，前提是其方差结构得到恰当处理（如白化）；该方法在两个公开数据集上超越现有端到端基线，为无标注Ki67定量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ki67 is a key marker of tumor proliferation in breast cancer, but its quantification from immunohistochemistry images normally depends on supervised classifiers trained per dataset. We examine the role of frozen pretrained visual representations in annotation-free Ki67 cell typing. To this end, we use K-CLUE, a framework that segments individual nuclei, represents each nucleus with a pretrained encoder, reduces the resulting embeddings with principal component analysis, and separates Ki67-positive from Ki67-negative nuclei using k-means clustering, without task-specific training or manual annotations. Clustering the raw components is highly sensitive to the choice of encoder: across seven encoders, the Ki67-positive F1-score on matched MIST nuclei varies by 44 points, although Ki67 status remains linearly decodable from every representation, with supervised probe AUCs above 0.98. Analysis of the embedding spaces shows that clustering succeeds when DAB-related variation occupies the leading directions of variance. Whitening the retained principal components removes this dependence, reducing the spread across encoders to 1.00 F1-score point. Handcrafted color representations are also competitive, confirming that Ki67 status is strongly reflected in chromatic information, but they require an explicit choice of color space. In end-to-end evaluation, K-CLUE reaches F1-scores of 85.00% and 81.91% for Ki67-positive and Ki67-negative nuclei on MIST, and 79.59% and 81.16% on BCData, outperforming DeepLIIF and IHCScoreGAN on both datasets. These results show that pretrained representations can support annotation-free Ki67 typing when their variance structure is accounted for.

</details>


### [75] [Distortion-Aware Spatiotemporal Learning for Real-Time Wide-Angle Video Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32500)
*Lang Nie,Yihan Yang,Wenbo Nie,Hongguang Zhu,Yang Wei,Chunyu Lin,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FastVC：基于畸变感知时空学习的全身体广角视频矫正框架，通过DA-TPS重映射控制点为周边密集布局实现更准几何拟合且不增计算，并用邻域时空注意力与稀疏时空监督实现因果稳定的视频矫正，在首个广角全身体视频矫正基准上达到SOTA保真度与视觉稳定性，57.0 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广角镜头扩大视场但造成图像边界附近严重非线性畸变、拉伸人物；现有方法多聚焦人脸、计算开销大、缺乏时序建模，导致全身矫正不完整、难以实时部署、帧间抖动明显。

Method: 1) 基于镜头物理的畸变感知薄板样条模块（DA-TPS），将控制点非线性重映射为周边密集布局，在不增加计算代价下提升几何拟合精度；2) 双层时空学习：模型层面引入邻域时空注意力（NST），以低开销聚合局部上下文并选择性融合历史特征；优化层面采用稀疏时空监督，仅对锚帧施加空间对齐，解耦逐帧精度与时序稳定性；3) 构建首个广角全身体视频矫正基准数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明FastVC在矫正保真度与视觉稳定性上均达SOTA，运行速度57.0 FPS，满足实时性需求。

Conclusion: FastVC以畸变感知的几何映射与双层时空学习实现了高效、因果、稳定的全身体广角视频矫正，兼顾精度、时序稳定性与实时性能，并提供了该任务的基准数据集。

Abstract: While wide-angle lenses provide an expanded field of view (FoV), they exhibit severe nonlinear distortion near image boundaries, unnaturally stretching people in these regions. Existing methods mostly focus on facial regions, require high computational costs, and lack temporal modeling, resulting in incomplete full-body rectification, impractical real-time deployment, and noticeable inter-frame shakes. To tackle these issues, we propose \textbf{FastVC}, an efficient full-body video rectification framework driven by distortion-aware spatiotemporal learning. Grounded in lens physics, FastVC introduces a distortion-aware thin-plate spline (DA-TPS) module that nonlinearly remaps control points into a peripheral-dense layout, enabling more accurate geometric fitting without additional computational cost. We further develop a dual-level spatiotemporal learning scheme for causal and stable video rectification. At the model level, neighborhood spatiotemporal attention (NST) aggregates local context and selectively fuses historical features with low overhead. At the optimization level, sparse spatiotemporal supervision applies spatial alignment only to anchor frames, decoupling frame-wise accuracy from temporal stability. Besides, we contribute the first benchmark dataset for wide-angle full-body video correction. Extensive experiments show that FastVC achieves state-of-the-art rectification fidelity and visual stability at 57.0 FPS.

</details>


### [76] [Augmentation Strategy for DINO in Glaucoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32511)
*Ashish Kumar Meena,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出DINO默认的裁剪增强在视网膜眼底图像上产生不可靠表征，依据“裁剪视图冗余最小化且保留临床信号”原则重新设计增强策略，使表征偏向诊断关键特征，在七个基准数据集上实现强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习（SSL）在医学图像表征学习中颇具前景，但在自然图像上验证有效的增强策略难以迁移到专业医学影像域。作者对DINO进行实证分析，发现其默认增强策略在视网膜眼底图像上产生不可靠表征，并使下游青光眼分类性能下降，因此需要针对医学影像重新设计增强。

Method: 对基于知识蒸馏的DINO框架及其默认多裁剪增强进行实证分析；以“有效裁剪视图应共享最少冗余信息同时保留临床相关信号”为准则，重新设计DINO的增强策略，引导学习到的表征聚焦诊断关键特征而非低级图像统计量。

Result: 在冻结表征上训练线性分类器进行青光眼分类，在七个基准数据集上取得强泛化性能；代码与权重已公开。

Conclusion: 自然图像验证的SSL增强不能直接迁移到医学影像；基于冗余最小化与临床信号保留原则定制的增强可显著提升DINO表征在青光眼分类中的可靠性，并具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a promising approach to medical image representation learning, yet augmentation strategies of SSL frameworks validated on natural images do not reliably transfer to specialized medical imaging domains. We analyze DINO, a knowledge-distillation based SSL framework, and empirically demonstrate that its default augmentation strategy produces unreliable representations for retinal fundus images, degrading downstream glaucoma classification performance. Guided by the principle that effective cropped views should share minimal redundant information while retaining clinically relevant signal, we redesign DINO's augmentation strategy. Our approach biases the learned representations toward diagnostically critical features rather than low-level image statistics. We evaluate our method for glaucoma classification by training a linear classifier on the frozen representations, achieving strong generalization across seven benchmark datasets. URL for code and weights: https://github.com/ashish-256/task_dino

</details>


### [77] [Fast and Useful: Adapting Distilled Generators for Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32512)
*Patryk Bartkowiak,Dominik Michels,Sören Pirk,Wojciech Pałubicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对蒸馏文本到图像模型的学生第一步预测拟合低秩更新，使其跃迁到无引导基模型的中间状态，随后禁用更新、由原学生完成生成；适配后的图像可训练出显著更好的分类器，且推理保持快速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏生成器采样快，但其生成的图像训练出的分类器性能明显弱于慢速基模型生成的图像，因此需要在不牺牲生成速度的前提下改善蒸馏模型的训练数据质量。

Method: 向学生模型首次预测加一个低秩更新，使其到达无引导基模型产生的中间状态，然后关闭该更新，让未改动的学生继续完成生成；基模型仅提供监督目标，推理时不需要。通过消融比较不同监督状态与跳步长度。

Result: 在三个生成器家族上，适配图像训练的分类器优于已发布学生模型生成的图像。全规模ImageNet-1k、类名模板下，ResNet-50 top-1由8.4%升至34.2%（FLUX.2-klein，每图2次去噪评估）和由12.7%升至32.1%（SD-Turbo，每图3次）。消融显示引导目标与更短跳步会降低分类器精度。固定适配器在更多类别、细粒度数据集、其他学习器架构与训练配方上同样有效。

Conclusion: 仅适配单个生成步即可大幅提升快速生成器作为视觉模型训练数据源的价值；模板化适配相对更丰富提示方案仍有效，而与LLM生成提示结合带来的增益因生成器而异。

Abstract: Distilled text-to-image models generate images quickly, but classifiers trained on those images can perform substantially worse than classifiers trained on images from their slower base models. We adapt released distilled generators to produce better training data while retaining fast generation. We fit a low-rank update to the student's first prediction so that it reaches an intermediate state produced by its unguided base, then disable the update and let the unchanged student finish generation. The base supplies training targets and is absent at inference. Guided targets and shorter jumps give classifiers lower accuracy in our ablations, showing why the state used for supervision matters. Across three generator families, the adapted images train better classifiers than images from the released students. With class-name templates at full ImageNet-1k scale, ResNet-50 top-1 rises from 8.4% to 34.2% for FLUX.2-klein and from 12.7% to 32.1% for SD-Turbo, using two and three denoiser evaluations per image, respectively. Fixed adapters also improve results on additional classes, fine-grained datasets, and other learner architectures and training recipes. Template-based adaptation remains useful against richer-prompt alternatives, while the benefit of combining adaptation with LLM generation prompts depends on the generator. Adapting a single generation step makes fast generators substantially more useful for training visual models.

</details>


### [78] [VERA-Hand: A Verified Multidimensional Benchmark for 2D Hand Pose Estimation in Third-Person Dynamic Gesture Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32517)
*Gibran Benitez-Garcia,Hiroki Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VERA-Hand 手部姿态估计评测基准，用人工核验的 10,690 只手帧级 2D 标注，从检测、定位、身份识别、时间稳定性四个维度及联合 Pose-F1 指标评测估计器，发现无单一方法全面占优且排序随维度剧烈变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部姿态估计器多用 PCK、MPJPE 等帧级匹配定位指标评测，忽略了漏检/误检手、左右手判别错误及时间不稳定等问题，评测视角单一。

Method: 构建 VERA-Hand 基准：提供人工核验的帧级 2D 真值标注（10,690 只手），设计覆盖检测、定位、身份识别、时间稳定性四个互补维度的评测协议，并提出要求检测与定位均正确的联合 Pose-F1 指标；对 6 个估计器（手部专用、全身、重建类、基础模型四条范式）进行评估，并开展事后清理案例研究与分层分析。

Result: 无估计器在所有维度占优，排名随维度显著重排；基准揭示了仅定位或下游评测难以发现的失效，例如某近期基础模型在超过 85% 的帧中存在左右手重叠；事后清理案例验证了基准可用于评估纠错干预，分层分析显示鲁棒性随运动模糊类别与手部区域而变化。

Conclusion: 多维联合评测对手部姿态估计至关重要，VERA-Hand 提供了可复现的标注、协议与代码用于诊断与改进估计器。

Abstract: Hand landmarks serve as input for gesture recognition, sign language understanding, and touchless human-computer interaction. Yet hand pose estimators are typically evaluated with frame-wise localization metrics such as PCK or MPJPE, which are computed over matched predictions and overlook missed or spurious hands, handedness errors, and temporal instability. To address these limitations, we present VERA-Hand, a benchmark that evaluates estimators using manually verified, frame-level 2D annotations for 10,690 hands across four complementary dimensions (detection, localization, identification, and temporal stability), as well as a joint Pose-F1 metric that requires both correct detection and accurate localization. Evaluating six estimators spanning hand-specific, whole-body, reconstruction-based, and foundation-model paradigms, we find that no estimator dominates: rankings reorder sharply across dimensions. VERA-Hand also exposes failures that localization-only and downstream evaluations may not reveal, including cross-hand overlap in over 85% of frames for a recent foundation model. A post-hoc cleanup case study demonstrates how the benchmark can validate corrective interventions, while stratified analyses show that robustness varies across motion-blur classes and hand regions. We release the verified annotations, protocol, and code at https://gibranbenitez.github.io/VERA_Hand/

</details>


### [79] [HEALPix-Based Implicit Neural Compression of Omnidirectional Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32561)
*Yulong Cheng,Youneng Bao,Junfeng Zhou,Mu Li,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于多尺度HEALPix球面表示的过拟合编解码器，实现等面积采样与高效层级建模，在全景图像上显著优于VVC及ERP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像实时解码对沉浸式VR至关重要。过拟合编解码器用轻量隐式神经表示(INR)替代变换式LIC以降低解码复杂度，但现有方法在ERP投影上工作，存在球面采样非均匀、空间畸变、两极冗余采样和纬度相关感受野等问题。

Method: 采用多尺度Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization (HEALPix)表示构建球面过拟合编解码器，实现等面积采样与球面信号的高效层级建模；提出Spherical Inter-Scale Conditional熵模型，利用HEALPix隐变量间的空间依赖与跨尺度相关性提升编码效率；引入Multi-Ring Spherical Convolution，在各纬度上以近似均匀的球面感受野聚合邻域像素。

Result: 在公开全景图像数据集上，相对VVC在V-PSNR、V-MS-SSIM、V-LPIPS上分别取得16.3%、26.5%、19.5%的BD-rate降低；相较基于ERP的Cool-Chic 5.0在V-PSNR上再降13.1% BD-rate，同时解码速度比所评估的学习型自编码器编解码器快2.6–3.6倍。

Conclusion: HEALPix等面积球面表示结合跨尺度条件熵建模与球面卷积，可有效消除ERP投影缺陷，在提升全景压缩率的同时保持低解码复杂度，适合实时VR应用。

Abstract: Real-time decoding of omnidirectional images is essential for immersive virtual reality (VR) experiences. Compared with conventional transform-based learned image compression (LIC), overfitted codecs offer lower decoding complexity by representing each image with a lightweight implicit neural representation (INR). However, existing approaches typically operate on the equirectangular projection (ERP), which introduces nonuniform spherical sampling and spatial distortion, leading to redundant sampling near the poles and latitude-dependent receptive fields. To address these limitations, we propose a spherical overfitted codec based on a multi-scale Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization (HEALPix) representation, which enables equal-area sampling and efficient hierarchical modeling of spherical signals. Building on this representation, we develop a Spherical Inter-Scale Conditional entropy model that exploits spatial dependencies and cross-scale correlations among HEALPix latents to improve coding efficiency. We further introduce a Multi-Ring Spherical Convolution that aggregates neighboring pixels over a nearly uniform spherical receptive field across latitudes. Experiments on a public omnidirectional image dataset demonstrate BD-rate reductions of 16.3%, 26.5%, and 19.5% over VVC in terms of V-PSNR, V-MS-SSIM, and V-LPIPS, respectively. Our method also achieves a 13.1% BD-rate reduction over ERP-based Cool-Chic 5.0 in terms of V-PSNR, while providing 2.6-3.6x faster decoding than the evaluated learned autoencoder codecs.

</details>


### [80] [Reuse, Don't Retrain: Efficient, Modality-Versatile Geospatial Foundation Models through Feature Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32566)
*Mario Hamon,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究以特征蒸馏高效复用已有地理空间基础模型（GeoFM）表示：将冻结的CROMA与TerraMind教师蒸馏到小型ViT，并提出融合多模态专家的非配对多专家方法TEDI，显著提升精度-算力前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeoFM预训练成本高昂，而现有模型家族在不同骨干规模与传感模态上重复预训练，造成算力浪费。作者希望以蒸馏替代独立预训练，低成本复用已有表示，并整合互补的多模态专家。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1) 通过patch级特征匹配，将冻结的CROMA与TerraMind教师蒸馏到更小的ViT（Tiny/Small/Base）；(2) 提出TEDI——非配对多专家架构，用独立patch embedding将非配对的输入分别路由至共享Transformer主干，融合TerraMind（多光谱）与DINOv3（高分辨率RGB），无需共配准数据。

Result: 在PANGAEA九个任务上，蒸馏的TerraMind Tiny/Small/Base分别比同规模已发布模型高出2.76、3.02、1.87 mean IoU，且各自达到或超过大一级的已发布模型；蒸馏CROMA-Base提升2.05点。TerraMind-Base仅需43 A6000 GPU小时，约为独立预训练成本的0.5%。TEDI-Large取得PANGAEA-9最高单模型平均62.51 mIoU，整体超过两位教师并保留各自模态优势；TEDI-Base以少72%参数达到61.75 mIoU。

Conclusion: 特征蒸馏可显著改善GeoFM的精度-算力权衡，并将互补的多模态专家整合为单一通用骨干，为GeoFM的高效复用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) are costly to pretrain, yet model families often repeat this process across backbone scales and sensing modalities. We investigate feature distillation as a more efficient way to reuse existing representations. First, we distill frozen CROMA and TerraMind teachers into smaller Vision Transformers through patch-level feature matching. Across nine PANGAEA tasks, distilled TerraMind Tiny, Small, and Base outperform their scale-matched released counterparts by 2.76, 3.02, and 1.87 mean IoU points, respectively; each also matches or exceeds the next-larger released model. Distilled CROMA-Base gains 2.05 points. TerraMind-Base required only 43 A6000 GPU-hours, approximately 0.5\% of the reported cost of independent pretraining. Second, we introduce TEDI, an unpaired multi-expert approach combining TerraMind for multispectral imagery and DINOv3 for high-resolution RGB. Separate patch embeddings route unpaired inputs into a shared Transformer trunk, removing the need for co-registered data. TEDI-Large achieves the highest single-model mean in our PANGAEA-9 comparison (62.51 mIoU), outperforming both teachers overall while retaining their modality-specific strengths. TEDI-Base reaches 61.75 mIoU with 72\% fewer parameters. These results show that distillation improves the GeoFM accuracy-compute frontier while consolidating complementary experts into a single versatile backbone.

</details>


### [81] [Standing on Open Shoulders: Regional Fine-Tuning of Open-Weight Camera-Trap Models for UK Wildlife with Modest Data and Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32573)
*Paul Fergus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 小团队利用开源权重在单张GPU上微调DeepFaune，构建英国区域分类器，strict准确率达90.6%，但最大的同类比较增益来自他人训练的新基座模型，凸显开源权重民主化的依赖性与脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱物种分类由资源充足机构的大模型主导，而区域保护（如英国本土红松鼠与入侵灰松鼠的区分）所需的物种级细分往往缺失。作者提出问题：开放权重能否让小团队以适度数据集和单张GPU实质参与AI开发。

Method: 微调开放式DeepFaune分类器至28个英国类别，搭配未修改的MegaDetector v6；在20,212张人工核验的MammalWeb公民科学图像上，去除与训练档案共享1,039张图像中任一的序列后，与DeepFaune v1.5.0和SpeciesNet对比。随后将相同流程（不加新数据）应用于基于DINOv3的新分类器；并评估仅用10%训练裁剪的效果与资源开销。

Result: DeepFaune-UK strict图像级准确率88.8%（含空帧），基线为56.5%与59.0%；但在DeepFaune较粗标签粒度上v1.5.0更高（91.4% vs 88.8%，重采样下不显著）。新流程得到v1.3 strict准确率90.6%，同类差距缩至0.8点，误判为黇鹿的狍减少69%。在1,850张无重叠图像的26位贡献者子集上，三模型无显著差异（90.5%/90.6%/89.9%）。仅用10%训练裁剪（每类约150）OOD准确率与原全量相近，DINOv2子集训练约22分钟、显存不足4 GB。

Conclusion: 开放权重使小团队开发区域模型可行，但最大的同类增益来自他人训练的新基座模型，因此这种民主化依赖于大机构持续以开放权重发布，且许可条款可能变化。

Abstract: Camera-trap species classification is dominated by large models from well-resourced organisations, which often lack the species-level distinctions regional conservation needs, such as native red versus invasive grey squirrels in the UK. We ask whether open weights let a small team with a modest dataset and a single GPU take a real part in AI development. We fine-tuned the open DeepFaune classifier on 28 UK classes, paired it with the unmodified MegaDetector v6, and compared the resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.2 with DeepFaune v1.5.0 and SpeciesNet on 20,212 hand-verified MammalWeb citizen-science images, after removing sequences sharing any of 1,039 images with our training archive. DeepFaune-UK achieved a strict image-level accuracy of 88.8%, including empty frames, against 56.5% and 59.0% for the baselines, but at DeepFaune's coarser label resolution DeepFaune v1.5.0 was more accurate, though not significantly under contributor resampling (91.4% vs. 88.8%). As DeepFaune v1.5.0 uses a newer DINOv3-based classifier, we applied the identical recipe, without new data, to that classifier. The resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.3 reached 90.6% strict accuracy, narrowed the like-for-like gap to 0.8 points and reduced roe deer misclassified as fallow deer by 69%. On 1,850 images from the 26 contributors with no overlapping images, the three DeepFaune-based models did not differ significantly (90.5%, 90.6%, 89.9% like-for-like). Fine-tuning on 10% of the training crops (about 150 per class) gave out-of-distribution accuracy similar to using all of them, and a DINOv2 run on this subset took about 22 minutes with under 4 GB of GPU memory. Open weights thus make regional model development feasible for small teams, but the largest like-for-like gain came from a newer base model trained by others, so this democratisation depends on large actors continuing to release open weights, under licences that may change.

</details>


### [82] [Hair Segmentation and Skin Characterization on Macroscopic Dermatological Images for Laser Treatment Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32609)
*Alex Navarro Navarro,Marc Arriaga Hidalgo,Carlos Moreno Rubio,Maxime Talarmain,Noelia Vaquero Gallardo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个面向宏观皮肤镜图像的统一毛发分割与皮肤特征表征系统，结合定制YOLOv9实例分割与经典图像处理，用分光光度法做颜色校准，提取光型、毛发密度、颜色与粗细等客观参数，mAP@50≈0.80。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光疗设备（激光脱毛、IPL、血管激光、皮肤光疗）依赖主观人工设参或未校准的点式色度计，缺乏客观、可量化的皮肤与毛发特征描述。

Method: 构建统一流水线：定制训练YOLOv9变体做实例分割，与经典图像处理融合于单一工作流；引入基于分光光度计测量的颜色校准环节以消除光照影响；数据集为复现真实临床激光治疗的宏观图像（含治疗后常见的短、剃毛毛发），而非受控皮肤镜数据。

Result: 在自建短/剃毛图像数据集上取得具竞争力的分割指标（mAP@50≈0.80），并可输出光型、毛发密度、颜色与粗细等客观参数；颜色校准保证不同光照下特征估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 该流水线可为未来自动化激光参数设定提供客观的基础皮肤与毛发特征，具备可行性，并可推广至更广泛的光基医学治疗。

Abstract: Current light-based devices, such as laser hair removal, intense pulsed light (IPL), vascular laser therapy, and dermatologic phototherapy, are widely used in clinical dermatology but rely heavily on subjective manual parameterization or uncalibrated point-based colorimeters. To overcome these limitations, this work presents a unified system for hair segmentation and skin characterization in macroscopic dermatological images. Unlike most existing literature that relies on dermoscopic datasets captured under highly controlled conditions, our approach is specifically designed for macroscopic images that replicate real clinical laser treatments. The proposed pipeline integrates a custom-trained variant of the YOLOv9 deep learning architecture for instance segmentation and classical image processing within a single workflow to extract objective skin and hair parameters such as phototype, hair density, color, and thickness. A color calibration stage using spectrophotometric measurements ensures accurate feature estimation regardless of illumination conditions. Experimental results show competitive segmentation metrics (mAP@50 $\approx$ 0.80) on a custom dataset featuring short, shaved hairs, typical of pre-treatment scenarios. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed pipeline in providing the objective, foundational skin and hair features required for future automated laser parameterization, paving the way for its extension to broader light-based medical therapies.

</details>


### [83] [DriveOSS: Dataset and Benchmark for 3D Anomaly Segmentation and Open-set Semantic Segmentation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32612)
*Zhiran Yan,Savankumar Bhanderi,Sourabh Lolge,Rui Song,Girdharbhai Paneliya,Shiva Agrawal,Gordon Elger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个真实世界多模态数据集与基准 DriveOSS，面向 3D 异常分割与开放集语义分割，包含闭环检测之外的未知目标场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 语义分割方法均在闭集假设、标准驾驶条件下开发评估，所有类别预先已知；而真实自动驾驶必然遇到未见过的目标，闭集感知无法支撑安全部署，且缺乏真实世界多模态开放集/异常分割数据与基准。

Method: 构建 DriveOSS 数据集：6 路环视 RGB 相机 + 128 线 LiDAR + GPS/IMU 同步采集并标注，共 12,661 帧；覆盖晴天、夜间、多云、雨天、雪天五种天气下的 52 个真实驾驶场景；划分为 ID（仅已知类，用于训练）与 OOD（额外包含标注为 outlier 类的未知目标，用于验证与测试）。同时给出基准协议与代码。

Result: 大量实验表明，未见目标仍是当前 3D 感知系统的主要挑战；现有方法在开放集/异常分割上性能不足。

Conclusion: DriveOSS 为 3D 异常分割与开放集语义分割提供首个真实世界多模态基准，暴露闭集感知在安全部署中的局限；数据集、基准协议与代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D semantic segmentation have significantly improved scene understanding in autonomous driving. However, existing methods are typically developed under a closed-set assumption and evaluated under standard driving conditions, where all categories are known in advance. In real-world driving, autonomous vehicles inevitably encounter previously unseen objects, making closed-set perception insufficient for safe deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce DriveOSS, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first real-world multimodal dataset and benchmark specifically designed for 3D anomaly segmentation and open-set semantic segmentation. It contains 12,661 synchronized and annotated frames captured using six surround-view RGB cameras, a 128-beam LiDAR, and a GPS/IMU unit. The dataset comprises 52 real-world driving scenes collected under clear, nighttime, cloudy, rainy, and snowy conditions and is organized into ID (in-distribution) and OOD (out-of-distribution) subsets. The ID scenes contain only known classes and are used for training, whereas the OOD scenes additionally contain unknown objects annotated as the outlier class and are used for validation and testing. Extensive experiments show that unseen objects remain a major challenge for current 3D perception systems. The dataset, benchmark protocols, and code are available at https://github.com/chrisyan/DriveOSS.

</details>


### [84] [EditJev: System One Routing for Image Editing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32635)
*Zhile Guan,Xu Jiang,Yule Duan,Gongbo Zhang,Shuyu Wang,Xiao Chen,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EditJev——一种"系统1"式路由模型，用单次前向直接预测样本级模型偏好分数，结合速度分数在质量与延迟间权衡，用于指令式图像编辑模型的动态选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式图像编辑模型存在显著的样本级性能差异：同一图像与指令下不同模型输出差异大，最优模型随请求变化；同时各模型推理延迟差异明显，固定单一模型部署效率低。因此需研究动态选择编辑器的路由问题。

Method: EditJev在视觉-语言骨干上接轻量偏好头，一次前向输出固定维度的分数向量，避免自回归生成模型名与响应解析。训练时以离线候选输出构建成对偏好，优化成对排序目标以学习相对模型适配性；推理时将预测偏好分数与模型级速度分数结合。

Result: 在ImgEdit上聚合质量由最强固定编辑器的4.540提升至4.592，GEdit-En上由8.194提升至8.255；估计端到端延迟分别降低60.8%与31.7%。

Conclusion: 直接、结构化的模型路由对高效图像编辑有效，可在提升整体编辑质量的同时大幅降低延迟。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models exhibit pronounced sample-specific performance variation: different models can produce substantially different results for the same source image and editing instruction, and the best-performing model often varies across requests. These models also differ substantially in inference latency, making fixed-model deployment inefficient. We therefore study image editing model routing, which dynamically selects an editor from a candidate pool given an input image and editing instruction. We introduce EditJev, a System One routing model that directly predicts sample-level preference scores over candidate image editing models in a single forward pass. A lightweight preference head on a vision-language backbone produces a fixed-dimensional score vector without autoregressive model-name generation or response parsing. To train EditJev, we construct pairwise preferences from offline candidate outputs and optimize a pairwise ranking objective to learn relative model suitability. At inference time, EditJev combines the predicted preference scores with model-level speed scores to balance editing quality and generation latency. Experiments on ImgEdit and GEdit-En show that EditJev improves aggregate quality over the strongest fixed editor, from 4.540 to 4.592 and from 8.194 to 8.255, respectively, while reducing estimated end-to-end latency by 60.8% and 31.7%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of direct, structured model routing for efficient image editing.

</details>


### [85] [SemGeo-Gen: Unsupervised Generation of Approximate Cross-Instance Semantic-Geometric Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32663)
*Roy Amoyal,Shira Ifergane,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemGeo-Gen 是一种无监督方法，从弱对齐的 3D 物体集合中生成近似的跨实例语义-几何对应监督，可用于 3D-3D、3D-2D、2D-2D 训练，并在 SPair-71k 上带来最高 5 PCK@0.10 的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的点级对应在不同实例间往往存在歧义，且大规模人工标注代价高昂、不切实际。作者转而追求语义有意义、几何一致、且足够多样的近似对应，以替代昂贵的手工标注。

Method: 仅依赖粗粒度的类别级旋转对齐：将多视角 DINOv2 特征提升到 3D，使用连续分段仿射（piecewise-affine）配准对齐物体实例，并利用语义一致性剪枝候选匹配；所得 3D 对应可投影到渲染视图，从而无需人工标注即可生成 3D-3D、3D-2D、2D-2D 监督。

Result: 与 KeypointNet 的稀疏人工标注对比，达到 93% PCK@0.10 与 97% PCK@0.15；将生成对应用于合成预训练后，在真实数据上微调可使当前先进语义对应模型在 SPair-71k 上提升最多 5 个 PCK@0.10 点。

Conclusion: 从 3D 资产生成的近似语义-几何监督能够改进真实图像上的对应学习，可作为昂贵人工标注的可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce SemGeo-Gen, an unsupervised method for generating approximate cross-instance semantic-geometric supervision from weakly aligned 3D object collections. Our goal is not to recover exact point-level correspondences, which are often ambiguous across different instances and would require expensive, impractical manual supervision at scale. Instead, we automatically produce approximate correspondences that are semantically meaningful, geometrically consistent, and diverse enough to train modern correspondence models. Given only coarse category-level rotation alignment, SemGeo-Gen lifts multi-view DINOv2 features to 3D, aligns object instances using continuous piecewise-affine registration, and prunes candidate matches using semantic consistency. The resulting 3D correspondences can be projected into rendered views, yielding scalable 3D-3D, 3D-2D, and 2D-2D supervision without manual annotation. We validate the generated correspondences against sparse human annotations from KeypointNet, obtaining 93% PCK@0.10 and 97% PCK@0.15. More importantly, we show that using the generated correspondences for synthetic pretraining improves recent state-of-the-art semantic correspondence models on SPair-71k after fine-tuning on real data, with gains of up to 5 PCK@0.10 points. Together, these results indicate that approximate semantic-geometric supervision generated from 3D assets can improve real-image correspondence learning and serve as a scalable alternative to costly manual annotation. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/SemGeo-Gen/.

</details>


### [86] [The Answer Knows Where to Look: Zooming before MLLMs Answer under Visual-Token Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32664)
*Zhensheng Hu,Guanglong Xu,Guanqiang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现MLLM的答案token从中间层起就能定位证据，但答题前的注意力仅在最顶层才定位且分散；据此提出"look token"，在单次prefill中以事后监督学习按自身正确答案的注意力模式指向证据，再由其隐状态选择缩放方式，在Qwen3-VL三个规模、五个文本密集基准上以98视觉token/视图超越最强基线4.8–9.1分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效部署要求每图视觉token预算很小，导致文档、图表、信息图中的细粒度文字不可读；放大可恢复证据，但必须在模型作答前选好区域。现有做法（注意力引导缩放、外部定位器/框标注）要么定位信号出现太晚且分散，要么需额外标注、逐模型层搜索与生成开销。

Method: 先做诊断分析：答案token自中间层起即可定位证据，而可用的答题前注意力只在顶层定位且随模型深度漂移、分布弥散；直接监督答题前注意力反而损害阅读，缩放收益由每张图的信息缺口决定。据此提出look token：在单次prefill中处理、不进入答题pass，以事后（hindsight）方式学习模仿模型自身正确答案的注意力，将定位知识蒸馏为集中的指针；其隐状态在无缩放、裁剪、方形窗口、指针自适应窗口、foveated warp等视图中选择，由每种视图的反事实结果监督。无需框标注、逐模型层搜索、外部定位器或答题前生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL三个规模与五个文本密集基准上，98视觉token/视图时超过最强基线4.8–9.1分，超过注意力引导缩放11.1–17.0分，且运行速度快于注意力引导与基于grounding的缩放方法。

Conclusion: MLLM具备证据位置知识，但答题前不可直接读取；通过look token将答案中的定位知识事后提取为显式指针并用于选择缩放策略，可在小视觉token预算下显著提升文本密集任务表现，且不依赖标注、外部定位器或答题前生成。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) read images through visual tokens, and under the small per-image budgets that efficient deployment requires, the fine text of documents, charts and infographics becomes illegible. Zooming in recovers the evidence, but the region must be chosen before the model answers. We find that MLLMs know where the evidence is, but not in a form that can be read before they answer: the answer tokens localize it from the middle layers on, whereas the attention available before answering does so only in the top layers, at a depth that varies across models, and diffusely. Supervising the pre-answer attention to point degrades reading, and the benefit of zooming is governed by each image's information gap. Building on these findings, we introduce ours, which lets an MLLM look where its answer would. A dedicated look token, processed in a single prefill and kept out of the answering pass, learns in hindsight to attend as the model's own correct answers do, extracting this knowledge into a concentrated pointer before any answer exists. Its hidden state then selects how to look, from no zoom to crops, square windows, windows that adapt to the pointer and foveated warps, supervised by the counterfactual outcome of every view. ours needs no box annotations, per-model layer search, external localizers or generation before the answer. On Qwen3-VL at three scales and five text-rich benchmarks, it surpasses the strongest baseline by 4.8-9.1 points at 98 visual tokens per view and attention-guided zooming by 11.1-17.0 points, while running faster than attention-guided and grounding-based zooming.

</details>


### [87] [SP-Guide: Enhancing Aerial Vision-Language Navigation with Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32697)
*Haoxiang Tang,Dong Zhou,Shen Tan,Minxuan Jiang,Xitong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SP-Guide——一种基于Transformer的目标预测框架，通过空间先验跨模态编码器、指令增强解码器与目标收敛滤波器提升空中视觉语言导航（AVLN）的跨模态空间对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVLN方法将视图中心RGB观测与全局锚定历史地图融合为通用视觉嵌入，跨模态空间对齐只能依赖稀疏导航监督隐式学习，对齐能力不足。

Method: SP-Guide由三部分组成：(1) Spatial Prior Cross-Modal Encoder，将物理落地的空间先验注入跨模态编码以显式对齐；(2) Instruction Enhanced Decoder，增强指令信息用于目标预测；(3) Goal Convergence Filter，对终端目标进行细化。

Result: 在CityNav基准上，成功率优于代表性基线，且在未见环境（unseen environments）上提升最大。

Conclusion: 物理落地的空间先验为鲁棒的AVLN提供了有效的归纳偏置，可显著改善跨模态对齐与泛化性能。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (AVLN) requires a UAV to reach a language-described target from partial egocentric observations while reasoning over large outdoor scenes. Existing approaches typically fuse view-centered RGB observations and globally anchored history maps as generic visual embeddings, leaving cross-modal spatial alignment to be learned from sparse navigation supervision. We present SP-Guide, a transformer- based goal-prediction framework that addresses this weakness of alignment through a Spatial Prior Cross-Modal Encoder, an Instruction Enhanced Decoder, and a Goal Convergence Filter for terminal target refinement. Experiments implemented on the CityNav benchmark show that SP-Guide improves success rate over the representative baselines, with the largest gains on unseen environments. These results indicate that physically grounded spatial priors provide an effective inductive bias for robust AVLN.

</details>


### [88] [Are Textual Residuals Truly Complementary? Learning Visual Complements under Semantic Superposition for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32709)
*Xiaoyang Li,Mingming Lu,Wei Zhou,Hao Li,Canhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Superposition-Aware Residual Calibration (SARC)，将稠密文本模态差异转化为稀疏、可选择且视觉对齐的补丁残差，用于文本增强的可见光-红外行人重识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本增强VI-ReID方法默认"文本模态差异即视觉互补信息"，但通过语义叠加分析发现稠密文本残差会把有用外观变化与冗余干扰纠缠，直接残差融合不可靠。

Method: 1) Low-Interference Sparse Residual Learning：将面向模态的文本嵌入表示为共享字典上的稀疏激活强度，选取正向强度差，抑制字典层重叠与激活加权干扰，重构紧凑可控残差；2) Visual Complement and Counterfactual Calibration：依据身份特定的RGB-IR视觉差异对残差进行反事实校准，保持残差结构并促使保留语义提供有意义补偿。

Result: 在Tri-SYSU-MM01、Tri-RegDB、Tri-LLCM上，SARC稳定提升VI-ReID骨干并具竞争力；机制分析显示文本变化更集中、字典级特征干扰降低、多数样本的残差-视觉互补对齐改善。

Conclusion: 有效的跨模态补偿要求文本残差被选择性表示并显式校准，而非直接当作视觉互补信息使用。

Abstract: Text-enhanced visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) exploits textual descriptions to compensate for appearance cues that are weak or unavailable under infrared imaging. Existing methods construct compensation from differences between modality-oriented textual representations, implicitly assuming that textual differences are visually complementary. We revisit this assumption through semantic superposition and show that dense textual residuals may entangle useful appearance variations with redundant, making direct residual fusion unreliable. We propose Superposition-Aware Residual Calibration, which transforms dense textual differences into selective and visually grounded complements. Low-Interference Sparse Residual Learning represents modality-oriented text embeddings as sparse activation strengths over a shared dictionary, selects positive strength differences, and suppresses both dictionary-level overlap and activation-weighted interference to reconstruct a compact, selectively controlled residual. Visual Complement and Counterfactual Calibration further calibrates the residual against identity-specific RGB-IR visual discrepancies, encouraging retained semantics to provide meaningful compensation while preserving the residual structure. Experiments on Tri-SYSU-MM01, Tri-RegDB, and Tri-LLCM show that SARC consistently improves the VI-ReID backbone and remains competitive with text-assisted methods. Mechanism analyses show that SARC produces more concentrated textual variations, reduces dictionary-level feature interference, and improves residual-visual-complement alignment for most samples. These results demonstrate that effective cross-modal compensation requires textual residuals to be selectively represented and explicitly calibrated, rather than directly treated as visual complements. Code and implementation details will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [89] [CityLift: Compositional 3D Urban Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32728)
*Haoran Gong,Jie Feng,Chenxu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityLift：从单张城市图像联合建模场景级布局与建筑级生成，输出可摆放回场景的独立带纹理建筑网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成可编辑3D城市场景需要完整建筑资产及一致的空间排布；但遮挡与透视歧义使同时推断完整建筑几何与一致的相对尺度、位置十分困难，且缺少场景图像与单体建筑配对的数据。

Method: 双流耦合：场景流与实例流联合学习建筑形状与摆放，在统一坐标系下产出粗稀疏结构；提出"生成式空间继承"，将每栋建筑的粗占据与场景条件3D特征场映射到局部体素，引导流模型生成精细结构；同一坐标映射把生成网格放回场景，保持其与骨架的空间关系；并构建CityLift-5K数据集（4,997个合成场景，含实例掩码、建筑网格与摆放标注）。

Result: 在CityLift-5K及UrbanScene3D、InstanceBuilding的场景区块上，建筑几何质量与场景构图均有提升。

Conclusion: 将场景级空间建模与建筑级生成耦合，可在单一框架内同时获得高质量单体建筑与一致的可编辑场景排布。

Abstract: Generating editable 3D urban scenes from a single image requires detailed building assets with a coherent spatial arrangement. Occlusion and perspective ambiguity make it difficult to infer complete building geometry together with consistent relative scales and positions. We present CityLift, a framework that generates individual textured building meshes from an urban image by coupling scene-level spatial modeling with building-level synthesis. CityLift first learns building shape and placement jointly through coupled scene and instance streams, producing coarse sparse structures in a common coordinate system. We then introduce generative spatial inheritance, which maps each building's coarse occupancy and scene-conditioned 3D feature field into a local volume to guide a flow model that generates finer structure. The same coordinate mapping places the generated meshes back into the scene, preserving their spatial relationship to the scaffold. To address the scarcity of urban data pairing scene images with individual 3D buildings, we construct CityLift-5K, comprising 4,997 synthetic scenes with instance masks, building meshes, and placement annotations. Experiments on CityLift-5K and scene blocks from UrbanScene3D and InstanceBuilding demonstrate improved building geometry and scene composition. The project page is available at https://ranhaogong.github.io/CityLift/.

</details>


### [90] [Cross-Architectural Feature Fusion for Coronary Artery Stenosis Detection in X-ray Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32730)
*Haobo Zhu,Mojtaba Lashgari,Jason Chai,Giovanni Luigi De Maria,Vicente Grau,Abhirup Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fusion-YOLOv11-L混合框架，将YOLOv11-L检测器与预训练UNet++通过通道注意力门控模块融合，实现XCA图像中狭窄区域的自动检测，并借助测试时自适应与增强提升跨域泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉疾病是主要致死原因，XCA狭窄检测对诊断至关重要；人工判读耗时、主观且变异性大，尤其在低对比度和血管重叠情况下。现有深度学习方法往往仅依赖检测或分割单一范式，性能受限。因此需要融合语义特征与血管结构特征以提升定位精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Fusion-YOLOv11-L：以YOLOv11-L为检测主干，与在ARCADE血管分割数据集上预训练的UNet++并联，通过通道注意力门控模块（channel attention gating）融合语义特征与血管感知的结构特征。在重标注的ARCADE*数据集上训练评估，并在牛津急性心肌梗死研究（OxAMI）数据集上做跨域评测，采用测试时自适应（test-time adaptation）与测试时增强（TTA）缓解域偏移。

Result: 在ARCADE*上优于YOLOv11-L基线，AP@50达0.585，F1为0.596。跨域评估于OxAMI数据集显示因域偏移导致性能下降，经TTA后AP@50由0.434提升至0.480，泛化性改善。

Conclusion: 跨架构特征融合与轻量化自适应策略可有效提升XCA自动狭窄检测的鲁棒性与泛化能力，验证了检测-分割协同建模的可行性。

Abstract: Coronary artery disease remains a leading cause of mortality, with stenosis detection from invasive X-ray Coronary Angiography (XCA) critical for diagnosis. Manual interpretation is time-consuming, subjective, and variable, especially under low contrast and vessel overlap. Existing deep learning methods often rely solely on detection or segmentation, limiting performance.We propose a hybrid framework, Fusion-YOLOv11-L, combining a YOLOv11-L detector with a pretrained UNet++ via a channel attention gating module. This enables fusion of semantic and vessel-aware structural features, improving localisation of stenotic regions. The UNet++ is pretrained on ARCADE vessel segmentation dataset to provide anatomically informed representations. Experiments on the relabeled ARCADE (ARCADE*) dataset show improved performance over YOLOv11-L, achieving AP@50 of 0.585 and F1-score of 0.596. Cross-domain evaluation on a dataset from the Oxford Acute Myocardial Infarction (OxAMI) Study reveals performance degradation due to domain shift, mitigated using test-time adaptation and augmentation. Test-time augmentation improves AP@50 from 0.434 to 0.480, indicating better generalisability. These results show that cross-architecture feature fusion and lightweight adaptation improve robustness and generalisability of automated stenosis detection in XCA.

</details>


### [91] [SCDM-HTD: Spectral-Wise Conditional Diffusion Model for Hyperspectral Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32768)
*Dunbin Shen,Wenfeng Kong,Jianjun Liu,Xiaorui Ma,Hongyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出谱维条件扩散模型，将高光谱目标检测重构为检测图生成任务，通过背景-目标分布差异学习提升检测鲁棒性，并采用轻量MLP去噪网络、确定性反向非马尔可夫链加速采样，结合约束能量最小化损失与二元交叉熵复合损失，在四个基准数据集上取得定量与定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱目标检测方法主要聚焦特征比较，忽视了对背景-目标分离的分布学习，导致实际应用中检测鲁棒性受限。

Method: 将HTD重构为检测图生成任务：以背景与目标的置信度分数作为训练样本、对应像素光谱作为引导条件，通过扩散与去噪刻画两类分布差异；设计基于多层感知机的轻量条件去噪网络，将噪声置信度映射至理想值；采用确定性反向非马尔可夫链跳过采样步骤以加速生成；引入复合损失——约束能量最小化损失（最大化目标响应、最小化平均输出能量以增强目标显著性并抑制背景）与像素级二元交叉熵损失。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在定量指标与定性视觉效果上均有提升。

Conclusion: 以谱维条件扩散建模背景-目标分布差异是高光谱目标检测的有效途径，配合轻量化网络设计与复合损失可在保证实用性的同时提升检测性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral target detection (HTD) aims to locate and recognize objects at a fine-grained scale, playing a pivotal role in remote sensing. Despite significant progress, current HTD methods primarily focus on feature comparison while neglecting distribution learning for background-target separation, which can limit detection robustness in practical applications. This paper addresses this issue by introducing a spectral-wise conditional diffusion model that reformulates HTD as a task of generating a detection map. Specifically, we treat background and target confidence scores as training samples and use the corresponding pixel spectra as guiding conditions to capture the distributional discrepancy between background and target through diffusion and denoising. To enhance practical applicability, we propose a lightweight conditional denoising network based on multilayer perceptron to map noisy confidence scores to their ideal counterparts and adopt a deterministic reverse non-Markov chain to skip sampling steps, thereby accelerating the generation process. Furthermore, we introduce a composite loss to improve background-target separation. This loss integrates constrained energy minimization loss to enhance target saliency and suppress the background by maximizing the target response while minimizing average output energy, alongside binary cross-entropy loss to classify background and target using pixel-wise labels. Experimental results on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with improvements in both quantitative metrics and qualitative visual effects.

</details>


### [92] [Video-MemComp: Taylor-Guided Sensitivity-Aware Memory Management for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32784)
*Yonghui Niu,Yuhao Ji,Chang Nie,Jiao Zhang,Yilong Li,Junlan Feng,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、敏感性感知的上下文管理框架Video-MemComp，用于内存受限的流式视频理解；通过一阶泰勒展开将压缩误差解耦为正交的局部近似误差与层级结构敏感性，分别用pre-RoPE安全区的token聚合与动态KV预算分配加以抑制，在节省90%以上KV内存的同时超越或媲美全缓存与SOTA压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频数据快速增长，需要能处理长时连续视觉输入的流式视频理解系统；MLLM面临严重显存瓶颈，而现有压缩方法忽视了近似误差的爆炸式累积问题。

Method: 通过一阶泰勒展开理论分解压缩误差为局部近似误差与层级结构敏感性两部分。针对局部误差：在pre-RoPE安全区内进行token聚合，并引入attention-conserving logit bias，缓解RoPE旋转造成的几何失真。针对层级敏感性：基于离线梯度先验与在线上下文熵动态分配KV cache预算，优先保障高敏感层。整体为免训练框架。

Result: 在MLVU、VideoMME、EgoSchema等基准上优于或媲美全缓存与SOTA压缩方法；将Qwen2.5-VL-3B整体准确率从62.3%提升至64.1%，同时将KV cache压缩至每层等效6,000 tokens，节省超过90%的KV内存。

Conclusion: 以误差分解为理论依据的免训练KV压缩方案可在显著降低显存占用的同时保持甚至提升流式视频理解性能，验证了误差感知与层级差异化预算分配的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of video data has created an urgent need for efficient video understanding systems that can handle long and continuous visual inputs, posing significant challenges for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to severe memory bottlenecks. Moreover, existing compression methods often overlook the explosive accumulation of approximation errors. To address this issue, we propose Video-MemComp, a training-free and sensitivity-aware context management framework for memory-constrained streaming video understanding. Our method theoretically decomposes compression error into orthogonal local approximation error and layer-wise structural sensitivity via a first-order Taylor expansion. To minimize local approximation error, we perform token aggregation in a pre-RoPE safe zone with an attention-conserving logit bias, mitigating geometric distortions caused by RoPE rotations. To manage layer-wise structural sensitivity, we dynamically allocate the KV cache budget based on offline gradient priors and online context entropy, prioritizing high-sensitivity layers. Video-MemComp outperforms or achieves performance comparable to full-cache and state-of-the-art compression methods on benchmarks including MLVU, VideoMME, and EgoSchema. Notably, it improves the overall accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-3B from 62.3% to 64.1% while reducing the KV cache to an equivalent of 6,000 tokens per layer, saving over 90% of KV memory.

</details>


### [93] [SREdit: Scene Response Guided Appearance Editing for Projection Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32792)
*Yuqiao Xia,Yuchen Deng,Ziheng Chang,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SREdit是一个基于场景响应引导的高效投影式外观编辑框架，利用场景响应图约束扩散采样过程，使LLM图像编辑器的输出与固定物理对象保持配准并适配投影能力，在提升投影外观质量与还原保真度的同时仅增加不到1秒耗时与极小显存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影映射中的投影仪补偿只能逼近可投影范围内的目标外观，无法修改外观本身；而预训练LLM图像编辑器虽能直接生成期望外观，却缺乏场景几何与场景响应的准确度量，导致输出存在几何错位、明暗不一致或超出可投影范围的变化，下游投影仪补偿无法修正。

Method: 提出SREdit框架：从重建场景估计场景响应图，反映投影光照对不同表面区域的影响强度；在采样早期阶段用源外观约束以保持与固定物理对象的配准，同时由场景响应图自适应调整各区域编辑强度。

Result: 在两种预训练图像编辑器上实验表明，SREdit提升了投影外观质量与还原保真度；每张图额外耗时低于1秒、显存开销可忽略；用户研究中被试一致偏好使用SREdit的结果。

Conclusion: 通过引入场景响应引导对生成式编辑过程施加约束，可有效弥合通用图像编辑器与投影显示物理限制之间的差距，实现高效、保真且受用户偏好的投影式外观编辑。

Abstract: Projection-based appearance editing changes selected visual attributes of a physical object through projection mapping (PM). To display a desired appearance, PM typically uses projector compensation, which computes a projector input that counteracts the geometric and photometric distortions of the scene. Compensation can only approximate this appearance within the available projection range, and it cannot revise the appearance itself. Pretrained LLM-based image editors provide a direct way to produce the desired appearance from a text prompt or reference image; projector compensation can then attempt to reproduce the edited result through projection. However, these editors do not have an accurate measure of the scene geometry or scene response. Therefore, their outputs may contain geometric misalignment, inconsistent shading, or changes outside the available projection range, which downstream projector compensation cannot correct. To address this problem, we introduce SREdit, an efficient response-guided framework for projection-based appearance editing. SREdit estimates a scene response map from the reconstructed scene that reflects how strongly projected illumination can affect different surface regions. SREdit uses the source appearance to constrain the early sampling process and preserve registration with the fixed physical object, while the scene response map adapts the amount of editing across surface regions. Experiments across two pretrained image editors show that SREdit improves projected appearance quality and reproduction fidelity. It adds less than one second per image with negligible VRAM overhead. Participants also consistently preferred the results produced with SREdit in our user study.

</details>


### [94] [Consommé: Conditioned Token Merging for Split Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32794)
*Man Ngo,Jan S. Rellermeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Consommé：在冻结骨干下做拆分推理的 token 缩减，利用 CLS 的双重角色（分类摘要 + patch 保留信号），通过信息瓶颈压缩 CLS 为紧凑隐变量传输，并以其条件化可学习 patch 门，软分配把 patch 合并为恰好 k 个 token，单步达到任意预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拆分推理把 ViT 分布在资源受限边缘设备与服务器之间，但中间 token 传输开销大；为每个端点部署微调骨干代价也高。目标是在冻结骨干、仅传小型适配器的设定下降低传输 token 数。

Method: 1) 观察到 CLS token 具双重作用：既是接收端分类所需的全局摘要，又是决定保留哪些 patch 的信号；2) 用信息瓶颈把 CLS 压缩为紧凑潜表示，替代完整 CLS 传输；3) 该潜表示条件化一个可学习 patch 门，通过软分配将 patch 合并为恰好 k 个 token，单步实现任意 token 预算，突破二分匹配（BPM）的逐层上限。

Result: 在 DeiT-S/T 上，激进 token 预算下优于所有方法；在 BPM 无法工作的浅层拆分处仍可运行；温和预算下与微调方法相当，而部署成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 冻结骨干＋轻量适配器＋CLS 信息瓶颈驱动的软分配 patch 合并，是通信高效的拆分推理 token 缩减可行方案，兼顾激进预算性能与低部署成本。

Abstract: Split inference partitions a Vision Transformer between a resource-constrained edge device and a server. However, transmitting intermediate tokens across the communication channel is costly. Deploying fine-tuned backbones to every endpoint becomes expensive as well. We study token reduction for split inference under a frozen backbone, where only a small adapter is shipped. We observe that the CLS token plays a dual role: it acts as the global summary the receiver needs for classification, and it serves as a signal on which patches to keep. We propose Consommé, a method utilizing both characteristics. There, an information bottleneck compresses the CLS token into a compact latent which is transmitted in place of the full CLS token and conditions a learned patch gate merging patches into exactly $k$ tokens via soft assignment. It reaches any budget in a single step, thus escaping the per-layer cap of bipartite matching (BPM). On DeiT-S/T, it leads all methods at aggressive token budgets and remains operable at shallow splits where BPM cannot operate, while matching fine-tuned methods at gentle budgets at a fraction of their deployment cost.

</details>


### [95] [CompMVR: Compression-Aware Multi-View Restoration Using Diffusion Models for Geometrically Consistent 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32800)
*Dong-hwi Kim,Chaewon Moon,Hojun Song,Dongbeom Kim,Junyeong Jang,Aro Kim,Gahyeon Kim,Heejung Choi,Jehee Kim,Gianella Cravioto,Sohyun Lee,Gyeongjin Choi,EunHye Jeong,Soo Ye Kim,Jaehyup Lee,Sang-hyo Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CompMVR——一种压缩感知的多视角扩散修复框架，在联合修复压缩视角图像的同时保持跨视角一致性，并构建压缩多视角数据集，将“压缩多视角修复”确立为独立问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实3D重建流程依赖的多视角图像常受有损压缩退化，但压缩伪影对多视角几何的影响长期被忽视。作者识别出“压缩导致的跨视角几何不一致”这一关键失效模式：视角相关的伪影破坏特征匹配、稠密对应、相机参数估计及新视角合成等下游任务。传统修复方法逐图独立优化2D感知质量，无法解决该几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出CompMVR框架：利用学到的压缩先验与跨视角对应关系，联合精炼压缩视角，在恢复视觉细节的同时提升跨视角几何可靠性；并构建系统性评估所需的压缩多视角数据集。

Result: 在多种数据集、编解码器与压缩等级下，修复质量及下游3D任务（新视角合成、相机位姿估计、视角匹配）均取得提升。

Conclusion: 压缩多视角修复应被视为一个独立问题，其对有损压缩下的3D重建具有重要意义。

Abstract: Real-world 3D reconstruction pipelines often rely on multi-view images degraded by lossy compression. While compression artifacts have been extensively studied in single-image or video restoration, their impact on multi-view geometry remains largely overlooked. In this work, we identify compression-induced cross-view geometric inconsistency as a critical failure mode, where view-dependent artifacts disrupt feature matching, dense correspondence, camera parameter estimation, and downstream 3D reconstruction tasks such as novel view synthesis. To address this problem, we propose CompMVR, a compression-aware multi-view diffusion restoration framework that jointly refines compressed views while preserving cross-view consistency, and construct compressed datasets for systematic evaluation. Unlike conventional restoration methods that optimize each image independently for 2D perceptual quality, CompMVR leverages learned compression priors and cross-view correspondence to recover visual details while improving geometric reliability across views. Experiments across diverse datasets, codecs, and compression levels demonstrate gains in restoration quality and downstream 3D tasks, including novel-view synthesis, camera pose estimation, and view matching. These results establish compressed multi-view restoration as a distinct problem and highlight its importance for 3D reconstruction under lossy compression. Code and data for this paper are available at https://mchaewon.github.io/CompMVR-project-page/.

</details>


### [96] [SPOOK: Sparse Pixel Optimization for Off-Manifold Knowledge in Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32835)
*Ankit Gupta,Akshat Pandya,Christoph Adami,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现代视觉编码器在远离自然图像流形处，并不清楚嵌入相似度与人类可见语义是否一致。作者用稀疏像素编辑的空白画布做黑盒"爬山"优化，在OpenCLIP与DINOv3上都能得到强靶向检索/对齐性能，揭示了共享的"off-manifold"失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉编码器被广泛用作语义表示，但在远离自然图像流形的区域，嵌入相似度与人类可感知语义之间的关系可能变得不确定；作者希望探测这一区域，验证编码器是否会把视觉上无语义的输入映射进目标语义盆地。

Method: 黑盒探测：在空白画布上做稀疏像素编辑，用简单的像素级爬山（hill-climbing）过程优化。数据集为ImageNet与Flickr30k；评估对象包括多模态对比编码器（OpenCLIP）与自监督视觉编码器（DINOv3）。OpenCLIP以类提示词（class prompts）为目标，DINOv3以类原型（class prototypes）为目标；用靶向检索（top-1/top-5）与原型排名衡量对齐程度。

Result: OpenCLIP上，朝类提示词优化的输入取得很强的top-1与top-5靶向检索性能；DINOv3上，朝类原型优化的输入取得高目标对齐度与接近顶部的原型排名。表明稀疏编辑后的近空白、视觉非语义输入可进入语言对齐与自监督两种表示空间的目标语义盆地。

Conclusion: 现代视觉编码器存在共享的off-manifold失效模式；稀疏空白画布优化可作为探测表示几何的简洁探针。

Abstract: Modern vision encoders are widely used as semantic representations, but the relationship between embedding similarity and human-visible semantics can become unclear far from the natural-image manifold. We probe this regime in a black-box setting by applying sparse pixel edits to blank canvases with a simple pixel-level hill-climbing procedure. Across ImageNet and Flickr30k, we evaluate whether these edited blank canvases can retrieve chosen targets in multimodal contrastive encoders and self-supervised visual encoders. On OpenCLIP, inputs optimized toward class prompts achieve strong top-1 and top-5 targeted retrieval performance; on DINOv3, inputs optimized toward class prototypes achieve high target alignment and near-top prototype ranks. These results show that sparse edits on otherwise blank, visually non-semantic inputs can enter target semantic basins in both language-aligned and self-supervised visual representation spaces. Our findings identify a shared off-manifold failure mode of modern vision encoders and suggest sparse blank-canvas optimization as a simple probe of representation geometry.

</details>


### [97] [OpenStripe supports open-set identification and spatial stripe profiling in wild Amur tigers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32848)
*Yichun Peng,Yansong Li,Jin Hou,Qi Song,Xi Guo,Ning Zhang,Degang Wei,Huang Yang,Yu Guan,Jianping Ge,Lei Bao,Tianming Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenStripe：多智能体框架通过重建虎躯干2D标准图案、编码四类可解释条纹结构，在开放集设定下实现野生东北虎个体自动分组（匹配准确率0.90，ARI 0.85）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外相机对濒危野生动物的非侵入式监测需要开放集个体识别（无预定义类别、种群规模未知），但姿态形变与杂乱背景使图像比对困难；现有方法缺乏可解释表征。

Method: 提出OpenStripe多智能体框架：先将虎躯干图案重建为规范化的2D表示，再编码四种可解释的局部条纹结构（断点、分叉、不规则结构、圆形结构），并融合这四类互补结构进行身份分组。在28只野生东北虎的100条相机记录上评估，与49种基线配置对比（同一后端）。

Result: 融合四类结构后自动身份分组达到0.90匹配准确率与0.85 ARI，显著优于49种基线配置（最高仅0.43准确率、0.14 ARI）；条纹相似度随地理距离衰减，分形维数与条纹间距等性状与地理邻近性相关。

Conclusion: OpenStripe提供了可解释的个体表征，将自动个体分组与野生种群的空间表型分析相连接，可用于濒危虎种的相机陷阱监测。

Abstract: Non-invasive camera-trap monitoring of endangered wildlife requires open-set individual identification---grouping field images into identities without predefined classes or a known population size---yet pose deformation and cluttered backgrounds complicate image comparison. Here, we present OpenStripe, a multi-agent framework that reconstructs canonical two-dimensional (2D) tiger torso patterns and encodes four interpretable local stripe structures: breakpoints, bifurcations, irregular structures, and round structures. We evaluated OpenStripe on 100 camera-trap records from 28 wild Amur tigers (Panthera tigris altaica), representing nearly one-third of the documented population in the Northeast China Tiger and Leopard National Park. Combining all four complementary structures enabled automatic identity grouping with 0.90 matched accuracy and a 0.85 adjusted Rand index (ARI), outperforming 49 baseline configurations under the same backend (up to 0.43 accuracy and 0.14 ARI). In addition, results from OpenStripe indicate that stripe similarity in Amur tigers declines with geographic distance and suggest that traits such as fractal dimension and inter-stripe spacing are associated with geographic proximity. OpenStripe thus provides an interpretable representation that connects automated individual grouping with spatial phenotypic profiling in wild populations.

</details>


### [98] [Multimodal Generative AI Assists Comprehensive Echocardiographic Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32880)
*Yi Qin,Marawan Elbatel,Shanshan Song,Taoran Huang,Jiarong Guo,Ningshu Li,Lehan Wang,Chenxu Zhao,Jiexin He,Machteld J. Boonstra,Stephan van der Zwaard,Marielle C. van de Veerdonk,Pere Lopez-Gutierrez,Lydia Dux-Santoy,Andrea Guala,Enrique Almar-Munoz,Sebastian Johannes Reinstadler,Radek Debiec,Yi-Hang Zhu,Huiyu Zhou,Adrian P Cheong,Yike Guo,Agnes Mayr,Karim Lekadir,Yong Jiang,Nilesh J. Samani,Dacheng Tao,Folkert W. Asselbergs,Hongwen Fei,Alex PW Lee,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoChat——一个面向超声心动图的生成式多模态视觉-语言模型，通过模拟心内科医生的认知工作流与思维链推理，实现覆盖33种心脏病变的综合诊断与量化，并在六个国际中心的多队列中达到SOTA，且在盲法阅片者研究中显著提升报告质量与诊断F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图是心血管诊断的基石，但全球范围内合格解读医师严重短缺。现有AI方案存在两点局限：一是黑箱式、缺乏可解释性；二是任务狭窄（如单病种分类或单指标测量），无法复现临床指南驱动的整体性推理。因此需要一种能显式将结论锚定于视觉证据、覆盖广泛诊断谱系的辅助解读模型。

Method: 构建生成式多模态视觉-语言模型EchoChat，将海量医学知识与思维链推理机制结合，显式地将诊断结论与视觉证据对齐，以模拟心脏科医师的认知流程。训练数据为来自46,358例检查的超声心动图—指令配对（instruction pairs）；在来自六个国际医学中心的多队列上进行评估，并开展源盲法（source-blinded）超声医师阅片者研究以验证临床增益。

Result: 模型支持最广的诊断范围，共33种心脏病变与发现，其中15种为新增病种，在可用的外部评估中平均F1达73.7%。在共有任务上，心脏病变识别平均召回率提升13.54%，量化任务平均R²提升0.275，优于现有最佳基础模型。盲法阅片者研究中，两位资深超声医师对与EchoChat协同生成的报告在诊断准确性与完整性上评分更高；相较仅依据常规阅片者所写报告，F1由72.78%提升至82.75%。

Conclusion: 将临床推理机制融入多模态AI，可有效提升超声心动图解读的准确性、可解释性与覆盖广度，并具备缩小专科经验差距、推动优质心血管诊疗普及的潜力。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the cornerstone of cardiovascular diagnostics, yet its global utility is constrained by a critical shortage of experienced interpreters. Current artificial intelligence (AI) solutions remain limited, typically operating as opaque "black boxes" or focusing on narrow tasks that fail to capture the holistic, guideline-driven reasoning of a clinician. Here we introduce EchoChat, a generative multimodal vision-language model that performs comprehensive echocardiographic analysis to support clinician interpretation. By emulating the cognitive workflow of a cardiologist, EchoChat integrates vast medical knowledge with a chain-of-thought reasoning mechanism to explicitly ground its conclusions in visual evidence. Trained on echocardiography-instruction pairs from 46,358 examinations, our model establishes a new state-of-the-art across diverse cohorts from six international medical centers. It supports the broadest diagnostic scope of 33 cardiac conditions and findings, including 15 newly added conditions for which it achieves an average F1 of 73.7% across available external evaluations. It surpasses existing best-performing foundation models in cardiac condition recognition (improving average recall by 13.54% on shared tasks) and quantification (average R2 score increase of 0.275 on shared tasks). Furthermore, in a source-blinded echocardiographer reader study, echocardiography reports co-generated with EchoChat were rated higher in diagnostic accuracy and completeness by two senior echocardiographers, and improved diagnostic performance from 72.78% to 82.75% in terms of F1 score over routine-reader only reports. These results demonstrate that integrating clinical reasoning into multimodal AI can democratize specialist expertise and transform the standard of cardiovascular care.

</details>


### [99] [VTRL-Med: Progressive Visual Refinement with Topology-Guided Regularization for Medical Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32884)
*Guangyu Meng,Pengfei Gu,Peixian Liang,John P. Lalor,Erin Wolf Chambers,Danny Z. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTRL-Med：基于SAM视觉-结构特征与持续同调拓扑正则的渐进式医学图像聚类框架，在13个基准上平均准确率提升4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度聚类方法忽视两类互补信息：捕获目标边界与区域的类别无关结构先验，以及连通性、孔洞等拓扑模式；前者应作为主要判别表示，后者可缓解残余视觉歧义。

Method: 1) 适配SAM提取保留掩码监督预训练结构线索的图像特征；2) 通过"原型选择 + k近邻一致性验证"两步过滤生成可靠伪标签；3) 渐进式优化视觉分支，同时以持续同调(PH)描述子构建辅助拓扑分支，在训练中提供几何正则。

Result: 13个医学图像基准上达到SOTA，平均准确率较现有方法提升4.48%；在三个代表性数据集上，拓扑引导正则较单纯视觉精炼再提升1.3%–4.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉-结构特征与拓扑特征角色互补，将SAM结构先验与持续同调正则结合可有效提升无标签医学图像聚类性能。

Abstract: Deep clustering offers a promising approach for analyzing large unlabeled medical image datasets, yet prevailing methods still face two limitations: they may neglect class-agnostic structural priors that capture object boundaries and regions, and they do not explicitly encode topological patterns (e.g., connectivity and holes). These sources of information play distinct and complementary roles: visual-structural features provide the primary discriminative representation for clustering, while topological features provide additional global geometric cues for residual visual ambiguities. We introduce VTRL-Med, a progressive visual-structural learning framework with topology-guided regularization for medical image clustering. VTRL-Med first adapts the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to extract image features that retain structural cues inherited from mask-supervised pretraining. It then generates reliable pseudo-labels through a two-step filtering process that combines prototype-based selection with k-nearest neighbor consistency validation. Finally, it progressively refines the visual branch while an auxiliary topology branch, constructed from persistent homology (PH) descriptors, provides additional geometric regularization during training. Across 13 medical image benchmarks, VTRL-Med achieves state-of-the-art clustering performance, improving average accuracy by 4.48% over existing methods. On three representative datasets, topology-guided regularization further improves accuracy by 1.3--4.3% over visual refinement alone.

</details>


### [100] [LoCA: Enabling Multi-Modality in Geospatial Location Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32894)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Eleanna Vali,Diogenis Antonopoulos,Eleni Saka,Dimitris Christofis,Nikolaos Ioannis Bountos,Konstantin Klemmer,Ioannis Papoutsis,Yannis Panagakis,Mihalis A. Nicolaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文证明任意Lipschitz位置编码器的内在维度至多2，导致对比损失严格高于渐近最优并出现协方差低秩坍缩；据此提出两阶段LoCA流程（多模态联合对比预训练+冻结图像编码器训练位置/元数据适配器），在17项任务上取得最多胜场，胜率68.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码器（将地理坐标映射为高维描述子并与配对遥感影像做对比对齐）在结构上存在内在维度上限与谱坍缩问题；同时扩展到多模态输入时，单一位置需映射到多个可能不兼容的描述子，缺乏可同时保证跨模态共对齐与高有效秩的正则化方案。

Method: 理论分析：证明任何Lipschitz位置编码器输出内在维度≤2，对比损失严格超过渐近最优，联合训练会使嵌入协方差坍缩为低秩；并将"对冻结图像编码器训练"重新解释为提供高秩目标的协方差正则器。据此提出需求：跨模态共对齐与高有效秩。方法LoCA为两阶段：阶段1用对比学习联合训练Sentinel-1与Sentinel-2编码器，得到共对齐、高秩的多模态目标；阶段2冻结图像编码器，以多视图对比学习训练位置编码器与表格元数据编码器作为投影适配器。

Result: 在17项任务（土地覆盖、野火预测、地球物理、社会经济、生物多样性与气候）上与5个SOTA编码器对比，LoCA取得最多任务胜场，是唯一在多数任务上优于所有其他模型的模型，赢得全部逐任务比较的68.8%。

Conclusion: 位置编码的维度限制是结构性而非架构或带宽所致；通过满足共对齐与高有效秩的正则化设计，LoCA在多模态位置编码中有效规避谱坍缩，并在广泛下游任务上带来一致的性能优势。

Abstract: Location encoders map geographic coordinates to high-dimensional descriptors through contrastive alignment with paired Earth Observation imagery. We prove that this construction is structurally limited: any Lipschitz location encoder produces an output of intrinsic dimension at most 2, regardless of architecture or bandwidth, which forces the contrastive loss to strictly exceed its asymptotic optimum. Empirically, joint training also collapses the encoder's embedding covariance to low rank. Although dimension-limited, current location encoders sidestep this spectral collapse by training against a frozen image encoder, which we reinterpret as a covariance regularizer that supplies a high-rank target for the location encoder to fold onto. However, extending this approach to multimodal inputs is challenging, as location encoders should simultaneously map a single location to multiple, potentially incompatible, descriptors. We identify two requirements a regularizer must satisfy for location encoding in multimodal regimes: co-alignment across modalities, since adapters alone cannot align independently pretrained encoders; and high effective rank, since the location encoder inherits its target's spectrum. We propose LoCA (Location Contrastive Alignment), a two-stage pipeline that satisfies both. Stage 1 jointly trains Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 encoders with contrastive learning, producing a co-aligned, high-rank multimodal target. Stage 2 freezes the image encoders and trains a location and a tabular metadata encoder as projection adapters with multi-view contrastive learning. Against five state-of-the-art encoders on 17 tasks ranging from land cover and wildfire forecasting to geophysical, socio-economic, biodiversity and climate applications, LoCA achieves the most task wins, is the only model to outperform every other on a majority of tasks, and wins 68.8% of all per-task comparisons.

</details>


### [101] [STROKE: Simple Sparse Structural Occlusions for Black-Box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32932)
*Ankit Gupta,Akshat Pandya,Paul Lin,Christoph Adami,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STROKE：用有向矩形遮挡作为扰动的查询高效黑盒对抗攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于补丁的黑盒对抗攻击在查询效率与计算开销上存在不足，作者希望探索一种更简单、更易搜索的扰动空间——结构化的矩形遮挡。

Method: 构造由简单有向矩形遮挡组成的扰动，并通过轻量级贪心"添加—精炼"（add-and-refine）过程优化这些矩形参数，实现基于分数的黑盒攻击。

Result: 在多种图像分类器上，于名义1–2%面积设置下以较低查询数取得高攻击成功率，在若干配置下优于现有基于补丁的攻击；匹配比较显示实际攻击时间显著缩短，消融实验验证了关键设计选择及不同结构基元下的有效性。

Conclusion: 简单的结构化遮挡构成有效且计算高效的黑盒对抗攻击搜索空间，为低查询、低开销攻击提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce STROKE, a query-efficient score-based black-box adversarial attack that constructs perturbations from simple oriented rectangular occlusions. STROKE optimizes these perturbations through a lightweight greedy add-and-refine procedure. Across diverse image classifiers, STROKE achieves high attack success with low query counts at nominal 1--2\% area settings, showing favorable performance relative to existing patch-based attacks in several configurations. Matched comparisons further demonstrate substantial reductions in wall-clock attack time, while ablation studies examine the key design choices and effectiveness across structural primitives. These findings suggest that simple structural occlusions offer an effective and computationally efficient search space for black-box adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Discrete Tokens: Preserving Numerical Continuity in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32948)
*Dianye Huang,Jiamei Guo,Yue Zhou,Mingde Li,Yi Xiong,Wei Wang,Nassir Navab,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HAD-VLM混合自回归-扩散框架，在共享骨干内结合文本生成与连续测量预测，实现提示条件化的定量超声测量，显著降低测量误差并保持问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续测量目标与离散token数值预测之间存在不匹配，现有视觉-语言模型难以准确完成提示条件化的定量超声测量。

Method: HAD-VLM在共享骨干中融合自回归文本生成与扩散式连续预测；采用视觉提示调优使视觉编码器适配超声图像；扩散过程预测由卡尺端点坐标与测量长度组成的五分量表示；并引入坐标-长度一致性机制结合集成预测的后处理策略精化结果。在EchoNet-LVH上评估四项超声心动图测量任务，另考察医学问答与X线椎体测量。

Result: IVSd、LVIDd、LVIDs、LVPWd的平均绝对误差分别为1.36、2.77、3.05、1.16 mm，较Vicuna自回归基线降低21.4–37.6%；四项任务均优于微调Qwen3-VL与直接MLP回归；医学问答性能得以保持；X线椎体测量MAE由11.4降至6.7像素。

Conclusion: 将自回归语言生成与扩散式连续预测相结合，可在保持提示条件化任务灵活性的同时提升定量医学图像测量精度，为语言交互与解剖定位数值预测的统一建模提供可行路径。

Abstract: Objective: To develop a unified vision-language model for prompt-conditioned quantitative ultrasound measurement that addresses the mismatch between continuous measurement targets and discrete token-based numerical prediction. Methods: This article presents HAD-VLM, a hybrid autoregressive-diffusion framework that combines autoregressive text generation with continuous measurement prediction within a shared backbone. Visual prompt tuning adapts the vision encoder to ultrasound images. A diffusion process predicts a five-component representation comprising caliper endpoint coordinates and measurement length. A post-processing strategy combining the proposed coordinate-length consistency mechanism with ensemble prediction is introduced to refine measurement estimates. We evaluate four echocardiographic measurement tasks on EchoNet-LVH, with additional assessments of medical question answering and X-ray vertebral measurement. Results: HAD-VLM achieves mean absolute errors of 1.36, 2.77, 3.05, and 1.16 mm for IVSd, LVIDd, LVIDs, and LVPWd, respectively, reducing errors by 21.4-37.6\% relative to the Vicuna-based autoregressive baseline. It also outperforms fine-tuned Qwen3-VL and direct MLP regression across all four tasks. Medical question answering performance is preserved on the evaluated dataset. For X-ray vertebral measurement, HAD-VLM reduces mean absolute error from 11.4 to 6.7 pixels. Conclusion: Integrating autoregressive language generation with diffusion-based continuous prediction improves quantitative medical image measurement while retaining prompt-conditioned task flexibility. Significance: HAD-VLM provides a unified approach to combining language interaction with anatomically grounded numerical prediction for quantitative medical image analysis.

</details>


### [103] [Flownomaly: Single-Step Conditional-Mean Recovery for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32964)
*Bernardo Perrone Ribeiro,Erkut Akdag,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flownomaly将少样本工业异常检测重构为冻结DINOv2特征空间中的“正常对应物恢复”问题，用条件流匹配学习速度场，以单步恢复得到正常令牌后验均值，并通过仅用正常样本的Jacobian增益-噪声准则选择恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本工业异常检测（仅几张无缺陷图像）需建模类别特定正常性。现有方法存在三类缺陷：推理依赖存储的样例（exemplar）、施加刚性线性结构、或存在复现异常内容的风险。

Method: 在冻结DINOv2 patch特征上，用条件流匹配学习速度场，将高斯噪声沿直线路径输运至正常特征分布；推理时仅用一次最优场评估的单步恢复即得干净正常令牌的后验均值，无需轨迹积分；以恢复时间控制修改强度，并采用normal-only的Jacobian增益-噪声准则仅凭支持集选择该时间；查询令牌与恢复对应物之间的平方残差用于密集定位与图像级检测。

Result: 在MVTec-AD与VisA的1/2/4-shot基准及Real-IAD的sample-shot评测上，相对近期类别特定方法达到SOTA。1-shot下MVTec-AD图像/像素AUROC为97.8%/98.0%，VisA为95.3%/98.7%，VisA图像级AUROC较最强基线提升2.1个点。

Conclusion: 以流匹配式正常对应物恢复替代样例检索与线性假设，可在无需异常验证样本调参的条件下实现高效、准确的少样本工业异常检测与定位。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection requires modeling category-specific normality from only a handful of defect-free images. Common approaches tie inference to stored exemplars, impose rigid linear structure, or risk reproducing anomalous content. Flownomaly instead casts the problem as normal-counterpart recovery in frozen DINOv2 feature space. Given normal supports, a velocity field is learned through conditional flow matching over frozen patch tokens to transport Gaussian noise toward normal features. Along straight paths, a single recovery step using one evaluation of the optimal field yields the posterior mean of the clean normal token without trajectory integration. The operating time governs how strongly recovery modifies a query token. With no anomalous validation samples available for tuning, a normal-only Jacobian gain-to-noise criterion selects this time from the supports alone. Squared residuals between query tokens and their recovered counterparts yield anomaly signals for both dense localization and image-level detection. Across 1-, 2-, and 4-shot benchmarks on MVTec-AD and VisA, together with sample-shot evaluations on Real-IAD, Flownomaly achieves state-of-the-art performance against recent category-specific methods. In the 1-shot setting, image- and pixel-level AUROC reach 97.8% and 98.0% on MVTec-AD and 95.3% and 98.7% on VisA, with a 2.1-point image-level AUROC improvement over the strongest baseline on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/b-rbmp/Flownomaly.

</details>


### [104] [MiD-VLM: Detecting and Grounding Global Semantic Misinformation in Image-Caption Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32967)
*Tarunya Prasad,Bharatesh Chakravarthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合SigLIP图文表征与DINOv2场景特征的LoRA微调Qwen2-VL-7B多模态模型，用于检测"真实图像+误导性字幕"的语义不实信息，在DGM4+验证集二分类F1达0.9679。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像搭配扭曲叙事的字幕会产生语义层面误导，而图像本身可能不含像素级篡改痕迹，使依赖像素伪影的检测方法失效，需要面向图文语义不一致的检测方案。

Method: 使用DGM4+数据集评估全局场景与语义不一致检测；通过投影MLP将SigLIP的图文表征与DINOv2的视觉场景特征融合，并以LoRA对Qwen2-VL-7B进行参数高效适配；联合执行三项任务：真实性二分类、操纵类型识别、误导性字幕token定位。

Result: DGM4+验证集上真实性二分类F1为0.9679；token级精确grounding仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的参数高效适配在语义不一致检测上具有前景，但精确的token级定位仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Semantic misinformation can arise when an authentic image is paired with a caption that changes or distorts its narrative. Because the image itself may contain no visible signs of manipulation, methods designed to detect pixel-level artifacts can miss these misleading image-caption pairs. We study this problem using DGM4+, a dataset designed to evaluate the detection of global scene and semantic inconsistencies. We propose a multimodal model that combines image-text representations from SigLIP with visual scene features from DINOv2 through a projection MLP, and adapts Qwen2-VL-7B using LoRA. The model jointly predicts whether an image-caption pair is authentic, identifies the manipulation type, and grounds the misleading caption tokens. On the DGM4+ validation split, the model achieves an F1 score of $0.9679$ for binary authenticity classification. These results suggest that parameter-efficient adaptation of vision-language models is promising for detecting semantic inconsistencies, while also motivating further work on precise token-level grounding.

</details>


### [105] [FlexGlue: Sparse-to-Dense Feature Matching and Visual Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32976)
*Philipp Lindenberger,Paul-Edouard Sarlin,Ondrej Miksik,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexGlue：非对称稀疏到稠密匹配与消歧网络


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像对应估计方法分两类：稀疏兴趣点匹配高效但受可重复性限制；稠密像素网格匹配鲁棒但昂贵。且二者仅用匹配对训练，难以拒绝存在视觉对称性的无关图像对，而这对SfM等应用同样重要。

Method: 提出FlexGlue神经网络，输入两张图像及第一张图中点坐标，在第二张图稠密像素中搜索有限数量对应点，输出亚像素级坐标，同时判定并拒绝非匹配对。
