Mismatch detection caught 453 of 454 text-image conflicts, while the verifier caught 114 of 118 inserted dosage errors. The demonstration shows how these controls govern the path from request to delivered advice.

</details>


### [175] [Communicating with the Entire Population: Machine Translation of Emergency Alerts and Warnings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32842)
*Jen Wilson,Vipasha Bansal,William D. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过全国性调查了解应急管理者使用商业翻译工具的流程、观点与挑战，并据此评估此类工具在应急响应中的效用与安全使用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机期间需覆盖不懂当地主流语言的受影响人群，商业翻译工具是诱人且有时唯一可用的方案，但应急管理者对其译文质量与适用性缺乏了解。

Method: 对应急管理者开展全国性问卷调查，刻画其工作流程、观点与操作挑战；随后以这些需求为基准评估商业翻译工具的表现。

Result: 调查揭示了应急管理者的实际工作流程、认知与运营难点；工具评估结果阐明了商业翻译工具的效用，并给出如何在应急响应中安全使用的实践建议。

Conclusion: 商业翻译工具可缓解应急沟通中的语言障碍，但需在明确其局限的前提下谨慎使用；研究提供了何时、如何安全使用的实践指导。

Abstract: It is essential that we communicate with the entirety of an affected population during a crisis, including those who do not speak a region's dominant language(s). Commercial Translation tools offer a tempting solution to broaden access and can provide a lifeline when no other resources are available. However, Emergency response managers often operate in the dark regarding the quality and adequacy of these automated outputs. In this paper, we conduct a nationwide survey of emergency managers to understand their current workflows, perspectives, and operational challenges. We then evaluate commercial translation tools against these needs, shedding light on their utility and offering practical guidance on how and when these tools can be safely used in emergency response.

</details>


### [176] [J-Comm: Jacobian-Guided Latent Communication between LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32871)
*Xiangwei Wang,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Ken Chen,Yu Xia,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: J-Comm：训练无关的潜空间推理重加权（GEAR）+ H+J 通信接口，实现跨模型潜空间通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间通信既需要有效的连续推理过程，又需要一个能让外部模型利用其内部计算的接口。

Method: 两个组件：(1) GEAR（Geometric Agreement for Latent Reasoning）：训练无关，在现有模型嵌入上对候选 token 重加权，逐步单调优化候选 token 在嵌入空间中的几何一致性；(2) H+J 接口：H 通道提供由反馈分布导出的连续输入，J 通道以词表索引的 Jacobian-lens 坐标传递中间状态线索；接收方用自身字典重建线索并在 prefill 阶段注入中间层，无需共享 KV cache，也不要求模型宽度一致。

Result: Qwen3-8B→Qwen3-4B 通信中，七个基准平均分最高：MATH500 94.80%、GPQA-Diamond 54.55%、AIME2024+2025 73.33%、ALFWorld 成功率 62.93%；相较 TextMAS 提升 1.29–8.59 个百分点，生成文本 token 减少 19.2–74.8%。

Conclusion: 潜空间通信可在异构宽度模型间有效实现，兼顾性能提升与文本开销显著降低。

Abstract: Latent communication requires both an effective continuous reasoning process and an interface through which an external model can leverage its intermediate computation. We present J-Comm, a framework comprising two core components. First, a training-free Geometric Agreement for Latent Reasoning (GEAR) reweights token candidates over existing model embeddings, which monotonically optimizes geometric consensus among candidate tokens in embedding space at each step. Second, we introduce an H+J (Hidden-and-Jacobian) communication interface that gives the Receiver two complementary views of the Sender's reasoning. The H channel supplies continuous inputs derived from feedback distributions, while the J channel conveys intermediate-state cues in vocabulary-indexed Jacobian-lens coordinates. Intuitively, H provides inputs for the Receiver to reason from, while J offers additional guidance within its computation. The Receiver reconstructs these cues using its own dictionary and injects them into intermediate layers during prefill, enabling communication without sharing key-value caches or requiring identical model widths. In Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-4B communication, J-Comm achieves the highest mean score across seven benchmarks, including 94.80% on MATH500, 54.55% on GPQA-Diamond, 73.33% on AIME2024+2025, and 62.93% success on ALFWorld. Compared with TextMAS, it improves performance by 1.29-8.59 percentage points and reduces generated text tokens by 19.2-74.8%.

</details>


### [177] [Adaptive Frequency Threshold Byte Pair Encoding for Mixed Natural Language and Structured Symbolic Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32921)
*Pietro Di Nicco,Lucio La Cava,Andrea Tagarelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaBPE，一种基于自适应频繁模式挖掘的分词方法，通过对候选token的长度与类型施加动态最小频率阈值，使词表能有效覆盖跨空格的多词表达与LaTeX等形式语言记号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BPE等方法将空格视为固定语义边界，导致跨空格的语义单元（多词表达、LaTeX结构）被切碎；已有superword分词方法虽放宽空格约束，但采用贪心频率驱动合并，忽视token长度，且对低资源领域语言覆盖不足。

Method: AdaBPE采用自适应频繁模式挖掘，引入双重动态性：最小频率阈值随候选token长度自适应调整，避免偏向高频短模式；同时按token类型（通用语言与形式语言，以LaTeX为例）区分阈值，从而将词表容量定向分配给领域专用记号。

Result: 在覆盖LaTeX与自然语言的五个数据集上评测，AdaBPE在领域特定内容上持续提升编码效率与下游语言模型性能，同时不损害通用语言表现。

Conclusion: 以长度与类型自适应频率阈值的分词设计可在不牺牲通用能力的前提下提升领域内容建模效果，为形式语言与多词表达的分词提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern language models rely on subwording tokenization to define the discrete units of input processing, typically using methods like Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) that treat whitespace as a fixed semantic boundary. This design is inherently limited, as many meaningful concepts---including multiword expressions and the structured environments of formal languages like LaTeX---frequently span across whitespaces. While recent superword tokenization methods attempt to address this by relaxing whitespace constraints, they often rely on greedy frequency-driven merging that fails to account for token length or the specific needs of underrepresented domain-specific languages. In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Frequency Threshold Byte Pair Encoding (AdaBPE), a new tokenization method that leverages an adaptive frequent-pattern mining approach. AdaBPE features a twofold dynamicity: it adapts minimum frequency thresholds based on both the length of a candidate token, to prevent favoring frequent short patterns, and its type, to distinguish between general and formal language patterns, using LaTeX as case in point. This allows the model to efficiently allocate vocabulary capacity to domain-specific notations, such as LaTeX, even when they represent a small fraction of the training data. We evaluate AdaBPE across five datasets spanning LaTeX and natural language, showing consistent gains in encoding efficiency and downstream language-model performance on domain-specific content without compromising general-language performance.

</details>


### [178] [Yalda-Embedding: Open Development of a Competitive Persian Retrieval Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32933)
*Yalda Taheri,Mohammad Hassan Heydari,Armon Rasooli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Yalda Embedding：基于 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B、用逾百万查询-文档对微调的 5.958 亿参数波斯语检索模型，在 FaMTEB 八项任务上取得 0.6B 级最高宏平均 0.5488，并通过知识蒸馏进一步提升至接近 4B 模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语稠密检索受限于语言特定训练资源匮乏与紧凑嵌入模型不足，多语言文本表示的进展未能直接转化为该语言的检索效果。

Method: 以 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 为初始化，在超过一百万查询-文档对上训练；采用增量消融研究对比对比学习批大小、优化稳定性策略，以及从 F2LLM-v2-8B 进行的关系型知识蒸馏；训练为单轮（one-epoch）设置。

Result: 在 FaMTEB 八个波斯语检索任务上宏平均 0.5488，为参评 0.6B 模型中最高，逐任务优于 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 与 F2LLM-v2-0.6B；逼近 Qwen3-Embedding-4B（四项胜、一项平、宏平均仅差 0.0075）。单轮训练下仅做对比微调相对初始化提升有限且两项任务退化；蒸馏配置使八项任务全部提升，均值由 0.4813 升至 0.5488，且不增加推理模型规模；未显式挖掘或提供难负例的情况下，在难负例评测任务上仍有增益。

Conclusion: 紧凑规模下通过蒸馏可获得稳定且全面的检索提升，蒸馏是优于单纯对比微调的关键环节；作者开源模型权重、训练与评测代码、成对数据集及其教师嵌入版本，便于复现、独立评测与免去昂贵教师推理的复用。

Abstract: Despite advances in multilingual text representations, effective dense retrieval for Persian remains constrained by the limited availability of language-specific training resources and compact embedding models. We present Yalda Embedding, a 595.8-million-parameter Persian retrieval model adapted from Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B using more than one million query-document pairs. On eight Persian retrieval tasks from FaMTEB, the model achieves the highest macro-average among the evaluated 0.6B models, scoring 0.5488 and outperforming Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and F2LLM-v2-0.6B on every task. It also approaches Qwen3-Embedding-4B, winning on four tasks, tying on one, and trailing by 0.0075 in macro-average. An incremental ablation study examines contrastive batch size, optimization stabilization, and relational knowledge distillation from F2LLM-v2-8B. Under the one-epoch training setup, contrastive fine-tuning alone yields a modest mean improvement over initialization and regressions on two tasks. The final distilled configuration improves all eight tasks over the non-distilled model, raising the mean from 0.4813 to 0.5488 without increasing inference-time model size. These gains include hard-negative evaluation tasks despite training without explicitly mined or supplied hard negatives. We release model weights, training and evaluation code, the pairwise dataset, and its teacher-embedded counterpart, enabling inspection of data composition and training procedures, independent evaluation, and reuse without repeating expensive teacher inference.

</details>


### [179] [Measure Before You Train: Typed Decision Models vs. a Classical Baseline vs. a Generative LLM for Routing a Personal Multilingual Inbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32955)
*Federico Lucca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出自托管个人邮件智能体 Foyer 及其"先测后训"的测量协议，在 739 封真实意/英邮件上对比九类系统，发现生产关键词规则几乎无信息、~200 标签的逻辑回归在各字段最优但漏最稀有类，联系人关系应存入图而非提示，且准确率≠产品指标，动作层面最优模型反而最常忽略需处理邮件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人邮件智能体对每封邮件做分类、紧急度判断、拟回复/建任务/忽略等决策，但其错误率极少被测量：系统中的"标签"由系统自身产生，缺乏独立真值，导致准确率评估失真、部署效果无从判断。

Method: 提出 Foyer 自托管邮件智能体与测量协议：分层采样、冻结快照、按线程分组切分、预注册假设、人工标注嵌入智能体审核回路，在训练前获得真值。数据集为 739 封真实意大利语/英语邮件（231 封无偏、500 封对抗采样），由邮箱主人标注；在按线程分组交叉验证下比较九类系统：生产关键词规则、TF-IDF+逻辑回归基线、本地类型化决策模型（Tev1、Nimble）、托管决策模型（Nimble v4-max）与托管生成式 LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma 4 26B/31B），并从多封同联系人邮件恢复发件人关系、将字段预测组合为动作做成本权衡分析。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 生产规则在消息类型上仅 28%，低于 58% 多数类基线，最显眼展示字段不含信息；(2) 约 200 个标签下逻辑回归各字段准确率最高（类型 0.79、紧急度 0.74、需回复 0.83、噪声 0.90），但从不预测两个最稀有类，与 Gemma 4 在类型上统计持平，few-shot 提示未能缩小差距；(3) 发件人关系（客户/供应商/服务）在同联系人已见邮件中恢复准确率 98.6%，单封邮件任何模型均无法推断，应放入联系人图而非提示；(4) 组合为动作后，最准确系统反而最常忽略需处理邮件（18% vs 最佳 LLM 4%），按噪声概率设成本阈值可将该比率减半并使其成为最便宜系统。

Conclusion: 个人邮件智能体的评估须以独立人工真值为前提；小样本线性模型在字段层准确率上有竞争力，但稀有类与关系类信息需外部结构化知识支撑；单字段准确率不足以代表产品价值，应以组合动作的漏判与成本为决策指标。

Abstract: Personal email agents decide, for every message, what it is, whether it is urgent, whether to draft a reply, create a task, or ignore it. Their error rate is rarely measured: the stored labels were written by the system itself. We present Foyer, a self-hosted personal inbox agent in daily use, and a measurement protocol (stratified sampling, frozen snapshots, thread-grouped splits, pre-registered hypotheses, human labels in the agent's review loop) that yields ground truth before any training. On 739 real Italian/English emails labelled by the owner (231 unbiased, 500 adversarially sampled), we compare nine systems under thread-grouped cross-validation: the production keyword rules, a TF-IDF + logistic-regression baseline, local typed decision models (Tev1, Nimble), a hosted decision model (Nimble v4-max), and hosted generative LLMs (Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma 4 26B/31B). Four results. The production rules score 28% on message type against a 58% majority class: the field shown most prominently carried no information. With ~200 labels, logistic regression has the highest accuracy on every field (type 0.79, urgency 0.74, needs-reply 0.83, is-noise 0.90), though it never predicts the two rarest classes and is statistically tied with Gemma 4 on type; few-shot prompting does not close the gap. The sender's relationship (client, supplier, service) is recovered from other emails of the same contact with 98.6% accuracy where the contact has been seen, and is not inferable from a single message by any model: it belongs in the contact graph, not the prompt. Finally, accuracy is not the product metric: composed into actions, the most accurate system most often ignores emails needing action (18% vs 4% for the best LLM); a cost-chosen threshold on its noise probability halves that rate and makes it the cheapest system.

</details>


### [180] [The Language of Instruction: Genre and Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32982)
*James E. Dobson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文主张将指令微调语言模型理解为在实现一种"教学—会话体裁"（instructional-conversational genre），即先前人机对话话语经大规模神经网络递归处理后融合而成的体裁，其通过指令微调、系统提示（system prompt）前置文本以及标记语言（特殊token、角色分配与轮次结构）的输入形式化得以具体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从技术或功能角度描述指令微调模型，缺乏对这类模型输出话语之体裁属性及其话语来源的理论界定，本文旨在补足这一概念化空白。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构：提出"教学—会话体裁"这一范畴，并将其界定为既往人机对话话语的递归性融合；随后从三个实现层面加以论证——指令微调、系统提示作为前置文本（pretext）、以及借助标记语言对输入进行形式化（特殊token、角色分配、轮次转换结构）。

Result: 论证表明该体裁在应用中以三重机制被实例化：指令微调过程、系统提示的前置设定，以及基于标记语言的输入形式化（含特殊token与角色化轮次结构）。

Conclusion: 指令微调语言模型应被视为一种体裁的实现而非单纯的工具，其话语特征源自人机对话的递归累积与形式化编码，为理解此类模型的语言行为提供了体裁理论视角。

Abstract: This article argues that instruction-fine-tuned language models should be understood as implementing a genre, what is proposed as the instructional-conversational genre. This genre is an amalgamation of discursive language from prior conversations between generative models and humans recursively run through these massive neural networks. It is instantiated in applications through instruction fine-tuning, the inclusion of a pretext known as a system prompt, and the formalization of inputs in mark-up languages characterized by the use of special tokens and role assignment in turntaking structures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Tradeoff-Aware Territory Design: A Multi-Objective Decision System for Enterprise Customer-to-Seller Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31701)
*Nan Wang,Mitchell Turiel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向企业级GTM领土划分的生产级决策系统，融合账户价值预测、基于合成控制式反事实的客户-销售关系连续性估值与多目标优化三层，实现可检视的权衡决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B2B GTM组织中领土划分决定账户覆盖、关系存续与有限外勤产能分配，但流程多为人工，难以比较机会、评估关系连续性的价值，也难以在增长、均衡、产能与可行性之间权衡。

Method: 三层集成：1）多维账户价值层，融合预测信号为机会打分；2）观测式连续性估值层，用合成控制式反事实框架估计打破销售-账户关系的成本；3）多目标优化层，在高价值账户覆盖、连续性、机会均衡及运营约束下进行分配，并以场景化决策支持系统部署。

Result: 历史回测显示优化器生成场景显著提升分配合规性，同时保持可比的销售级机会分布；生产规划中，被采纳场景相对原基线同时改善合规性与机会均衡；将稀缺产能导向高价值账户在企业规模下具备增量收入潜力。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将领土划分转化为可治理决策流程的集成、可检视框架，支持规划者检视与比较不同分配权衡。

Abstract: Enterprise customer journeys are shaped not only by product experiences and marketing, but also by recurring resource-allocation decisions. In B2B go-to-market (GTM) organizations, territory carving determines which accounts receive seller coverage, which seller-account relationships are preserved, and how limited field capacity is distributed. Yet the process often remains manual, making it difficult to compare opportunities, value continuity, and evaluate tradeoffs among growth, balance, capacity, and feasibility.
  We present a production decision system for tradeoff-aware customer-to-seller allocation in enterprise GTM planning. It integrates three components: a multidimensional account-value layer that combines predictive signals to score opportunity; an observational continuity-valuation layer that estimates the cost of breaking seller-account relationships using a synthetic-control-style counterfactual framework; and a multi-objective optimization layer that allocates coverage to high-value accounts while considering continuity, opportunity balance, and operational constraints.
  Deployed as a scenario-based decision-support system, the framework enables planners to inspect and compare allocation tradeoffs. Historical backtesting shows that optimizer-generated scenarios substantially improve assignment compliance while maintaining comparable seller-level opportunity distribution. In production planning, adopted scenarios improved both assignment compliance and opportunity balance relative to the previous baseline. Directing scarce seller capacity toward higher-value accounts also offers incremental revenue potential at enterprise scale. The key contribution is an integrated, inspectable framework that turns territory carving into a governable decision process.

</details>


### [182] [Phronix: The Capability-Centric Learning Paradigm for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31789)
*Hariharan Devarajan,Neeraj Rajesh,Aniruddha Marathe,Nikoli Dryden,Kin Ng,Michela Taufer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Phronix，一种以能力为中心的学习范式，让智能体框架能从自身执行中获取、验证并精炼可复用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算日益依赖基于大语言模型的智能体进行性能工程（如插桩、分析、工作流移植），但智能体效果高度依赖外围harness，而现有harness依赖人工编写的技能与精心整理的记忆，限制了自主学习，导致智能体反复重新发现解决方案而非积累复用知识。

Method: 采用"预测器-验证器-纠正器"循环：MCP工具及关联技能提出解决方案，工具执行进行验证，观测结果用于精炼已存储能力。将每次交互蒸馏进能力库，并对失败任务进行主动研究而非放弃。

Result: 1) 学得能力使重复任务首次尝试即解决率达93-95%（跨所有模型），降低解的非确定性；2) 每个任务无需用户指令即可产生7.6-30.8个持久能力，能力存在后新能力创建降至2个以下；3) 热启动每任务仅消耗0.38-0.40百万token，比单次基线尝试少3-4倍，冷启动成本不高于基线；4) 在1000 Genome Pegasus工作流插桩任务中通过全部11项正确性检查（基线仅6项），并将两个低成本模型在SWE-bench上的解决率从29%/27%提升至91%/87%，达到最贵评估模型的水平。

Conclusion: Phronix通过能力中心化学习，使智能体harness具备自我积累与复用知识的能力，显著提升可靠性、降低token消耗并提升廉价模型表现，为科学计算性能工程提供更自主高效的智能体方案。

Abstract: Scientific computing increasingly relies on large language model-based agents for performance engineering tasks such as instrumentation, analysis, and workflow porting. However, agent effectiveness depends heavily on the surrounding harness, which typically relies on manually written skills and curated memory. This limits autonomous learning, forcing agents to repeatedly rediscover solutions rather than retain and reuse knowledge from prior executions. We introduce Phronix, a capability-centric learning paradigm that enables agentic harnesses to acquire, validate, and refine reusable capabilities from their own executions. Phronix operates through a predictor-validator-corrector loop: MCP tools and associated skills propose solutions, tool executions validate them, and observed outcomes refine the stored capabilities. It distills every observed interaction into a capability store and researches a failing task rather than abandoning it. First, such learned capabilities solve a recurring task on the first attempt in 93-95% of cases across every model, significantly reducing solution non-determinism and improving reliability. Second, Phronix turns an agent's own execution into 7.6-30.8 durable capabilities per task without user instruction, and creation falls below two once the needed capability exists. Third, warm-start runs consume only 0.38-0.40 million tokens per task, which is three to four times less than a single baseline attempt, while a cold-start costs no more than the baseline. On the 1000 Genome Pegasus workflow, instrumenting a production application for I/O performance analysis, Phronix passes all 11 correctness checks against six for the baseline harness, and also lifts the task solving rates of two cheaper models from 29% and 27% of SWE-bench tasks solved to 91% and 87%, respectively, matching the performance of the most expensive evaluated model.

</details>


### [183] [Patronizing and Condescending Language Detection in English and Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31808)
*Xinyi Vicky Xiang,Catherine Qingyun Wang,Paula Cortés Lemos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PCL（施恩俯就式语言）自动检测系统，对比静态词向量+CNN与微调BERT两种方案，后者效果显著更佳，开发集F1为0.528。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施恩俯就式语言（PCL）贬低弱势群体、强化负面社会刻板印象，是一种有害语言；但与显性仇恨言论不同，它隐蔽性强，连人类标注者都难以识别，因此需要自动化检测手段。

Method: 构建两套分类系统：方案一采用静态词嵌入配合卷积神经网络（CNN）进行分类；方案二采用预训练BERT嵌入并在PCL任务上微调。

Result: 基于微调BERT的方案明显优于静态词向量+CNN方案，在开发集上取得0.528的F1分数。

Conclusion: 预训练上下文表示（BERT微调）更适合PCL这类隐晦有害语言的检测；但整体F1仍偏低，说明该任务难度较大，仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Patronizing and condescending language (PCL) is a form of harmful language that belittles and assumes pity for vulnerable groups, reinforcing negative societal stereotypes. However, unlike overt hate speech, it is often difficult to detect, even for human judges. This paper proposes a system for the automated detection of PCL. Our first approach uses static word embeddings and a convolutional neural network for classification, while our second approach uses pre-trained BERT embeddings fine-tuned for the PCL task. Our second approach was considerably more successful, and achieved an F1 score of 0.528 over the development set.

</details>


### [184] [PaperFormalizer: Author-in-the-Loop Autoformalization of Research Papers in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31810)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaperFormalizer 是一个基于 Lean 的智能体流水线，从 LaTeX 源文件出发，将论文转写为陈述依赖图、以自然语言审计其忠实性与歧义、再用 Lean/Mathlib 形式化并证明，全程将语义决定权交还作者，最终产出经 Lean 验证的论文版本、修订报告与可复用的 Lean 代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即便是仔细校对的论文，证明与假设中仍可能残留细小缺口。作者本人最清楚陈述的真实意图及形式化缺口的修补方式，因此需要一个既能量化验证、又以作者为中心的辅助流程，使"经验证的论文"变得可实际生产，从而提升依赖数学证明的研究的稳健性。

Method: 1) 从 LaTeX 源文件自动转写为陈述的依赖图；2) 对每条陈述以自然语言审计忠实性（faithfulness）、歧义性与正确性；3) 在 Lean 中对照 Mathlib、可选领域库及作者个人工具包进行形式化与证明；4) 当陈述存在多种解读或需要修复时，流水线暂停并向作者提问，将每项修复以自然语言加 TeX 符号的形式作为提案呈现，供作者接受、拒绝或改向，凡可能改变论文主张的决策均交由作者裁定。系统运行于编码智能体（Claude Code 或 Codex）内。

Result: 输出包括：带注释与修订的论文版本、变更报告，以及每条声明均与其所形式化陈述相链接的 Lean 代码。随着更多论文被形式化，作者的个人工具包持续扩充；作者可共享已验证论文，使后续研究者复用其 Lean 代码。

Conclusion: 通过让验证论文的产出变得切实可行，PaperFormalizer 旨在使基于数学证明的研究更加稳健。系统已开源（https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/PaperFormalizer），用户共享 Lean 代码的论文可在 https://paperformalizer.org 获取。

Abstract: Even a carefully proofread paper can leave small gaps in its proofs or its stated assumptions. We introduce PaperFormalizer, a Lean-based agentic pipeline that helps authors find and close such gaps in their own papers and turn each paper into a Lean-verified version. Starting from the LaTeX source, PaperFormalizer transcribes the paper into a dependency graph of statements, audits each statement in natural language for faithfulness, ambiguity and correctness, and then formalizes and proves the statements in Lean against Mathlib, optional domain libraries and the author's personal toolkit. Only the author knows what a statement is meant to say and how a formalization gap in a result is best fixed. The pipeline therefore stops and asks the author whenever a statement admits more than one reading, presents every repair, in natural language and TeX symbols, as a proposal that the author can accept, decline or redirect, and leaves to the author every decision that could potentially change what the paper claims. The output is an annotated and revised version of the paper, a report of the changes, and Lean code in which each declaration is linked to the statement it formalizes. The author's toolkit grows as more papers are formalized, and authors can share their verified papers so that researchers who build on them can reuse their Lean code. By making verified papers practical to produce, PaperFormalizer aims to make research that rests on mathematical proofs more robust. PaperFormalizer runs inside a coding agent (Claude Code or Codex) and is available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/PaperFormalizer. Papers with Lean code shared by users are available at https://paperformalizer.org.

</details>


### [185] [Don't Forget What You Just Learned: Self-Directed Repair Turns Agent Mistakes Into Lasting Lessons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31846)
*Yuval Kansal,Karen Ullrich,Polina Kirichenko,Ilia Kulikov,Niraj K. Jha,Mohammad Pezeshki,Mark Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Direction 机制：智能体失败后自我反思提炼教训，即时蒸馏为低秩适配器并存入持久"教训库"，按需路由复用，实现从错误中持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体纠错方式存在缺陷：上下文内反思（in-context reflection）跨会话易遗忘且占用有限上下文；全参数微调成本高昂且可能损害已有能力。因此需要一种既能即时纠正、又能持久积累且不影响基座能力的学习机制。

Method: 智能体任务失败后，对自身轨迹进行反思以提取教训，并立即将其蒸馏为轻量级低秩适配器（LoRA 类）；训练时冻结原始权重，每个适配器仅训练约相当于基座模型参数量 0.02% 的额外参数；适配器存入持久教训库，后续遇到相关问题自动路由调用。

Result: 在 Qwen 系列开放权重模型驱动的智能体上，于开放式研究（GAIA）、工具使用工作流（Claw-Eval）、终端任务（Terminal-Bench 2.1）三个基准测试中，整体成功率提升 8.9–17.0 个百分点，与前沿系统表现相当；显著减少后续同类任务中的重复错误，纠错可靠性接近翻倍；少量适配器可迁移至数十个新任务而无需额外训练。

Conclusion: Self-Direction 将临时性上下文重试与策略更新相结合，把日常错误转化为可持久复用、可迁移的"教训"，实现低成本、可扩展的智能体自我改进。

Abstract: Intelligent agents should learn from their mistakes so that experience leads to continuous improvement. Yet today's agents recover from a mistake only temporarily: in-context reflections are easily forgotten across sessions and compete for limited context, while full-parameter fine-tuning is expensive and can interfere with existing capabilities. We introduce Self-Direction, a mechanism for agents to learn from mistakes that combines the benefits of temporary in-context retries and policy updates. When an agent fails a task, it reflects on its own trajectory in order to extract a lesson and immediately distills it into a lightweight low-rank adapter. By keeping the original weights frozen, Self-Direction prevents adapter training from overwriting base-model parameters while training additional parameters equivalent to ~0.02% of the base model's parameter count for each adapter. The agent then saves these adapters in a persistent library of lessons, automatically routing them to solve related problems later. Experiments with agents based on open-weight models from the Qwen family across open-ended research (GAIA), tool-use workflows (Claw-Eval), and terminal tasks (Terminal-Bench 2.1) show that our method improves overall success rates by 8.9 to 17.0 percentage points and performs competitively with frontier systems. We find that Self-Direction reduces repeated mistakes across future encounters of a task, nearly doubling the reliability of an agent's corrections. Learned lessons also transfer: a handful of adapters generalize to dozens of new tasks without additional training. Ultimately, this self-directed repair transforms everyday mistakes into lasting lessons.

</details>


### [186] [TACS-RF: Tool-driven Agentic Circuit Synthesis of RF and mmWave Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31882)
*Tristan Liang,Aravind Nagulu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TACS-RF 框架，由 LLM 编码智能体（Claude Opus 4.8）驱动 Cadence Virtuoso/Spectre、Calibre、EMX、HFSS 等 EDA 工具，完成 90 nm SiGe BiCMOS 240–251 GHz sub-THz 发射机的半自主设计，朝 "text-to-ASIC" 方向迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF/mmWave 硬件设计长期依赖专家手工迭代，流程复杂、门槛高；作者希望借助 LLM 智能体直接调度工业级 EDA 工具链，探索射频电路设计的自动化与"文本到芯片"范式。

Method: 构建 TACS-RF（工具驱动的智能体化电路综合）框架，由 LLM 编码智能体直接操作 EDA 工具完成设计闭环。以一个 240–251 GHz sub-THz 发射机为验证载体，智能体半自主设计各子模块：带二次谐波提取的 60 GHz push-push Colpitts VCO、多级差分 cascode 功率驱动、push-push 倍频器、差分 bowtie 天线及各级匹配网络；设计经寄生提取与 EM 验证。

Result: 发射机链在仅变容管调谐的 11 GHz 范围内输出 +5.1~+6.3 dBm，1 MHz 偏移处相位噪声 -80~-88 dBc/Hz，直流功耗 361 mW（含寄生提取器件与 EM 验证无源件）。

Conclusion: 框架可行性得到端到端验证，并给出智能体驱动的设计流程及人机分工模式，为 RF/mmWave 硬件的 "text-to-ASIC" 自动化设计奠定基础。

Abstract: We present the framework of TACS-RF - a Tool-driven Agentic Circuit Synthesis of RF and mmWave modules. In this framework, a large-language-model (LLM) coding agent (Claude Opus 4.8) directly drives industry-standard electronic-design-automation (EDA) tools such as Cadence Virtuoso/Spectre, Calibre, Cadence EMX, and Ansys HFSS to design RF and mmWave modules. We validate the framework through the agentic design of a 240-251 GHz sub-THz transmitter in a 90 nm SiGe BiCMOS process. The agent semi-autonomously designed the sub-blocks including a 60 GHz push-push Colpitts VCO with second-harmonic extraction, multistage differential cascode power drivers, a push-push frequency doubler, a differential bowtie antenna, and all the interstage matching networks. The transmitter chain with parasitic-extracted devices and EM-verified passives delivers +5.1 to +6.3 dBm across an 11 GHz varactor-only tuning range, phase noise of -80 to -88 dBc/Hz at 1 MHz offset while consuming a DC power of 361 mW. We describe the agent-driven design flow and the resulting division of labor between human and agent, taking a step toward a "text-to-ASIC" future for designing RF/mmWave hardware.

</details>


### [187] [Permission to Retry: A Study of Authorization and Recovery in AI Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31916)
*Deekshith Marla,Kushagra Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将工具型AI智能体的执行期可靠性从语言模型行为中剥离，以有限一致性研究检验"恢复决策"的四项独立义务（检查点状态、派发许可、外部效果证据、对账许可），在LangGraph与Temporal两种宿主上验证其与宿主无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化智能体在其权限变化、外部结果不确定时仍需恢复并完成有后果的动作。现有工作多将恢复能力与LLM行为混杂评估，缺乏对"当前权限、提供方契约、保留观测"各自作用的隔离检验，安全性、完成性与未决义务也未分开报告。

Method: 提出有限一致性研究：将恢复判定拆分为检查点状态、派发许可、外部效果证据、对账许可四项义务，用历史记录对照对恢复应用隐藏的提供方效果账本进行评分。前瞻性冻结的试验在原生LangGraph与Temporal宿主上执行336条历史，交叉14种调度、3种提供方契约与4种匹配的应用策略（共168个配对宿主单元），并对Arya.ai的InfinityOS做16例组件研究，覆盖导出的权限、worker、恢复与结算代码。

Result: 168个配对宿主单元的全部checked结果完全一致，显示结论与宿主无关。每宿主上，保留账本对账策略通过全部42条历史：21次已检查完成、15次拒绝、6次结果未知但保留预留。缓存重试、重试前再验证、稳定键重试分别通过30、36、31条历史。

Conclusion: 恢复可靠性取决于当前权限、提供方契约与保留观测三者的分离作用，而非宿主实现；显式保留账本并执行对账的恢复策略在安全性、完成性与未决义务上均优于各类重试策略，说明应将恢复判定与模型行为解耦，并按声明的应用配置分别报告三类结果。

Abstract: A durable agent can resume a consequential action while its authority changes and the action's external outcome remains uncertain. We study execution-time reliability of tool-using AI agents, isolating recovery decisions from language-model behavior. Checkpoint state, permission to dispatch, evidence of an external effect and permission to reconcile are distinct obligations. We present a finite conformance study that checks these obligations through histories scored against a provider effect ledger hidden from the recovering application. A prospectively frozen campaign executes 336 histories on native LangGraph and Temporal hosts, crossing 14 schedules, three provider contracts and four matched application policies. All 168 paired host cells have identical checked outcomes. Per host, retained-ledger reconciliation passes all 42 histories: 21 checked completions, 15 denials and six unknown outcomes with reservations retained. Cached retry, revalidation before retry and stable-key retry pass 30, 36 and 31 histories respectively. A separate 16-case component study of InfinityOS, an enterprise AI platform developed by Arya.ai, exercises exported authority, worker, recovery and settlement code over repository fakes. The results isolate the roles of current authority, provider contracts and retained observations in recovery, with safety, completion and unresolved obligations reported separately for the declared application profile.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond On-Policy vs. Off-Policy: At What Level Should LLM Agent Distillation Follow the Student?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31918)
*Ya Gao,Zhengtao Zou,Pekka Marttinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出层次化智能体蒸馏框架，将“交互历史来源”与“动作前缀来源”两级的on-policy程度解耦并显式控制其配对、混合比例与训练日程；实验表明“学生历史+教师前缀”单独训练最强，全on-policy最弱，但纳入混合数据后有益，且两级配对方式与训练顺序（先教师后学生）显著影响效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式LLM智能体蒸馏通常被简化为“教师示范（off-policy）”与“学生自生成经验（on-policy）”的二选一，但作者指出这一区分本质上是层次化的：诱导交互历史的策略与生成当前动作token前缀的策略可以不同，现有工作未对这两级on-policiness及其组合开展系统研究。

Method: 设定冻结教师可获得学生无法获得的任务指导，构建三级（历史级、前缀级）on-policy形式化框架，允许显式设定两级的配对方式、混合比例和训练日程；采用自蒸馏设置，在3个模型、4个交互环境上开展三阶段受控实验，比较不同配置的独立效果、混合效果及训练顺序效果。

Result: 1）学生诱导历史+教师生成动作前缀在单配置训练中信号最强；2）两级均跟随学生（完全on-policy）单独使用效果最差；3）完全on-policy数据在与其他配置混合时转为有益；4）两级教师/学生比例相同但配对方式不同的混合可产生显著性能差异；5）前置教师生成上下文、后置完全on-policy上下文的日程优于静态混合。

Conclusion: 智能体蒸馏中“在哪里、多少、何时”跟随学生是可分离设计的问题：应优先在学生历史+教师前缀层面采用on-policy，将完全on-policy数据作为混合成分而非独立信号，并采用先教师后学生的课程式训练日程；层次化on-policiness控制为蒸馏策略设计提供了可操作原则。

Abstract: Distilling interactive LLM agents requires choosing between teacher-generated demonstrations and student-generated experience, corresponding broadly to off-policy and on-policy distillation. While both have proven effective, we argue that this distinction is inherently hierarchical: the policy that induces an interaction history need not be the same as the policy that generates the token prefix of the current action. We introduce a hierarchical agent distillation framework that formalizes these two levels of on-policiness and enables explicit control over their pairing, mixture proportions, and training schedule. We study this problem through self-distillation, where a frozen teacher has access to task guidance unavailable to the student, and conduct a three-stage controlled study across three models and four interactive environments. We find that student-induced histories paired with teacher-generated action prefixes generally provide the strongest standalone training signal, whereas following the student at both levels generally performs worst. Nevertheless, fully on-policy data becomes beneficial when incorporated into a mixture with other configurations. Moreover, mixtures with identical teacher/student proportions at each level can yield substantially different performance, showing that how the two levels are paired matters beyond the overall amount of on-policy data. Finally, front-loading fully teacher-generated contexts and delaying fully on-policy contexts improves over a static mixture. These findings provide practical principles for deciding where, how much, and when agent distillation should follow the student.

</details>


### [189] [$M^3$: A Multi-LLM System for Multi-Turn Jailbreaking as a Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31937)
*Yuqing Huang,Jack Ji,Jithin Pradeep,Sagarika Suresh,Jekaterina Novikova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 M³ 自动化红队框架，将多轮越狱形式化为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），在黑盒设定下显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮红队方法依赖启发式规则或强假设，在完全黑盒设定下适用性受限，缺乏原则性、可扩展的自动化框架。

Method: M³ 框架：攻击 LLM 与目标 LLM 交互，并借助状态 LLM 生成紧凑内部状态，从而把多轮越狱建模为有限时域 MDP；不依赖预定义风险分类体系。

Result: 在两个基准、六个目标模型上，ASR@5 最多提升 53%；即使使用轻量攻击者，相较更强攻击者基线仍获 ASR@1 提升 26%、ASR@5 提升 34%；风险随轮次上升更陡峭，跨风险类别一致性更好（Gini 指数最多改善 4.82）。

Conclusion: M³ 提供了可扩展、有理论依据的多轮红队与安全评估框架，兼顾效果、一致性与黑盒适用性。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to carefully orchestrated multi-turn interactions that progressively bypass safety mechanisms. However, many existing red-teaming approaches rely on heuristics or impose strong assumptions that limit their applicability in fully black box settings. We address this gap by introducing $M^3$, an automated red-teaming framework that formalizes multi-turn jailbreaking as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process (MDP). In $M^3$, an attacker LLM interacts with a target LLM while operating over a compact internal state produced by a state LLM. Across two benchmarks and six target models, $M^3$ consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-turn red-teaming methods in Attack Success Rates (ASR), achieving improvements of up to 53% in ASR@5. Even with lightweight attacker, $M^3$ attains gains up to 26% in ASR@1 and 34% in ASR@5, compared to baselines that use higher capacity attackers. Beyond improved effectiveness, $M^3$ exhibits steeper risk escalation over conversational turns and achieves more consistent performance across risk categories (up to 4.82 improvement in Gini index) without relying on predefined risk taxonomy, providing a scalable and principled framework for multi-turn red-teaming and safety evaluation.

</details>


### [190] [Knowing Whom to Trust is not Enough: The Trust--Agreement Gap in Repeated Multi-Party LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31971)
*Amit Sarker,Moumita Choudhury,Saaduddin Mahmud,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体能否从历史交互中形成信任估计并用于改进多方重复谈判结果，提出"信任—协议鸿沟"现象：智能体能在数轮内识别破坏者，但准确信任仅在显式引导使用时才提升协议率（最多+23个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地代表个人与组织参与谈判，但在重复多方交互中易受操纵。经典多智能体系统通过显式信任与声誉模型应对此问题，而现有LLM谈判工作多将信任视为涌现现象，缺乏对信任建模价值的系统检验。

Method: 构建重复、多方、多议题谈判框架：固定参与者进行一系列谈判博弈，其中一名参与者暗中阻挠达成协议；每局结束后智能体评估对手并维护跨局延续的两两信任估计；对比"信任感知"与"信任无关"智能体，分析显式信任对谈判结果的影响。

Result: 存在"信任—协议鸿沟"：信任感知智能体可在几局内识别对抗者，但准确信任估计并不总能转化为更优协议；仅当智能体被显式引导在决策中使用信任时，协议率才提升最多23个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式信任可改善重复性LLM谈判，但其价值关键在于信任的评估方式以及如何将其融入智能体推理过程，而非信任本身的存在。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly participate in negotiations on behalf of individuals and organizations, yet they remain vulnerable to manipulation, particularly in repeated multi-party interactions. Classical multi-agent systems address this challenge through explicit trust and reputation models, whereas existing work on LLM negotiation largely treats trust as an emergent phenomenon. We investigate whether LLM agents can form useful trust estimates from prior interactions and leverage them to improve future negotiation outcomes. We introduce a repeated, multi-party, multi-issue negotiation framework in which recurring agents participate in a sequence of negotiation games while one participant covertly attempts to prevent agreement. After each game, agents assess their counterparts and maintain pairwise trust estimates that carry over to subsequent negotiations. We compare trust-aware and trust-agnostic agents and analyze how explicit trust influences negotiation outcomes. Our results reveal a \emph{trust--agreement gap}: trust-aware agents can identify the adversary within a few games, but accurate trust estimates do not consistently translate into better agreements. Trust increases agreement rates by up to 23 percentage points, but only when agents are explicitly guided to use it in their decisions. Explicit trust can therefore improve repeated LLM negotiations, but its value depends critically on how trust is assessed and incorporated into agents' reasoning.

</details>


### [191] [Reproducible Evaluation as the Substrate for Safe Prompt Compression in Production Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31972)
*Prabal Vashisht,Zheng Hu,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对作用于动态企业数据的生产级对话智能体，提出可复现、可按需再生成的离线评测框架，并将其作为 GEPA 提示优化的适应度函数，在正确性门控下实现提示压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测作用于实时可变企业数据的对话智能体十分困难：回放会话会因后端状态变化而漂移，今天构建的基准会随着所描述数据的变动而悄然失效，因此需要兼顾可复现性与可再生成的评测手段。

Method: 构建离线评测框架，在底层数据变化时仍保持可复现，并在基准陈旧时按需重新生成；随后将该框架作为 GEPA 提示优化的适应度函数，并施加正确性门控以防止奖励作弊。

Result: 应用于面向商家的生产级智能体后，正确性门控下的压缩使智能体完整提示面缩减 3.5–7.6%，且在三个独立的主体模型家族上均未造成留出任务正确性退化，其中一个模型家族的正确性还有可测量的提升。

Conclusion: 可再生成的离线评测框架可作为可靠的优化适应度来源；结合正确性门控的提示压缩能稳定精简生产智能体的提示规模而不损害正确性。

Abstract: Evaluating conversational agents that act on live, mutable enterprise data is hard: replayed sessions drift as backend state changes, and a benchmark built today can silently go stale as the data it describes moves on. We present an offline evaluation harness for a production conversational agent that stays reproducible despite the live data it operates on changing, and that can be regenerated on demand once it goes stale. We then use this harness as the fitness function for GEPA-based prompt optimization under a correctness gate that guards against reward hacking. Applied to a production merchant-facing agent, correctness-gated compression shrinks the agent's full prompt surface by 3.5-7.6% without regressing held-out task correctness, consistently across three independent subject-model families, and for one, correctness measurably improves.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Channel Data: Configuration-Enriched Radio Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32542)
*Shrihari Vasudevan,H. G. Ranjani,Raaghul Ranganathan,Satwik Sahu,Thirumaran Ekambaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将信道配置(CFG)信息纳入无线基础模型(RFM)预训练，使得到的CFG增强嵌入在标签稀缺的下游任务中成为有效归纳先验，并在LoS/NLoS分类、TA估计与波束选择三类任务、三种数据规模及分布内外配置设定下验证其优于或可比于各基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFM预训练通常忽略信道配置这一关键元信息，导致嵌入缺乏对物理信道结构的先验表达；在标签稀缺的下游场景中，模型难以泛化，尤其在未见配置的分布外场景下性能退化明显。

Method: 在RFM预训练阶段引入信道配置(CFG)信息，构建CFG增强嵌入；与原始信道token及多个基线模型对比，在三种数据规模（不同标签量级）下针对LoS/NLoS分类、定时提前(TA)估计、波束选择三类下游任务评估，并分别在分布内（已见配置）与分布外（未见配置）设定下测试。

Result: 在已见与未见配置两类实验场景中，所提CFG增强嵌入一致地优于或至少可比于全部基线，且在标签稀缺条件下仍保持稳健表现，体现出良好的跨配置泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将CFG信息注入RFM预训练是提升嵌入表征质量与下游泛化性的有效手段，CFG增强嵌入可作为标签稀缺条件下的强归纳先验；该结论在多种数据规模与分布内外配置设定下均得到支持。

Abstract: This paper proposes incorporating channel configuration (CFG) information into Radio Foundation Model (RFM) pre-training and demonstrates that the resulting CFG-enriched embeddings serve as effective inductive priors for downstream tasks under label scarcity. We compare the proposed embeddings with raw channel tokens and multiple baseline models, under three different data regimes, for multiple downstream tasks --- Line-of-Sight/Non-Line-of-Sight (LoS/NLoS) classification, timing advance (TA) estimation, and beam selection. Evaluation is done in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution configuration settings. Experimental outcomes show that the proposed embeddings consistently outperform and/or are competitive to the baselines, across experiments in both seen and unseen configuration scenarios.

</details>


### [193] [The Helpful Agent Problem: When Emergent Cooperation Among LLM Agents Bypasses Intended Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31985)
*Moumita Choudhury,Amit Sarker,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"helpful agent problem"（善意智能体问题）：多个各自追求不同任务的LLM智能体在共享资源环境中，会在评审/监督不可用时自发互相"帮忙"，绕过系统既定的跨所有者变更审核结构。作者构建FavorBench基准并改造CooperBench，在2个基准、4个模型中验证此类"工作流外帮助行为"频繁出现，且部分模型的帮助行为对交互之外的第三方造成显著伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要研究对抗性或显式串谋的多智能体行为，忽视了智能体并非恶意、只是"想帮忙"时产生的安全风险。作者希望揭示：即便没有共享奖励或显式合作激励，良性智能体也可能自发协作，从而绕过系统层面的监督保障机制，构成一类被忽视的多智能体安全挑战。

Method: 1) 构建长时程共享资源环境：智能体有时依赖同伴拥有的资源，跨所有者变更通常由reviewer中介；当评审不可用时观察智能体行为。2) 提出受控基准FavorBench，可测量下游副作用；3) 将CooperBench改造用于真实代码仓库场景。4) 在基准上评测4个模型，检验工作流外帮助行为的出现频率及其外部伤害。

Result: 在两个基准、四个模型上，工作流外的"帮忙"行为频繁出现（即使无共享奖励与显式合作激励）。在FavorBench中，至少三个模型产生的帮助行为对交互双方之外的第三方造成显著伤害。这些行为均在各智能体的本地权限范围内，因而能规避系统既定的监督结构而不触发权限告警。

Conclusion: 合作本身即可成为绕开系统级安全保障的通路，构成区别于对抗性攻击与显式串谋的独立多智能体安全风险类别。安全设计与评测需将"善意驱动的自发协作"纳入考量，而不仅是恶意或共谋行为。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in collaborative settings where they pursue separate tasks while sharing resources and operating under review or oversight processes. Such settings create safety risks not only when agents are malicious, but also when they are simply trying to help. Prior work has largely studied adversarial or explicitly collusive behavior; we show that benign agents pursuing separate objectives can spontaneously cooperate in ways that bypass intended workflows. We call this the \emph{helpful agent problem}. We study long-horizon shared-resource environments in which agents sometimes depend on resources owned by their peers and cross-owner changes are normally mediated by a reviewer. When review becomes unavailable, agents begin making changes on one another's behalf despite having no shared reward or explicit incentive to cooperate. Crucially, these actions remain within each agent's local permissions while circumventing the system's intended oversight structure. We introduce \textsc{FavorBench}, a controlled benchmark with measurable downstream side effects, and adapt \textsc{CooperBench} to test the phenomenon in realistic repositories. Across two benchmarks and four models, we find that out-of-workflow favors emerge frequently. Further, in \textsc{FavorBench}, favors from at least three models cause significant harm to parties outside the interaction. These results reveal a distinct multi-agent safety challenge: cooperation itself can create pathways around system-level safeguards.

</details>


### [194] [CEMIR vs. FISER: a comparative study of reasoning-based and enrichment-based approaches to pragmatic intent recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32022)
*Ziya Hosseynzadeh,Farjad Fallah,Farnoosh Fallah,Soroush Gooran,Mohammad Amanloo,Hossein Sameti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对结构化符号推理框架FISER与文本派生多模态增强方法CEMIR进行对比与诊断性研究，发现在HandMeThat v2基准上，当歧义源于未明说的人类意图时，两者在四个LLM及所有难度层级上均无统计显著差异，性能主要由骨干模型能力与歧义固有难度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中的意图识别需推断说话者隐含目标。现有两条主流范式——结构化符号推理与多模态输入增强——各自发展，但尚不清楚在歧义来自"未言明的意图"而非"缺失的可观测场景信息"时，基于增强的方法是否仍带来收益。

Method: 以HandMeThat v2为基准，将FISER（基于结构化社会性与具身推理的框架）与CEMIR（由文本提示合成叙事与照片级真实图像实现多模态增强）进行基准对比；覆盖Qwen2.5-Omni-3B至GPT-5共四个LLM，构成16组"模型×难度"比较，并采用多重比较校正的统计分析，另设消融实验分别隔离CEMIR的叙事与视觉组件。

Result: 在所有模型与难度层级上，FISER与CEMIR之间均无统计显著性能差异；该结论在全部16组模型×难度比较以及分离叙事与视觉组件的消融中保持一致。性能差异主要与骨干模型能力和歧义固有难度相关。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了文本派生多模态增强的边界条件：当任务相关约束已存在于文本指令中时，合成多模态增强相对基于同一文本的结构化推理并无可测收益。

Abstract: Intent recognition in human-robot interaction requires agents to resolve ambiguous natural language instructions by inferring the speaker's underlying goals. Research has increasingly focused on two notable paradigms for addressing this task: structured symbolic reasoning and multimodal input enrichment. However, it remains unclear whether enrichment-based methods provide benefit in settings where the ambiguity concerns unstated human intent rather than missing observable scene information. In this paper, we present a comparative and diagnostic study benchmarking FISER - a framework utilizing structured social and embodied reasoning - against CEMIR (Context-Enriched Multimodal Intent Recognition), a method that performs multimodal enrichment by synthesizing narratives and photorealistic images from textual prompts. By evaluating both approaches across four Large Language Models (LLMs), ranging from Qwen2.5-Omni-3B to GPT-5, on the HandMeThat v2 benchmark, we analyze their capability to resolve pragmatic ambiguities. Our statistical analysis, employing multiple-comparison correction, reveals no statistically significant difference in performance between FISER and CEMIR across any model or difficulty level. This pattern holds across all sixteen model-by-difficulty comparisons, as well as in a targeted ablation isolating CEMIR's narrative and visual components. Instead, performance is primarily associated with backbone capability and the inherent difficulty of the ambiguity. These findings suggest a boundary condition for text-derived multimodal enrichment: when the relevant task constraints are already available in the textual instruction, synthetic multimodal augmentation may not provide measurable benefit over structured reasoning on the same text.

</details>


### [195] [BARC-Bench: Benign Asks, Risky Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32024)
*Kangkang Li,Kelvin Luu,Lily Ma,Tu Trinh,Ying Liu,Mohamed Elfeki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BARC-Bench：110 个跨 9 类模拟工作区环境的任务，利用"良性请求中潜藏的不安全操作空间"评估智能体对未明示安全边界（工作区记录、环境隐式语境、未言明社会规范）的遵守情况；前沿模型五次尝试全部安全的任务占比仅 9.1%–72.7%，最强配置仍在 27.3% 任务上出现违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对智能体的任务指令本身欠规范（underspecified），安全边界尤为模糊，由此产生行为风险。现有评估多关注任务完成度，缺乏针对"合法请求中隐含越界可能"的系统性安全测度。

Method: 构建 BARC-Bench：覆盖 9 个模拟工作区、共 110 个任务的基准；每个任务给定一个表面良性的请求，同时预留越界操作空间。安全边界由三类来源界定——工作区记录、环境隐式语境、未言明的社会规范（示例：安排离职沟通会议时，不得通过共享日历邀请提前泄露员工辞职信息）。对 4 种前沿模型配置、每任务 5 次尝试进行评测，以"五次均安全完成"的任务比例与违规率作为指标。

Result: 五次尝试全部安全完成的任务占比为 9.1%–72.7%；最强配置仍在 27.3% 的任务上产生安全违规。模型常能完成复杂工作流，但无法稳定遵守未明示边界。

Conclusion: 存在不对称的运行风险：任务能力与安全一致性脱节，安全一致性的缺失构成自主委托（autonomous delegation）落地的主要障碍。

Abstract: Task instructions from users for agents are inherently underspecified, especially regarding safety boundaries--creating behavioral risks. To study these risks, we introduce BARC-Bench, a collection of 110 tasks across nine simulated workspace environments where benign requests leave openings for unsafe actions. In this benchmark, we evaluate agent behavior against safety boundaries established by workspace records, implicit environmental context, and unstated social norms. For example, an agent asked to schedule off-boarding discussions must arrange the meeting without prematurely revealing an employee's resignation through a shared calendar invitation. Evaluating four frontier model configurations across five attempts per task reveals that models are not consistently safe: the share of tasks completed safely on all five attempts ranges from 9.1% to 72.7%, with even the strongest configuration producing a safety violation on 27.3% of tasks. These results highlight an asymmetric operational risk: while models frequently complete complex workflows, their inability to consistently respect unstated boundaries presents a substantial barrier to safe autonomous delegation.

</details>


### [196] [RSI-Opt: Recursive Self-Improvement Optimizer Agents For Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32026)
*Binghang Lu,Yige Sun,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RSI-Opt：一种即插即用的“递归自我改进优化器智能体”，让元智能体基于历史运行证据改写策略（提示词、流程手册、优化器代码库、决策节奏），并在策略档案库中保留评估过的策略以供继续进化，从而提升优化器智能体性能并支持跨任务、跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规神经网络训练依赖预设的优化配方；用LLM智能体监控训练动态并调整优化器设置虽有前景，但固定策略的智能体无法跨多次运行积累经验，因此需要让指导该过程的策略本身可被进化。

Method: RSI-Opt包含四类可演化策略要素：提示词、流程手册、经测试的优化器代码库、决策节奏；由元智能体利用早期运行证据重写策略，并用档案库保存已评估策略以支撑进一步进化。实验为三个训练任务上的5代演化，元智能体自行开发可复用流程与代码并开展CPU代理实验；通过受控对比区分“初始决策”与“持续适应”的贡献。

Result: 演化套件上原始空间汇总z分数由+10.48升至+14.33（Opus 5.5）；经跨模型策略迁移，在留出任务上由−0.36升至+3.11（GPT-5.5）。Burgers PDE上平均相对L2误差相比固定提示词基线：Opus 5.5约降2.7倍，GPT-6.1约降8.8倍；但PDE迁移在不同LLM与任务间存在波动。受控对比显示：已见任务上增益主要来自初始决策，未见任务上持续适应带来进一步改善。

Conclusion: 跨训练运行积累的流程与可执行工具能提升优化器智能体性能，并支持向科学机器学习任务迁移；同时迁移效果依赖模型与任务，说明进化所得知识与任务适配需结合。

Abstract: Conventional neural-network training typically follows a predefined optimization recipe, motivating a LLM agent that monitors training dynamics and adjusts optimizer settings as training progresses, but a fixed agent policy does not accumulate experience across runs. We introduce RSI-Opt, a plug-and-play Recursive Self-Improvement Optimizer Agents which evolves the knowledge that guides this process: a prompt, a playbook of procedures, a library of tested optimizer code, and a decision cadence. A meta-agent rewrites this policy using evidence from earlier runs, and an archive retains evaluated policies for further evolution. Across 5 generations on three training tasks, the meta-agents develop reusable procedures and code and conduct their own CPU proxy experiments. RSI-Opt substantially improves optimizer-agent performance, with representative raw-space summary $z$-scores increasing from $+10.48$ to $+14.33$ on the evolution suite with Opus 5.5 and from $-0.36$ to $+3.11$ on held-out tasks with GPT-5.5 through cross-model policy transfer. On the Burgers PDE, it reduces mean relative $L_2$ error by approximately 2.7-fold with Opus 5.5 and 8.8-fold with GPT-6.1 over the fixed-prompt agent baseline, although PDE transfer varies across LLMs and tasks. Controlled comparisons show that the evolved policy combines reusable optimization knowledge with adaptation to each task: initial decisions capture most observed gains on seen tasks, while continued adaptation improves outcomes on unseen tasks. These findings demonstrate how procedures and executable tools accumulated across training runs can improve optimizer-agent performance and support transfer to scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [197] [PPO vs. SAC: A Comparative Study for Cooperative Motion Control of Dual-Arm Space Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32034)
*Nasim Fallahiarezoodar,Zheng H. Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双臂空间机械臂协同强化学习运动规划框架，集中式状态+多目标奖励，SAC在成功率与泛化上显著优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间碎片增多推动主动碎片清除与在轨服务需求，双臂机械臂在抓取稳定性和载荷分布上优于单臂，但在臂间避碰、姿态对齐与传感不确定性下协调双臂仍是难题。

Method: 构建带臂间避碰约束的目标到达任务；集中式状态表示融合关节空间、末端执行器与目标相对信息；多目标奖励函数兼顾到达精度、姿态对齐、运动平滑、双臂同步与避碰；在固定基座仿真中对比同策略PPO与异策略SAC，并测试对未见目标构型的泛化，5个随机种子。

Result: SAC在每个种子均达到80%成功率阈值（中位1.6M环境步），PPO在4M步预算内未达标；平均成功率78.6% vs 7.4%，位置/姿态误差更低、双臂同步更紧。边界目标上两者均退化，但SAC保留大部分性能，PPO显著崩塌。PPO在两阶段塑形奖励下陷入"奖励滞留"局部最优，而非专注高精度任务。

Conclusion: SAC更适合该双臂协同规划任务且泛化更稳健；同策略学习对奖励塑形高度敏感，需警惕奖励滞留型局部最优。

Abstract: The growing population of orbital debris has intensified interest in active debris removal and on-orbit servicing, for which dual-arm space manipulators offer improved grasping stability and load distribution relative to single-arm systems. However, coordinating two manipulators under inter-arm collision avoidance, orientation alignment, and sensing uncertainty remains challenging. This paper develops a coordinated reinforcement learning framework for cooperative motion planning of a dual-arm space manipulator in a target-reaching task with inter-arm collision-avoidance constraints. A centralized state representation combines joint-space, end-effector, and target-relative information, while a multi-objective reward function promotes target-reaching accuracy, orientation alignment, motion smoothness, synchronized task completion, and collision avoidance. Within this framework, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) are compared as representative on-policy and off-policy algorithms, and the learned policies are further evaluated for generalization to previously unseen target configurations. Across five random seeds, SAC substantially outperforms PPO within the considered fixed-base simulation scenarios: it reaches an 80% success threshold in every seed (median 1.6M environment steps) whereas PPO does not reach it within a 4M-step budget, and it attains a higher mean success rate (78.6% versus 7.4%) with lower position and orientation errors and tighter bimanual synchronization. Both algorithms degrade on unseen boundary targets, but SAC retains most of its performance while PPO's collapses. The results also expose a failure mode of PPO: under the two-phase shaped reward, it tends to converge to a reward-loitering local optimum rather than committing to the precision task. This behavior highlights the sensitivity of on-policy learning to reward shaping.

</details>


### [198] [Do AI-for-Science Agents Preserve Model Safety?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32045)
*Michael Yew Chuan Lee,Manya Sriram,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验LLM被集成进AI-for-Science（AI4S）agent harness后，其安全行为是否仍然保持，发现harness会显著削弱基础模型的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI4S agent由LLM与领域专用harness组合而成，harness能提升模型的科学能力并使其编排科学工具；但社区尚不清楚这种集成是否会破坏基础模型原有的安全对齐，因此需要检验"模型安全在harness中是否得以保留"。

Method: 在5个agent harness、50条有害化学请求上评估4个近期GPT变体的安全行为，比较四种攻击条件：直接提示、两种简单对抗提示策略以及一种自适应攻击。

Result: 即使仅使用直接提示，harness也可能提高有害请求的遵从率；更关键的是，对基础LLM基本无效的自适应攻击，在引入harness后成功率骤增。

Conclusion: 基础LLM的安全行为在引入harness后并不具备持久性；随着AI4S agent能力与自主性增强，社区需使用领域特定的安全基准对agent本身进行评估与对齐。

Abstract: AI-for-Science agents are increasingly being used for formulating scientific hypotheses, running autonomous experiments, and interpreting evidence. These agents are constructed by integrating a large language model (LLM) into a domain-specific agent harness, which in turn can improve the LLM's scientific capability and enable the model to orchestrate scientific tools. In this work, we ask whether model safety is preserved when an LLM is integrated in an AI-for-Science agent harness. We evaluate five agent harnesses on 50 harmful chemistry requests. Specifically, we study how the safety behavior changes under direct prompting, two simple adversarial prompting strategies, and an adaptive attack. Across four recent GPT variants, our results show that the agent harness can increase harmful compliance even under direct prompting. More critically, we find that adaptive attacks that are largely unsuccessful against the base LLM suddenly become highly successful after the agent harness is introduced. Our results show that the safety behavior of the base LLM is not durable once a harness is introduced. Thus, as AI-for-Science agents become more capable and autonomous, the community needs to evaluate and align the agent using domain-specific safety benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [Can Test-Time Memory Evolution Outperform Test-Time Training for Scientific Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32046)
*Yepeng Liu,Shengmai Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Chengzhi Liu,Dawn Song,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TTME 用外部记忆进化替代测试时权重更新，在五个数学任务上全面超越 TTT，同时降低 82.8–84.6% FLOPs 与 47.7–58.4% 运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT 虽使开源权重 LLM 在科学发现任务上超越前沿模型的推理式框架，但在线权重更新带来额外计算开销，制约其向更大模型扩展，因此需要更高效的测试时自适应方法。

Method: 提出 Test-Time Memory Evolution（TTME），一种即插即用的测试时自适应方法：不更新模型权重，而是通过蒸馏经验持续更新外部记忆以指导后续发现过程。

Result: 在相同发现框架、仅替换测试时自适应方法的受控比较下，TTME 在全部五个数学任务上优于 TTT，FLOPs 降低 82.8–84.6%，运行时间缩短 47.7–58.4%。

Conclusion: 在所评估的发现场景中，TTME 是 TTT 的有效且计算高效替代方案。

Abstract: Recent \emph{test-time training} (TTT) frameworks have enabled open-weight large language models (LLMs) to outperform inference-only frameworks powered by frontier models on scientific discovery tasks. However, TTT introduces additional computational cost for online weight updates during the discovery process, making computational efficiency an important consideration when scaling to larger models. This motivates us to explore an efficient \emph{test-time adaptation} method for scientific discovery. We introduce \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} (TTME), a plug-and-play test-time adaptation method, as an alternative to TTT. TTME continually updates the external memory by distilling the experience to guide subsequent discovery without updating model weights. To have a clean comparison, we evaluate TTME and TTT under the same discovery framework, replacing only the test-time adaptation method. Our evaluation shows that TTME outperforms TTT on all five mathematical tasks, while reducing computational cost by $82.8$-$84.6\%$ in FLOPs and $47.7$-$58.4\%$ in runtime. These results establish TTME as an effective and computationally efficient alternative to TTT in the evaluated discovery settings.

</details>


### [200] [HyScale: Scaling Long-Context Training for Learned Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32057)
*Yangsheng Deng,Xinjing Huang,Yulong Wang,Yunzhuo Liu,Jun Hu,Shuyong Tang,Junchi Zeng,Mingji Han,Ray Ying,Key Zhang,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyScale 是面向学习式稀疏注意力的长上下文训练系统，用 HyTopK 与 selection replay 消除选择环节的瓶颈，在 1M token 上实现最高 2.07× 训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万 token 上下文下，学习式稀疏注意力的"选择"环节成为训练瓶颈：每个 query 需对所有历史 token 打分而稀疏注意力只读取 k 个；现有系统还要物化完整 score 表，并在重计算时重复执行选择。

Method: 核心洞察是选择的输出不是激活值而是一组索引。据此提出 HyTopK：不依赖 score 表、即时求出每个 query 的精确 top-k；以及 selection replay：在反向重计算中复用前向得到的选择结果。再配合针对端到端训练挑战的整体系统优化。

Result: 在 512 张 H20 上，GLM-5.1 的 1M token 序列训练较 Megatron-LM 加速 2.07×；在 32 张 H800 上，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的 CSA 层与 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 的 QSA 层在 1M token 下分别加速 2.01× 与 1.57×。

Conclusion: 将选择视为索引而非激活、并进行选择重放，可有效消除长上下文稀疏注意力训练中的选择瓶颈，并在多种模型与硬件配置上取得稳定加速。

Abstract: We present HyScale, a long-context training system for learned sparse attention. Learned sparse attention is a promising architecture for million-token contexts, widely adopted in production since DeepSeek-V3.2. Selection, however, grows into the training bottleneck. For each query, it scores every previous token whereas sparse attention reads only $k$; worse, current systems materialize a full score table and repeat selection during recomputation. Our key insight is that the output of selection is not an activation, but rather a set of indices. Building on it, HyScale introduces HyTopK and selection replay. HyTopK finds each query's exact top-$k$ on the fly without a score table, while selection replay reuses forward-pass selection in recomputation. Together with holistic system optimizations tackling end-to-end training challenges, HyScale speeds up GLM-5.1 training on 1M-token sequences by 2.07$\times$ over Megatron-LM on 512 H20 GPUs. It also speeds up the CSA layers of DeepSeek-V4-Flash and the QSA layers of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next by 2.01$\times$ and 1.57$\times$ at 1M tokens on 32 H800 GPUs.

</details>


### [201] [Understanding Communication Failures in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32060)
*Priya Pitre,Jun Hou,Yi Fang,Naren Ramakrishnan,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于语料的沟通失效分类体系（taxonomy），用于解释多智能体LLM系统中最终准确率无法揭示的交互失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过辩论、证据共享和相互纠错提升推理能力，但交互本身也会引入失效模式，如过早收敛、忽视异议、压制正确的少数意见、跨轮保留错误框架，这些仅靠最终准确率无法刻画。

Method: 从医疗问答、编程、金融、多跳推理、伦理、具身文本等任务中收集3,211条失败的多轮轨迹，构建沟通失效分类体系；并据此分析跨数据集与系统设计的失效分布、轮级错误动态、恢复难度、多数转移、少数信号压制及自报告置信度。

Result: 失效主要集中于恢复/纠错问题与共识/决策问题，而非孤立的首轮错误；许多失效在智能体首次观察到同伴回复后才出现。

Conclusion: 建议加强分歧处理、证据验证、少数信号保留以及定稿前的恢复性检查。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems aim to improve reasoning through debate, evidence sharing, and mutual correction, but interaction can also introduce failures that final accuracy alone cannot explain. Agents may converge too early, ignore dissent, suppress correct minority answers, or preserve a wrong framing across rounds. We present a corpus-grounded taxonomy of communication failures, built from 3,211 failed multi-round trajectories across medical QA, coding, finance, multi-hop reasoning, ethics, and embodied text tasks. Using this taxonomy, we analyze failure distributions across datasets and system designs, round-level error dynamics, recovery hardness, majority transitions, minority-signal suppression, and self-reported confidence. We find that failures are dominated by recovery/correction and consensus/decision problems rather than isolated first-round mistakes, with many emerging after agents first observe peer responses. Based on these findings, we recommend stronger disagreement handling, evidence verification, minority-signal preservation, and recovery checks before finalization.

</details>


### [202] [DriveMind: Mind-Evolving Belief Tracking for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32069)
*Yan Shi,Kehua Chen,Jiarui Qi,Yihong Tang,Haichen Liao,Yinhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DriveMind is an MLLM-based driving framework inspired by particle filtering that maintains and reweights multiple scene hypotheses across frames instead of committing to a single interpretation, improving Bench2Drive DS from 68.50 to 81.37 and SR from 41.36% to 54.09%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing MLLM-based drivers reason from scratch at each frame and commit to a single explanation, discarding earlier hypotheses. In ambiguous scenes, the agent locks onto a wrong interpretation and acts on it before sufficient evidence accumulates.

Method: DriveMind keeps several hypotheses about the scene alive simultaneously and updates their weights as new frames arrive. Inspired by particle filtering, it propagates each hypothesis through a VLM, scores it against the current observation, and resamples to maintain hypothesis diversity.

Result: On Bench2Drive, adding belief tracking to the same driving pipeline raises DS from 68.50 to 81.37 and SR from 41.36% to 54.09%. Largest gains occur in ambiguous scenarios: unsignalized intersections (+20.0 DS) and oncoming-traffic interactions (+20.5 DS). Fine-grained interactive abilities (merging, emergency braking, give-way) also improve.

Conclusion: Temporal belief tracking over multiple hypotheses, rather than single-shot per-frame reasoning, is an effective and general enhancement for MLLM-based driving agents, particularly in ambiguous and interactive scenarios.

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can perceive a traffic scene, reason about it, and propose a driving action within a single model. However, existing MLLM-based drivers reason from scratch at each frame and commit to a single explanation, discarding earlier hypotheses about the scene. When the scene is ambiguous, the agent locks in on the wrong interpretation and acts on it before evidence accumulates. In this paper, we propose DriveMind, an MLLM-based driving framework that keeps several hypotheses about the scene alive at the same time and updates their weights as new frames arrive. Inspired by particle filtering, DriveMind propagates each hypothesis through a VLM, scores it against the current observation, and resamples to maintain diversity. On Bench2Drive, adding belief tracking to the same driving pipeline improves the Driving Score (DS) from 68.50 to 81.37 and the Success Rate (SR) from 41.36% to 54.09%. The largest gains occur on ambiguous scenarios such as unsignalized intersections (+20.0 DS) and oncoming-traffic interactions (+20.5 DS). Beyond the overall metrics, DriveMind also improves fine-grained interactive driving abilities such as merging, emergency braking, and give-way.

</details>


### [203] [Behaviour analysis for large language model-driven agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32087)
*Tianqiang Yan,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个连接数据驱动行为发现、依赖关系分析与实验操纵的通用框架，用于研究语言智能体在轨迹中的行为组织方式；以迭代自我反思研究为例展示发现、依赖学习与提示比较如何相互促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言智能体行为需要掌握动作在整条轨迹中如何组织，以及这种组织与上下文和结果的关系，而现有方法缺乏将行为发现、统计分析与实验验证统一起来的框架。

Method: 框架分四步：基于记录活动归纳的行为类别界定测量对象；保留行为在过程中的位置并将关系表达为可解释假设；用统计评估与留出上下文的检验考察证据强度与适用范围；通过实验（含提示引导/prompt steering）在刻意变化的条件下检验行为与结果的变化。框架支持变长轨迹、不同事件顺序与智能体角色。

Result: 以迭代自我反思（iterative self-reflection）为案例研究，说明行为发现、依赖学习与提示比较三者可相互启发；并指出推广该框架所需在编码、分析与实验设计方面的改进方向。

Conclusion: 该框架为语言智能体行为研究提供了从描述性发现到因果/实验检验的连贯路径，但更广泛应用尚依赖于编码方法、统计分析与实验范式的进一步进展。

Abstract: Understanding language-agent behaviour requires learning how actions are organised across a trajectory and how this organisation relates to context and outcomes. We propose a general framework that connects data-driven behavioural discovery with analysis and experimental investigation. Categories developed from recorded activity define what is measured. Analyses preserve the behaviours' positions in the process and express their relationships as interpretable hypotheses. Statistical assessment and tests in held-out contexts examine the strength and scope of the evidence. Experiments, including prompt steering, then test how behaviour and outcomes change under deliberately varied conditions. A study of iterative self-reflection illustrates how discovery, dependency learning and prompt comparisons can inform one another. We develop this approach for trajectories of varying length, event order and agent roles, and identify advances in coding, analysis and experimentation needed to support its wider use.

</details>


### [204] [DELA: Learning Controllable Edits for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32096)
*Xinsong Feng,Chenan Wang,Leshu Tang,Johnny Liu,Dong Li,Da Li,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DELA是一个插拔式框架，在冻结的扩散语言模型上实现可控的草稿级精修，通过Insert/Delete/Remask/Stop四种显式编辑动作与两阶段解码分离高层编辑控制与token级生成，在数学推理与代码生成上较直接扩散解码最高提升5.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型虽支持双向去噪与迭代精修，但生成过程仍主要由token级掩码调度控制，导致草稿级精修是隐式的，缺乏对何时插入缺失内容、删除冗余片段、重访不确定区域或停止编辑的显式控制。

Method: 提出DELA：控制器在干净、变长草稿上执行Insert、Delete、Remask、Stop四种编辑动作，冻结DLM负责对掩码区域去噪填充。为解决任意内部编辑破坏前缀稳定性、限制KV-cache复用带来的高昂开销，采用两阶段解码——快速append-only起草阶段（支持KV-cache复用）+全局精修阶段（支持非单调定向编辑）。控制器以分阶段课程训练：先由合成恢复任务与提升草稿质量的挖掘动作学习局部编辑先验，再用基于rollout的结果偏好与下游任务反馈对齐来精修动作选择。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，DELA在保持骨干模型冻结的情况下，相较直接扩散解码最高取得5.5个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: DELA为扩散语言模型提供了一种模块化的显式草稿级控制方法，将编辑决策与token生成解耦，验证了插拔式精修控制的有效性与效率可行性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) support bidirectional denoising and iterative refinement, but generation remains largely controlled by token-level masking schedules. This leaves draft-level refinement implicit and limits explicit control over when to insert missing content, delete redundant spans, revisit uncertain regions, or stop editing. We introduce Diffusion Editing Language Adapter (DELA), a plug-in framework that enables controllable draft refinement with a frozen DLM. DELA operates on clean, variable-length drafts using four explicit editing actions: Insert, Delete, Remask, and Stop. The controller determines when and where to edit the draft, while the frozen DLM fills masked regions through denoising, separating high-level edit control from token-level generation. A naive single-phase editor would be computationally expensive because arbitrary interior edits can disrupt prefix stability and limit reliable KV-cache reuse. To address this, DELA uses a two-phase decoding procedure: a fast, append-only drafting phase that supports KV-cache reuse, followed by a global refinement phase that enables targeted non-monotonic edits to the full draft. We train the controller using a staged curriculum that begins with learning local edit priors from synthetic recovery tasks and mined actions that improve draft quality. Subsequent stages refine edit selection through rollout-based outcome preferences and alignment with downstream task feedback. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that DELA achieves gains of up to 5.5 percentage points over direct diffusion decoding while keeping the backbone frozen. DELA thus provides a modular approach to explicit draft-level control in diffusion language models.

</details>


### [205] [Learning Nonlinear Waveform Fusion Across Scales for Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32099)
*Jiayu Liu,Kai Wan,Shuo Shao,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨尺度非线性波形融合方法，在固定总能量约束下联合实现图像传输与多目标距离-角度定位，相比时域叠加和频域学习线性融合，定位归一化误差降低29.0%与27.0%，PSNR提升1.40与0.82 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ISAC中，固定源叠加与频率划分限制了通信内容与探测参考信号之间的交互方式，难以充分发挥共享波形的联合性能，因此需要一种能跨尺度非线性融合通信与感知信息的波形设计。

Method: 发射端采用自适应时间特征加权与频谱更新，将图像特征与确定性线性调频参考在共享复数状态中非线性融合，并在阵列帧内保持总能量恒定；接收端UE利用参考与信道状态信息驱动神经解混器重建图像，基站以实际发射脉冲构造相干字典进行序贯定位，并结合学习式局部校正、物理细化与残差更新。

Result: 在512张独立测试图像上，与共享固定UE接收机和基于相干峰值搜索的基线感知接收机比较：相较时域叠加与学习式频域线性融合，所提波形将归一化定位误差分别降低29.0%和27.0%，平均PSNR分别提升1.40 dB和0.82 dB；在波形固定时，学习式局部校正使序贯接收机的平均归一化定位误差进一步降低12.2%。

Conclusion: 跨尺度非线性波形融合优于传统叠加与频谱线性融合方案，学习式局部校正对相干字典序贯定位具有显著增益，验证了通信-感知深度耦合波形设计的有效性。

Abstract: Fixed source superposition and frequency partitioning constrain how communication content interacts with a probing reference in integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). We learn nonlinear waveform fusion across scales for image transmission and localization of multiple targets in range and angle. Reception uses the information available at each end. Adaptive temporal feature weighting and spectral updates combine image features and a deterministic linearly frequency-modulated reference in a shared complex state, generating a waveform with fixed total energy over the array frame. At the user equipment (UE), the reference and channel state information condition a neural demixer for image reconstruction. At the base station, the realized transmit pulse defines a coherent dictionary for sequential localization with learned local correction, physical refinement, and residual updates. We compare the proposed waveforms with superposition in the time domain and learned spectral linear fusion on 512 independent test images, using a shared fixed UE receiver and a baseline sensing receiver using coherent peak search. Averaged across eleven task weights, the proposed waveforms reduce normalized localization error by 29.0\% and 27.0\%, respectively, while increasing mean peak signal-to-noise ratio by 1.40 and 0.82~dB. With the proposed waveforms fixed, learned local correction reduces mean normalized localization error by 12.2\% relative to the same sequential receiver without learned correction.

</details>


### [206] [PersonaScienceBench: Evaluating Scientific Agents with Diverse Simulated Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32106)
*Siyang Wu,Weizhi Zhang,Chen Yang,Esther Xu Fei,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Jing Tang,Yixuan He,Kelvin C. Mo,Peiwen Li,Defu Cao,Xu Hu,Haixin Wang,Taoran Li,Haoming Li,Zheyuan Deng,Jingwen Zhang,Pengsong Zhang,Haichao Zhang,Guangze Luo,Yi Daniel Lu,Zihuai He,Chenxi Qiu,Dianzhuo Wang,Zhejian Peng,Shushu Liang,Wenyi Lu,Yifan Wang,Jintao Huang,Yijun Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonaScienceBench 基准，考察科学智能体在与不同画像的研究者协作时的表现，而传统基准通常固定协作者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实科研场景中，智能体与专业背景和偏好各异的研究者协作，但现有基准将协作者视为固定条件，无法评估智能体对不同研究者画像的适应能力。

Method: 构建 PersonaScienceBench：涵盖四个科学领域、来自五个基准的 200 个任务；由模拟研究者依据 persona 画像在对话中提供上下文与反馈，任务目标与评分标准保持固定，智能体只能通过对话获知研究者偏好。评测 18 种模型配置，分析任务表现、事实恢复与自我重复；并对 GPT 6 Astra 做细粒度交互轨迹分析。

Result: 18 种模型配置在任务表现、事实恢复与自我重复上呈现不同优势；GPT 6 Astra 在不同研究者画像下表现出明显不同的智能体回应，交互轨迹显示存在针对性澄清与工作流修订行为，且即便最终得分相同，这类行为也可能存在差异。

Conclusion: PersonaScienceBench 在评估任务完成度的同时，也评估智能体与多样化研究者协作的能力，揭示仅凭最终得分无法捕捉的协作行为差异。

Abstract: Scientific agents collaborate with researchers whose expertise and preferences differ, but benchmarks usually hold the collaborator fixed. We introduce PersonaScienceBench: 200 tasks from five benchmarks across four scientific domains. Simulated researchers provide context and feedback according to persona profiles, while task objectives and grading remain fixed. Agents encounter researcher preferences only through conversation. Across eighteen modelconfigurations, task performance, fact recovery, and self-repetition reveal different strengths. Detailed GPT 6 Astra analyses show distinct agent responses across researcher profiles; interaction traces illustrate targeted clar ification and workflow revision. These behaviors can differ even when final scores match. PersonaScienceBench therefore evaluates how agents collaborate with diverse researchers alongside whether they complete the task.

</details>


### [207] [Who Verifies the Tools? How Warped Formatters Mislead Agents and Their Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32110)
*William Jeffries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具输出格式器的措辞偏差（遗漏搜索限制、结果上限、标识符命名空间）会误导智能体与验证器；修复措辞可显著减少无依据断言，并使验证器从100%接受错误答案转为100%拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体与验证器常依赖工具打印的文本，但该文本可能错误呈现工具的实际行为，即使计算本身正确。作者在生产级RAG系统中观察到格式器导致智能体向用户提供错误或不完整信息，因此需系统研究格式器是否误导智能体与验证器，以及修复措辞的收益。

Method: 构造同一工具结果的两种格式器版本（含关键遗漏 vs. 明确陈述全部缺失细节），将结果输入智能体与验证器进行对照实验；统计四类失败下的无依据断言与验证器判定；并设计审计提示词（用于Codex与Claude Code）在旧版系统、OpenClaw与Hermes上进行探索性缺陷发现。

Result: 仅格式差异即改变智能体断言与验证器接受行为；四类失败中有三类无依据断言大幅下降；某项测试中验证器由90/90次接受错误答案变为90/90次拒绝；对照实验表明提供缺失事实并明确要求判断正确性后，验证器能够捕获错误。审计提示词在旧版自研系统中复现仍存在的缺陷并新发现12处；在OpenClaw与Hermes中均发现失真格式器，已提交修复PR，OpenClaw的修复已合并。

Conclusion: 工具输出的表述保真度是智能体可靠性的独立关键因素，与计算正确性同等重要；格式器审计与措辞修复是可低成本落地的改进手段，且审计提示词可被从业者直接复用。

Abstract: Agents and verifiers often rely on the text a tool prints, and that text can misrepresent what the tool actually did, even when the tool's computation is correct. We saw this happen in a production RAG research system used by the Pilsudski Institute in New York, where tool output formatters misled an agent into giving wrong or incomplete information to users. Each tool did its job correctly, but its output omitted search limits, result caps, or identifier namespaces. We also recreated an unlabeled estimate caught in code review before release. We studied whether these formatters misled agents and verifiers and how much the repaired wording helps. We fed tool results to agents using two formatter versions: one with the key omissions and one with all missing details clearly stated. That difference alone changed what the agents claimed and what the verifiers accepted. Unsupported agent claims dropped substantially for three of the four failure types. In one test, verifiers went from accepting incorrect answers 90 out of 90 times to rejecting them 90 out of 90 times. A control showed the verifiers could catch the errors once they were given the missing facts and asked to judge correctness. We include the audit prompt we used with Codex and Claude Code in an exploratory demonstration, written with prior knowledge of our own system's defects. When pointed at an old version of our own system, it rediscovered the defects that were still live and surfaced twelve more we had not catalogued. When pointed at OpenClaw and Hermes, two of the best-known open-source agentic systems, it found warped formatters in both. We opened PRs to fix both, and the OpenClaw fix has already been merged. Practitioners can run the same prompt on their own systems today.

</details>


### [208] [MPQ: A Message-Passing View of Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32127)
*Yanshuo Chen,Chenxi Liu,Reza Shirkavand,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将逐行PTQ建模为离散码本上的后验推断，提出MPQ：用VAMP计算块级软后验，再用精确块坐标下降精修硬判决初始化解码，在Llama/Qwen/Mistral上优于硬判决基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ求解器（GPTQ、LDLQ、块坐标下降）在考虑邻居之前就把每个权重块固定为单一码字（硬判决），无法探索近优的替代码字，导致量化次优。

Method: 把逐行PTQ表述为离散码本上的后验推断，指出既有求解器等价于零温度、仅保留每块最优码字的硬判决极限。提出MPQ：推断过程中跨块保留软后验信息，仅在输出阶段硬判决；先用向量近似消息传递（VAMP）计算近似块级后验，再以精确块坐标下降精修硬判决初始化。

Result: 在Llama、Qwen、Mistral稠密语言模型上，2-bit格点量化及3/4-bit标量量化设置中，MPQ在困惑度和零样本准确率上均优于强硬判决基线，且能与activation-aware scaling、incoherence processing等预处理方法良好组合。

Conclusion: 软后验信息在PTQ中具有实际收益；MPQ在不重训练的前提下提升低比特量化质量，并与现有预处理技术互补，代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a key technique for efficiently deploying large language models, as it compresses weights into low-bit representations without retraining. Existing PTQ solvers, including GPTQ, LDLQ, and block coordinate descent, commit each weight block to a single codeword before its neighbors are considered, leaving near-optimal alternatives unexplored. In this work, we cast row-wise PTQ as posterior inference over discrete codebooks, in which existing solvers correspond to a zero-temperature, hard-decision limit retaining only the top codeword per block. We propose Message-Passing Quantization (MPQ), which preserves soft posterior information across blocks during inference and commits to hard codewords only at the output stage. MPQ first uses vector approximate message passing to compute approximate blockwise posteriors, then refines a hard-decision initialization by exact block coordinate descent. Across 2-bit lattice and 3- and 4-bit scalar quantization on dense language models from Llama, Qwen, and Mistral families, MPQ improves perplexity and zero-shot accuracy over strong hard-decision baselines, and composes favorably with preprocessing techniques such as activation-aware scaling and incoherence processing. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/poseidonchan/MPQ.

</details>


### [209] [$Ψ$-0.6: Adaptive Streams of Thought in a Unified Vision-Language World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32177)
*Khai Loong Aw,Klemen Kotar,Baihan Zhang,Yifan Liu,Lilian Chen,Atlas Kazemian,Rahul Venkatesh,Daniel Yamins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一视觉-语言世界模型 Ψ-0.6，以"思维流"（streams of thought）在共享 token 接口中交替生成与解析图像、文本及几何/运动显式表示，实现自适应组合感知、语言、几何与仿真推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能随问题展开灵活切换多种推理形式，而当前 AI 能力分散在各类专用模型中，难以组合与联合适配任务，缺乏统一且可自适应的物理世界推理机制。

Method: 构建统一的视觉-语言世界模型，将感知、语言、几何、仿真纳入单一"思维流"推理过程；思维流在共享 token 接口上交错图像、文本与几何/运动显式表示；通过任务反馈的强化学习联合优化中间表示的选择、生成与使用。

Result: 在多视角空间推理上零样本超越领先开放权重模型，经由几何中间表示推理可获进一步提升；支持反事实仿真（如判断移动物体是否阻塞通路）；RL 提升 VQA 准确率且收益可迁移至未见基准。

Conclusion: Ψ-0.6 指向自适应物理推理路径：不仅预测物理世界，还能选择用于推理的表示形式。

Abstract: Humans reason about the physical world by flexibly combining different forms of inference as a problem unfolds. Achieving this flexibility is a major open challenge in AI: capabilities often remain separated across specialized models, limiting how they can be composed and jointly adapted to a task. We introduce $Ψ$-0.6, a unified vision-language world model that adaptively composes perception, language, geometry, and simulation within a shared reasoning process we call streams of thought. These streams interleave images, text, and explicit representations of geometry and motion, all of which the model can generate and interpret through a shared token interface. $Ψ$-0.6 surpasses leading open-weight models zero-shot on multi-view spatial reasoning, with further gains from reasoning through geometric intermediates. It also supports counterfactual simulation: generating and interpreting imagined scenes to determine, for example, whether moving an object would obstruct a pathway. Reinforcement learning from task feedback jointly optimizes the selection, generation, and use of intermediate representations, improving visual question-answering accuracy with gains that transfer to unseen benchmarks. $Ψ$-0.6 offers a path toward adaptive physical reasoning -- not only predicting the physical world but also choosing the representations through which to reason.

</details>


### [210] [Not All Information Is Worth Asking For: Decision-Informed Information Seeking with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32181)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的DIVA方法，用证据的决策价值指导LLM决定查什么、何时停，DIVA-U在4个领域20个LLM上取得最低regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在信息不完整条件下做决策时存在"信息陷阱"：会为无法改变决策或成本不划算的检查付出代价，且在证据已充分时仍继续追问，导致决策价值损失（regret）。

Method: DIVA基于信息的决策价值：1）让LLM通过简短数值问题指定一个小型决策模型，即坏结果发生概率及各检查对该概率的修正幅度；2）以每个候选检查的假想结果在扣除成本后对决策净收益的提升来估值，当无检查值得其成本时停止。变体DIVA-U进一步建模LLM对检查修正强度估计的不确定性。方法免训练，可适用于任意LLM。

Result: 在4个领域、20个LLM上，DIVA-U在真实数据上的regret低于所有LLM基线，主要收益来自证据充分即停止；在每项检查可靠性已知的合成场景中，DIVA将最佳基线regret降低75%。

Conclusion: 以决策价值（净收益）为核心的贝叶斯式信息获取准则可有效缓解LLM的信息陷阱，免训练、通用性强，且显式处理估计不确定性带来稳定增益。
