We further show that this capability is learnable, demonstrating that visibility recovery can be strengthened consistently across model families.

</details>


### [243] [LLaCO: Continuous Diffusion for Large Language Models with Competition-Aware Semantic Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32604)
*Zijian Jia,Peixuan Zhang,Genying Li,Di Wu,Yujie Fu,Si Li,Boxin Shi,Shuchen Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示连续扩散文本生成中嵌入范数衰减导致欧氏距离产生虚假超越，提出Competition Threshold Theory（CTT）与Semantic Retention Scheduling（SRS），并据此构建LLaCO框架，通过球面加噪与竞争感知调度消除范数衰减、缓解时间压缩，8B模型性能超越连续扩散基线并媲美同规模离散扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型在连续嵌入空间中生成文本时，几何轨迹与时间进程错位，导致严重的"时间压缩"：token身份衰减被压缩在靠近噪声端点的狭窄窗口内。现有方法未意识到根本原因是嵌入范数衰减使欧氏距离产生虚假超越，从而错误评估token竞争。

Method: 1) 指出范数衰减导致欧氏距离的虚假超越，主张token竞争应方向性评估：仅当有效噪声幅度超过语义间隔时，候选token才超越源token；2) 形式化为Competition Threshold Theory（CTT），说明高维超越需极端噪声，导致身份衰减集中于噪声端点附近；3) 提出Semantic Retention Scheduling（SRS），使时间分配与身份衰减进程对齐，并证明现有token身份指标本质上是这些成对超越的统计聚合；4) 构建LLaCO框架：用嵌入方向适配器与时间步条件调制将预训练骨干适配到连续表示；采用球面加噪（向掩码偏置端点的测地线插值）消除范数衰减并保持准确的token竞争；按对数秩衰减比例重新分配去噪轨迹。

Result: 以8B规模实例化的LLaCO优于先前的连续扩散基线，并与同规模离散扩散模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 连续扩散文本生成中的时间压缩源于范数衰减引起的虚假超越，通过方向性竞争评估、球面加噪与语义保留调度可有效对齐时间与身份衰减进程，为连续扩散文本生成提供了理论与架构上的统一改进方案。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion models have emerged as a promising non-autoregressive paradigm for text generation over continuous embedding spaces. Along the continuous trajectory, the residual token identity directly shapes the denoising objective. However, the geometric trajectory misaligns with temporal progression, triggering severe temporal compression. In this paper, we show that embedding norm decay causes Euclidean distance to yield spurious overtaking, indicating that token competition must be evaluated directionally. In this space, a candidate overtakes the source token only when the effective noise magnitude surpasses their semantic separation. We formalize these pairwise dynamics as the Competition Threshold Theory (CTT). Crucially, high-dimensional overtaking requires extreme noise levels, causing standard trajectories to concentrate identity decay into a narrow window near the noisy endpoint. To resolve this, we propose Semantic Retention Scheduling (SRS) to align time allocation with the progression of identity decay, and demonstrate that existing token identity metrics are fundamentally statistical aggregations of these pairwise crossings. Building on these theoretical insights, we introduce LLaCO, a generative framework powered by competition-aware semantic scheduling. LLaCO adapts a pretrained backbone to continuous representations via an embedding directional adapter and timestep-conditioned modulation. To operate within the directional space, LLaCO employs a spherical noising process formulated as a geodesic interpolation toward a mask-biased endpoint, eliminating norm decay while preserving accurate token competition. Guided by SRS, LLaCO reallocates the denoising trajectory proportionally to the logarithmic rank decay. Instantiated as an 8B model, LLaCO outperforms previous continuous diffusion baselines and rivals discrete diffusion models of comparable scale.

</details>


### [244] [What Transfers Across Protein Pockets? Experience Reuse in Molecular Optimization for Agentic Structure-Based Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32620)
*Zaifei Yang,Weiyu Chen,Yaqing Wang,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用一次 CrossDocked2020 运行日志检验 LLM 配体编辑智能体的经验库键：以接触、动作、轮次为键能区分改进与恶化编辑，但迁移信号主要描述编辑与产物而非靶点，故经验库更宜用于排序自身提议而非直接规定构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体依赖针对当前复合物的额外对接探针获得指导，且探针随实例丢弃；作者希望把探针留存为可跨口袋继承的经验库，需要先检验其检索键在未见口袋上是否可复发并保持区分能力。

Method: 回顾性分析：取单次运行于 CrossDocked2020 测试集口袋的完整日志；以被编辑位点的接触、编辑动作、轮次构造键，检验键在未出现口袋上的复发率与对改进/恶化编辑的区分度，并以靶点身份作为上限对照、以变体描述与特征（尺寸变化、动作、轮次）作归因分析。

Result: 该键能清晰区分改进与恶化编辑，但区分度大部分可由编辑引起的分子尺寸变化、动作与轮次解释，均不描述靶点；直接给出靶点身份可将区分度提升数倍。精确匹配描述中较具体的很少复发，较粗的区分度不足；可迁移信号主要刻画编辑及其产物而非靶点。

Conclusion: 在所研究的表示与检索方案下，经验库更适合为智能体自身提出的候选做对接优先级排序，而非直接规定应构建何种分子；跨口袋迁移需要能描述靶点且足够具体的键。

Abstract: LLM agents optimize a ligand in a protein pocket by editing it step by step and docking each edit. Recent agents first spend extra dockings probing the complex with controlled edits; guidance induced from these probes is reported to help more than the chemical knowledge the model already carries. The probes are then discarded with the instance that produced them, so the obvious next step is to keep them in an experience base, letting a new pocket inherit what earlier ones already paid for. Such a base is read through a \emph{key}: the record properties under which past edits are pooled and a new one is looked up. Using the full log of one such run over the CrossDocked2020 test-set pockets, we ask whether its keys recur on a pocket the base has never seen and still separate improving from worsening edits. \crchange{Using} the contacts at the site being edited, the action, and the round as \crchange{a} key, the base separates them sharply. Within our analysis, this separation is largely explained by how much the edit changed the molecule's size, the action, and the round, none of which describes the target. That is not because the target is irrelevant: naming it outright separates them several times more sharply. For a pocket absent from the base, transfer must rely on a description rather than its identity. Among the exact-match descriptions we evaluate, the more specific ones rarely recur, whereas the coarser ones provide little separation; the signals that do transfer primarily describe the edit and the molecule it produced rather than the target. Under the representations and lookup scheme studied here, an experience base may be more useful for prioritizing an agent's own proposals for docking than for directly prescribing what to build.

</details>


### [245] [LGMT: A Tool for Testing the Logical Reasoning Reliability of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32627)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoke Fang,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LGMT：基于一阶逻辑蜕变关系的LLM推理一致性检测开源工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为软件系统的推理组件，对同一逻辑问题的等价重述可能给出自相矛盾的答案；静态基准孤立评测查询，无法暴露此类不一致。

Method: 给定前提-结论形式的源测试用例，LGMT生成逻辑等价的后继用例，并在无真值标签情况下检验模型对成对用例的蕴含预测是否一致；内置20种蜕变关系，支持经Z3定理证明器验证的自定义关系，提供CLI与Web Studio两种交互方式，并含无需API密钥的离线模拟模式。

Result: 在包含3,091个蜕变组的基准上，六个常用LLM的不一致率为20.84%–29.44%。

Conclusion: LGMT以蜕变测试思想将推理一致性评估从"单点正确性"拓展为"等价变换下的稳定性"，工具已开源并附带演示视频。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as reasoning components in modern software systems, yet a model that answers a logical query correctly can contradict itself when the same problem is restated in an equivalent form. Static benchmarks evaluate queries in isolation and therefore fail to expose such inconsistencies. To address this limitation, we present LGMT, an open-source tool that detects reasoning inconsistencies through metamorphic relations grounded in first-order logic. Given a source test case in premise-conclusion form, LGMT generates logically equivalent follow-up cases and checks whether the model's entailment predictions agree across the pair, without ground-truth labels. The tool provides 20 built-in metamorphic relations, supports custom ones verified by the Z3 theorem prover, and runs from a CLI or a web studio, including an offline mock mode requiring no API key. Across six widely used LLMs on a bundled benchmark of 3,091 metamorphic groups, LGMT reveals inconsistency rates ranging from 20.84% to 29.44%. LGMT is publicly available on GitHub at https://github.com/MaxwelsDonc/lgmt-tool, with a demonstration video at https://youtu.be/gou4opptu1U.

</details>


### [246] [WiMoT: Replay-Free Continual Wi-Fi Sensing via Moment Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32660)
*Chunchun Yang,Yang Cao,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WiMoT面向无法保留CSI的持续Wi-Fi感知场景，改用保存各类特征均值与协方差的"矩"而非样本，并通过冻结特征基上的仿射映射同步迁移统计量，在3DO、WiMANS、Widar3.0上分别取得94.02%/89.61%/95.16%的最终准确率，遗忘不超过0.58pp，且优于21个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续Wi-Fi感知需在可塑性（学习新房间/新用户）与稳定性（保持旧知识）之间平衡，但受隐私与存储限制，边缘节点往往无法保留历史CSI样本，因此需要无样本的类增量方案。

Method: 以每类特征均值与协方差作为类原型统计量替代样本存储；当表示变化导致统计量失效时，将表示变化约束为冻结特征基上的仿射映射，并对统计量施加同一映射使其保持有效；用监督对比学习学习新域，用基于存储统计量的闭式损失维持旧类可分性；分类采用由迁移后统计量重新拟合、各域等权的高斯判别分类器，推理时无需域标签；全协方差以秩-32因子存储。

Result: 3DO、WiMANS、Widar3.0最终平均准确率为94.02%、89.61%、95.16%，遗忘≤0.58pp；在3DO与Widar3.0上新旧域精度均衡度最优，分别领先21个基线中最强（replay）方法3.77pp与13.11pp，并在不同域顺序与标签预算下保持领先；WiMANS上准确率与最强replay基线持平（+0.41pp），遗忘从2.78pp降至0.00pp，六种顺序平均准确率最高；秩-32因子将矩存储降至6.4%，精度损失≤0.49pp。

Conclusion: 以统计矩替代样本、配合仿射等变迁移与闭式判别，可在无样本回放的约束下同时实现低遗忘与高精度，内存开销可压入边缘节点预算，为隐私受限的持续Wi-Fi感知提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Continual Wi-Fi sensing must balance plasticity, learning new rooms or users, against stability, keeping earlier ones, yet nodes often cannot retain channel state information (CSI) for privacy and storage reasons. We propose WiMoT (Wi-Fi Moment Transport), which stores the per-class feature mean and covariance of every earlier domain instead of samples. When the representation changes, stored statistics become stale. WiMoT restricts the change to an affine map on a frozen feature basis and applies the same map to the statistics, so they stay valid without old samples. Supervised contrastive learning learns the new domain, a closed-form loss on the stored statistics keeps earlier classes separable, and a Gaussian discriminant classifier, refitted from the transported statistics with equal weight per domain, predicts without domain identity. On 3DO, WiMANS, and Widar3.0, WiMoT's average final accuracies are 94.02%, 89.61%, and 95.16% with at most 0.58 percentage points (pp) of forgetting. On 3DO and Widar3.0, it also has the highest balance of new- and earlier-domain accuracy and is 3.77 and 13.11 pp ahead of the strongest of 21 baselines, which uses replay, staying ahead across domain orders and label budgets. On WiMANS, it matches the strongest replay baseline in accuracy (+0.41 pp) while reducing forgetting from 2.78 to 0.00 pp, and has the highest six-order average accuracy. Because full covariances dominate the memory, storing them as rank-32 factors shrinks the moment memory to 6.4%, losing at most 0.49 pp of accuracy, within edge-node storage budgets.

</details>


### [247] [Do Language Models Trust Their Own Justifications? A Study on Functional Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32665)
*Alisson Rosa Pereira,Bruno Iochins Grisci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"功能自洽性"为核心的实验框架，通过充分性、完备性与反事实三类指标，检验LLM文本分类中自生成解释的高亮token是否与其预测行为一致；在IMDB与Steam评论上评测GPT-4o-mini、Gemma3、Granite8B与DeepSeek，发现解释可靠性随模型家族与规模而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在文本分类中不仅输出标签，还生成高亮关键token的解释，但现有文献多评估解释的文本合理性（plausibility），较少检验这些高亮元素在定向干预下是否与模型自身预测行为一致，即"功能自洽性"缺失评估手段。

Method: 基于自洽性原则构建实验框架：若模型将某些token判为决定性，则对其隔离、删除或语义反转应引起系统性、可解释的预测变化。将该原则操作化为充分性（sufficiency）、完备性（comprehensiveness）与反事实性（counterfactuality）三类指标，在IMDB与Steam评论数据集上，对闭源GPT-4o-mini与开源Gemma3、Granite8B、DeepSeek进行干预实验。

Result: GPT-4o-mini在全部指标上呈现预期的一致递进；Gemma3与Granite8B在充分性下保持连贯，但在更强干预下失去一致性；DeepSeek各变体出现结构性偏差，或无法保持充分性，或在完备性与反事实性下反应过度。

Conclusion: 解释可靠性随LLM家族与规模变化：较小模型表现出自相矛盾，较大模型表现出过度敏感。综合充分性、完备性与反事实性三指标，为评估LLM自解释的功能一致性提供了系统化方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely adopted in text classification tasks, where they not only output class predictions but also generate explanations that highlight the tokens deemed most relevant for reaching the predicted label. Yet it remains unclear whether these highlighted elements are behaviorally consistent with the model's own predictions under targeted interventions, which we refer to as functional auto-consistency. While much of the literature evaluates the textual plausibility of such explanations, few studies assess their functional consistency with the model's actual behavior. In this work, we propose an experimental framework based on the principle of auto-consistency: if a model identifies certain tokens as decisive, then isolating, removing, or semantically inverting them should produce systematic and interpretable changes in its predictions. We operationalize this evaluation through sufficiency, comprehensiveness, and counterfactuality metrics, and conduct experiments on IMDB and Steam reviews across both closed-source (GPT-4o-mini) and open-source LLMs (Gemma3, Granite8B, DeepSeek). Results show that GPT-4o-mini follows the expected progression across all metrics, Gemma3 and Granite8B maintain coherence under sufficiency but lose consistency under more demanding interventions, while DeepSeek variants display structural deviations, either failing to preserve sufficiency or overreacting under comprehensiveness and counterfactuality. These findings show that explanation reliability varies across LLM families and scales, with smaller models displaying contradictions and larger ones exhibiting over-sensitivity. By combining sufficiency, comprehensiveness, and counterfactuality, our approach provides a systematic methodology for assessing the functional consistency of LLM self-explanations.

</details>


### [248] [Retiming Robot Demonstrations with a Learned Speed Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32676)
*Seungyong Moon,Deokjae Lee,Hansang Lee,Munkhtselmeg Battsengel,Youngin Kim,Seonghyeon Yun,Sungjin Jeon,SongEun Kim,Jeongmin Park,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 从大规模示教数据中学习"速度先验"，为每条示教预测沿路径的执行速度分布，用选定分位数作为重定时的统一时钟，使机器人策略获得一致、平滑且更快的执行节奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示教节奏由遥操作者而非任务本身决定，同一动作在不同示教中速度差异大，策略会把操作者特有的时序与任务行为一起学进去；而已有加速方法需从待加速数据本身推导加速度信号，每个新任务都要额外拟合、标注或预设速率对。

Method: 输入任务指令、运动路径及沿路径图像，学习速度先验以预测沿路径的速度分布，取所选分位数定义重定时时钟；在机器人遥操作数据与人手（而非机械臂）持夹爪的手持示教数据上训练单一先验，覆盖语料中所有任务；用它把机器人示教重定时到手持节奏后再微调视觉-语言-动作策略；执行时配合实时最优控制层，在机器人约束下按预测节奏跟踪动作块。

Result: 在120个RoboLab任务上，使用学习时钟微调的策略成功率更高，且抖动（jerk）最多仅为原始时序微调并统一加速到相同速度的策略的一半；真实机器人叠杯耗时3.50s，比默认策略的6.02s快1.72倍，成功率相当（30次试验成功29次）。

Conclusion: 单一学习速度先验可替代逐任务的加速拟合或标注，为任意任务提供一致时钟，在提升成功率与平滑性的同时显著加速真实机器人执行。

Abstract: The pace of a robot demonstration is often determined by the teleoperator rather than by the task itself. As a result, the same motion can appear at widely different speeds across demonstrations, and policies trained on such data may inherit operator-specific timing along with task behavior. We propose to learn a speed prior directly from large-scale demonstration data. Given a task instruction, a motion path and the images along it, it predicts a distribution over execution speed along the path, from which a chosen quantile defines a consistent clock for retiming each demonstration. Existing methods derive the acceleration signal from the very data they accelerate, so each new task costs another fit, annotation, or pair of predefined rates. A single trained speed prior instead covers every task in a corpus. We fit a speed prior on both robot teleoperation data and handheld demonstrations in which a human arm, rather than a robot arm, moves the gripper, and use it to retime robot demonstrations to the handheld pace before fine-tuning a vision-language-action policy. For execution, we pair the policy with a real-time optimal-control layer that tracks predicted action chunks under robot constraints using the predicted pace. On 120 RoboLab tasks, policies fine-tuned on our learned clocks succeed more often and move with up to half the jerk compared with policies fine-tuned on the original demonstration timing and uniformly accelerated to the same speed. On a real robot, our policy stacks cups in 3.50s, 1.72$\times$ faster than the default policy's 6.02s while succeeding as often, in 29 of 30 trials. Videos and further material are available at https://mllab.snu.ac.kr/projects/learned_speed_prior.

</details>


### [249] [Agentic Hazard Mapper: Emulating Expert Map-Making with AI Agents for Risk Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32738)
*Sidi Wu,Lifeng Lin,Yizi Chen,Anna Yokokubo,Lorenz Hurni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Hazard Mapper——一个模拟专业制图流程与迭代式设计权衡的多智能体系统，可跨数据、地点与灾害情境自适应地自动生成灾害地图，用户研究显示其生成地图在空间理解与疏散规划上优于市政地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害地图是公众风险沟通、备灾与疏散规划的关键工具，但地方政府与社区往往缺乏制图专业知识，难以保证地图的有效性。

Method: 采用形成性研究（与地图专家合作）指导系统设计；系统以多智能体架构模拟专业制图工作流与迭代设计权衡，并依数据、地点、灾害情境自适应调整设计决策；随后开展用户研究与访谈（对象为地图专家与灾害实务工作者）。

Result: 生成地图提升了参与者的空间理解与疏散规划表现，降低了主观工作量感知与任务完成时间，且总体优于对应的市政地图。

Conclusion: 讨论了人工监督、面向不同用户与沟通情境的适配，以及将智能体支持扩展到灾害地图制作与风险沟通更广泛环节的机会。

Abstract: Hazard maps are essential tools for public risk communication, preparedness, and evacuation planning, yet effective cartographic design is not always ensured due to limited access to cartographic expertise across local governments and communities. We present Agentic Hazard Mapper, a multi-agent system that automates hazard-map production by emulating professional cartographic workflows and iterative design trade-offs while adapting design decisions to different data, locations, and hazard contexts. We conducted a formative study with map experts to inform the system design, followed by a user study and interviews with map experts and hazard practitioners. The generated maps improved participants' spatial understanding and evacuation-planning performance, reduced perceived workload and task completion time, and were generally preferred over corresponding municipal maps. We further discuss human oversight, adaptation across users and communication contexts, and opportunities to extend agentic support across broader stages of hazard-map production and risk communication.

</details>


### [250] [ActionRAG: Evidence-Refining Graph Diffusion for Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32752)
*Navyansh Mahla,Sashank Vandrangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActionRAG：一种将“谨慎索引”与“查询时图回溯重评”耦合的图记忆架构，在MemoryAgentBench八列确定性打分上取得55.1平均准确率，较最强检索式记忆基线提升5.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需要能从不断增长的交互历史中恢复精确且相互一致的证据，尤其当答案依赖事实间关系而非孤立语义匹配时。现有记忆流水线在两个环节存在脆弱性：(1) 索引错误导致重复实体被割裂或不同实体被错误合并；(2) 在线检索把初始相似度分数当作最终结果，即使局部图结构已提供修正依据。

Method: 索引阶段：协调类型标签并执行分层实体解析（tiered entity resolution）。检索阶段：双通道（实体+三元组）检索形成初始证据集并诱导有界局部图；采用查询条件化、接收者归一化的扩散（query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion）利用局部路径结构精炼三元组分数；辅以种子保护（seed protection）、枢纽惩罚流量（hub-penalized flux）与三元组物化（triple materialization），在保留直接证据的同时恢复遗漏连接。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench（MAB）上评估，覆盖准确检索、冲突消解、长程理解与测试时学习四项能力。八列确定性打分下平均准确率55.1，较最强检索式记忆基线提升5.4个点。按MAB查询时成本核算，四个成本列上平均综合成本低于每查询$0.001；消费级Apple Silicon环境下，分项测量的检索侧p99耗时之和为440.7 ms。

Conclusion: 将索引阶段的实体一致性维护与查询阶段的图结构重评相结合，可在低成本、低延迟（亚秒级p99）条件下显著提升长时记忆检索效果，说明“初始相似度非最终分数”的图回溯机制是当前记忆流水线的有效补强方向。

Abstract: LLM agents need long-term memory that can recover precise and mutually consistent evidence from growing interaction histories, particularly when answers depend on relations among facts rather than isolated semantic matches. Current memory pipelines remain vulnerable at two stages: indexing errors can fragment repeated entities or merge distinct ones, and online retrieval often treats initial similarity scores as final even when the surrounding graph provides evidence for revising them. We introduce ActionRAG, a graph memory architecture that couples cautious indexing with query-time graph retroevaluation. Its indexing pipeline reconciles type labels and applies tiered entity resolution. Given a query, dual-channel entity and triple retrieval forms an initial evidence set and induces a bounded local graph. Query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion refines triple scores using local path structure. Seed protection, hub-penalized flux, and triple materialization preserve direct evidence while recovering missed connections. We evaluate ActionRAG on MemoryAgentBench (MAB), which tests accurate retrieval, conflict resolution, long-range understanding, and test-time learning. Across its eight deterministic-scoring columns, ActionRAG achieves 55.1 average accuracy, improving over the strongest retrieval-based memory baseline by 5.4 points. Under MAB's query-time cost accounting, mean synthesis cost stays below \$0.001 per query on the four reported cost columns, and the sum of separately measured retrieval-side p99 components is 440.7 ms on our consumer Apple Silicon setup.

</details>


### [251] [HarnessMM: Recursive Self-Improvement for Harness Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32813)
*Yuhua Jiang,Chang Xu,Feifei Gao,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessMM，通过“受保护决策复用”与递归自我改进（RSI）在模型-工具边界缓存并替换重复推理回调，大幅降低 agent harness token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 在工具反馈后反复触发推理，即使上一次调用已确定下一步动作，造成冗余推理与 token 浪费。

Method: 在模型-工具边界设置控制器，在 actor 权重固定的前提下复用已解析决策；用可执行决策图（Executable Decision Graph）存储精确动作、字面参数、因果依赖与公开守卫；通过“受保护决策复用”依据观测回执激活决策，并经“认证回调替换”在原生接口执行、记录移除慢速回调；遇到新语义决策、证据不足或选择器弃权时回退原始 actor；RSI 依据训练反馈迭代优化 harness 配置，并用验证集选择部署配置。

Result: HumanEval 上保持 Codex 成功率 90.82%，Tokens/task 由 66.72k 降至 47.02k（节省 29.54%）；DeepSeek Harness 成功率由 71.43% 升至 85.71%，Tokens/task 由 96.28k 降至 11.62k（节省 87.93%）；MBPP 消融显示成本比单次使用图低 26.91%，比保留回调低 19.52%。

Conclusion: 受保护决策复用是减少现有 harness 重复推理的可行方案，在不改动模型权重的前提下兼顾质量与成本。

Abstract: Agent harnesses repeatedly invoke reasoning after tool feedback, even when an earlier call has already fixed the next action. We introduce HarnessMM (Harness Minus Minus), a method for improving harness token efficiency through guarded decision reuse and recursive self-improvement (RSI). RSI iteratively refines companion harness configurations from training feedback, with validation selecting the configuration for deployment. A controller at the model-tool boundary reuses resolved decisions to replace later reasoning callbacks while actor weights remain fixed. Executable Decision Graphs store exact actions, literal arguments, causal dependencies, and public guards. Guarded Decision Reuse activates these persistent decisions from observed receipts; Certified Callback Substitution executes them through the native interface and records removal of the registered slow callback. New semantic decisions, unsupported evidence, and selector abstention invoke the original actor again. The representation restricts what feedback may change, while complete-task measurements establish quality and cost. On HumanEval, HarnessMM preserves Codex success at 90.82\% while reducing Tokens/task (k) from 66.72 to 47.02 (29.54\% saving). For DeepSeek Harness, success rises from 71.43\% to 85.71\% while Tokens/task (k) fall from 96.28 to 11.62 (87.93\% saving). A separate MBPP ablation measures 26.91\% lower cost than single-use graphs and 19.52\% lower cost than retained callbacks. These results establish guarded decision reuse as a practical way to reduce repeated reasoning in existing harnesses.

</details>


### [252] [Reimagining Enterprise RAG for Multi-Agent Systems: A Knowledge Layer That Organizes Before It Retrieves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32851)
*Garima Agrawal,Prasun Das,Akshay Sarvade,Priyanka L,Minisha N,Sravani Joshna,Hemath Manivannan,Lavanya Korada,Sambhrit Pattanayak,Srashti Gupta,Siddu R,Prasad Kalyanasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在工件层面构建知识层（自描述目录+稀疏元数据注册表+导航超图），实现"先组织后检索"，使跨服务同名实体的规则消歧成为结构性而非统计性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需要的是系统行为逻辑而非利益相关者文档；七 Agent 逆向工程流水线虽能从 241 个仓库、18 个服务中恢复海量工件，但"恢复≠可用"。offer、eligibility、activation 等实体跨服务共享词汇却语义不同，一条端到端旅程涉及上千条近似的规则，扁平向量检索会把错误服务的相同规则以高相似度返回且无错误信号；内容级本体构建成本高、不存在且难以跨迭代维持。

Method: 将本体置于工件层级：利用机器生成语料中每个工件自带的生产者、组件、服务、域与类型等溯源信息，在读取内容前完成消歧。构建知识层包含三部分——自描述目录（self-describing catalog）、稀疏元数据注册表、以及定义在工件而非抽取实体之上的导航超图。查询先解析为工件标识符，再用其约束向量检索。

Result: 竞争性规则直接被排除而非在排序中被压低；不足一周建成，可通过约定纳入新服务；服务对象覆盖 SDLC Agent、开发者、质量工程师与分析人员；支持 100+ 生产缺陷的根因分析与修复（经开发者验证），并将 35 次发布回归失败归因到具体规则与服务。

Conclusion: 消歧应当结构化而非统计化；以工件级溯源元数据承载本体，可在低成本、可维护的前提下使逆向工程产物真正可用，并有效支撑 SDLC 智能体的实际生产任务。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying agentic AI across the software development lifecycle, and they need the behavioral logic of the systems they act on, not stakeholder documentation. A seven-agent reverse-engineering pipeline recovered this logic, processing 241 repositories across 18 services into thousands of artifacts describing scope, journeys, rules and acceptance criteria. Recovery does not confer usability. Entities like offer, eligibility, and activation recur across services under a shared vocabulary, related in name but operationally distinct, so one end-to-end journey implicates over a thousand near-identical rules that must not be conflated. Flat vector retrieval fails, returning an identical rule from the wrong service at high similarity with no signal of error. Content-level ontology, the traditional remedy, did not exist, and relating every occurrence of recurring entities would have cost more than the engagement and not survived a release cycle. We place the ontology at the artifact level. In this machine-generated corpus, each artifact retains its producer, component, service, domain and type, provenance that disambiguates before content is read. We present a knowledge layer that organizes before retrieving: a self-describing catalog, a sparse metadata registry, and a navigation hypergraph defined over artifacts rather than extracted entities. Queries resolve to artifact identifiers that constrain vector search, so the competing rule from another service is absent rather than outranked, making disambiguation structural, not statistical. Built in under a week and admitting new services by convention, the layer serves SDLC agents, developers, quality engineers and analysts. It supported root-cause analysis and resolution of 100+ production defects with developer verification and attributed 35 release regression failures to specific rules and services.

</details>


### [253] [A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863)
*Wenxin Chen,Kyra Gan,Mingyu Lu,Zilin Jing,Fei Wang,Hoifung Poon,Tristan Naumann,Peniel Argaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PICLE-PFN，首个面向纵向因果效应估计的基础模型，通过上下文内历史-决策演示统一表示动态治疗策略，预训练后可单次前向预测 CAPO，无需任务特定训练与调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果效应估计受时变混杂与治疗-混杂反馈环困扰；现有方法需针对数据拟合 nuisance 模型，且多局限于预设治疗序列或预测时域，部署时计算耗时、缺乏通用性。

Method: 以 Prior-data Fitted Networks 为基础，构建跨数据集摊销的纵向因果基础模型；用上下文内的历史-决策演示表征目标策略，形成统一接口；在纵向结构因果模型采样的合成数据上预训练，使模型对未来观测数据与目标策略直接输出条件平均潜在结果（CAPO）。

Result: 在合成与半合成纵向基准上，RMSE 与 SOTA 传统估计器（逐数据集训练与调参）相当，不确定性校准显著更优，部署运行时间仅为其中一小部分。

Conclusion: PICLE-PFN 将摊销式基础模型范式引入纵向因果推断，实现无需任务特定训练的动态策略效应估计，在精度、校准与部署效率上具综合优势。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal effect estimation is complicated by time-varying confounding and treatment-confounder feedback loops. Existing methods generally require data-specific nuisance-model fitting, and many are tailored to prespecified treatment sequences or prediction horizons, leading to time-consuming computation at deployment. We introduce *Policy In-Context Longitudinal causal Effect estimation with Prior-data Fitted Networks* (**PICLE-PFN**), the first foundation model for longitudinal causal effect estimation that supports *dynamic* treatment policies. PICLE-PFN represents target policies through in-context history-decision demonstrations, providing a unified interface for a broad class of treatment policies. Pretrained over synthetic data sampled from longitudinal structural causal models, PICLE-PFN amortizes causal effect estimation across datasets, policies, and horizons. Given a new observational dataset and target policy, it directly predicts conditional average potential outcomes (CAPOs) in one forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic longitudinal benchmarks, PICLE-PFN achieves competitive RMSE and substantially improved uncertainty calibration with a fraction of deployment runtime, compared to state-of-the-art conventional estimators trained and tuned separately for each dataset.

</details>


### [254] [From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869)
*Maxim Osovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出消息生成与理解的五层组织框架，论证LLM上下文无法呈现言语形成的基础，并给出正向与逆向的重构路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型依赖上下文与训练权重生成文本，但二者本身无法证明存在刻意构建的系统性世界表征；需要一种可检验、可调整的消息基础组织方式，以控制意义转换的后果。

Method: 构建五层组织：活动世界的系统表征、从中切出的具体情境、情境的叙事描述、消息的完整意义、文本；区分情境的图式模型与关于它的故事；提出正向（受者依共同构建并认可的共享表征与合作活动，从简略文本重建完整意义）与逆向（从完整意义经扩展描述、情境图式模型回到系统基础的重建）两条工作路径。理论来源为Shchedrovitskiy的系统方法论、Korzybski的结构微分与Mel'chuk的意义—文本理论。

Result: 形成一种具体的系统—活动式内容组织，可用于审查和调整消息基础，并控制其转换后果；明确图式模型不等于叙事，上下文与权重均不能充当系统表征的证明。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为消息基础的检验与调整提供系统—活动框架；后续研究方向是可重建系统基础上的活动大型图模型，以区别于从上下文生成文本。

Abstract: This paper proposes a five-level organization of message formation and understanding within thinking and activity in which ASI participates. Its central thesis is that a language model's context is fundamentally incapable of presenting the foundations of utterance formation for examination and verification. These foundations are established in advance by acquired schemas, systemic representations of the world of activity, and connections accepted as true within them. Neither context nor trained LLM weights, in themselves, attest to the presence of deliberately constructed systemic representations. The five levels distinguish systemic representations of the world of activity, a particular situation cut out from those representations, its narrative description, the full sense of a message, and text. A situation's schema-model is not identical to a story about it. A recipient reconstructs the full sense from an abbreviated text through jointly constructed and agreed-upon representations and joint activity. Reverse work proceeds from the full sense through an extended description and the situation's schema-model to the reconstruction of systemic foundations. The theoretical foundations draw on the systems methodology of G. P. Shchedrovitskiy, the structural differential of A. Korzybski, and the Meaning-Text theory of I. A. Mel'chuk. The contribution is a specific systemic-activity organization of content for examining and adjusting message foundations and controlling the consequences of their transformation. Further research concerns large graphical models of activity on reconstructible systemic foundations, distinct from text generation from context.

</details>


### [255] [Do "True" and "False" Add Up to One? Complement Consistency in LLM-Agent Opinion Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32874)
*Anouar Imel,Jin Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文测试LLM智能体意见模拟中的"互补一致性"：将主张改写为互补命题并把概率p换成1−p后，两个报告应加和为1；但在Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、Gemma-3-4B上系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若智能体报告的数值代表其对主张的信念（位置）而非被展示句子的表征，则互补改写应产生互补答案（两报告之和为1）。这一公理若被违反，则模拟中的概率不能被解读为信念，现有关于"意见动力学"结果的可解释性受到质疑。

Method: 在三类数据上做配对扰动实验：(1) 取自运行中网络模拟的2,304个智能体状态；(2) AVeriTeC中48条真实事实核查主张；(3) 24个仅有两个可能结果的受控世界基准。对每个条目同时查询原句与互补句，并比较|p+(1−p')−1|的偏差；另做同义改写对照、重复采样、不同同伴群体规模与报告强度（即报告概率的极性与幅度分布）的稳健性检验；再将报告反馈进两个从互补初始状态出发的种群，观察五轮后的镜像性；最后检验互补平均修正对Brier损失的影响，并与"日程—目标（schedule-by-targeting）"实验中措辞固定带来的小交互作对比。

Result: Qwen在2,304个状态上配对报告平均和为1.18，在真实事实核查主张上同样超过1；24世界受控基准中三个模型均失败。偏差比同义改写带来的变化大2–10倍，且在重复采样、不同同伴群体规模与报告强度下持续存在。两个互补初始种群五轮后不再互为镜像（分歧为正）。互补平均修正以构造方式恢复一致性并降低两措辞平均的Brier损失，但末轮仅在一个模型上优于两种原始措辞中较好者；措辞固定的schedule-by-targeting扫描交互小且稳定。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的报告概率不满足互补改写下的加和为一，因而不能直接等同于信念；这种不一致是稳健的、并非采样噪声或表层释义效应。将模拟概率解读为意见/信念前，需要单独的一致性检验；固定措辞的实验（如schedule-by-targeting）因不触发该失效而掩盖问题。

Abstract: In LLM-agent opinion simulations, each agent reports the probability that a claim is true after seeing its own previous probability and the probabilities reported by its neighbors, and that number becomes its state in the next round. If the number describes the agent's position rather than the sentence it was shown, then rewriting the claim as its complement and replacing every probability p with 1 - p should return the complementary answer, so the two reports should sum to one. We test this with three small open models: Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.2-3B and Gemma-3-4B. On 2,304 agent states taken from a running network simulation, Qwen's paired reports sum to 1.18 on average, and on 48 real fact-checking claims from AVeriTeC they again exceed one. A controlled benchmark of 24 worlds, each with exactly two possible outcomes, shows the same failure in all three models. The discrepancy is two to ten times larger than the change produced by a same-meaning paraphrase, and it persists under repeated sampling and across peer group sizes and report strengths. When the reports are fed back into two populations that start from complementary states, the populations do not remain mirror images: their divergence is positive after five rounds for every model. Averaging each answer with the complement of its paired answer restores consistency by construction and lowers Brier loss averaged over the two wordings, but at the final round it beats the better of the two original wordings for only one of the three models. A schedule-by-targeting experiment on the same model gives a small, stable interaction because such sweeps keep the claim wording fixed. Consistency under rewording therefore needs its own test before simulated probabilities are read as beliefs

</details>


### [256] [Advancing SVD-based LLM Compression via Layer-Wise Error Model Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32878)
*Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Groezinger,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Emad Aghajanzadeh,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Pierpaolo Mori,Nael Fasfous,Alexander Frickenstein,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LEMS 与 KFAC-SVD，分别解决低秩 SVD 压缩中的全局秩分配与分解质量问题，在 Mistral、Qwen3、Llama 3 上零样本精度最高提升 4.8 个百分点，困惑度平均改善 15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩 SVD 压缩存在两大局限：(i) 秩分配采用全局方式，未经校准的误差代理无法刻画复杂的误差传播；(ii) 分解质量不足，基于 Fisher 的估计器会出现严重的秩崩塌。

Method: 提出 Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS)：构建层次化误差代理，融合局部与全局层重要性并引入传播偏置，通过整数线性规划（ILP）实现有效的全局秩分配。提出 KFAC-SVD：利用 token 级统计量改进分解，缓解先前 Fisher 型 SVD 方法的秩亏问题。

Result: 在 Mistral、Qwen3、Llama 3 系列模型上，LEMS 稳定优于现有搜索策略，零样本准确率最高提升 4.8 p.p.，并可泛化至 70B 规模；KFAC-SVD 带来平均 15% 的困惑度改善。

Conclusion: 层次化误差建模结合 ILP 的秩分配，配合基于 token 级统计的 KFAC-SVD 分解，可显著提升 LLM 低秩 SVD 压缩的效果，并具备跨模型家族与规模的泛化能力。

Abstract: Low-rank SVD-based compression offers a powerful strategy to reduce the computational costs of LLMs. However, existing methods face two key limitations: (i) global rank allocation, where uncalibrated error proxies fail to capture complex error propagation, and (ii) decomposition quality, where Fisher-based estimators suffer from severe rank collapse. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS) and KFAC-SVD. LEMS advances rank allocation by introducing a layer-wise error surrogate that integrates local and global layer importance alongside a propagation bias, enabling effective global rank allocation via an ILP formulation. KFAC-SVD improves decomposition quality by utilizing token-wise statistics, mitigating the rank deficiency observed in prior Fisher-based SVD approaches. Across Mistral, Qwen3, and Llama 3 model families, we show that LEMS consistently outperforms existing search strategies, delivering significant zero-shot accuracy gains of up to 4.8 p.p. that generalize to model sizes of 70B parameters, while KFAC-SVD achieves an average perplexity improvement of 15%. Project page and code: https://lems-svd.github.io

</details>


### [257] [NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jangseon Park,Jason Kong,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 NgramRL：一种基于每提示近期 rollout 历史的 n-gram 草稿器，无需辅助模型或 GPU 常驻状态，配合自适应扇出预取器隐藏 CPU 查表开销，显著提升 RL 后训练的 rollout 吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练中自回归 rollout 生成成本高（可占训练时间 70% 以上）。现有投机解码不适配：基于模型的草稿器占用稀缺 GPU 显存，且随策略演化可能失配；无模型方法未显式利用跨 epoch 的重复性。

Method: 观察同一提示的近期 rollout 可作为高质量、自更新的草稿来源；据此构建 bounded recent rollout history 上的每提示 n-gram 草稿器，无辅助模型、无草稿器训练、无常驻 GPU 状态，并由目标模型验证保持 rollout 分布不变；另引入自适应扇出预取器预测验证结果 (j, b)（接受草稿长度 j 与目标续写 token b），用因果预算分配器将预取集中于高概率结果，未覆盖结果回退到顺序查表且不改变已提交 token。

Result: 在两个模型族、三类推理与结构化生成任务上，热缓存下每次目标模型前向提交 3.60–7.77 个 token，比 SuffixDecoding 最高多 1.99x、比 prompt-lookup decoding 多 7.0x；在 Spider 上，候选预算 16 时预取器覆盖 Qwen3-8B 的 96.6% 结果，实测 rollout 吞吐较同步 NgramRL 提升 1.38x，接近 1.40x 的重叠上限。

Conclusion: 利用 rollout 历史的自更新 n-gram 草稿 + CPU 预取隐藏，可为 RL 后训练提供低成本、分布无损的加速方案，接近重叠上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but its cost is dominated by autoregressive rollout generation, which can exceed 70% of training time. Existing speculative-decoding methods are poorly suited to this setting: model-based drafters consume scarce GPU memory and may become misaligned as the policy evolves, while existing model-free methods do not explicitly exploit repetition across training epochs. We observe that recent rollouts of the same prompt provide a high-quality, self-updating draft source. Based on this insight, we propose NgramRL, a per-prompt n-gram drafter constructed from bounded recent rollout history. NgramRL requires no auxiliary model, drafter training, or persistent GPU-resident drafter state, while target-model verification preserves the rollout distribution. To hide CPU cache lookup and scheduling, we further introduce an adaptive fan-out prefetcher that predicts likely verification outcomes (j, b), consisting of the accepted draft length j and target-emitted continuation token b. A causal budget allocator concentrates prefetches on high-probability outcomes, while uncovered outcomes fall back to sequential lookup without changing committed tokens. Across two model families and three reasoning and structured-generation workloads, NgramRL commits 3.60-7.77 tokens per target-model forward in the warm-cache regime, up to 1.99x more than SuffixDecoding and 7.0x more than prompt-lookup decoding. On Spider, the prefetcher covers 96.6% of Qwen3-8B outcomes at a candidate budget of 16 and improves measured rollout throughput by 1.38x over synchronous NgramRL, approaching its 1.40x overlap ceiling.

</details>


### [258] [Closing the Modality Gap via Simplex-Constrained Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32890)
*Shubham Gupta,Siva Reddy,Perouz Taslakian,Valentina Zantedeschi,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将多模态对比学习的嵌入域从单位球面S^{d-1}改为概率单纯形Δ^{d-1}，借助非负、单位质量和约束作为跨模态校准先验，配合轻量softmax适配器与全变差相似度，显著缩小模态间隙并保持/超越检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP、CLAP等多模态对比模型虽能学到共享表示，但嵌入存在持续的"模态间隙"（modality gap）；作者希望用纯几何手段消除两模态嵌入的分离。

Method: 把嵌入域由单位球面S^{d-1}换成概率单纯形Δ^{d-1}（二者内在维数相同）；单纯形的非负、单位质量约束充当跨模态校准，迫使两模态落在同一坐标系；并附加轻量softmax适配器，以Total Variation作为相似度度量。

Result: 在五个多模态检索基准上，质心ℓ2间隙降低97–99%，检索性能与标准无约束模型持平或更优。

Conclusion: 单纯形几何能诱导真正的语义对齐，是一种有效的几何先验手段。

Abstract: While multimodal contrastive models like CLIP and CLAP successfully learn shared representations, their embeddings typically exhibit a persistent \emph{modality gap}. We propose addressing this separation through a purely geometric approach: changing the embedding domain from the unit sphere, $S^{d-1}$, to the probability simplex, $Δ^{d-1}$. Although both manifolds share the same intrinsic dimension, the simplex acts as a structural prior; its nonnegative, unit-mass constraints serve as a cross-modal calibration that forces both modalities to ground themselves in the same coordinate system. By attaching lightweight softmax adapters and using Total Variation similarity, we reduce the centroid $\ell_2$ gap by 97--99\% across five multimodal retrieval benchmarks. This confirms that simplicial geometry induces genuine semantic alignment, matching or exceeding the retrieval performance of standard unconstrained models.

</details>


### [259] [SemantEq: Faithful Bidirectional Equivalence of Formal Statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32919)
*Xinrui Li,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Siddharth Bhat,Rianna Jamdar,Yongxi Lin,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SemantEq：以"允许步骤链"约束的形式化等价关系，并用 Lean 证明项（而非策略脚本）判定语句自动形式化的忠实性；配套 SemantEq-Eval（无模型搜索）与 SemantEq-ProofAudit（模型证明审计）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化语句翻译的正确性负担已从"证明"转移到"语句是否忠实翻译原文"。以可信参考形式化为基准，逻辑等价 P↔Q 作为检验标准过强：只要库能分别证明 P 与 Q，即可经 P↔True↔Q 平凡成立，证明器越强、误判越多。既有方法通过限制证明器、检查证明中是否引用对方语句来规避，原理性不足。

Method: 提出 SemantEq 关系：要求两语句只能经由逻辑重排、用库自带等式重写等"允许步骤"相互转换，排除经由 True 等平凡中转。判定依据是 Lean 校验的证明项而非 tactic 脚本。据此构建两部分：SemantEq-Eval，无需语言模型的搜索流程；SemantEq-ProofAudit，依据该关系导出的策略审计模型撰写的证明。

Result: 在 ProofNetVerif 测试集（人工裁定标签）上，SemantEq-Eval 达到 100% 精确率与 65.6% 召回率，BEq+ 为 95.8%/61.8%；其 CPU 时间增至 8.9 倍，最多仅新增三对判定。语言模型能找出更多证明，但在四种提示中的两种下，对约五分之二的非等价对同时证明了两个方向。SemantEq-ProofAudit 对 104 个非等价对全部拒收，并在最优提示下将 407 个等价对的召回率从 65.4% 提升至 81.1%。

Conclusion: 将等价证明的"生成"与"验收"分离：更强的证明器可提升召回率而不改变验收语义，从而在保证精确率的同时获得可扩展的忠实性判定。

Abstract: Statement autoformalization translates mathematics from natural language into a proof assistant such as Lean 4, and models now do it at scale. As provers get stronger, the burden of correctness shifts from the proof to the statement, that is, to whether the formal statement faithfully translates its source. Given a trusted reference formalization, the natural test is logical equivalence, and it proves too much. $P \leftrightarrow Q$ holds trivially for any two statements $P$ and $Q$ that the library proves, because a prover can prove each separately and pass through $P \leftrightarrow \mathrm{True} \leftrightarrow Q$. The stronger the prover, the more unrelated pairs pass. Earlier methods restrict the prover and check that each proof names the other statement. We introduce SemantEq, a more principled relation that instead requires each statement to turn into the other only through a chain of allowed steps, such as logical rearrangement and rewriting with the library's own equations. Our programs judge a proof by the proof term Lean checks, not by its tactic script. SemantEq-Eval, a search without a model, reaches 100% precision and 65.6% recall on the ProofNetVerif test split under our adjudicated labels, against 95.8% and 61.8% for BEq+, and an 8.9-fold increase in its CPU time adds at most three pairs. A language model finds more proofs, but under two of four prompts it proves both directions of about two in five of the non-equivalent pairs. SemantEq-ProofAudit audits each model-written proof under a policy derived from the relation and accepts none of the 104 non-equivalent pairs, and it raises recall on 407 equivalent pairs from 65.4% to 81.1% under the best prompt. Separating the generation of equivalence proofs from their acceptance lets a stronger prover raise recall without changing what an acceptance means. We release code and data at: https://github.com/hlxr89/SemantEq

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Collaborative In-Context Learning for Indoor Radio Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31702)
*Yuanzhe Peng,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Poly-Ripple 首次将室内无线电地图估计（RME）建模为协作式上下文学习（ICL）问题，多个客户端用大型自回归视觉模型（LAVM）在无参数更新下做 ICL，结合波纹序自回归建模与重要性感知聚合，在公开室内 RME 基准上稳定优于代表性 ICL 基线，并给出理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内 RME 方法多采用监督学习范式，依赖昂贵标注数据且需针对任务单独训练模型；同时室内环境包含建筑布局与结构等隐私敏感信息，难以集中化。因此需要一种无需模型更新、且能保护数据不集中/不外传的协作式估计方案。

Method: 将室内 RME 形式化为协作式 ICL 问题，提出 Poly-Ripple 框架：多个客户端各自以私有演示为提示、用 LAVM 进行无模型更新的 ICL，对同一场景查询预测无线电地图；采用波纹序（ripple-ordered）自回归建模刻画以发射机为中心向外传播的因果结构；在上下文长度约束下引入重要性感知聚合，融合分布在客户端间的异构上下文知识；并给出理论分析。

Result: 在公开室内 RME 基准上开展大量实验，Poly-Ripple 一致优于代表性 ICL 基线，实验结果与理论分析相符。

Conclusion: 以协作式 ICL 替代监督范式可规避标注依赖与模型重训，并借助分布式私有上下文实现隐私友好的室内无线电地图估计；波纹序建模与重要性感知聚合是其中的关键设计。

Abstract: Indoor radio map estimation (RME) is essential for wireless applications such as coverage planning, localization, and network deployment. However, most existing methods follow a supervised learning paradigm that requires costly labeled data and task-specific model training. Moreover, indoor environments involve privacy-sensitive information, such as building layouts and structures, that often cannot be centralized. To address these challenges, we make the first attempt to formulate indoor RME as a collaborative in-context learning (ICL) problem. We propose Poly-Ripple, a framework in which multiple clients perform ICL using large autoregressive vision models (LAVMs) without model updates. Each client is prompted by its private demonstrations and predicts the radio map for the same scene query. To capture the causal structure of wireless propagation outward from the transmitter (Tx), we adopt ripple-ordered autoregressive modeling. We further introduce importance-aware aggregation to exploit heterogeneous contextual knowledge distributed across clients under the context length constraint. We provide theoretical analysis and conduct extensive experiments on a public indoor RME benchmark. The experimental results show that Poly-Ripple consistently outperforms representative ICL baselines, while aligning with our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [261] [The Path to NDE 4.0: A Review of Algorithms, Techniques, and Strategies for Autonomous Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31704)
*Ashiqur Rahmana,Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi,Zachary Kral,Austin Yunker,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Hamed Alhoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述无损检测（NDI）领域，通过计算算法、物理技术、工业应用与研究目标四个维度梳理文献，指出NDI正从人工模拟判读迈向以数据为中心的NDE 4.0范式，并给出未来研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NDI是保障基础设施与制造构件安全可靠的关键手段，但缺乏对算法、物理技术、应用与目标的整体性把握；同时深度学习兴起带来范式转变，也暴露出数据集与可解释性等瓶颈，需要系统性梳理以明确研究缺口。

Method: 采用综述性文献综合分析，从四个视角（计算算法、物理技术、工业应用、研究目标）组织并综合广泛学术文献，归纳技术演进脉络并识别关键研究空白。

Result: 分析显示超声与视觉检测为主要物理技术；缺陷自动识别显著转向深度学习与生成模型；主要瓶颈为标准化开放数据集稀缺与AI模型"黑箱"特性，制约其规模化应用。

Conclusion: 未来NDI研究应聚焦可解释AI（XAI）、标准化数字孪生协议，以及将智能、自主的检测数据融合与先进传感技术集成到资产全生命周期管理中。

Abstract: Non-Destructive Inspection (NDI) is a cornerstone of modern quality assurance, critical for ensuring the safety and reliability of infrastructure and manufactured components without causing damage. This paper presents a comprehensive review of the current NDI landscape, analyzing the field through four distinct lenses, namely, computational algorithms, physical techniques, industrial applications, and research objectives. We synthesize a broad range of academic literature to illustrate the rapid evolution from manual, analog interpretation to a digitized, data-centric paradigm known as NDE 4.0. Our analysis highlights the dominance of ultrasound and visual inspection as primary physical techniques, while revealing a significant shift toward deep learning and generative models for automated defect recognition. Despite these technological strides, the industry faces critical bottlenecks, notably the scarcity of standardized open-access datasets and the "black box" nature of AI models, which hinders widespread adoption. By charting these developments and identifying key research gaps, such as the need for explainable AI (XAI) and standardized digital twin protocols, this review offers a strategic roadmap for future research. It concludes that the future of NDI research should focus on the successful integration of intelligent, autonomous fusion of inspection data with advanced sensor technologies into asset lifecycle management.

</details>


### [262] [Does LeWorldModel Preserve Local Action Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31716)
*Vasil Vasilev,Siddharth Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较预训练LeWorldModel (LeWM) 与小动作扰动下模拟器响应的吻合程度，用Gram矩阵在三档扰动尺度上度量动作敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是否忠实复现"小幅动作改变"引起的影响尚不清楚，而这直接关系到其作为控制/规划用的可靠性，因此需要定量刻画其动作敏感性几何是否与真实环境一致。

Method: 在Reacher与OGBench Cube近千个状态上，对模拟器与LeWM施加匹配的动作扰动（三档尺度），以Gram矩阵度量动作敏感性的局部几何；比较最敏感方向间的夹角、归一化Gram差异随尺度的变化，并在像素、嵌入与物理状态几何间对比；最后在留出方向上比较环境Gram与同迹各向同性参考的嵌入响应误差。

Result: 最小扰动尺度下，最敏感动作方向的中位夹角约为36°(Reacher)与42°(Cube)；扰动增大十倍后，归一化Gram差异中位值在Reacher下降约70%、Cube下降约47%。像素与嵌入几何的跨尺度变化大于所选物理状态几何。留出方向上，环境Gram的中位嵌入响应误差为17%与28%，同迹各向同性参考为26%与38%；该尺度下LeWM误差更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持对动作敏感性进行尺度特定（scale-specific）的评估，说明LeWM在此尺度下的敏感方向与幅度均与真实环境存在偏差；其误差对下游控制的影响仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We examine how closely pretrained LeWorldModel (LeWM) reproduces the effects of small action changes. Using matched simulator and model responses, we measure action sensitivity with Gram matrices at three perturbation scales. Across nearly a thousand states from Reacher and OGBench Cube, the most sensitive action directions differ by median angles of about $36^\circ$ and $42^\circ$, respectively, at the smallest scale. Increasing the perturbation tenfold reduces median normalized Gram discrepancy by roughly 70\% on Reacher and 47\% on Cube. Geometry measured in pixels and embeddings varies more across scales than the chosen physical-state geometry. On held-out directions, environment Grams achieve median embedding response errors of 17\% and 28\%, compared with 26\% and 38\% for an isotropic reference with the same trace. LeWM's errors are higher at this scale. These findings support scale-specific evaluation of action sensitivity; its effect on control remains an open question.

</details>


### [263] [Towards Adversarially Robust Multigraph Neural Networks for Financial Crime Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31718)
*Dennis Heijmans,H. Çağrı Bilgi,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向多重图神经网络的可扩展结构攻击与对抗训练框架，基于节点-边关联矩阵优化结构扰动，并引入有向2/3环计数正则化约束攻击合理性，在AML任务上验证了攻击有效性与对抗训练防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融交易网络天然是多图（同一账户对间多次交易为不同边），近期多重图神经网络在反洗钱（AML）任务上表现优异，但其对操纵交易结构以规避检测的对抗攻击的鲁棒性尚缺乏研究。

Method: 将投影梯度下降（PGD）与投影随机块坐标下降（PRBCD）推广到节点-边关联矩阵上，实现可扩展的结构扰动优化；并引入基于环的正则项，惩罚有向2环和3环全局计数的变化，作为攻击合理性的结构度量；同时开展对抗训练。

Result: 在合成长模式检测与真实AML数据集上，学习式攻击在降低检测性能（PR-AUC）方面显著优于不可学习的扰动基线；对抗训练能持续提升对这些攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多重图神经网络对学习式结构攻击具有脆弱性，对抗训练是有效的防御手段。

Abstract: Financial transaction networks are naturally represented as multigraphs, where repeated transactions between the same pair of accounts are modeled as distinct edges. Recently proposed multigraph neural network architectures leverage this structure explicitly to achieve a strong performance on anti-money laundering (AML) tasks. However, their robustness to adversaries that manipulate transaction structures to evade detection remains largely unexplored. We present the first scalable framework for structural attacks and adversarial training of multigraph neural networks using node-edge incidence matrices, adapting projected gradient descent and projected randomized block coordinate descent methods to optimize structural perturbations over such matrices. We further introduce a cycle-based regularizer that penalizes changes in the global counts of directed 2- and 3-cycles, providing a structural measure of attack plausibility. Experiments on synthetic pattern-detection and realistic AML datasets show that our learned attacks substantially outperform non-learnable perturbation baselines in reducing detection performance (PR-AUC), while adversarial training consistently improves robustness against these attacks. These results demonstrate that multigraph neural networks are vulnerable to learned structural attacks and that adversarial training provides an effective defense. Our code is available at https://github.com/denheijmans/robust_multignn.

</details>


### [264] [Decode Late, Multiply Once: Rethinking the Dot Product for Quantized Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31913)
*Jonathan Tapson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 低比特权重的点积可通过"权重码作地址偏移+分散累加"重构，用 n+2^k 次加法、最多 2^k 次乘法替代 n 次乘法，并给出 SA-PE 硬件实现与量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特（1–4bit）权重、16/32bit 激活的网络仍按每元素一个乘法器计算点积，乘法器数量与算术能耗偏高；同时解码后的权重需要存储，占用本地权重内存。作者希望利用权重码空间小（2^k）的特性，以减少乘法次数和权重存储。

Method: 将每个 k 位权重码直接作为地址偏移，从 2^k 个累加器中选一个，把激活值累加进去（scatter-accumulate）；最后对 2^k 个累加和做一次融合的"解码-相乘"。整数算术下该重构精确等价，支持浮点操作数与任意（含非线性）解码器，并可扩展到分组 scale、矩阵与张量积。提出 SA-PE（scatter-accumulate processing element）替换权重驻留脉动阵列中的 MAC 单元，采用 load/stream/reduce/drain 调度，使小型可时分复用的乘法器每 tile 仅运行 2^k 次；由于不存储解码权重，本地权重内存最多缩减 w_d/k（w_d 为解码权重位宽）。

Result: 乘法器数量下降 n/2^k；总算术能耗改善倍数较小且有上界，作者用代价模型加以量化；软件模型验证整数精确等价、浮点误差不劣于传统流水线；FPGA 综合的 RTL 对比匹配 MAC 阵列显示：非共享 SA-PE 面积大于 MAC PE，只有当归约乘法器在多个 PE 间共享时才出现收益。

Conclusion: 该重构在数学上成立并可扩展到矩阵/张量运算，主要收益是乘法器与权重存储的减少，但面积/能耗收益有条件——需共享归约乘法器；作者声明该恒等式并非首创，并讨论了权重共享与查表核的相关工作。

Abstract: Large neural networks are increasingly deployed with 1-4 bit weights and 16- or 32-bit activations, yet their dot products still use one multiplier per element. We show that a length-n dot product with k-bit weight codes can be computed with n+2^k additions and at most 2^k decodes and 2^k multiplications. Each weight code is used directly as an address offset that selects one of 2^k accumulators; each activation is added into the selected accumulator; and the accumulated sums are decoded and multiplied only once, at the end, in a fused decode-and-multiply step. The decoded weight vector is never formed. The refactoring is exact for integer arithmetic, works with integer or floating-point operands and with arbitrary, including nonlinear, decoders, and extends to group-wise scales and to matrix and tensor products. The multiplier count falls by n/2^k, while total arithmetic energy improves by a smaller, bounded factor that we quantify with a cost model. We describe a scatter-accumulate processing element (SA-PE) that replaces the MAC unit in a weight-stationary systolic array, with a load/stream/reduce/drain schedule in which a small, time-shareable multiplier runs only 2^k times per tile. Because decoded weights are not stored, local weight memory shrinks by up to w_d/k for w_d-bit decoded weights. An RTL implementation synthesized for FPGA and compared with a matched MAC array shows that an unshared SA-PE is larger than a MAC PE, and that the saving appears when the reduction multiplier is shared across PEs. Software models confirm exact integer equivalence and floating-point error no worse than the conventional pipeline. We do not claim the underlying identity is new, and we discuss related work on weight sharing and lookup-based kernels.

</details>


### [265] [End-to-End Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning for Physics-Informed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31721)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Vineet Gundecha,Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微扩散条件化方法D2C，利用确定性采样使反向去噪链对条件信号可微，从而将冻结的条件扩散模型转化为端到端梯度优化器，在离线模型基优化中通过替代目标与物理惩罚回传梯度直接优化条件变量，并在激光脉冲、数据中心调度及Design-Bench任务上取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维物理系统在昂贵评估与复杂约束下优化困难；离线MBO仅能依赖固定数据集、无测试时真实目标访问。现有方法存在过度保守、无约束地利用替代模型，或仅为静态推理时采样器、无法针对精确物理目标精修候选解等问题。

Method: D2C基于确定性采样使反向去噪链对条件信号可微这一性质，将冻结的条件扩散模型变为端到端梯度优化器：在测试时通过从冻结替代目标与物理信息惩罚项回传的梯度，穿过反向链直接优化条件变量；实现为组合式"提议器—评估器"(proposer-evaluator)框架。

Result: 在惯性约束聚变激光脉冲优化、可持续数据中心负载调度两个真实任务上取得最强结果，并在四个Design-Bench任务上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 将确定性扩散采样的可微性用于条件变量优化，可在不更新模型参数的前提下实现受物理约束引导的候选精修，为离线高维约束优化提供有效范式。

Abstract: Optimizing high-dimensional physical systems under expensive evaluations and complex constraints is a central challenge in science and engineering. In offline model-based optimization (MBO), methods must improve designs using only a fixed dataset and no test-time access to the true objective. However, existing methods typically suffer from conservatism, unconstrained surrogate exploitation, or function as static inference-time samplers that cannot refine candidates against precise physical objectives. In this paper, we propose Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning (D2C) for offline MBO, which leverages the fact that deterministic sampling makes the reverse denoising chain differentiable with respect to the conditioning signal. D2C turns a frozen conditional diffusion model into an end-to-end gradient-based optimizer by directly optimizing conditioning variables at test time via gradients from frozen surrogate objectives and physics-informed penalties backpropagated through the reverse chain. We implement this idea in a composed proposer-evaluator framework and evaluate it on laser pulse optimization for inertial confinement fusion, sustainable data-center workload scheduling, and four Design-Bench tasks. Our method achieves the strongest results on the two real-world tasks and the best average rank across four Design-Bench tasks.

</details>


### [266] [Autoregressive Multimodal Anomaly Detection with Large Language Models for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31722)
*Yixiang Tang,Fagui Liu,Guoxiang Zhong,Xi Yao,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMMAD：基于大语言模型的自回归多模态微服务异常检测框架，通过统一序列建模跨模态关系、模态专属重建头保留模态特性、预测驱动目标消除"身份捷径"，在三个真实数据集上F1分别提升3.1%、1.4%、0.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统可靠性依赖有效异常检测，因为故障与性能异常会迅速跨服务传播。现有多模态异常检测方法存在两大局限：(i) 偏向跨模态融合而忽视模态特有特征；(ii) 特征重建过程中的"身份捷径"（identity-shortcut）问题，即模型倾向复制输入而非真正学习表征。

Method: 提出LLMMAD自回归多模态异常检测框架：1) 将多模态数据嵌入并重组为统一序列供LLM处理以建模模态间关系，同时采用模态级重建头保留模态特有信息，兼顾融合与特性保持；2) 采用预测驱动的自回归目标，令模型预测未来状态而非复制当前输入，从而消除身份捷径。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上开展大量实验，LLMMAD一致优于SOTA方法，F1分数分别提升3.1%、1.4%和0.9%。

Conclusion: LLMMAD为微服务监控提供了更可靠的解决方案，验证了统一序列建模+模态专属头+预测驱动目标这一设计在兼顾跨模态融合与模态特性保留、规避身份捷径方面的有效性。

Abstract: Microservice systems have become a dominant architecture for large-scale cloud applications, enabling rapid deployment and elastic scaling. However, ensuring the reliability and availability of microservice systems critically relies on effective anomaly detection, as failures and performance anomalies can quickly propagate across services and severely impact end-to-end system behavior. Existing multimodal anomaly detection approaches for microservice systems often struggle with two fundamental limitations: (i) the neglect of modality-specific characteristics in favor of cross-modality fusion, and (ii) the "identity-shortcut" problem that affects the feature reconstruction process. To address these issues, we propose LLMMAD, an autoregressive multimodal anomaly detection framework for microservice systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs). Initially, to reconcile cross-modality fusion with modality-specific preservation, LLMMAD embeds and recombines multimodal data into a unified sequence for LLM processing to model inter-modality relationships, while employing modality-wise reconstruction heads to retain modality-specific information. Subsequently, to eliminate the identity-shortcut, we implement a prediction-driven autoregressive objective, under which the model predicts future states rather than copies current inputs. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments on three real-world datasets. The results demonstrate that LLMMAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving F1-score improvements of 3.1%, 1.4%, and 0.9%, respectively, thereby providing a more reliable solution for microservice monitoring.

</details>


### [267] [Cognitively Constrained Partners for Zero-Shot Coordination in Real-Time Partitioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31726)
*Ash Simmons,George De Ath,Richard M. Everson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"认知受限伙伴"（CCP）框架：通过反应延迟与感知局限等生理学约束程序化生成多样化模拟伙伴，用于训练人机协作智能体，在改造的Lunar Lander中初步验证其伙伴行为更接近真人、所训智能体达到最强交叉对战基线且性能不依赖训练伙伴生成方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作需要智能体能配合不可预测的人类伙伴，但从真人反馈或大规模行为数据做强化学习代价高昂，纯模拟训练又易过拟合人造惯例，导致对真实人类的零样本协作（ZSC）失败。

Method: 以人类生理学文献为依据设置反应延迟和感知局限等过程性约束，程序化生成多样的模拟伙伴（Cognitively Constrained Partners），不依赖奖励塑形或历史训练检查点；在改造的Lunar Lander环境中开展交叉对战（cross-play）评测，并设计可直接对比交叉对战排序的人类被试研究。

Result: 初步证据显示CCP的行为比无约束智能体更接近真人；对CCP训练出的智能体达到最强交叉对战基线水平，且是唯一性能不受训练伙伴生成方式影响的方案；人类被试研究尚待报告。

Conclusion: 将生理认知约束而非奖励或检查点作为伙伴多样性来源，可为ZSC训练提供更贴近人类、更稳健的策略；后续将用人类被试数据检验交叉对战这一标准评测对真实人机协作的预测效度。

Abstract: Effective human-machine teaming requires agents that coordinate with unpredictable human partners. Reinforcement learning from live human feedback or large behavioural datasets is expensive, while purely simulated training overfits to artificial conventions and fails at Zero-Shot Coordination with real people. We propose a framework for training teaming agents against Cognitively Constrained Partners: simulated partners whose diversity emerges from procedural constraints grounded in human physiological literature, specifically reaction delays and perceptual limitations, rather than from reward shaping or historical training checkpoints. In a modified Lunar Lander environment, we report preliminary evidence that these partners play more like real people than unconstrained agents do, and that agents trained against them match the strongest cross-play baselines while being the only ones whose performance does not depend on how their training partners were generated. We also describe a human-participant study designed so that its rankings can be compared directly against cross-play, testing how well that standard evaluation predicts real human-agent teaming.

</details>


### [268] [When and How Does Muon Precondition? The Role of Structured Gradient Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31728)
*Xiaoran Chen,Binghui Li,Liwei Wang,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从梯度噪声几何角度理论解释Muon预条件优势：各向同性噪声下Muon仅等价于学习率缩放，无加速；而在异构任务频率的联想记忆结构化噪声下，Muon将对任务频率的线性依赖软化为平方根依赖，平衡频繁与稀有任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon已成为大语言模型训练的常用优化器，但其理论基础薄弱，尤其缺乏对"梯度噪声几何如何塑造其预条件收益"的理解，需要厘清Muon加速的来源与边界条件。

Method: 理论分析与数值实验相结合：先在经典各向同性噪声假设下分析Muon与学习率缩放的关系；再构建源自小批量采样、任务频率异构的联想记忆结构化噪声模型，推导两任务的精确平衡阈值并推广至多任务；进一步分析SignSGD中旋转导致的负迁移及动量在降低采样噪声与跟踪梯度变化中的作用；用受控数值实验验证，并开展初步LLM预训练研究。

Result: 各向同性噪声下Muon相对SGD无加速，仅等价于学习率重缩放；结构化噪声下，Muon将任务频率依赖由线性降为平方根，改善频繁/稀有任务平衡；识别出SignSGD的旋转诱导负迁移而Muon可规避；动量可在降低采样噪声的同时跟踪变化梯度。

Conclusion: Muon的收益本质上依赖于梯度噪声的结构而非普遍加速；其价值在于缓解任务频率异质性带来的学习不平衡并规避符号类方法的负迁移，快慢动量机制在LLM预训练中展现出实践潜力。

Abstract: Muon has become a popular optimizer for large language model training, yet its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this work, we study how gradient noise geometry shapes Muon's preconditioning benefits. We first show, theoretically and empirically, that under the classical isotropic noise assumption, Muon offers no acceleration beyond a rescaling of the learning rate. This motivates us to investigate structured noise arising from mini-batch sampling in associative memory with heterogeneous task frequencies. We establish an exact balancing threshold for two tasks and extend the analysis to multiple tasks, revealing how task coverage and gradient reliability jointly shape learning. Our analysis shows that Muon can soften the dependence on task frequency from linear to square-root, helping balance the learning of frequent and rare tasks. We further identify rotation-induced negative transfer in SignSGD that Muon avoids, and explain how momentum reduces sampling noise while tracking changing gradients. Controlled numerical experiments validate these mechanisms, and a preliminary LLM pretraining study illustrates the practical promise of fast-slow momentum.

</details>


### [269] [ConsMSA: Semantic Distribution Consistency Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31731)
*Pan Wang,Lipeng Ke,Huajun Ying,Pritish Mohapatra,Rohan Sarkar,Suresh Lakhani,Sankar Venkataraman,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConsMSA，将跨模态与模态内的语义分布一致性显式形式化为一致性分数I²CS，并据此实现正则化、token重加权与冗余token剪枝，在多模态情感分析中同时取得SOTA精度与高效压缩鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始多模态序列存在语义不一致（模态内冗余、跨模态冲突），既削弱鲁棒理解又增加计算开销；现有方法缺乏对"语义分布一致性"的显式建模。

Method: 将多模态token特征投影到共享情感空间，计算模态内与模态间一致性分数I²CS；将该分数与预测相关性耦合形成一致性感知重要性信号，用于三点：(i)作为一致性正则项对齐训练中的隐分布；(ii)推导语义感知权重进行自适应token重加权；(iii)作为剪枝准则剔除冗余或冲突token。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI、CH-SIMS上达到SOTA；在激进token压缩下仍保持鲁棒，仅保留10% token即可获得可比精度。

Conclusion: 语义分布一致性可作为协同提升预测鲁棒性与计算效率的有效基础范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to predict human sentiments by integrating signals from different modalities such as text, video, and audio. However, raw multimodal sequences often suffer from semantic inconsistency--exhibiting redundancy or conflicts within and across modalities--which hinders robust understanding and increases computational cost. To this end, we introduce ConsMSA, which explicitly formalizes semantic distribution consistency across both $\textit{intra}$- and $\textit{inter}$-modality, providing a consistency-aware mechanism for robust and efficient multimodal sentiment prediction. Specifically, ConsMSA projects multimodal token features into a shared sentiment space to compute an Intra- and Inter-modality Consistency Score ($I^2CS$). By coupling this score with predictive relevance, we formulate consistency-aware importance signals that are utilized: (i) as a consistency regularizer to align latent distributions during training, (ii) to derive semantic-aware weights for adaptive multimodal token reweighting, and (iii) as a practical criterion to prune redundant or conflicting tokens. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS demonstrate that ConsMSA achieves state-of-the-art performance while remaining robust under aggressive token compression--retaining only 10% of tokens yields comparable accuracy. These results establish semantic distribution consistency as a promising foundation for synergizing predictive robustness with computational efficiency.

</details>


### [270] [Learning to Be Monitored: Causal Bounds and Intervention-Aware Post-Training Recipes for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31733)
*Qiang Fu,Xinyue Li,Andre Wibisono,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 会高估监控器检测由干预实际导致的目标行为的频率，将其确立为因果检测率上界并给出互补下界，形成更紧的 CoT 可监控性评估框架；并构建配对控制-干预训练集，用离策略自蒸馏、强化学习和在策略自蒸馏三种方法训练模型以提升可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于干预的 CoT 监控评估通常采用最小准则灵敏度 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 衡量监控器能否从 CoT 中识别干预的作用，但干预条件下观察到的目标行为未必由干预引起，因此该指标可能高估监控器对"真正由干预引起的行为"的检测率，需要更严谨的评估框架。

Method: 1) 将 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 形式化证明为目标行为因果检测率的上界，并推导互补下界，构建更紧的评估框架；2) 构建涵盖多种干预类型的配对"控制-干预"任务训练数据集；3) 基于离策略自蒸馏、强化学习与在策略自蒸馏三种策略实现干预特异性的训练方法，为每个基座模型训练对应变体。

Result: 在多种模型-监控器配置下，三种训练方法在扩展评估框架中总体优于对应基座模型，提升 CoT 可监控性；原有 $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ 指标存在高估因果检测率的偏差。

Conclusion: 因果视角下的上下界框架能更准确地评估 CoT 可监控性；LLM 可通过针对性训练（自蒸馏与强化学习）主动提升其 CoT 被监控的程度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring aims to detect potentially harmful behaviors in large language models (LLMs) from their CoTs. Intervention-based evaluations measure this property by asking whether a monitor can detect the role of an intervention from the CoT when the targeted behavior occurs, typically using minimal-criterion sensitivity ($\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$) proposed by~\citep{guan2025monitoring}. However, a target behavior observed under intervention is not necessarily caused by it, and we show that this metric can overestimate how often a monitor detects target behaviors actually caused by the intervention. Specifically, we establish $\mathsf{TPR_{mc}}$ as an upper bound on this causal detection rate and derive a complementary lower bound, which provides a tighter evaluation framework for CoT monitorability. With this framework, we study whether LLMs can be trained to make their CoTs easier to monitor. We first construct a training dataset of paired control--intervention tasks spanning multiple intervention types. Using the dataset, we design and apply three intervention-specific training methods based on off-policy self-distillation, reinforcement learning, and on-policy self-distillation to train separate variants of each base model. Across various model-monitor configurations, all three methods generally improve CoT monitorability over the corresponding base models under our extended evaluation framework. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyue-lxy/learning-to-be-monitored.

</details>


### [271] [Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Model Inference via Per-Query Modality Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31768)
*Shuqi Liu,Yuzhou Cao,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究全模态大模型的按查询模态选择问题，将其建模为精度-成本优化，采用成本敏感学习实现自适应模态选择，在WorldSense上以更低成本取得不劣于全模态的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大模型虽可联合处理文本、音频、视频等模态以增强理解与推理，但推理成本高；且额外模态带来的收益随查询而异，固定模态配置效率低下。

Method: 保持底层Omni-LLM参数固定，仅对每个查询自适应选择输入模态；将模态选择形式化为精度-成本优化问题，并提出成本敏感学习方法求解。

Result: 在WorldSense数据集上，基于Qwen2.5-Omni与Qwen3-Omni，在输入token、延迟、货币成本三种度量下，按查询模态选择均持续改善精度-成本折中，甚至优于提供全部模态的标准设置；Qwen2.5-Omni下达到或超过全模态精度，仅需约41%输入token、39%延迟、61%货币成本。

Conclusion: 按查询的模态选择是提升Omni-LLM推理效率的有效手段，可在显著降低多维度推理成本的同时保持甚至提升精度。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) can jointly process a broad range of modalities, such as text, audio, and video, enabling stronger multimodal understanding and reasoning capabilities, but often at substantial inference cost. We observe that the benefit of additional modalities varies across queries, indicating that a fixed modality configuration can be inefficient. Motivated by this, we study query-wise modality selection, which keeps the underlying Omni-LLM fixed while adaptively selecting which modalities to provide for each query. We formulate the problem as an accuracy-cost optimization problem and develop a cost-sensitive learning approach for modality selection. We evaluate our approach on WorldSense using Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni under three inference-cost measures: input tokens, latency, and monetary cost. Across both models, query-wise modality selection consistently improves the accuracy-cost trade-off and can even outperform the standard setting in which all available modalities are provided to the Omni-LLM. For Qwen2.5-Omni, our cost-sensitive approach matches or exceeds this full-modality accuracy while requiring only approximately 41% of the input tokens, 39% of the latency, and 61% of the monetary cost.

</details>


### [272] [Benchmarks Are Surprisingly Compaction-Friendly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31778)
*Jeanne Salle,Hardik Bhatnagar,Matthias Bethge,Sahar Abdelnabi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了长程智能体任务中的"上下文压缩（compaction）"问题：固定OpenCode脚手架、实现五种压缩策略、在三个前沿模型与三个长程基准上评测，发现压缩阈值对百万级上下文模型几乎无影响，无策略优于默认自总结，但压缩会静默丢失约三分之一的任务约束（反作弊规则），且用户会话中"如何工作/禁止事项"类指令被丢弃的频率约为"要构建什么"规格的6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务已超出LLM上下文窗口，脚手架必须通过上下文压缩腾出空间，而压缩本身是有损的，可能静默丢失任务约束、指令与早期推理依据。压缩虽是编码智能体的标准组件，却缺乏在前沿模型、真实长程任务上的系统研究——已有工作仅在玩具/合成轨迹上评测较小的开放权重模型。

Method: 固定同一智能体脚手架OpenCode，在其内部实现五种压缩策略；在三个长程基准（PaperBench、ProgramBench、PostTrainBench）上评测三个前沿模型，覆盖不同上下文窗口规模；另在SWE-chat的真实用户-智能体会话上分析压缩对指令的保留情况。

Result: (1) 两个1M上下文模型的任务性能对压缩阈值不敏感；上下文窗口最小的GPT-5.3-Codex在较低阈值下PaperBench表现变差，PostTrainBench上趋势不明显。(2) 没有任何压缩方法优于默认自总结；丢弃内容更多的策略可使部分模型下降最多20分，而另一些模型不变。(3) 压缩会静默删除约束：ProgramBench上首次压缩摘要平均即丢失约三分之一反作弊规则，且随压缩次数单调增加；SWE-chat真实会话中，关于"如何工作、不应做什么"的指令被丢弃频率约为"构建什么"的规格的6倍。

Conclusion: 现有基准是"压缩友好"的：目标固定且智能体进度保存在磁盘上，压缩丢弃的信息大多可恢复，唯独那些不存在于其他地方的约束无法恢复。因此真正需要关注的是约束丢失而非性能下降，未来基准应显式评测约束保持能力。

Abstract: Long horizon agentic tasks are now pushing LLM agents past their context window. To continue the task, the agent scaffold has to free up the context window, a process known as context compaction. Compaction is generally lossy by design: agents can silently lose task constraints, instructions, or their reasoning behind earlier decisions. Although compaction is a standard component of coding agent scaffolds, it has not been studied systematically with frontier models on realistic, long horizon agentic tasks. Previous work evaluate smaller open weight models on toy or synthetic trajectories. We fix the agent scaffold (OpenCode), implement five compaction strategies inside it, and evaluate three frontier models on three long horizon benchmarks (PaperBench, ProgramBench, PostTrainBench). For the two 1M context models, the compaction threshold does not measurably affect the task performance, whereas GPT-5.3-Codex, the model with the smallest context window, performs worse at lower thresholds on PaperBench and, less clearly, on PostTrainBench. No compaction method outperforms default self-summarization, and methods that discard more content cost some models up to 20 points while leaving other models the same. Compaction can, however, silently remove task constraints: on ProgramBench, the first compaction summary already drops about one third of the task's anticheating rules on average, and this rises monotonically with the number of compactions. On real user-agent sessions from SWE-chat, instructions about how the agent should work and what it should not do are dropped around 6 times more than specifications of what to build. We argue that current benchmarks are compaction-friendly: i.e., the objective is fixed and the agent's progress stays on disk, so most of what compaction discards can be recovered, except constraints that don't exist anywhere else.

</details>


### [273] [U-NeXt: General-purpose event detection for neonatal EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32811)
*Robert Hogan,Sean R. Mathieson,Aurel Luca,Soraia Ventura,Sean Griffin,Geraldine B. Boylan,John M. O'Toole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于语义分割的通用新生儿EEG事件检测框架U-NeXt，在低电压间期、伪迹和癫痫三类事件上均优于ConvNeXt，并在独立数据集达到专家级癫痫检测水平且推理速度大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿EEG事件（低电压间期、伪迹、癫痫发作）检测缺乏统一框架，任务专用分类器泛化性与效率受限，需兼顾时序上下文保持、跨事件类型通用性及临床可部署的推理效率。

Method: 将事件检测建模为语义分割：卷积编码器-解码器以长输入片段直接对单通道逐点分割；共享架构与训练框架，分别使用含51、98、278名新生儿、超过11,000小时EEG、82,000个标注事件的三类数据集；与任务专用ConvNeXt分类器对比，并在Helsinki与Cork两个独立留出发作数据集（130名新生儿、三位专家标注）上评估U-NeXt+。

Result: U-NeXt在三类事件上均提升检测性能，短事件灵敏度增益尤为明显；U-NeXt+在Helsinki与Cork上MCC分别为0.862与0.891，每小时发作负荷相关性较ConvNeXt提升30-40%，达专家级标准（p<0.001）；批次推理时间在CPU上缩短254倍、GPU上缩短200倍。

Conclusion: 统一分割框架可跨多种新生儿EEG事件类型提升检测性能并显著降低推理时间；准确的发作负荷估计与接近专家审阅者的表现支持其用于连续监测与临床决策支持。

Abstract: We developed U-NeXt, a general framework for neonatal electroencephalography (EEG) event detection based on semantic segmentation. Its convolutional encoder-decoder directly segments individual channels using long input segments to preserve temporal context. A shared architecture and training framework were developed using low-voltage interval, artefact, and seizure datasets containing 51, 98, and 278 neonates, respectively, comprising over 11,000 hours of EEG and 82,000 annotated events. Performance was compared with task-specific ConvNeXt classifiers. For seizure detection, we also evaluated U-NeXt+, trained on an expanded dataset. Both U-NeXt variants and the ConvNeXt baseline were evaluated on two independent held-out seizure datasets, Helsinki and Cork, comprising 130 neonates, with recordings annotated by three experts. U-NeXt improved detection across all three event types, with particular gains in sensitivity for short events. U-NeXt+ achieved Matthews correlation coefficients of 0.862 and 0.891 on Helsinki and Cork, respectively, and improved hourly seizure burden correlations by 30-40% relative to ConvNeXt. U-NeXt+ met the criterion for expert-level performance on both datasets (p < 0.001). In separate computational benchmarks, U-NeXt reduced batch inference time relative to ConvNeXt by 254-fold on a central processing unit and 200-fold on a graphics processing unit. A common segmentation framework improves detection across diverse neonatal EEG event types while substantially reducing inference time. Accurate seizure burden estimates and performance comparable to expert reviewers support its potential use in continuous monitoring and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [274] [Not All Retained Clients Are Equal: Client Selection for Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31786)
*Thanh Linh Nguyen,Quoc-Viet Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDF：一种基于路径依赖的联邦遗忘客户端选择框架，仅用top-k客户端少量轮重训练即可实现有效遗忘与高保留效用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法将遗忘视为后验、无状态操作，忽视客户端贡献的路径依赖性（关键学习期CLP中贡献影响不成比例且持久）；同时知识重叠与客户端间歇可用使依赖全部保留客户端低效且不现实

Method: 训练期间维护轻量、目标无关、CLP加权的历史，记录各客户端累积更新方向及与全局模型的对齐度；遗忘请求到来时据此识别并排序"全局信息量大但与目标耦合弱"的保留客户端，仅对top-k客户端进行少量轮次的重训练式遗忘

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100的IID与非IID设置下：非IID CIFAR-10上遗忘准确率达0%，MIA AUC接近重训练水平；CIFAR-100上保留准确率较最强基线最高提升6.91个百分点；相比Retrain通信降低约69%、计算降低57–61%

Conclusion: 保留客户端对遗忘的价值并不均等，路径依赖的客户端选择可在显著减少参与客户端与通信/计算开销的同时实现有效遗忘与强保留效用

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) aims to remove a designated target client's influence from a trained model without the prohibitive cost of retraining from scratch. Existing methods largely treat unlearning as a post-hoc, stateless operation, overlooking that client influence is path-dependent: contributions made during training, particularly during critical learning periods (CLPs), can have disproportionate and persistent effects. Moreover, knowledge overlap and intermittent client availability make relying on all retained clients inefficient and impractical. We introduce PDF, a path-dependent client-selection framework based on the insight that retained clients are not equally valuable for unlearning. During training, PDF maintains lightweight, target-agnostic, CLP-weighted histories summarizing each client's accumulated update direction and global-model alignment. Upon an unlearning request, these histories identify and rank retained clients that are globally informative yet weakly coupled to the target, enabling retraining-based unlearning with only the top-$k$ clients over a small number of rounds. Experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 under IID and non-IID settings show that PDF achieves effective forgetting and strong retained utility with substantially fewer retained clients. In particular, PDF achieves $0\%$ forgetting accuracy with retraining-like MIA AUC on non-IID CIFAR-10, improves retained accuracy by up to $6.91$ percentage points over the strongest competing FU baseline on CIFAR-100, and reduces communication by approximately $69\%$ and computation by $57$--$61\%$ relative to Retrain.

</details>


### [275] [PaperScorer: How Predictable Is Paper Acceptance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31791)
*Sachin Konan,Jonathan Liu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PaperScorer，仅凭论文本身（文本或页面截图）预测同行评审的录用/拒稿决定，视觉模型准确率最高（ICLR 68%、arXiv ML 77%），置信度良好校准，但中等评分论文难以预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究常以基准分数衡量，但研究价值难以简化为单一指标；人类通过同行评审判断整体贡献，而评审噪声大、因素多、难以建模。作者追问：仅给定论文，能否直接预测其同行评审决定。

Method: 构建 PaperScorer 模型族，在去除作者身份与排版"捷径"后仅以决定标签训练，输入为抽取的文本或页面截图；对比视觉模型、纯文本模型与前沿推理模型。

Result: 视觉模型预测力最强（ICLR 68%、arXiv ML 77%），优于纯文本与前沿推理模型；信号稳健，小模型亦可、甚至整篇压缩为单图仍有效；对最低与最高评分论文最准，中等评分论文难以预测（前沿系统亦然）；置信度经过校准，最有把握的预测也最准确。

Conclusion: PaperScorer 揭示了论文本身承载了多少评审决定信息，是迈向 AI 驱动研究评估的第一步。

Abstract: Automated research is largely evaluated by benchmark performance, but the value of research rarely reduces to a single score. Humans judge a work's holistic contribution through peer review, which is hard to model because it is noisy and depends on many factors. However, what if, given only the paper, we could predict its peer-review decision directly? We introduce PaperScorer, a family of models that predict a paper's accept or reject decision from its extracted text or page screenshots, training only on the decision after removing author identity and formatting shortcuts. Vision models are the most predictive (68% accuracy on ICLR papers and 77% on ML papers from arXiv), beating text-only and frontier reasoning models. Unlike plain text, page images retain figures, layout, and presentation. This signal is robust, surviving smaller models and even compressing the whole paper into a single image. But accurate predictions are not evenly spread. PaperScorer is strongest on the lowest and highest rated papers, while papers rated in the middle stay unpredictable for our models and frontier systems alike. Additionally, its confidence is calibrated, so its most confident predictions are also its most accurate. Overall, PaperScorer shows how much of the decision a paper carries on its own, marking a first step toward AI-driven research evaluation.

</details>


### [276] [CuraLoc: Repository-Level Fault Localization via Planned Code Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31809)
*Sangeeta Warrier,Anush Sankaran,Ryen White*

Main category: cs.LG
