Result: 对任意区间 $[a,b]$ 取得数据依赖遗憾 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt{(D^2+DP_{[a,b]})\sum_{t\in[a,b]}(\|g_t\|^2+\lambda_t\|g_t\|)}\big)$；首次建立延迟反馈下的强自适应保证；在带时变记忆的 OCO 中给出优于已知最优界的强自适应保证。

Conclusion: 带移动成本的睡眠专家归约为强自适应分析提供了统一而有效的工具，兼具独立理论价值，并自然延伸到延迟反馈与时变记忆等扩展场景，改进了现有最优界。

Abstract: We study strongly adaptive regret for Online Convex Optimization (OCO) with time-varying movement costs, a setting that evaluates a learner's performance over arbitrary sub-intervals. This framework captures complex sequential decision-making problems where both the environment and the cost of switching decisions fluctuate over time. We propose a novel algorithm which, for any interval $[a,b] \subseteq [T]$, achieves a data-dependent regret bound of $ \widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( \sqrt{(D^2+DP_{[a,b]})\sum_{t \in [a,b]}(\|g_t\|^2+λ_t\|g_t\|)} \right) $, where $D$ is the diameter of the decision domain, $P_{[a,b]}$ is the path length of the comparator sequence over $[a,b]$, $g_t$ is the gradient of the loss, and $λ_t$ is the movement-cost coefficient at round $t$. Key to our approach is a novel reduction to sleeping experts with movement costs, a previously unexplored variant that is of independent interest. As a result of this development, we further establish the first strongly-adaptive guarantees under delayed feedback. Our approach also naturally extends to OCO with time-varying memory, where we obtain strongly-adaptive guarantees which improve the best-known bounds.

</details>


### [348] [On the Identifiability of Hybrid Deep Generative Models: Meta-Learning as a Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32748)
*Yubo Ye,Maryam Toloubidokhti,Sumeet Vadhavkar,Xiajun Jiang,Huafeng Liu,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上探讨混合深度生成模型（hybrid-DGM）的可辨识性，指出采用无条件先验的现有hybrid-DGM不可辨识，并提出基于元学习（meta-learning）的元表述来构造可理论证明可辨识的hybrid-DGM，在合成与真实数据基准上给出了实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将物理归纳偏置引入深度生成模型已催生hybrid-DGM，这类模型需要同时推断物理组件参数与神经组件参数，但其可辨识性尚未被理论检验或确立；同时需厘清一般DGM不可辨识性理论对hybrid-DGM的适用性。

Method: 给出hybrid-DGM可辨识性的首个理论分析，将一般DGM的不可辨识性结论推广至hybrid-DGM场景，并引入元学习构建元表述（meta-formulation）的hybrid-DGM以实现可证明的可辨识性。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据基准上，实证表明采用无条件先验的现有hybrid-DGM确实不可辨识，而所提出的元表述具有强可辨识性。

Conclusion: 无条件先验是现有hybrid-DGM不可辨识的根源，元学习表述可作为构造可辨识hybrid-DGM的有效方案，为物理-神经混合生成建模提供理论保障。

Abstract: The interest in leveraging physics-based inductive bias in deep learning has resulted in recent development of hybrid deep generative models (hybrid-DGMs) that integrate known physics-based mathematical expressions in neural generative models. To identify these hybrid-DGMs requires inferring parameters of the physics-based component along with their neural component. The identifiability of these hybrid-DGMs, however, has not yet been theoretically probed or established. How does the existing theory of the un-identifiability of general DGMs apply to hybrid-DGMs? What may be an effective approach to construct a hybrid-DGM with theoretically-proven identifiability? This paper provides the first theoretical probe into the identifiability of hybrid-DGMs, and presents meta-learning as a novel solution to construct identifiable hybrid-DGMs. On synthetic and real-data benchmarks, we provide strong empirical evidence for the un-identifiability of existing hybrid-DGMs using unconditional priors, and strong identifiability results of the presented meta-formulations of hybrid-DGMs. Code is available at https://github.com/GoForIt-YYB/Meta-Hybrid-VAE.

</details>


### [349] [Direction-Aware Token Credit Assignment with Reward-Model Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32772)
*Yikuan Hu,Jifeng Zhu,Minle Su,Jiaqin Yue,Gujie Shao,Yanzhe Chen,Haoxuan Jia,Haoran Xu,Wangzhou Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方向感知的Token信用分配方法DATC：将冻结奖励模型梯度映射到策略logit空间，依据奖励方向与GRPO带符号采样动作更新方向的对齐程度分配token权重，同时保持response级优势的符号与均值，在两个模型族共四个设置上均优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO仅在response级别共享优势，未解决每个token应多大程度参与学习的问题。共享优势诱导的更新会在每个位置强化或抑制被采样token，但这些更新并不总指向token级别奖励的提升，因此需要更合理的token级信用度量。

Method: 以"功"为类比：功取决于力沿位移方向的分量，token信用应反映奖励沿策略更新方向的局部变化。DATC将冻结奖励模型的梯度映射到策略logit空间，计算奖励方向与GRPO带符号采样动作更新方向之间的对齐度来分配token权重，并约束权重使response级优势的符号和均值保持不变。

Result: 在两个模型族的全部四个设置中，DATC均优于GRPO。

Conclusion: 基于方向对齐的token级信用分配是对response级共享优势的有效改进，可在不改变整体优势语义的前提下提升策略优化效果。

Abstract: GRPO shares a response-level advantage across tokens, leaving unresolved how strongly each token should contribute to learning. Updates induced by the shared advantage reinforce or suppress the sampled token at each position, but these updates do not always point toward increasing reward at the token level. This motivates a work-based view of credit: just as work depends on the component of force along a displacement, token credit should reflect the local reward change along the policy update direction. We introduce Direction-Aware Token Credit Assignment (DATC), which maps frozen reward-model gradients into policy logit space and allocates weights by evaluating the alignment between the reward direction and GRPO's signed sampled-action update direction, while preserving the sign and mean of the response-level advantage. DATC outperforms GRPO in all four settings across two model families.

</details>


### [350] [Deep Learning-Based Prediction of HER2 Status from Whole-Brain MRI in Breast Cancer Brain Metastases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32774)
*Sara Early,Chris Kang,Vibujithan Vigneshwaran,Kimberly Amador,Roger Y. Tsang,Nils D. Forkert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用深度学习方法，直接从增强T1加权全脑MRI预测乳腺癌脑转移患者的HER2状态，并评估显式肿瘤分割信息是否提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HER2状态是转移性乳腺癌预后判断与治疗决策的关键生物标志物，若能通过无创影像无创预测，可减少对组织活检的依赖。作者旨在探究结构MRI是否携带足够信号，以及显式分割是否必要。

Method: 基于公开BCBM-Radiogenomics数据集，纳入135例患者（70例HER2+、65例HER2-）。比较三种方法：传统影像组学模型、仅用T1w的简单全卷积网络（SFCN）、以及将专家勾画肿瘤掩膜作为额外输入通道的SFCN。

Result: 影像组学模型ROC-AUC为0.602（95%CI 0.528–0.677）；仅T1w的SFCN为0.667（95%CI 0.63–0.71）；加入显式肿瘤掩膜后提升至0.692（95%CI 0.58–0.81）。

Conclusion: 各方法性能均较有限，说明仅凭结构MRI预测HER2状态存在较大难度；无分割与掩膜引导SFCN之间的差异提示，深度学习模型所捕获的预测信号很大程度上可直接从全脑T1w中习得，无需显式肿瘤勾画。结果支持进一步研究无分割深度学习作为BCBM中HER2状态判定的无创辅助策略。

Abstract: Purpose: Human epidermal growth factor receptor 2 (HER2) status is a key biomarker relevant for prognostication and treatment decision making in metastatic breast cancer. Thus, the aim of this work was to explore whether HER2 status can be predicted directly from whole-brain post-contrast T1-weighted (T1w) MRI scans in breast cancer brain metastases (BCBM) using deep learning, and whether providing explicit tumour segmentation information improves prediction.
  Methods: Using the publicly available BCBM-Radiogenomics dataset, we analysed 135 patients, including 70 HER2+ and 65 HER2- patients. Three approaches were implemented and evaluated: a conventional radiomics model, a simple fully convolutional network (SFCN) using T1w MRI scans alone, and an SFCN using T1w MRI scans with an expert-derived tumour mask as an additional input channel.
  Results: The radiomics model achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.602 (95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.528--0.677) while the SFCN achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.667 (95% CI, 0.63--0.71) using T1w MRI scans alone, which increased to 0.692 (95% CI, 0.58--0.81) when an explicit tumour mask was additionally provided.
  Conclusion: However, the modest performance across all approaches demonstrates the difficulty of predicting HER2 status from structural MRI scans alone. The difference between the segmentation-free and mask-guided SFCN models suggests that much of the predictive signal captured by the deep-learning model may be learned directly from whole-brain T1w MRI without requiring explicit tumour delineation. These findings support further investigation of segmentation-free deep-learning approaches as a potential non-invasive strategy to support HER2 status determination in BCBM.

</details>


### [351] [CARES: Coverage-Aware Replay Exemplar Selection for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32799)
*Arundhati S. Shanbhag,Brian B. Moser,Federico Raue,Tobias C. Nauen,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARES：一种无需持续模型优化的回放缓冲区构建方法，利用冻结预训练特征空间的几何结构，以类感知、密度加权的覆盖度选取样本，在不使用持续学习器训练信号的前提下实现SOTA级精度并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于回放的持续学习在固定内存预算下，缓冲区组成至关重要。现有基于coreset的回放方法依赖模型导出的梯度、损失或辅助优化来提升缓冲区质量，但内存反复更新带来巨大计算开销。作者希望在不做模型优化的情况下获得高质量缓冲区。

Method: CARES采用与持续模型优化无关的缓冲区构建策略：在冻结的预训练特征空间中，基于类感知（class-aware）与密度加权（density-weighted）的覆盖度度量选择样本，无需梯度、损失或辅助优化等训练信号。评估覆盖平衡、不平衡和标签噪声三种数据流，以及在线类增量与任务增量两种设定。

Result: 在Tiny-ImageNet上，CARES比现有基于coreset的选择器快2.3–8.8倍；且在平衡、不平衡与标签噪声流上均处于SOTA水平，类不平衡下比最强基线最高提升7.58个百分点，并在Tiny-ImageNet上取得更高平均精度。

Conclusion: 利用冻结预训练特征的几何结构可在不使用持续学习器训练信号的情况下构建高质量回放缓冲区，兼顾效率与精度，为coreset式回放提供了一条低成本替代路径。

Abstract: Replay-based Continual Learning mitigates catastrophic forgetting by keeping a buffer of past samples, making buffer composition critical under a fixed memory budget. Recent coreset-based replay methods improve buffer quality using model-derived gradients, losses, or auxiliary optimization, but incur substantial computational overhead as memory is repeatedly updated. We propose CARES, a continual model-optimization-free approach for buffer construction that instead exploits the geometry of a frozen pretrained feature space using class-aware, density-weighted coverage. We evaluate CARES across balanced, imbalanced, and label-noisy streams under online class- and task-incremental settings. On Tiny-ImageNet, CARES is 2.3 to 8.8 times faster than existing coreset-based selectors. Although CARES achieves this speedup using no training signal from the continual learner, it ranks among the state of the art across balanced, imbalanced, and label-noisy streams under online class- and task-incremental settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 7.58 percentage points under class imbalance while also attaining higher average accuracy on Tiny-ImageNet. Code will be released upon publication

</details>


### [352] [Multiplicative vs. Additive State Coupling in Learned Optimizer Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32801)
*M. Harper Langston,Pierre-David Letourneau,Dalton Jones,Roland Memisevic,Peter Scott,Mingu Lee,Richard A. Lethin,Christopher Lott,Fatih Porikli,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过展开经典求解器并反向传播来学习 MAX-CUT 连续惩罚松弛的调度（schedule）；论文区分"是否利用求解器状态"与"是否具有状态自适应性"，指出双线性（乘性）状态注入结构是分布外迁移的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多以手工设计的调度求解 MAX-CUT 松弛，或仅关注调度是否对状态自适应；但尚不清楚调度结构与求解器状态的交互方式（乘性 vs 加性）是否才是决定分布外泛化能力的因素。作者希望在无正则化、相同输入与输出头的受控条件下隔离这一架构差异。

Method: 对 MAX-CUT 的连续惩罚松弛，将经典求解器逐步展开（unrolled），通过反向传播端到端学习调度。构建两类调度：状态盲（state-blind）调度与状态自适应双线性（bilinear）调度；并设置加性（additive）消融模型作为对照——其输入与输出头完全一致，仅在分布内与双线性模型不可区分。实验在 15 个随机种子上评估、比较各模型相对其未训练基线的性能，并进行 30 组权重衰减扫描。

Result: 状态盲调度可泛化到远大于训练规模的实例；双线性调度在分布外同样泛化，并在 15 个种子上优于未训练基线（t=4.1）；加性消融在分布内与双线性模型统计不可区分，却在分布外劣于自身基线（t=-11.7）。该分离在无正则化下即出现，并在 30 组权重衰减扫描中持续存在：双线性优势在所有测试值上均显著，加性模型始终未能恢复。

Conclusion: 在该实验设定下，决定分布外迁移的架构区别在于求解器状态如何进入调度（乘性/双线性注入），而非仅仅"是否状态自适应"。加性注入的结构性缺陷无法通过权重衰减调参弥补。

Abstract: We learn schedules for a continuous penalty relaxation of MAX-CUT by backpropagating through an unrolled classical solver. A state-blind schedule generalizes to instances substantially larger than those used for training. A state-adaptive bilinear schedule also generalizes out of distribution, outperforming its untrained baseline across 15 seeds ($t=4.1$). By contrast, an additive ablation with identical inputs and output heads is statistically indistinguishable from the bilinear model in distribution but performs worse than its own baseline out of distribution ($t=-11.7$). This separation appears without regularization and persists across a $30$ round weight-decay sweep: the bilinear advantage remains significant at every tested value, whereas the additive model never recovers. The results in this setting isolate how solver state enters the schedule, rather than state adaptivity alone, as the architectural distinction associated with out-of-distribution transfer.

</details>


### [353] [On the Limits of Sparse Linear Model with Shuffled Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32822)
*Yan Zhang,Jiong Tuo,Meng Ding,Yuheng Ma,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究稀疏线性回归在Shuffled DP（SDP）下的非交互与交互协议：非交互协议存在维度障碍（下界Ω(√(dk)/(nε))，上界Õ(√(k/n)+d√k/(nε))）；提出两轮交互用户级算法达到Õ(√(k/(nm))+k/(n√mε))，无d的多项式依赖，并通过扩展score attack证明其近最优（仅差log(d/k)因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏线性回归是高维统计的基本问题，其差分隐私版本在中心模型与本地模型之间存在巨大精度差距。SDP介于两者之间，可在无需中心 curator 信任的前提下接近中心模型的精度，但该模型下稀疏线性回归的理论刻画尚不完善。

Method: 分别研究非交互与交互式SDP协议：为非交互协议给出下界并通过算法设计给出匹配上界；针对用户级交互设置，设计两轮协议——先以heavy hitter步骤恢复支撑集，再进行参数估计；同时将score attack框架推广到用户级数据以导出下界。

Result: 非交互(ε,δ)-SDP的ℓ2误差下界为Ω(√(dk)/(nε))，揭示固有维度障碍，而上界为Õ(√(k/n)+d√k/(nε))；交互用户级两轮算法误差Õ(√(k/(nm))+k/(n√mε))，对d无多项式依赖；任意用户级(ε,δ)-DP下界为Ω(√(k log(d/k)/(nm))+k log(d/k)/(n√mε))。

Conclusion: 交互式用户级SDP可突破非交互协议的维度障碍；所提两轮算法与下界在log(d/k)因子内匹配，达到近最优，为SDP下稀疏线性回归的精度极限提供了较完整的上下界刻画。

Abstract: Sparse linear regression is a fundamental problem in high-dimensional statistics, and its private counterparts leave a large gap between the central and the local model of differential privacy. Shuffled differential privacy (SDP) sits between the two, offering accuracy closer to the central model without the trust requirement of a central curator. This paper studies sparse linear regression under both non-interactive and interactive SDP protocols. In the non-interactive setting, any non-interactive $(ε,δ)$-SDP protocol is shown to incur an $\ell_2$-norm estimation error of $Ω( \frac{\sqrt{dk}}{nε} )$ with dimension $d$, sparsity $k$, and $n$ users, revealing an inherent dimensionality barrier. In addition, a non-interactive algorithm is proposed that achieves an $\ell_2$-norm upper bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( \sqrt{\frac{k}{n}} + \frac{d\sqrt{k}}{nε})$. To overcome this barrier, we turn to the interactive user-level setting, where each user holds $m$ samples. A two-round interactive algorithm is developed that first recovers the support through a heavy hitter step and then estimates the parameter, achieving an error of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( \sqrt{\frac{k}{nm}} + \frac{k}{n\sqrt{m}ε} )$ with no polynomial dependence on $d$. Furthermore, by extending the score attack framework to user-level data, a new lower bound $Ω( \sqrt{\frac{k\log(d/k)}{nm}} + \frac{k\log(d/k)}{n\sqrt{m}ε} )$ is established for any user-level $(ε,δ)$-DP, showing that the proposed algorithm is near-optimal with only a gap of the factor $\log(d/k)$.

</details>


### [354] [Neural LoFi with Backward Coupling: A Spectral Theory of Cross-Layer Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32849)
*Yatin Dandi,Matteo Vilucchio,Hugo Tabanelli,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 Neural LoFi 层wise 谱代理扩展到反馈耦合版本：从精确反向传播梯度推导神经元输入权重与下一层列的局部耦合，得到跨层相关算子的奇异模放大动力学，并提出"反向耦合"算法与函数空间变分原理，证明其可恢复前向统计量看不到的低层方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯层wise学习缺少反向传播中"后层重塑前层表征"的反馈机制，而现有 Neural LoFi 这类层wise谱代理无法刻画该跨层交互，因此需要构建能纳入反向耦合的谱理论模型。

Method: 从小初始化、平滑激活、固定环境的精确反向传播梯度出发，推导神经元入边权重与对应下一层列之间的局部耦合；在此受控区制下动力学放大跨层相关算子的奇异模，每个增长模通过共享神经元系数将一个早期特征与上层响应配对。据此提出反向耦合算法：上层敏感度生成样本相关的有效标签供前向谱步使用，修正后的特征再精化向上传播。并用函数空间变分原理（核范数约束下最大化特征-响应乘积的标签相关性）刻画被选中的特征-响应对；对三层网络导出滤波隐层特征、激活核与网络输出的自洽连续时间动力学。

Result: 在层次多指标模型中建立特征恢复分离性：反向耦合可恢复原始前向标签加权统计所不可见的低层方向；线性特化情形复现残差相关的奇异模动力学；配对模为联动低秩协方差分量提供与 rainbow network 图景一致的动力学机制。在二分类 CIFAR-10 上，全连接与卷积网络在同等宽度下准确率优于前向 Neural LoFi。

Conclusion: 反向耦合为层wise谱代理补上了关键反馈项，理论上解释了跨层配对特征的涌现与可恢复性优势，经验上带来一致精度提升，为理解联合训练中的特征学习提供了可分析的连续时间框架。

Abstract: During joint training, backpropagation allows later layers to reshape earlier representations, a feedback mechanism absent from purely layer-wise learning. We extend Neural Low-Degree Filtering (Neural LoFi), a recently proposed layer-wise spectral surrogate for feature learning, to capture this interaction. Starting from the exact backpropagation gradients, we derive a local coupling between a neuron's incoming weights and its corresponding next-layer column. In a controlled small-initialization regime with smooth activations and fixed surroundings, these dynamics amplify singular modes of a cross-layer correlation operator. Each growing mode pairs an earlier feature with an upper-layer response through shared neuron coefficients. This motivates a backward-coupling algorithm in which upper-layer sensitivities generate sample-dependent effective labels for earlier spectral steps, while corrected features refine upward transmission. A function-space variational principle characterizes the selected feature-response pairs by maximizing the label correlation of their product under kernel-norm constraints. For three-layer networks, we derive self-consistent continuous-time dynamics for the filtered hidden-layer features, the activation kernels, and the network output under the proposed backward coupling. In a hierarchical multi-index model, we establish a feature-recovery separation: backward coupling recovers lower-layer directions invisible to the original forward label-weighted statistic. The linear specialization recovers residual-dependent singular-mode dynamics, while paired modes provide a dynamical mechanism for linked low-rank covariance components consistent with the rainbow-network picture. Experiments with fully connected and convolutional networks on binary CIFAR-10 show improved accuracy over forward Neural LoFi at matched widths.

</details>


### [355] [Near-Optimal List Replicability from Littlestone Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32853)
*Ari Blondal,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可实现PAC学习框架下，证明具有Littlestone维数d与对偶VC维数v*的概念类H的列表可复制数满足LR(H)≤2^{O(d·min{log d, v*})}，指数级改进了此前2^{2^{O(d)}}的上界，并给出与精度ε无关的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前最好的上界为Bun、Livni与Moran [FOCS 2020] 给出的2^{2^{O(d)}}，与已知最坏情形下界2^{Ω(d)}在指数上存在巨大差距；此外，Ghazi等 [STOC 2021] 与Lyu [STOC 2026] 推出的2^{O_ε(d^2)}界依赖固定精度ε>0。本文旨在收紧上界、缩小与下界的差距，并消除对ε的依赖。

Method: 提出两种技术路线：(1) 建立新的归纳式框架来界定列表可复制数，基于差分隐私学习中近期提出的不可约性（irreducibility）概念，导出2^{O(d log d)}界；(2) 构造一个简单的全局稳定学习器，对多个经验风险最小化器（ERM）取多数投票，并借助稳定正则性（stable regularity）工具证明其稳定性，导出2^{O(d v*)}界。

Result: 对可数域上任意概念类H，证明LR(H)≤2^{O(d·min{log d, v*})}。该结果在指数上仅比最坏情形下界2^{Ω(d)}多一个对数因子，并将此前上界2^{2^{O(d)}}指数级改进；且该界与精度ε无关，优于此前依赖ε的2^{O_ε(d^2)}界。

Conclusion: 列表可复制数的上界可显著收紧至接近最优的2^{O(d log d)}量级，且与精度参数无关。归纳框架与多数投票式全局稳定学习器分别提供了两条互补的证明途径，其中不可约性与稳定正则性成为关键分析工具。

Abstract: We study the list replicability number in the realizable PAC learning framework and prove that every concept class $H$ over a countable domain with Littlestone dimension $d$ and dual VC dimension $v^*$ satisfies \[ \mathrm{LR}(H)\leq 2^{O(d\min\{\log d,v^*\})}. \] This matches the worst-case lower bound of $2^{Ω(d)}$ up to a logarithmic factor in the exponent and exponentially improves the previous best upper bound $\mathrm{LR}(H)\le 2^{2^{O(d)}}$ of Bun, Livni, and Moran [FOCS 2020]. Unlike earlier bounds of $2^{O_ε(d^2)}$ for fixed accuracy $ε>0$ implied by the work of Ghazi, Golowich, Kumar, and Manurangsi [STOC 2021] and Lyu [STOC 2026], our bound is independent of $ε$.
  The $2^{O(d\log d)}$ bound follows from a new inductive framework for bounding list replicability that builds on the recent notion of irreducibility from differentially private learning. The $2^{O(dv^*)}$ bound follows from a simple globally stable learner that takes the majority vote of multiple empirical risk minimizers, with stability established using tools from stable regularity.

</details>


### [356] [Interpretable sparse fine-tuning with TopKLoRA enables faithful circuit discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32866)
*Marek Masiak,Andrzej Szablewski,Nicola Cancedda,Constantin Venhoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TopKLoRA，一种按设计可解释的 LoRA 变体，每个 token 只激活 k 个最大的 adapter latent，仅用下游损失训练，从而获得功能上因果的稀疏电路。在 sleeper-agent 后门案例中，该方法可在 gemma-2-2b、Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B 上定位并消融实现后门的必要且充分电路，电路比稠密 LoRA 小一个数量级，可精准切除恶意能力而不损害指令跟随性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知来源数据微调可能因投毒样本植入后门，后门难以定位与移除且可穿透安全训练。参数高效微调虽捕获全部参数改变但缺乏可解释性；SAE 类方法并非直接面向下游任务优化。因此需要一种"按设计可解释"、其单元具有功能因果性的微调方法。

Method: 提出 TopKLoRA：在每个 token 上仅保留 k 个最大的 adapter latent 处于激活状态（top-k 稀疏门控），且完全在 downstream loss 上训练，使优化压力促使模型学习功能上因果的 latent。案例研究为 sleeper-agent 后门：在投毒的指令跟随数据集上微调 gemma-2-2b、Qwen2.5-1.5B 与 Qwen2.5-7B 构造 model organisms；随后进行电路搜索，识别并消融实现后门所必需且充分的电路；另提出利用 TopKLoRA latent 与 gradient-routing 构造具有局部化电路的 model organisms 的技术，用于验证电路搜索算法的发现。

Result: 对每个 model organism 均找到并消融了实现后门既必要又充分的电路；TopKLoRA 产生的稀疏电路比稠密 LoRA 基线小至多一个数量级，从而可对恶意能力进行"外科式"消融而不牺牲指令跟随性能；基于 latent 与梯度路由构造的局部化电路模型验证了电路搜索结论。

Conclusion: TopKLoRA 证明按设计可解释的架构能在不牺牲模型性能的前提下提供忠实的分析单元，是迈向可解释微调与可安全移除后门的一步；代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning on datasets of unknown origin can create a backdoor in the trained model when the data includes poisoned examples. Difficult to localise and remove, backdoors can persist through safety training. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods capture the full parameter changes introduced during training, they are not easily interpretable. We present the full method of TopKLoRA, extending the preliminary proposal of Masiak et al. (2025), an interpretable-by-design LoRA variant in which only the $k$ largest adapter latents are active at each token. Unlike SAEs, TopKLoRA is trained purely on downstream loss, where optimisation pressure incentivises learning functionally causal latents. We demonstrate this sparse LoRA adapter in a sleeper-agent backdoor case study where we create model organisms by fine-tuning gemma-2-2b, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-7B on a poisoned instruction-following dataset. For each organism, we identify and ablate a circuit that is both necessary and sufficient to implement the backdoor. Furthermore, our new fine-tuning method produces sparse circuits that are up to an order of magnitude smaller than a dense LoRA baseline, allowing surgical ablation of the malicious capability without sacrificing instruction-following performance. Finally, we propose a technique to construct model organisms with localised circuits using TopKLoRA latents and gradient-routing, which we use to validate the findings of our circuit search algorithm. We consider these findings as a step toward interpretable-by-design architectures that provide faithful units of analysis without compromising model performance. We share our code: https://github.com/interpretable-finetuning/TopKLoRA.

</details>


### [357] [Regularised RL improves sim-to-real transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32855)
*Danilo Vucetic,Mohamad H. Danesh,Florence Clerc,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究正则化强化学习在sim-to-real中的实际效果，提出基于soft mellowmax值函数的SMM-AC算法，实验表明其在真实机器人上回报更高、对观测/环境/动力学扰动的鲁棒性优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化RL虽具理论鲁棒性保证、有利sim-to-real迁移，但实践中PPO等弱正则算法仍是主流，正则化对迁移的实际收益缺乏刻画，因此需系统研究正则化对机器人性能的影响。

Method: 推导出Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic（SMM-AC），一种基于soft mellowmax值函数的新型正则化RL算法，具备强鲁棒性保证；在MuJoCo连续控制基准上与SAC等基线对比，并部署于真实机器人评估回报及对观测、环境、动力学扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MuJoCo上SMM-AC与SAC等基线相当；在真实机器人上SMM-AC回报更高，且对观测、环境和动力学扰动的鲁棒性均强于基线。

Conclusion: 正则化RL通过对sim-to-real gap及真实世界条件变化更好的鲁棒性，改善了真实场景的策略部署，为sim-to-real流程中选择算法提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) has provable robustness guarantees that should ease the transfer of policies from simulation to robots, known as sim-to-real. Yet, weakly regularised algorithms like Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) remain the de facto choice in sim-to-real pipelines, while the practical benefits of regularisation for transfer remain poorly characterised. In this work, we study the sim-to-real performance of commonly used RL algorithms, with the aim of characterising the effects of regularisation on robotic performance. To that end, we derive Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic (SMM-AC), a novel regularised RL algorithm based on the soft mellowmax value function, which carries strong robustness guarantees. On MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks, SMM-AC is competitive with baselines like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). On real robots, SMM-AC achieves higher return, while exhibiting greater robustness to perturbations in observations, the environment, and dynamics than baselines. Together, these results demonstrate that regularised RL improves real-world policy deployment through better robustness to the sim-to-real gap, and changing real-world conditions.

</details>


### [358] [Sustainability of TSFM Deployment and Pretraining: From Carbon Footprint Analysis to Mitigation via Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32859)
*Cécile Luc,Nathanaël Lemonnier,Ievgen Redko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以全部署生命周期的累积碳排放为视角，比较时间序列基础模型（TSFM）与需周期性重训的监督式独立模型的经济与碳成本，并提出基于无梯度剪枝准则的轻量化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM 零样本预测能力强，但预训练成本高昂；实践中缺乏"前期投入能否在完整部署周期内被摊销"的量化答案，尤其在碳排放可持续性维度上缺乏系统分析。

Method: 构建跨实际工业场景的累积碳成本模型，对比 Chronos-2 与经典监督模型的部署全生命周期成本；同时采用无梯度（gradient-free）剪枝准则移除单个编码器块（占参数 7.9%），评估其对 FLOPs、CO2 与延迟的影响。

Result: Chronos-2 在大型或高频查询部署中可在数月内摊销预训练成本，但在小型或低频查询场景下经典模型始终更便宜；剪枝后 FLOPs、CO2 排放与延迟一致降低 6–9%，在 25 项任务基准上零样本预测性能基本无损。

Conclusion: TSFM 的可持续性收益高度依赖部署规模与查询频率，需按场景选择方案；无梯度块剪枝可在几乎不损失零样本性能的前提下同步降低算力、碳排与延迟，是压缩 TSFM 自身成本的有效手段。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) offer strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities, but their high pretraining cost raises a practical sustainability question: does this upfront investment pay off over a full deployment lifecycle, compared to supervised standalone models that must be periodically retrained? We model the cumulative carbon cost of different approaches across a realistic industrial scenario: Chronos-2 amortizes its pretraining cost within months for large or frequently queried deployments, but a classical model remains cheaper indefinitely for small or rarely queried ones. For cases where reducing the TSFM's own cost matters, we show that removing a single encoder block (7.9% of parameters) via a gradient-free pruning criterion yields a consistent 6 to 9% reduction in FLOPs, CO2 emissions, and latency, at essentially no cost to zero-shot forecasting performance across a 25-task benchmark.

</details>


### [359] [PoKER: A Probability of Kill Estimation Rate Model for Air-to-Air Missiles Using Machine Learning on Stochastic Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32864)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Andre N. Costa,Diego Geraldo,Marcos R. O. A. Maximo,Takashi Yoneyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PoKER 概率模型，用机器学习预测超视距空战中的导弹杀伤概率，替代传统基于静态距离的 WEZ 分区模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Weapon Engagement Zone（WEZ）模型仅用最大、最小、不可逃逸距离等静态距离划分交战区，忽略目标随机机动行为与导弹脱靶量的不确定性，难以准确刻画实际杀伤概率，导致超视距（BVR）空战决策依据粗糙。

Method: 构建 PoKER 概率模型，引入目标随机行为与导弹脱靶距离的随机性建模，采用机器学习方法预测杀伤概率；模型输入涵盖战斗部杀伤力、目标与射手姿态朝向、以及具体交战条件等要素，对目标行为进行动态评估从而扩展交战分析能力。

Result: PoKER 能够动态评估目标行为，显著拓展交战分析的预测能力，给出交战动态的重要洞察并量化成功概率，相比静态距离分区方法提供更精细的杀伤概率估计。

Conclusion: PoKER 有望成为 BVR 空战飞行员的重要辅助工具，改善该特定作战域内的作战决策；但摘要未给出定量验证与实测对比数据，其精度与实用性仍需进一步实验评估。

Abstract: This work introduces PoKER, a novel probabilistic model engineered to optimize missile launch effectiveness in air-to-air scenarios, specifically within Beyond Visual Range (BVR) air combat. Unlike conventional Weapon Engagement Zone (WEZ) models that delineate zones based on static distances such as maximum, minimum, and no-escape ranges, PoKER applies machine learning to predict kill probabilities more accurately by integrating the stochastic behaviors of targets and missile miss distances. This model dynamically evaluates target behavior, greatly expanding the predictive capabilities of engagement analysis. By factoring in elements such as warhead lethality, target and shooter orientations, and the specific conditions of engagement, PoKER provides important insights into engagement dynamics and quantifies success probabilities. Consequently, it can potentially be an important tool for BVR air combat pilots, improving operational decision-making within this specialized combat domain.

</details>


### [360] [Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Predictions: A Bellman Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32887)
*Chenbei Lu,Zaiwei Chen,Tongxin Li,Chenye Wu,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究折扣有限MDP中"块反馈"协议下的多步转移预测价值：在每个K周期块开始观察部分动作的K步概率转移预测，块内可依状态反馈调整决策。证明最优值满足原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程，揭示预测信息通过递归改变Bellman展开中"最大化与期望"的局部顺序产生价值，并用Bellman–Jensen分析量化预测时域、误差、动作覆盖与块内反馈的影响。提出基于模型的BF–BOLA算法学习近优策略，给出上下样本复杂度界（在所述区间内对数因子内匹配），表明预测可将转移学习缩减到未覆盖行，且学习近优策略可能比估计贝叶斯值函数需要更少的预测样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步预测有望提升强化学习，但其价值难以量化；将预测直接纳入状态会导致状态空间指数增长，使直接动态规划代价高昂。因此需要一个不扩张状态空间、又能刻画并量化预测信息价值的理论框架与可行算法。

Method: 采用块反馈协议（每K周期块开始观察动作子集的K步概率转移预测，块内依状态反馈适应决策）；在折扣有限MDP上推导原状态空间的贝叶斯Bellman方程，刻画预测通过递归改变最大化与期望顺序产生价值；用Bellman–Jensen分析量化预测时域、误差、动作覆盖及块内反馈效应；提出模型型算法BF–BOLA（块反馈贝叶斯离线学习+在线适应），并建立样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 最优值可由原状态空间上的贝叶斯Bellman方程表示，避免状态空间指数增长；Bellman–Jensen分析给出各因素（时域、误差、动作覆盖、块内反馈）对预测价值的量化影响；BF–BOLA的上下样本复杂度界在所述区间内对数因子内匹配；预测可把转移学习限制到未被覆盖的行，且学习近优策略所需的预测样本可少于估计贝叶斯值函数所需样本。

Conclusion: 多步转移预测的价值可通过Bellman展开中最大化与期望顺序的局部改变来精确刻画，且无需扩张状态空间；在块反馈协议下，预测信息显著降低转移学习需求，近优策略学习与值函数估计存在样本复杂度差异。

Abstract: Multi-step predictions of future transition realizations can improve reinforcement learning, but their value remains difficult to quantify. Directly including these predictions in the state can lead to exponential state-space growth, making direct dynamic programming computationally expensive. We study discounted finite MDPs under a block-feedback protocol: at the start of each K-period block, the decision maker observes $K$-step probabilistic transition predictions for a subset of actions and adapts decisions to observed states within the block. We show that the optimal value satisfies a Bayesian Bellman equation on the original state space. This representation reveals that predictive information creates value by recursively changing the local order of maximization and expectation in the Bellman expansion. We formalize this effect through a Bellman--Jensen analysis and quantify the effects of prediction horizon, error, action coverage, and within-block feedback. We propose block-feedback Bayesian offline learning with online adaptation (BF--BOLA), a model-based algorithm for learning near-optimal policies, and establish upper and lower sample-complexity bounds that match up to logarithmic factors in the stated regimes. The results show that predictions can reduce transition learning to uncovered rows and that learning a near-optimal policy can require fewer prediction samples than estimating the Bayesian value function.

</details>


### [361] [Recommender regret for improving regularised RL estimation with bandits and MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32910)
*Danilo Vucetic,Alexandre Larouche,Mohammad Pedramfar,Audrey Durand,Gauthier Gidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将估计生成形式化为推荐过程、以推荐遗憾评估正则化RL中bandit/搜索式估计质量，并提出统一的recommender MCTS，在多任务上取得更快收敛与更优下游表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于bandit的估计框架只衡量采样效率，而不衡量估计本身的质量，导致正则化RL中策略/价值/Q值估计的评价缺乏原则性指标。

Method: 把估计生成建模为推荐过程，将bandit采样视为改进算子；定义正则化器感知的推荐者遗憾（regulariser-aware recommender regrets），分析其下界并证明均匀采样等简单算法可达该下界；进而将bandit式与搜索式目标改进统一为recommender MCTS。

Result: 在合成树、街机游戏与科学发现任务上，推荐者往往带来更快的目标收敛和更优的下游性能；实验首次刻画了正则化MCTS在科学应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 以推荐遗憾为准则可统一评估并改进正则化RL中的估计生成，recommender MCTS为bandit与搜索方法提供了统一框架，并在科学场景中展现潜力。

Abstract: Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms rely on estimators, like Monte Carlo tree search or one-step look-ahead, to provide policy, value, or Q-value estimates in training and inference. However, existing bandit-based estimation frameworks evaluate sampling efficiency, not the quality of estimates. We address this by formalising estimate generation as a recommendation process, treating bandit-based sampling as an improvement operator. To evaluate the quality of a recommendation (estimate), we define regulariser-aware recommender regrets, analysing their lower bounds, and proving that simple algorithms like uniform sampling can achieve these bounds. Finally, we unify bandit- and search-based target improvement under recommender MCTS. Across synthetic trees, arcade games, and scientific discovery tasks, recommenders often yield faster target convergence and superior downstream performance. Our experiments characterise for the first time the potential of regularised MCTS in scientific applications.

</details>


### [362] [A Tutorial on Physics Constrained Machine Learning for Hybrid Modeling, Optimization, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32914)
*Rahul Golder,Bimol Nath Roy,MM Faruque Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模块化物理约束机器学习框架KKT-HardNet，借助微分投影层保证代数约束严格满足，并用隐函数定理实现高效端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCML需将领域知识与物理约束融入AI模型，但现有方法难以同时保证约束严格满足、推理高效并支持混合建模、过程模拟、优化与预测控制的统一平台。

Method: 构建模块化框架KKT-HardNet：神经网络快速推理+微分投影层保证线性和非线性等式/不等式约束；利用隐函数定理对投影层反向传播，避免昂贵自动微分；提出精确降阶Fischer-Burmeister重构与原始-对偶内点法作为替代投影求解策略；支持自定义网络骨干与投影机制、异构数据。

Result: 在多样化工程基准问题上，KKT-HardNet在约束满足度和推理时间上均取得数量级提升；代码与基准数据集已开源。

Conclusion: KKT-HardNet为PCML提供统一、可定制的教程式框架，兼顾约束严格性与计算效率，适用于混合建模、过程仿真、优化和模型预测控制。

Abstract: Physics-constrained machine learning (PCML) integrates domain knowledge, first-principles, and physical constraints into AI/ML models. In this work, we present a step-by-step tutorial of a modular PCML framework, KKT-HardNet, and illustrate how it could be used for hybrid modeling, process simulation, optimization and model predictive control in a single platform. KKT-HardNet combines fast inference capabilities of neural networks with guaranteed satisfaction of algebraic constraints through differential projection. It can handle heterogeneous data and enforce both linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. The modular design supports custom neural-network backbones and user-defined projection mechanisms for PCML. To enable efficient end-to-end training, KKT-HardNet exploits implicit function theorem to backpropagate through the projection layer. This avoids computationally expensive automatic differentiation. This work also introduces an exact reduced-order Fischer-Burmeister reformulation and a primal-dual interior-point method as an alternative projection solve strategy. We also provide a diverse set of benchmarking problems for PCML in engineering applications. KKT-HardNet achieves orders of magnitude better performance in terms of both constraint satisfaction and inference time. The KKT-HardNet code and the benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/souls-tamu/kkt-hardnet .

</details>


### [363] [ZENO: Nonlinear filtering with Zakai Equation Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32926)
*Aaditya Prasad,Steven W. Flavell,Brian DePasquale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZENO 通过学习满足 Zakai 方程物理信息损失的神经算子求解非线性滤波的未归一化滤波分布，并结合 EM 框架完成状态推断与参数学习，在三个仿真随机动力系统及线虫全脑成像数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波中的状态推断与参数学习是科学机器学习的核心计算，广泛用于工程与神经科学。未归一化滤波分布由 Zakai 方程（结合前向 Kolmogorov 算子与观测似然的 SPDE）支配，但高维 SPDE 通常无解析解、数值上不可行，亟需新方法。

Method: 提出 Zakai Equation Neural Operator (ZENO)：以 Zakai 方程为基础的物理信息损失优化神经算子，逼近潜随机动力系统的精确未归一化滤波分布；并将 ZENO 推断的后验分布嵌入 EM 框架，进行参数学习与状态推断。

Result: 在双势阱双稳系统、Van der Pol 振子和 Lorenz 系统三个仿真随机动力系统上准确推断后验分布并学习对应 SDE 参数，性能优于现有潜 SDE 推断与学习方法；从固定化线虫全脑成像数据中恢复了此前观察到的代表前向与后向运动的极限环。

Conclusion: ZENO 从偏微分方程与算子理论视角为非线性滤波与动力学推断提供了新方法。

Abstract: State inference and parameter learning for nonlinear filters is a core computation for scientific machine learning with varied applications in engineering and neuroscience. The unnormalized filtering distribution is governed by the Zakai equation, a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) that combines the forward Kolmogorov operator (known as the Fokker-Planck equation) with observed likelihood updates. Although solving these high-dimensional SPDEs is often impossible analytically and intractable numerically, advances in operator learning and physics-informed machine learning have made it feasible. Harnessing these advances, we introduce the Zakai Equation Neural Operator (ZENO), a model that optimizes a neural operator against a Zakai Equation based physics-informed loss to approximate the exact unnormalized filtering distribution for latent stochastic dynamical systems. We use this ZENO-inferred posterior distribution in an expectation-maximization (EM) framework to perform parameter learning and state inference. We demonstrate ZENO's ability to accurately infer the posterior distribution and learn its corresponding SDE parameters in three simulated stochastic dynamical systems (Double-Well bistable system, Van Der Pol (VDP) oscillator, and Lorenz system) and compare its performance against established methods for latent SDE inference and learning. We show that ZENO recovers previously observed limit cycles representing forward and backward locomotion from a whole-brain imaging dataset of immobilized \textit{C. elegans} nematodes. ZENO is a novel approach to the problem of nonlinear filtering and dynamical inference through the lens of partial differential equations and operator theory.

</details>


### [364] [Where Rounding Happens Matters: Rounding-Location Alignment Stabilizes LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32951)
*Yijing Ke,Tianle Cai,Ruosong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在BF16强化学习中，只需将训练端在RoPE、SwiGLU、残差RMSNorm三处的舍入位置与推理端对齐（保留FP32中间量直到输出BF16），即可避免训练崩溃，且无需改动推理引擎、矩阵乘/注意力核与学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM通常推理与训练分离，即便权重与BF16张量相同，实现差异仍使本应on-policy的更新变为off-policy并导致训练不稳定。既有方案要么整体对齐推理与训练栈，要么改全局精度，要么修改学习算法，代价大；本文探究是否只需对齐执行栈的部分环节即可稳定BF16 RL。

Method: 定位训练侧与推理侧舍入位置差异，在RoPE、SwiGLU、残差RMSNorm三个区域将选定中间量保持FP32直至其BF16输出，使训练端舍入点与推理端一致；保持推理引擎、矩阵乘与注意力核、学习目标不变，并与默认BF16基线对照。

Result: 在四个Qwen系列模型上，默认BF16基线出现崩溃；三区域全部对齐可避免崩溃，仅对齐RoPE路径在本文实验中也足以稳定训练，尽管仍存在残余数值差异。

Conclusion: 局部对齐执行栈的关键舍入点即可稳定BF16 RL训练，无需整体对齐或修改算法，说明数值不一致的稳定性影响主要集中于少数结构性位置。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models often uses separate systems for inference and training. Even with identical weights and BF16 tensors, implementation differences can make an intended on-policy update off-policy and destabilize training. Existing remedies align the entire inference and training stacks, change global precision, or modify the learning algorithm. We ask whether aligning selected parts of the execution stack can stabilize BF16 RL while other implementation differences remain. We align training-side rounding locations with inference in three regions---RoPE, SwiGLU, and residual RMSNorm---by retaining selected intermediates in FP32 until their BF16 outputs. Across four Qwen-family models, aligning all three regions prevents the collapse observed under the default BF16 baseline; RoPE-path alignment alone also suffices in the studied runs, despite remaining numerical discrepancies. The interventions keep the inference engine, matrix-multiplication and attention kernels, and learning objective unchanged.

</details>


### [365] [RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Reinforcement Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32971)
*Xi Chen,Fengqi Hu,Zeyu Shen,Jiaying Xu,Shangding Gu,Pan Xu,Anqi Liu,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustRLlib：一个算法中心的鲁棒强化学习库与基准，统一集成17种代表性在线/离线/安全鲁棒RL算法，并提供覆盖真实世界偏移与人形、视觉-语言-动作任务的高评测平台，通过273种扰动配置给出当前鲁棒RL的能力地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒RL对真实部署至关重要，现有算法机制丰富，但存在两大障碍：算法可复用性有限，以及经验能力边界不清晰，制约了其潜力发挥。

Method: 构建RobustRLlib：以统一接口集成17种代表性鲁棒在线、离线与安全RL算法以提升可复用性；同时搭建增强评测平台，覆盖多样化真实世界偏移及先进的人形机器人与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）任务；开展跨越273种扰动配置的系统性评测。

Result: 在273种扰动配置上的系统评测给出了当前鲁棒RL算法的最新能力地图，明确了其有效场景以及仍存在显著鲁棒性差距之处。

Conclusion: 作为一个开源、易用的平台，RobustRLlib提供可复现、可扩展的代码库，支持将现有方法应用于新问题并开发新的鲁棒RL算法。

Abstract: Robust reinforcement learning (RL) is critical for reliable real-world deployment, where policies may fail under diverse shifts and disruptions. Existing robust RL algorithms offer a rich set of mechanisms and principles, yet two major barriers limit their broader potential: limited algorithm reusability and an unclear empirical capability boundary. We introduce RobustRLlib, an algorithm-centric library and benchmark that addresses both challenges. It integrates 17 representative robust online, offline, and safe RL algorithms under a unified interface for substantially improved reusability, together with an enhanced evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world shifts and advanced humanoid and vision-language-action tasks. Systematic evaluation over 273 perturbation configurations provides an up-to-date capability map of current robust RL algorithms, revealing where they remain effective and where substantial robustness gaps persist. As an open-source, user-friendly platform, RobustRLlib offers a reproducible and extensible codebase for applying existing methods to new problems and developing new robust RL algorithms.

</details>


### [366] [Physics-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining on Multi-Domain Raman Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23955)
*Charalampos Davalas,Georgios Levantinos,Anastasios Dimitriou,Nikolaos Papanikolaou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Dimitrios Michail*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于掩码自编码器（MAE）的多域自监督框架，结合物理感知的稀疏Voigt峰形解码与去噪目标，从异构拉曼数据中学习可迁移表征，显著提升少标签任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱可产生大量无标注数据，传统监督学习难以利用；需从跨生物、化学、法医、材料等异构数据中学习可迁移表征，并克服线性评测低估表征质量的问题。

Method: MAE式多域自监督预训练；物理感知目标：掩码强度重建 + 稀疏Voigt峰形解码器（可微峰中心、宽度、幅度建模谱峰结构）+ 去噪目标增强鲁棒性。下游评测涵盖物质分类、组分识别、浓度估计、谱去噪；采用线性探针、注意力探针、端到端微调与从零训练四种范式，并考察编码器容量影响。

Result: 自监督预训练在标签有限的困难任务上收益最大；注意力探针从冻结表征中提取的信息显著多于线性探针，说明常规线性评测可能低估表征质量；预训练收益随编码器容量增大而更明显。

Conclusion: 物理感知的多域自监督预训练可提升拉曼光谱任务中的标签效率、鲁棒性与跨任务可迁移性，并为表征评测方式（探针选择）提供重要启示。

Abstract: Raman spectroscopy generates large volumes of unlabeled data that are difficult to exploit with conventional supervised learning. This work introduces a multi-domain self-supervised learning framework based on a masked autoencoder (MAE) for learning transferable Raman spectral representations from heterogeneous biological, chemical, forensic, and materials datasets. The proposed pretraining strategy incorporates physics-aware objectives by combining masked intensity reconstruction with a sparse Voigt-profile decoder that models spectral peak structure through differentiable peak centers, widths, and amplitudes. A denoising objective is further included to improve robustness to spectral corruption. The learned encoder is evaluated on substance classification, component identification, concentration estimation, and spectral denoising under linear probing, attention-based probing, end-to-end fine-tuning, and training from scratch. Experimental results show that self-supervised pretraining is most beneficial for challenging tasks with limited labeled data. Attention-based probing also reveals substantially more useful information in the frozen representation than linear probing, indicating that conventional linear evaluation may underestimate representation quality. In addition, the benefits of pretraining become more pronounced with increasing encoder capacity. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates that physics-aware, multi-domain self-supervised pretraining can improve label efficiency, robustness, and transferability across diverse Raman spectroscopy tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [367] [TranSAGE: Automating Spatial Accelerator Generation for Large-Scale Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32279)
*Chi Zhang,Yi Sun,Weichuang Zhang,Quan Chen,Jieru Zhao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TranSAGE 是一个基于编译器的框架，联合优化大规模 Transformer 的多算子流水线调度并自动生成空间加速器 RTL 实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推理的计算与访存需求高；空间加速器在片上带宽和能效上有优势，但针对大模型的设计成本高。现有加速器生成器多只支持单算子，忽视多算子流水线带来的吞吐与能效收益。

Method: 提出 TranSAGE：集成设计空间探索（DSE）引擎实现多算子数据流优化，并采用基于动态规划的图划分算法识别适合流水的算子簇及其最优数据流参数；设计空间架构原语与 lowering pass 自动生成高效 RTL 实现。

Result: 在四个代表性 LLM 上评估：相比商用 GPU/NPU 基线，吞吐最高提升 2.41x，能效最高提升 13.04x；相比 SOTA 加速器生成器，吞吐最高提升 11.33x，能效最高提升 6.07x。

Conclusion: 多算子流水线联合调度与自动生成是提升 Transformer 加速器吞吐与能效的有效途径，且框架具备可用性与可复现性（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Large-scale Transformers have achieved remarkable success across NLP, computer vision, and robotics, but their growing computational and memory demands challenge efficient inference. Spatial accelerators offer advantages in on-chip bandwidth and energy efficiency, yet designing them for large models remains costly and time-consuming. Existing accelerator generators typically focus on single-operation support, overlooking the benefits of multi-operation pipelining, which limits throughput and energy efficiency.
  We propose TranSAGE, a compiler-based framework that jointly optimizes scheduling for large-scale Transformers under multi-operation pipelining and generates corresponding accelerator implementations. Our approach integrates a design space exploration (DSE) engine with multi-operation dataflow optimization and a dynamic programming-based graph partitioning algorithm to efficiently identify pipeline-friendly operation clusters and their optimal dataflow parameters. We further design spatial architecture primitives and lowering passes to automatically generate efficient RTL implementations.
  Evaluations on four representative LLMs demonstrate that TranSAGE achieves up to 2.41x higher throughput and 13.04x higher energy efficiency than commercial GPU/NPU baselines. Compared with state-of-the-art accelerator generators, TranSAGE achieves up to 11.33x higher throughput and 6.07x higher energy efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/sjtu-zhao-lab/TranSAGE.

</details>


### [368] [PARIS: Parallel Arithmetic Reduction and Integrated Selection for MSDF Inner Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32410)
*Malik Zohaib Nisar,Ic-Pyo Hong,Dorit Merhof,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出PARIS，一种消除输入项串行化的MSDF（最高有效位优先）架构，通过并行位平面归约与共享进位保存残差选择实现精确MSDF输出，在面积、延迟与能耗上显著优于复用式MSDF实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSDF加速器对输入项处理是串行化的，导致内积延迟随扇入（fan-in）增大而增加，限制了吞吐与能效。

Method: PARIS将B位操作数分解为B²个位位置对进行并行处理，采用并行位平面归约与共享进位保存（carry-save）残差选择，在支持的tile内处理时间与扇入无关，并生成精确MSDF结果；另引入偏置感知ReLU检测以减少算术周期。

Result: 45nm、100MHz下784通道INT8内核占0.008701 mm²，88周期完成内积；两个MNIST MLP上算术加速240.44×与180.47×，算术能耗降98.89%与98.58%。16引擎加速器占0.90273 mm²，满足100MHz时序，投影吞吐24.33 GOPS、能效约1581.14 GOPS/W；偏置感知ReLU使建模算术周期再降5.52%与4.86%；256引擎RTL实现259周期完成Model A。

Conclusion: PARIS通过并行位平面归约消除MSDF输入项串行化，兼顾精确性与能效，并支持输出并行扩展。

Abstract: Existing most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) accelerators serialize input-term processing, causing inner-product latency to increase with fan-in. This paper presents PARIS, an MSDF architecture that eliminates input-term serialization through parallel bit-plane reduction and shared carry-save residual selection. For $B$-bit operands, PARIS processes $B^2$ bit-position pairs, independent of fan-in within the supported tile, while generating exact MSDF outputs. Synthesized in 45\,nm at 100\,MHz, the 784-lane INT8 core occupies 0.008701\,mm$^2$ and completes an inner product in 88 cycles. Evaluation on two MNIST multilayer perceptrons demonstrates arithmetic-workload speedups of 240.44$\times$ and 180.47$\times$ over a matched reused-MSDF implementation, reducing arithmetic energy by 98.89\% and 98.58\%, respectively. A synthesized 16-engine accelerator occupies 0.90273\,mm$^2$ and meets the 100\,MHz timing constraint. Assuming immediate weight availability, it delivers a projected throughput of 24.33\,GOPS and an estimated energy efficiency of 1,581.14\,GOPS/W at unchanged mapped power. Bias-aware ReLU detection further reduces modeled arithmetic cycles by 5.52\% and 4.86\%. A separate 256-engine RTL implementation completes Model A in 259 cycles, demonstrating output-parallel scalability.

</details>


### [369] [Automated Multi-Line Bug Repair Using Model Checker Aided LLMs: A Case Study on RISC-V Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32597)
*Debleena Das,Chandan Kumar Jha,Muhammad Hassan,Rolf Drechsler,Ansuman Banerjee,Subhra K. Das,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合大语言模型、断言与模型检查器的自动化RTL缺陷修复方法，可扩展且具形式化保证，在BRISC-V RISC-V核上可修复98%单行与93%多行缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL功能验证是现代半导体开发的瓶颈，调试尤耗资源（需分析仿真波形/反例）。已有LLM用于RTL调试修复，但通常只针对单行缺陷、开环运行、依赖提示或检索、缺乏可扩展性，且常产生错误修复。

Method: 以失败断言为引导，让LLM为多行缺陷生成候选补丁；每个候选补丁用模型检查器（JasperGold）进行形式化验证，仅接受通过验证的有效修复；整体构成带语义接地与形式化保证的闭环流程。

Result: 在BRISC-V RISC-V核上采用变异测试（引入多种变异类型）评估：可修复最高98%的单行RTL缺陷和93%的多行缺陷；据作者所知，这是首个在复杂RISC-V处理器设计上以可扩展方式实现LLM辅助多行RTL缺陷自动修复的工作。

Conclusion: LLM+断言+模型检查器的闭环方法为RTL缺陷修复提供可扩展、可信赖的形式化保证，显著提升单/多行缺陷修复率，具备在复杂处理器设计上的可扩展性。

Abstract: Functional verification of Register-Transfer Level(RTL) designs has become a critical bottleneck in modern semiconductor development, often consuming the majority of project resources. Debugging, in particular, remains an especially time-consuming task, requiring careful analysis of simulation traces or counterexamples. Recent work has explored applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to RTL debug and repair with great promise. However, the existing approaches typically are for single-line bugs, operate in open-loop, rely heavily on prompts or retrieval, lack scalability, and often produce incorrect fixes. In this paper, we propose a novel automated RTL bug repair methodology that combines LLMs, assertions, and a model checker to provide scalable, trustworthy semantic grounding and formal guarantees. Concretely, the methodology leverages failing assertions to guide the LLM in generating patches for multi-line bugs. Each candidate patch is formally verified using a model checker (JasperGold), ensuring only valid repairs are accepted. We evaluate the efficacy and scalability of our methodology on the BRISC-V RISC-V core using mutation testing, introducing several mutation types, and demonstrate that our methodology can repair up to 98% of single-line RTL bugs and 93% of the multi-line bugs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to show automated, LLM-assisted RTL multi-line bug repair results on a complex RISC-V processor design in a scalable manner.

</details>


### [370] [Embodied Emissions of 2.5D and 3D Scaling in Chipletized GPUs for Low-Carbon AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32830)
*Hannah Watson,Arjun Singh,Jennifer Stephenson,Job Soethoudt,Debjyoti Bhattacharjee,James Myers*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该文利用imec.netzero建模平台估算2.5D与3D封装扩展的chiplet GPU的隐含排放（EE），发现计算单元与HBM容量翻倍时，3D扩展使EE增加89%，低于2.5D的141%，表明3D扩展更有利于控制GPU扩展带来的隐含碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU算力随AI负载扩张，超大规模数据中心对高性能GPU的依赖加剧气候变化影响，需量化封装决策对碳足迹的作用。

Method: 基于imec.netzero建模平台，对采用2.5D和3D扩展的chiplet GPU进行隐含排放（EE）估算，并将两种方案的计算单元数量与HBM容量均翻倍以作对比。

Result: 3D扩展方案EE增加89%，2.5D扩展方案EE增加141%。

Conclusion: 3D扩展在限制GPU扩展所致隐含排放方面更具优势，是更具吸引力的封装方案。

Abstract: GPU compute resources are scaling to support growing AI workloads, leading to further rises in the climate change impact of hyperscale data centers that rely on high-performance GPUs. To model how packaging decisions impact the carbon footprint we have estimated the embodied emissions (EE) of a chiplet-based GPU that has been scaled in 2.5D and 3D using the imec.netzero modelling platform. We doubled the number of compute units and high bandwidth memory (HBM) capacity in the 2.5D and 3D scaled systems and found that 3D scaling results in an 89% increase in EE, as compared to an increase of 141% for 2.5D scaling, positioning 3D scaling as an attractive solution to limit the embodied emissions due to GPU scaling.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [371] [Perfectly aperiodic asymmetric scalable metasurface based on the hat monotile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32467)
*Yuma Otsuka,Hiroki Wakatsuchi*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并数值验证了一种基于非周期单调铺砖（"hat"多风筝形）的超表面：利用hat的12种可区分取向各自承载一个谐振单元，将几何的不可周期性与镜像不对称性转写为电磁响应，实现位置可辨识的电磁环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超表面多设计于周期晶格上，其电磁响应在空间上均匀，空间排布本身未被作为承载信息的设计资源加以利用。作者希望把"空间排布"本身变成信息载体。

Method: 采用数学上仅能非周期铺满平面的"hat"多风筝形单砖，以其12种不同取向为基础，每种取向配置调谐至4.600–4.914 GHz范围内12个吸收频率之一的谐振单元；在组成唯一（非重复）的6×6网格上做高斯波束扫描，提取位置相关的电磁特征；进行相干性引导的多频选择，并以位移相关分析（余弦相似度）检验平移序的缺失。

Result: 位置相关电磁特征在空间上不规则，两两分离度超过6 dB；相干性引导选取3个频率分量可将位置特征矩阵的稳定秩从1.0提升至约1.6，而累积彼此相似的特征无法增加有效维度；位移相关分析中余弦相似度始终不超过0.5，证实响应继承了铺砖无平移序的性质。

Conclusion: 该非周期超表面设计兼容标准印制电路工艺，为定位、无芯片识别及通感一体化等应用提供了一条确定性构建位置可辨识电磁环境的路径。

Abstract: Metasurfaces are conventionally designed on periodic lattices, which renders their electromagnetic response spatially uniform and leaves the spatial arrangement itself unused as an information-bearing design resource. In this study, we propose and numerically validate a metasurface built on an aperiodic monotile: the recently discovered "hat" polykite, a single tile mathematically guaranteed to tile the plane only nonperiodically. The twelve distinguishable orientations of the hat serve as a natural design basis; each carries resonant elements tuned to one of twelve absorption frequencies between 4.600 and 4.914 GHz, transcribing the provable aperiodicity and mirror asymmetry of the geometry into the electromagnetic response. Gaussian-beam scanning over a compositionally unique 6 times 6 grid yields irregular, position-dependent electromagnetic signatures whose pairwise separations reach beyond 6 dB. In particular, coherence-guided selection of multiple frequencies increases the stable rank of the position-signature matrix from 1.0 to approximately 1.6 with three frequency components, whereas the accumulation of mutually similar electromagnetic features fails to add effective dimensions, demonstrating that properly selected multifrequency characteristics enhance the spatial distinguishability embedded in the surface. A shift-correlation analysis in which the cosine similarity never exceeds 0.5 confirms that the response inherits the absence of translational order from the tiling. Compatible with standard printed-circuit fabrication, the aperiodic metasurface design establishes a deterministic route toward position-identifiable electromagnetic environments for localization, chipless identification, and integrated sensing and communication.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [372] [Resource Slicing for Time-Sensitive Data Traffic in Non-Terrestrial Networks with FSO ISLs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31802)
*Abid Afridi,Alexis A. Dowhuszko,Risto Wichman,Jyri Hämäläinen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多跳LEO卫星网络的多跳切片框架，通过逐跳预留时频资源块（RB）并时序对齐，保障时敏流的最大端到端时延与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳LEO网络中时敏分组在多颗卫星上排队，逐跳等待时间累积，导致最大端到端（E2E）时延难以保证。

Method: 为源自每颗卫星、发往地面站（GS）的每条时敏流预留时频资源，依据分组到达统计、E2E时延预算与目标可靠性确定每跳预留的RB数量；对相邻链路的预留进行时间对齐，实现来自各地面用户终端、汇聚于不同LEO卫星的时敏流的流水线式转发。与Pure-TDMA、FIFO、FSF三种基线在同一多跳路径上对比。

Result: 轻负载下各方案时延性能相当；随负载增长，基线的高分位时延急剧上升，而所提框架时延增长显著更慢。在高到达率与高可靠性目标下，所提方案约需31 ms，比FIFO（76 ms）、FSF（180 ms）、Pure-TDMA（210 ms）低2.5–6.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于统计到达与可靠性目标的逐跳时频预留加时序对齐，可有效抑制多跳累积排队时延，为LEO多跳时敏业务提供可保障的E2E时延性能。

Abstract: In multi-hop Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks, time-sensitive packets experience queueing across multiple satellite hops, causing per-hop waiting times that accumulate and make the maximum end-to-end (E2E) delay difficult to guarantee. This paper presents a novel multi-hop slicing framework that reserves time-frequency resources for each time-sensitive flow of data originating at each satellite, aiming to maintain the reservation of communication resources along every inter-satellite link (ISL) on its route to the ground station (GS). The number of time-frequency resource blocks (RBs) reserved per-hop is determined from the data packet arrival statistics, E2E delay budget, and target level of reliability. Reservations on consecutive links are temporally aligned to enable pipelined forwarding of the time-sensitive data flows aggregated from terrestrial user terminals on the different LEO satellites. We evaluate the proposed framework against conventional scheduling and resource-allocation baselines, including pure time-division multiple access (Pure-TDMA), first-in-first-out (FIFO), and farthest-satellite-first (FSF), over the same multi-hop LEO satellite path. Under light traffic conditions, the baseline schemes provide comparable delay performance; however, their high-percentile delays increase sharply as data traffic load grows, whereas the proposed framework exhibits substantially slower delay growth. At the high arrival rate and high reliability target considered, the proposed scheme requires about 31 ms, a factor of 2.5-6.8 lower than the 76, 180, and 210 ms required by FIFO, FSF, and Pure-TDMA.

</details>


### [373] [Pinching Antenna Systems (PASS): Principles, Positioning and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32068)
*Tianwei Hou,Yaoyu Zhang,Jinhua Wang,Guangyu Li,Zongyi Li,Minghao Jin,Anna Li,Mugen Peng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文是面向6G的针脚天线系统（PASS）综述，涵盖工作原理、定位方法、性能评估、资源分配及AI赋能应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PASS通过在辐射前利用波导导引电磁波，可提升无线网络容量与覆盖，被视为6G潜在使能技术，但缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 综述性研究：先从材料与物理层面阐明PASS基本原理，进而分析用户侧与基站侧的定位方法，并在不同阻塞模型下评估上/下行通信性能；再从性能优化角度系统梳理PASS增强网络的设计；最后总结利用AI应对动态复杂场景的研究。

Result: 形成了覆盖原理、定位、性能评估、资源分配与AI赋能五个维度的PASS研究现状全景图。

Conclusion: PASS是6G的重要候选使能技术，其定位、性能优化与AI融合是后续研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) have garnered significant attention owing to their ability to enhance the capacity and coverage of wireless networks by guiding electromagnetic waves through waveguides before radiation. As such, PASS are regarded as promising enabling technologies for sixth-generation (6G) communication networks. In this paper, we present a comprehensive survey of the state of the art in PASS, covering operating principles, positioning methodologies, performance evaluation, resource allocation, and the application of artificial intelligence (AI) to PASS-enhanced wireless networks. We first introduce the fundamental principles of PASS from both materials and physics perspectives. Building on this foundation, we present positioning methodologies for both the user side and the base station side. We then evaluate the communication performance of PASS in both uplink and downlink scenarios under different blockage models. Furthermore, we systematically review existing designs for PASS-enhanced wireless networks from a performance optimization perspective. Finally, we survey research efforts that leverage AI to address challenges in dynamic and complex scenarios.

</details>


### [374] [QoE-Driven Hybrid Resource Allocation for Multi-User OFDMA Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32077)
*Xiaotian Xu,Zhifeng Tang,Guangchen Wang,Nan Yang,Xiangyun Zhou,Salman Durrani,Yansha Deng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个QoE驱动的混合资源分配框架，用于多用户下行OFDMA语义通信系统，联合优化功率、子载波与语义决策，并用投影式MADDPG结合Gumbel-softmax自适应退火求解混合连续-离散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信中语义速率与相似度具有"两者皆需"的非补偿特性，传统QoE设计难以刻画，且资源分配问题同时含连续（功率）与离散（子载波、语义决策）变量，耦合约束下难以求解。

Method: 1) 建立混合连续-离散优化问题，在功率与频谱耦合约束下联合优化发射功率、子载波分配和语义决策；2) 提出偏好逆乘法指数型QoE模型，有界且非补偿，刻画语义速率与相似度的"both-required"属性；3) 设计基于投影的多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）框架，保证连续与离散动作的可行性；4) 引入Gumbel-softmax松弛与性能驱动的自适应温度退火，提升离散语义决策学习。

Result: 仿真表明，所提框架相比传统QoE设计及基于惩罚项、固定温度的基线，具有更优的收敛性、更强的稳定性和更高的QoE。

Conclusion: 结果验证了所提QoE模型的有效性以及混合决策学习在语义通信中的优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a quality-of-experience (QoE)-driven hybrid resource allocation framework for multi-user downlink orthogonal frequency-division multiple access semantic communication systems, where the hybrid framework jointly handles continuous and discrete decision variables. Specifically, we formulate a mixed continuous-discrete optimization problem to jointly optimize transmit power, subcarrier allocation, and semantic decisions under coupled power and spectrum constraints. To better characterize semantic service quality, we propose a preference-inverse multiplicative exponential QoE that is bounded and non-compensatory, capturing the intrinsic ``both-required'' property of semantic rate and similarity. To solve the optimization problem, we develop a projection-based multi-agent deep deterministic policy gradient framework that enforces feasibility for continuous and discrete actions. Furthermore, we integrate Gumbel-softmax relaxation with an adaptive performance-driven temperature annealing scheme to enhance the learning of discrete semantic decisions. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed framework achieves superior convergence, greater stability, and higher QoE compared to the conventional QoE design and penalty-based or fixed-temperature baselines, highlighting the benefits of our QoE design and the effectiveness of hybrid decision learning in semantic communications.

</details>


### [375] [Resilience in Wireless: A Lyapunov-Constrained Integrated Sensing, Communication, and Control Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32116)
*Yifan Ge,Ningyan Guo,Yuanhao Cui,Dingyou Ma,Zhiyong Feng,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文提出融合感知-通信-控制（ISCC）框架，以状态相关Lyapunov约束刻画通信可靠性与感知误差，实现自适应基站波束赋形下的发射功率最小化，并证明条件均方最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无线网络多用可靠性指标评估稳定性，但仅靠可靠数据传输无法刻画无线环境变化与物理任务下的网络稳定性，因此需要从"韧性（resilience）"视角出发，兼顾可接受服务的维持与干扰后的恢复能力。

Method: 构建ISCC统一框架，将通信可靠性与感知误差纳入状态相关Lyapunov约束，用于自适应基站波束赋形设计；由此得到发射功率最小化问题，该问题非凸，采用半正定松弛（SDR）与连续凸近似（SCA）求解，并进行秩一恢复。

Result: 理论上建立了条件均方最终有界性（conditional mean-square ultimate boundedness）；仿真表明相较静态基线方案，所提方法在韧性与恢复速度上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将通信可靠性与感知精度通过Lyapunov约束统一纳入波束赋形优化，可有效提升无线网络的韧性并加快扰动后的恢复，为ISCC场景下稳定性设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Traditional wireless networks mainly use reliability metrics to evaluate network stability. However, reliable data delivery alone cannot fully characterize network stability under variations in wireless environments and physical tasks. Wireless network resilience provides a broader perspective by describing the ability to maintain acceptable service and recover from disruptions. We develop an integrated sensing, communication, and control (ISCC) framework that incorporates communication reliability and sensing error into a state-dependent Lyapunov constraint for adaptive BS beamforming. The resulting transmit-power minimization problem is non-convex and solved via semidefinite relaxation and successive convex approximation with rank-one recovery. We establish conditional mean-square ultimate boundedness, while simulations demonstrate improved resilience and faster recovery over a static baseline.

</details>


### [376] [Type-based Over-the-Air Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32343)
*Marc Martinez-Gost,Ana Pérez-Neira*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 将基于类型的多址接入（TBMA）作为通用空中计算（OAC）框架，把分布式观测的直方图估计问题与数字检测相结合，从而在不改动底层传输方案的前提下计算广泛类别的非线性函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OAC框架通常针对特定类函数设计，难以适用于更一般的非线性计算，限制了其通用性。

Method: 把TBMA建模为直方图估计问题，提出两种数字检测器；证明低复杂度的连续消除算法可达到最优最大似然性能；推导MSE解析表达式，并从计算精度、传输时长、带宽与能耗等维度与常规方案进行对比分析。

Result: 解析结果与数值实验一致；TBMA的MSE随SNR呈指数衰减，且在恒定发射能量下与所计算函数无关，而现有OAC技术不具备这一特性。

Conclusion: TBMA是迈向更通用OAC架构的一步，兼容现有传输方案，拓宽了可直接在无线信道上计算的函数范围，为分布式计算与决策提供新途径。

Abstract: Over-the-air computation (OAC) exploits the superposition property of wireless channels to compute functions of distributed data, reducing the communication overhead associated with its multiple access. However, existing OAC frameworks are often designed around specific classes of functions, limiting their applicability to more general nonlinear computations. This paper investigates type-based multiple access (TBMA) as a general framework for OAC, enabling the receiver to recover a histogram of the distributed observations and compute a broad class of functions without modifying the underlying communication scheme. We formulate TBMA as a histogram estimation problem and propose two digital detectors. We prove that a low-complexity successive cancellation algorithm achieves the optimal maximum-likelihood (ML) performance. We derive analytical expressions for the mean squared error (MSE) and compare TBMA with conventional schemes in terms of computation accuracy, transmission duration, bandwidth and energy consumption. Numerical experiments validate the analytical results and demonstrate that, unlike existing OAC techniques, TBMA exhibits an exponential MSE decay with increasing signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and constant transmit energy, independently of the function being computed. These findings position TBMA as a step toward more versatile OAC architectures, compatible with existing transmission schemes, and broadening the range of functions that can be computed directly over the wireless channel and enabling new possibilities for distributed computation and decision-making.

</details>


### [377] [An Evidence-Guided Framework for Tissue-Barrier Impedance Review: Deletion and Resistance Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32579)
*Leo Yang,Yingtong Zhou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一个证据引导的审查框架，将频谱异常、电阻估计与实验情境关联。内部仿真表明剔除被标记频率在二次目标下降低电阻误差，但稳健拟合下剔除平均无益甚至使端点对扰动误差上升；误差集中于少数拟合电路参数组，提示需检查参数可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审查组织屏障阻抗时，需要把频谱异常与电阻估计及实验上下文联系起来，而现有流程缺乏可追溯、可复核的证据链条。

Method: 构建证据引导框架，协调数值核验与可追溯的人工审查操作：用内部仿真评估剔除被标记频率的影响，用公开数据集检验模型适应性，用本地测量演示审查工作流；并比较二次目标与稳健拟合两种目标下的表现。

Result: 二次目标下剔除被标记频率降低了电阻误差；稳健拟合下剔除在平均意义上收益甚微，且对端点对扰动平均误差反而增大。高误差集中在少数几组拟合电路参数上。

Conclusion: 在所测试的噪声与扰动条件下，支持采用稳健的全谱估计、标记引导的检查以及匹配的敏感性核验，并重视参数可辨识性分析。

Abstract: Reviewing tissue-barrier impedance requires connecting spectral anomalies with resistance estimates and experimental context. We present an evidence-guided framework that coordinates numerical checks and traceable actions for human review. In-house simulations assess the effect of removing flagged frequencies, public datasets test model adaptation, and local measurements demonstrate the review workflow. Removing flagged frequencies reduced resistance error under quadratic objectives, whereas robust fitting showed little average benefit from deletion and a mean error increase for endpoint-pair perturbations. High errors clustered around a few fitted circuit parameter sets, highlighting the value of examining parameter identifiability. Under the tested noise and perturbation conditions, these findings support robust full-spectrum estimation, flag-guided inspection, and matched sensitivity checks.

</details>


### [378] [Channel orthogonalization via movable antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32606)
*Heedong Do,Angel Lozano*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 通过优化天线阵列中阵元位置实现远场多用户信道的正交化，将连续几何优化转化为完全图上的离散图覆盖问题，并给出最少天线数与最紧凑阵列的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远场多用户场景下用户信道间存在相关/耦合，需借助阵列几何设计实现信道正交；直接求解连续几何优化问题困难，且需明确正交化所需的最小阵元规模与紧致布阵方案。

Method: 把成对用户信道相关系数映射为图边，构建图论框架，将信道正交化等价为完全图上的离散图覆盖问题；对1D/2D/3D阵列几何推导正交化所需最少天线数，并建立0-1线性规划求取最紧凑阵列；框架扩展至零陷（null-steering）与多径信道情形；分析精确正交与近似正交间的折衷，并提出不破坏正交性的最小阵元间距约束方法。

Result: 给出1D、2D、3D阵列正交化的最少天线数目；0-1线性规划可识别最紧凑正交阵列；揭示"放宽为近似正交可减少天线数但牺牲阵列紧致性"的基本折衷；可施加最小间距约束而不牺牲正交性。

Conclusion: 阵列几何位置设计可作为实现多用户信道正交化的有效手段，图覆盖建模使连续优化离散化并可高效求解，天线数量与阵列紧致性之间存在可量化的权衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates the orthogonalization of far-field multiuser channels through the optimal positioning of the antennas constituting an array. To solve the ensuing continuous geometry optimization problem, a graph-theoretic framework is introduced that maps pairwise user channel correlations to graph edges, translating channel orthogonalization into a discrete graph-covering problem on a complete graph. The minimum number of array antennas required for orthogonalization is established for 1D, 2D, and 3D array geometries, and a binary linear program is formulated that identifies the most compact array attaining such orthogonality. The graph framework is also shown to accommodate null-steering scenarios and multipath channels. Furthermore, a fundamental tradeoff is revealed whereby relaxing exact orthogonality to near-orthogonality reduces the antenna count at the expense of a less compact array, and a method is presented to enforce minimum antenna spacings without sacrificing orthogonality.

</details>


### [379] [EMG Tokenization Preserves Movement Information at a Fraction of the Bitrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32674)
*Raul C. Sîmpetru,Amin Olamazadeh,Devon Rohlf,Vlad Cnejevici,Alessandro Del Vecchio*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出以紧凑的EMG token作为可穿戴肌电接口与三维手部姿态解码器之间的中间表示，在几乎不损失解码精度的前提下将传输载荷降低约36–41倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线EMG接口需要在电极覆盖范围与传输带宽之间权衡；原始高通道EMG数据量大，直接连续传输代价高，因此需要一种既保留姿态解码所需信息、又大幅压缩载荷的表示方式。

Method: 使用两种在无姿态标签条件下因果训练的分词器：一种为预训练重建EMG频谱的编码器（学习式表示），另一种基于SPAR——一种由相邻电极构建的工程化频谱-空间表示。在9名受试者、320通道EMG的个性化同会话实验中，采用匹配的解码器结构与训练流程对比token化特征与连续特征；并在emg2pose公开基准测试集上（176名受试者，含20名训练中未见受试者）用采样窗口验证；另在Cortex-M55微控制器上进行计算基准测试。

Result: 跨解码器平均，token化使平均误差变化为SPAR +0.01 mm（95% CI: -0.12至0.14）、学习式表示 +0.45 mm（0.10至0.82），均满足1 mm非劣效标准。token数组相较原始EMG降低41倍载荷、相较连续SPAR特征降低36倍。emg2pose测试划分上，学习式token与连续特征的平均误差绝对差在176名受试者及20名未见受试者子集中均低于0.05 mm。电极覆盖、校准与未见记录对SPAR解码的影响大于量化本身。优化SPAR可在Cortex-M55上于20 ms内为63通道生成token。

Conclusion: token化可作为可穿戴传感与运动解码之间的紧凑接口：穿戴端编码器与传输表示固定不变，而解码器可被更新，从而兼顾带宽约束与解码灵活性。

Abstract: Wireless electromyography (EMG) interfaces must balance electrode coverage against transmission bandwidth. Here, we show that compact EMG tokens substantially reduce payload while retaining information for three-dimensional hand-pose decoding. We evaluate two causal tokenizers fitted without pose labels: an encoder pretrained to reconstruct EMG spectra and one based on SPAR, an engineered spectral-spatial representation of neighbouring electrodes. In personalized, within-session experiments with 320-channel EMG from nine participants, we compare tokenized and continuous features using matched decoder architectures and training procedures. Averaged across decoders, tokenization changed mean error by +0.01 mm for SPAR (95% confidence interval: -0.12 to 0.14) and +0.45 mm for the learned representation (0.10 to 0.82). Both increases met a 1 mm non-inferiority criterion. Stored token arrays reduced payloads 41-fold relative to raw EMG and 36-fold relative to continuous SPAR features. On sampled windows of the public emg2pose benchmark's test partition, absolute differences in mean error between learned tokens and continuous features were below 0.05 mm across 176 participants and in the subset of 20 participants unseen during training. Electrode coverage, calibration, and unseen recordings affected SPAR decoding more than quantization did. In a computational benchmark, optimized SPAR generated tokens for 63 channels within 20 ms on a Cortex-M55 microcontroller. These findings support tokenization as a compact interface between wearable sensing and movement decoding, allowing decoders to be updated while the wearable encoder and transmitted representation remain fixed.

</details>


### [380] [Automated Fetal Magnetocardiography Reconstruction Using a Biophysical Forward Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32682)
*Jonas Emrich,Karin Narushima,Lena Wunderl,Philipp Wunderl,Tobias Jensch,Maximilian Huber,Peter Ewert,Peter Fierlinger,Annette Wacker-Gussmann,Reinhard Heckel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生物物理正向模型的自动化信号重建方法，无需人工标注即可从fMCG测量中恢复胎儿与母体心脏活动，输出可解释的三维向量心电信号及其空间位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMCG信号估计依赖盲源分离或模板投影，需复杂且繁重的人工处理，亟需自动化方案。

Method: 构建以时变磁偶极子表示心脏源的、具生物物理基础的正向模型，刻画测量与心脏信号间的关系，从而在无需人工标注的情况下重建可解释的三维向量心电信号及其空间位置。

Result: 在具已知真值的合成数据上验证，可准确恢复心脏源参数；在真实fMCG记录上，重建结果与既有参考方法高度一致。

Conclusion: 该正向模型方法可自动从fMCG记录中重建胎儿与母体心脏活动，并在可解释的向量心电源域给出信号；自动化、可解释的fMCG重建有望提升胎儿心脏评估的可靠性并推动其向产前护理转化。

Abstract: Objective: Fetal magnetocardiography (fMCG) enables early detection of cardiac disease by non-invasively measuring fetal electrophysiological activity. However, current signal estimation methods for fMCG require complex, labor-intensive human processing because they rely on blind source separation or template-based projection techniques. Methods: We propose an automated signal reconstruction approach that recovers fetal and maternal cardiac activity from fMCG measurements without manual annotation. Our approach leverages the relationship between measurements and cardiac signals via a biophysically grounded forward model that represents cardiac sources as time-varying magnetic dipoles. This enables reconstruction of physiologically interpretable three-dimensional vectorcardiographic signals along with their spatial position. Results: Validation on synthetic data with known ground truth confirms accurate recovery of cardiac source parameters. On real fMCG recordings, the reconstruction shows strong agreement with established reference methods. Conclusion: The proposed forward-model approach automatically reconstructs fetal and maternal cardiac activity from fMCG recordings and produces signals in an interpretable vectorcardiographic source domain. Significance: Automated, interpretable fMCG reconstruction could make fetal cardiac assessment more reliable and support potential translation into prenatal care.

</details>


### [381] [Reducing 6G Energy Consumption: TDD-Aligned Drain Voltage Modulation for Power Amplifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32889)
*Meizhen Zhuo,Gunter Wolff,Stephan ten Brink,Silvio Mandelli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出TDD对齐漏极电压调制(TDD-DVM)，每TDD周期按下行资源负载选一次漏压并在上行窗口切换，结合POLITE链路自适应，在同等吞吐下最多省电35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基站功放在部分负载下效率低，峰值功率回退浪费供电余量；按瞬时信号自适应漏压虽可回收损失，但需频繁切换，增加硬件成本。

Method: TDD-DVM在每个TDD周期根据下行资源负载选择一次漏极电压等级，并在上行窗口完成切换；依托作者已有POLITE链路自适应——在部分负载下将发射功率分散到未使用资源，使功放进入较低漏压即可满足的工作区间。采用3GPP Urban Macro系统级仿真，在多种业务负载下与传统及POLITE链路自适应下的基线方案对比。

Result: 在同等交付吞吐下，TDD-DVM结合POLITE可维持满漏压时的丢包率，相比传统链路自适应最多节省35%功耗，相比POLITE最多节省7%功耗，约为理想化参考方案增益的一半。

Conclusion: 以TDD周期为粒度的粗粒度漏压调制可在不引入高频切换成本的前提下显著降低基站功耗，且与POLITE链路自适应协同增益明显，但相较理想化参考仍有约一半提升空间。

Abstract: The Power Amplifier (PA) of a base station operates inefficiently at partial load, where back-off from the peak output power wastes supply headroom. Adapting the PA drain voltage to the instantaneous signal recovers this loss but requires frequent switching, increasing hardware cost. We propose TDD-Aligned Drain Voltage Modulation (TDD-DVM), a drain voltage modulation scheme that instead selects the drain voltage level once per Time-Division Duplex (TDD) period based on the downlink resource load and switches the voltage during the uplink window. This is enabled by the authors' previous work on POLITE link adaptation, which spreads the transmit power over resources left unused at partial load and moves the PA into a regime where a lower drain voltage suffices. In a 3GPP Urban Macro system-level simulation across traffic loads, we compare TDD-DVM against baseline approaches under conventional and POLITE link adaptation. At equal delivered throughput, TDD-DVM with POLITE keeps the packet failure ratio of the full drain voltage while consuming up to 35% less power than it under conventional link adaptation and up to 7% less than it under POLITE, roughly half of the gain of an idealized reference.

</details>


### [382] [Low-Complexity Wideband Channel Estimation for ISAC in Analog Millimeter-Wave Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32953)
*Hamza Touati,Raffaele Viterbo,Mattia Brambilla,Sandra Roger,Carmen Botella-Mascarell*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出TSDCE的宽带扩展方法TSDCE-TD，通过引入时域支撑并解耦角度与时延估计，以低复杂度实现毫米波ISAC系统的宽带信道估计，并可用于高精度室内定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波ISAC系统需要精确的宽带信道估计。已有的TSDCE方法在窄带场景下以远低于OMP类压缩感知方法的复杂度实现快速准确的角度估计，但宽带ISAC还需估计时延，联合角度-时延稀疏恢复因多维字典搜索而计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出TSDCE-TD，引入时域支撑，将角度估计与时延估计解耦。时延估计给出两种变体：TSDCE-TD(Dict)使用一维时延字典；TSDCE-TD(FFT)采用FFT补零插值。各路径估计通过连续干扰消除与角度-时延交替更新迭代精化。

Result: 仿真表明，在实际字典规模下所提方法优于基于OMP的基线；在高分辨率设置下性能相当而计算复杂度显著更低。所获得的角度-时延估计在射线追踪环境中实现单锚点室内定位，定位误差低于20 cm，满足实际室内定位需求。

Conclusion: TSDCE-TD以解耦的低复杂度框架实现宽带毫米波ISAC的高精度角度-时延联合估计，兼顾性能与效率，并支撑亚20厘米级单锚点室内定位。

Abstract: Accurate wideband channel estimation is a key enabler for millimeter-wave (mmWave) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. In the narrowband scenario, the Transformed Spatial Domain Channel Estimation (TSDCE) method was previously shown to provide fast and accurate angle estimation with substantially lower complexity than conventional compressed-sensing approaches based on orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP). However, wideband ISAC scenarios additionally require delay estimation, making joint angle-delay sparse recovery computationally expensive due to the multidimensional dictionary search. In this paper, we propose a wideband extension of TSDCE, namely TSDCE-TD, which incorporates time-domain support and decouples angle and delay estimation. Two variations are proposed for delay estimation: TSDCE-TD (Dict), which uses a one-dimensional delay dictionary, and TSDCE-TD (FFT), which applies Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) zero-padding interpolation. Path estimates are iteratively refined through successive interference cancellation and alternating angle-delay updates. Simulation results show that the proposed methods outperform OMP-based baselines under practical dictionary sizes, while achieving comparable performance at high-resolution settings with substantially lower computational complexity. Moreover, the angle-delay estimates obtained using the proposed approach enable accurate single-anchor indoor localization in a ray-traced environment, achieving sub-20 cm positioning errors and satisfying practical indoor positioning requirements.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [383] [From Hollow Waveguides to Mie Voids: Low-Index Light Confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32494)
*Dominik Ludescher,Ivan Toftul,Harald Giessen,Yuri Kivshar,Mario Hentschel*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了在光学薄介质及低折射率区域（空腔、气泡、Mie 空洞谐振器）中实现光局域与导引的反直觉光子学结构，指出"低折射率/缺失"本身可作为功能性光子自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为光局域依赖高折射率介质与全内反射。然而光与物质相互作用虽带来传感、非线性与光操控能力，也不可避免引入吸收与损耗；将增强或导引的能量推入低折射率空间，为光-物质相互作用与色散调控提供新途径，值得系统总结。

Method: 综述性方法：从光学薄介质中的光导引（空芯光子晶体光纤、反谐振反射光波导）出发，进而讨论高折射率包围中的低折射率"气泡"光学，最终归结到 Mie 空洞谐振器——在低折射率区域内支持局域电磁模式的谐振结构。

Result: 归纳表明上述体系均能在无全内反射机制下实现（谐振）光学局域与导引，形成一条从波导到谐振腔的连贯谱系，证明低折射率区域可承载局域、增强与导引的电磁能量。

Conclusion: 存在超越简单全内反射的（谐振）光学；"缺失"（低折射率/空洞）本身可成为功能性光子自由度，为传感、量子纳米光子学、超表面及可编程光学物质等领域带来新机遇。

Abstract: The confinement of light within optically thin media challenges the conventional view that optical localization requires high-index dielectric materials and total internal reflection. Over the past decades, a broad range of photonic phenomena has demonstrated that electromagnetic energy can also be guided and localized inside low-index regions or nanoscale void cavities. What first appears to be a curiosity is, in fact, highly relevant: The interaction between light and matter lies at the heart of much in optics and photonics. It enables sensing, nonlinear optical phenomena, and optical manipulation, yet it also inevitably introduces absorption, dissipation, and loss. Pushing localized, enhanced, or guided energy into a lower-index space thus offers entirely new possibilities for light-matter interaction and dispersion control. In this review, we discuss some of these counterintuitive structures, starting with light guidance within optically thin media, such as hollow-core photonic crystal fibers or antiresonant reflecting optical waveguides. We then discuss the optics of bubbles, that is, low-index inclusions in a higher-index surrounding, and ultimately arrive at the so-called Mie void resonators, optical resonators that support localized electromagnetic modes confined within a region of lower refractive index. All these systems demonstrate that there are (resonant) optics beyond simple total internal reflection. Absence can itself become a functional photonic degree of freedom, opening opportunities across sensing, quantum nanophotonics, metasurfaces, and programmable optical matter.

</details>


### [384] [Integrated elliptical III-V nanopillar arrays on silicon for dual photodetection and light emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32744)
