*João D. G. Azevedo,Rafael Ramos Uña,Ángela Barreda,Stuart Edwards,J. Iwan Davies,Jérôme Borme,Braulio Garcia-Cámara,Jana B. Nieder,Bruno Romeira*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作在硅衬底上单片集成III-V族椭圆形纳米柱阵列，实现可见—近红外宽带光探测、类神经元分级响应及约865 nm电致发光的多功能一体化纳米光子器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代片上光子器件需要在硅平台上实现紧凑、低能耗且同时集成光探测与光发射的功能单元；而单一纳米尺度器件中兼顾宽带光探测与高效发光仍然困难。

Method: 采用椭圆形纳米柱阵列作为III-V-on-Si外延模板进行单片集成，通过椭圆几何纳米结构增强光吸收与载流子动力学，并在正向偏压下测试电致发光特性。

Result: 器件在可见至近红外波段呈宽带光响应，调制光下表现出类神经元的动态分级响应，正向偏压下在约865 nm处有明显电致发光；有源区面积显著小于商用探测器，纳米阵列在830 nm处响应度可达0.55 A/W。

Conclusion: 该方案推动了适用于硅衬底上集成传感、发光、通信电路及神经形态处理的小型化有源纳米光子器件的发展。

Abstract: Next-generation on-chip photonic devices require compact, energy-efficient components integrating photodetection and light emission on silicon platforms. Achieving broadband photodetection combined with efficient emission in a single nanoscale device remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate elliptical nanopillar arrays monolithically integrated as III-V-on-Si templates, exhibiting broadband photoresponse across visible to near-infrared wavelengths, dynamic neuron-like graded responses under modulated light, and pronounced electroluminescence at around 865 nm under forward bias. The nanostructured elliptical geometry approach enhances optical absorption and carrier dynamics within an active area significantly smaller than commercial detectors, enabling responsivities up to 0.55 A/W at 830 nm in compact nanoarrays. This work advances the development of miniaturized active nanophotonic devices suitable for integrated sensing, emitting and communication circuits, and neuromorphic processing on silicon substrates.

</details>


### [385] [Scaling the Wavelength to Optimize Ultrafast Laser Processing of Bandgap Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32924)
*Maxime Chambonneau,Mario Garcia-Lechuga,David Grojo,Stefan Nolte*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本章综述超快激光-物质相互作用中关键物理效应随波长的标度规律，及其对带隙材料加工（分辨率、介质内部加工、窄带隙材料内部处理）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超短脉冲的光谱特性为在时空上调控相互作用与材料响应提供独特手段，而波长预先决定了激光-物质相互作用的基本机制与能量沉积方式，因此需要系统梳理波长依赖的物理标度及其加工后果。

Method: 综述性方法：回顾带隙材料的主要光谱性质；分析加工分辨率随波长的预期变化；总结近期关于波长在超快激光-电介质相互作用中作用的研究；评述红外超快光源新进展用于窄带隙材料内部加工。

Result: 给出了带隙材料光谱特性与波长依赖加工分辨率的分析框架，汇总了波长对超快激光-电介质相互作用影响的最新研究，并说明红外超快光源可在克服增强非线性挑战的前提下实现窄带隙材料的内部加工。

Conclusion: 波长是超快激光材料加工的核心调控参数，其标度规律决定了分辨率与能量沉积机制；新兴红外超快光源为窄带隙材料的内部（三维）加工提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The spectral characteristics of ultrashort laser pulses offer unique possibilities to control interactions in space and time as well as the subsequent material responses. In particular, selecting the wavelength predefines the fundamental mechanisms governing laser-matter interaction, and thus, how energy is deposited. This Chapter provides an overview on how the essential physical effects in ultrafast laser-matter interaction scale with the wavelength, and the consequences for bandgap material processing applications. First, we recall the main spectral properties of bandgap materials and discuss the important question of the expected processing resolutions depending on wavelength. Then, recent studies on the impact of the wavelength in ultrafast laser-dielectric interaction are presented. Finally, we present how the latest developments in infrared ultrafast laser sources can be advantageously utilized to internally process narrow-gap materials despite challenges related to increased nonlinearities.

</details>


### [386] [Multiport photonic routing with a single cavity-based electro-optic phase shifter per port](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31757)
*Alessandro Buzzi,Bjarke A. Frederiksen,Dashiell L. P. Vitullo,Mikkel Heuck,Dirk R. Englund*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种紧凑反射式光子路由架构，仅用O(N)个可编程移相器即可在N步内连接任意输入-输出对；在x-cut薄膜铌酸锂上实现3×3路由器，具备1.92 GHz/V调谐效率、100 μm²占位、<5 V驱动、6.2 GHz带宽，各输出消光比>11 dB，并实现10 MHz顺序路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用N模线性变换需要O(N²)个可编程移相器，难以同时满足低驱动电压、GHz速度、零静态功耗、无损耗调制与小占位。而信号路由不需要通用变换，因此可通过电路简化大幅降低可编程器件数量与性能权衡。

Method: 设计单块无源N×N干涉仪加单列可编程反射式移相器的反射型电路；在x-cut薄膜铌酸锂上实现3×3路由器，采用无源多模干涉耦合器与基于单侧光子晶体纳米梁腔的移相器（Pockels效应）；通过电压空间表征与物理建模确定路由态。

Result: 移相器共振调谐效率1.92 GHz/V，占位100 μm²，驱动电压<5 V，电光带宽6.2 GHz；各输出端消光比>11 dB，顺序路由速度达10 MHz（受设备限制）。

Conclusion: 腔增强Pockels开关可作为经典与量子计算互连的路由层，覆盖可见光至近红外波段。

Abstract: Programmable photonic meshes can perform general linear transformations, but universal transformations on $N$ modes require $O(N^2)$ programmable phase shifters, which cannot simultaneously achieve low drive voltage, gigahertz speed, zero static power, lossless modulation, and a compact footprint. Signal routing does not require universal transformations, which allows considerable circuit simplifications. We present a compact reflective circuit architecture that connects all input-output pairs across $N$ modes in $N$ steps using only $O(N)$ programmable phase shifters. The circuit comprises a single passive $N\times N$ interferometer and a single column of programmable reflective phase shifters. We demonstrate the architecture by implementing a $3\times 3$ router in x-cut thin-film lithium niobate with a passive multimode interferometer and phase shifters based on single-sided photonic-crystal nanobeam cavities. Our programmable devices exhibit a $1.92$ GHz/V resonance tuning efficiency via the Pockels effect, a footprint of $100\,μ\mathrm{m}^2$, sub-5-V drive voltage, and a 6.2 GHz electro-optic bandwidth. Through voltage-space characterization and physical modeling, we identify routing states with extinction ratios exceeding 11 dB at each output and demonstrate sequential routing at an equipment-limited speed of 10 MHz. Our results establish cavity-enhanced Pockels switching as a routing layer for classical and quantum computing interconnects across visible and near-infrared wavelengths.

</details>


### [387] [Thermal-noise-limited stabilization of a self-injection-locked diode laser using a miniature Fabry-Perot cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31866)
*Alexander K. Vorobyev,Olga V. Khronusova,Nikita O. Zhadnov,Konstantin S. Kudeyarov,Denis S. Kryuchkov,Danila V. Morozov,Nikita Yu. Dmitriev,Artem E. Shitikov,Dmitry A. Chermoshentsev,Igor A. Bilenko,Nikolai N. Kolachevsky*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于微型核心组件的超稳激光系统：自注入锁定激光器（DFB二极管+集成微环谐振器）加3 mL真空隙法布里-珀罗腔，在1–10 s平均时间内实现热噪声极限的分数频率不稳定度5×10⁻¹⁵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型腔稳激光器的不稳定度通常受技术噪声（尤其是剩余幅度调制）与腔镜/精细度限制，难以兼顾小型化与高性能；本文旨在用常规多层镜和中等精细度实现接近热噪声极限的紧凑超稳激光，为可搬运光钟与低噪声微波合成的全集成光子频率参考铺路。

Method: 采用自注入锁定激光器（分布式反馈激光二极管与集成微环谐振器耦合）作为光源，配合3 mL真空隙法布里-珀罗腔进行频率稳定；系统分析并抑制技术噪声源，重点解决剩余幅度调制（RAM）；腔精细度为10⁵，使用常规多层反射镜。

Result: 在1–10 s平均时间范围内获得5×10⁻¹⁵的分数频率不稳定度，达到热噪声极限，属已报道紧凑腔稳激光中最低水平之一；该性能在常规多层镜与中等精细度条件下实现。核心组件体积小，且同一微环谐振器可产生克尔频率梳。

Conclusion: 通过抑制剩余幅度调制等技术噪声，紧凑腔稳激光可在常规镜片与中等精细度下达到热噪声极限性能，为实现全集成光子频率参考（面向可搬运光钟与低噪声微波合成）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present an ultrastable laser system based on miniature core components: a self-injection-locked laser comprising a distributed-feedback laser diode and an integrated microring resonator, together with a 3-ml vacuum-gap Fabry-Perot cavity. By carefully addressing technical noise sources, in particular residual amplitude modulation, we demonstrate a thermal-noise-limited fractional frequency instability of $5\cdot10^{-15}$ for averaging times between 1 and 10 s, which is among the lowest reported for compact cavity-stabilized lasers. This performance is achieved with conventional multilayer mirrors and a moderate cavity finesse of $10^5$. Such stability of the output frequency, combined with the small size of the core components and a possibility to generate a Kerr frequency comb in the very same microring resonator, provides a path towards fully integrated photonic frequency references for transportable optical clocks and low-noise microwave synthesis.

</details>


### [388] [RADICAL: Reconfiguration-Aware Direct-Connect AI Cluster using Spatial-and-Wavelength Selective Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31941)
*Brett George,Weiyang Wang,Yuyang Wang,Zhenguo Wu,S. C. Mancas,Xiang Meng,Manya Ghobadi,Keren Bergman*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: RADICAL 是一个硅光子使能的、可重构感知的直接连接 AI 集群，将定制的空分-波分选择开关（SWSS）与 Linux 网络栈集成，并在四 GPU 服务器集群上验证了可重构光互连结构及多租户 AI 训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 AI 集群对高带宽、可重构互连的需求，探索基于硅光子开关的直接连接光互连架构，并使主机网络栈具备可重构感知能力以支撑实际分布式训练负载。

Method: 构建 4×4×4λ 微环型 SWSS 并封装，使用标准 SFP28 光收发器接入四 GPU 服务器集群；同时设计可重构感知的主机网络接口并与 Linux 网络栈集成，实现动态空间与波长路由。

Result: SWSS 路径损耗 3.2–6.8 dB，平均波长与空间串扰隔离度超过 25 dB，各路由路径上实现无误码 25 Gbps 信号恢复；系统级完成 10 分钟内累计 6.4 TB 的连续批量数据传输；演示可重构链路拓扑下的多租户训练，包括四个 ResNet-18 实例与六个 nanoGPT 实例，后者持续运行超过 5 小时。

Conclusion: RADICAL 表明硅光子可重构开关与可重构感知网络栈结合，可支撑直接连接光互连 AI 集群的稳定运行与多租户训练，具备实际系统可行性。

Abstract: We present RADICAL, the silicon-photonic-enabled Reconfiguration-Aware Direct-Connect AI Cluster that integrates a custom spatial-and-wavelength selective switch (SWSS) with the Linux network stack. RADICAL deploys a packaged 4x4x4$λ$ microring-based SWSS into a four-GPU-server cluster using standard SFP28 optical transceivers, and a reconfiguration-aware host-networking interface to demonstrate a direct-connect reconfigurable optical fabric. The SWSS provides dynamic spatial and wavelength routing with 3.2-6.8 dB path loss, average wavelength and spatial crosstalk isolation exceeding 25 dB, and error-free 25 Gbps signal recovery across routing paths. At the system level, RADICAL validates reconfiguration-aware operation through continuous bulk data transfers totaling 6.4 TBytes over 10 minutes. We further demonstrate multitenant AI training across reconfigurable link topologies, including four ResNet-18 instances and six nanoGPT instances, with the latter sustained for more than five hours.

</details>


### [389] [Cross-coupled photon avalanche drives room-temperature intrinsic optical bistability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32032)
*Eva Conour,Jacob Wojcik,Jory Sanders,Abigale Puccini,Trey Stephens-Cherry,Artiom Skripka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文报道了Ho³⁺激活的纳米晶在常温下实现无腔本征光学双稳（IOB），其非热机制源于低声子能双钙钛矿基质中交叉耦合光子雪崩（XPA），室温可见波段实现最高46 dB对比度光开关，并具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学双稳多需低温条件，或依赖微弱的热光非线性；亟需在常温、无腔条件下实现本征、非热的高对比度光学双稳，以满足光信息处理、忆阻器件与多参量传感需求。

Method: 构建Ho³⁺激活的双钙钛矿纳米晶体系；利用低声子能基质抑制非辐射损耗，通过交叉耦合光子雪崩（XPA）机制在泵浦强度低于阈值时仍可积累激发态布居；结合时间分辨发光测量（双上升曲线）验证两个储能态的能量循环耦合。

Result: 在室温下于多条可见发射线观察到无腔IOB，光开关对比度最高达46 dB，支持波分复用并兼容常规探测器；IOB在纳米至微米尺度晶体及不同双钙钛矿组分中均得以保持。

Conclusion: 低声子能双钙钛矿中XPA驱动的非热IOB为常温、无腔、高对比度光学双稳提供了新范式，并具跨尺度、跨组分普适性，有望推动信息光子学与先进传感应用。

Abstract: Optically bistable materials promise technological advances in optical information processing, memristive devices, and multiparametric sensing. However, optical bistability is predominantly observed under cryogenic conditions or is governed by subtle thermo-optical nonlinearities. Here, we report on Ho3+-activated nanocrystals that exhibit cavity-free intrinsic optical bistability (IOB) at ambient conditions. Non-thermal IOB stems from a cross-coupled photon avalanche (XPA) mechanism enabled by a low-phonon-energy double-perovskite host, which reduces nonradiative losses and facilitates excited-state population via XPA, even when the pump intensity is below threshold. Time-resolved luminescence measurements support the proposed XPA, as evidenced by a double-rise profile consistent with two reservoir states interlocked in energy looping. IOB with high-contrast photoswitching (up to 46 dB) is observed at room temperature across multiple visible emission lines, enabling wavelength-division multiplexing and compatibility with common detectors. IOB is preserved across nano- to microscale crystals and different double-perovskite compositions, broadly enabling information photonics and advanced sensing.

</details>


### [390] [Revealing and Mitigating Transmittance and Reflectance Errors in Integrating-Sphere Characterization of High-Albedo Scattering Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32403)
*Zhijie Xu,Ernesto Pini,Lorenzo Pattelli,Ioannis Papakonstantinou*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文以点扩散函数(PSF)量化高反照率散射介质在积分球测量中由散射引起的光子横向重分布，揭示外置式积分球构型的系统误差并给出抑制误差的内置式测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高反照率散射介质的精确光学表征受积分球测量误差根本性限制：散射事件使光子空间重分布，产生横向光束扩展与角发散，从而偏置所测反射率与透射率，而常用外置式积分球构型带来的配置相关系统误差尚未被系统量化。

Method: 在辐射传输方程指导下，用点扩散函数(PSF)量化并解释测量偏差；结合实验与蒙特卡罗模拟，采用归一化PSF刻画光子重分布，在高反照率极限下于宽广的(τ, g)参数空间映射特征横向扩展长度，并分析测量几何（收集孔径）的影响；同时提出内置式积分球协议。

Result: 实验与蒙特卡罗模拟表明，广泛使用的外置式积分球构型引入系统性的、构型依赖的误差，在散射致横向扩展显著时尤为突出；在笔束极限下，维持 L/D_port ≲ 0.01 可将有限收集孔径所致偏差降至约1%；内置式积分球协议抑制散射相关损失，其透射率与反射率测量与理论预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: 本工作帮助积分球测量使用者识别散射相关误差，并通过几何条件约束(L/D_port ≲ 0.01)与内置式协议提升宽泛应用领域中透射率与反射率表征的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate optical characterization of high-albedo scattering media is fundamentally limited by integrating-sphere measurement errors. Scattering events spatially redistribute photons, leading to lateral beam spread and angular divergence that bias measured reflectance and transmittance. Here, we investigate these deviations, which are quantified and interpreted by using the point spread function (PSF), guided by the radiative transfer equation. Experiments and Monte Carlo modelling show that the widely used external-mount integrating-sphere configuration introduces systematic, configuration-dependent errors that become pronounced when scattering-induced lateral spreading is substantial. Using a normalised PSF to quantify photon redistribution, we map the characteristic lateral spreading length across a broad $(\taus,g)$ parameter space in the high-albedo limit, providing a basis for evaluating errors in specific measurement geometries. We further quantify the influence of measurement geometry and show that, in the pencil-beam limit, maintaining $L/D_{\mathrm{port}}\lesssim 0.01$ reduces the discrepancy arising from the finite collection aperture to approximately \qty{1}{\percent}. In addition, we introduce an internal-mount integrating-sphere protocol that suppresses scattering-related losses and yields transmittance and reflectance measurements in good agreement with theoretical predictions. Overall, our work helps users of integrating-sphere measurements identify scattering-related errors and improve the reliability of transmittance and reflectance characterization across a wide range of application areas.

</details>


### [391] [Stochastic Analysis of Laser Phase Noise in Photonics-Assisted Radio Frequency Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32563)
*Niloy Ghosh,David Prinsloo,Eduward Tangdiongga*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立一个随机模型，量化激光相位噪声对光子辅助射频波束赋形的影响，揭示其导致的空间相干退化与阵列规模上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性模型无法刻画有限线宽激光驱动波束赋形网络时射频阵列因子的统计随机性，因而需要随机化理论框架来准确评估激光相位噪声的真实影响。

Method: 构建捕获RF阵列因子统计随机性的随机理论框架，采用傅里叶域分析研究相位噪声对空间相干函数的重塑作用，分析相干RF孔径收缩、方向图畸变与方向性退化，并考察纳秒级光差分延迟结构及平均RF功率增益的缩放与涨落。

Result: 即使采用商用窄线宽（kHz量级）激光器，纳秒级光差分延迟的波束赋形网络仍可出现显著性能退化；大孔径网络中平均RF功率增益下降且涨落占主导，从而对最大可达RF天线阵规模形成上限。

Conclusion: 激光相位噪声给出了光子辅助RF波束赋形的定量性能与可扩展性极限，为下一代无线通信、传感、雷达和射电天文中的大规模波束赋形网络设计提供了关键准则。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic model for quantifying the impact of laser phase noise on photonics-assisted radio frequency (RF) beamforming. Unlike conventional deterministic models, the presented theoretical framework captures the statistical randomness associated with the RF array factor of beamforming networks driven by finite-linewidth lasers. Fourier-domain analysis reveals that laser phase noise reshapes the spatial coherence function of such beamforming networks, inducing coherent RF aperture shrinkage, beam-pattern distortion, and directivity degradation. The analysis highlights that beamforming network architectures employing nanosecond-scale optical differential delays can exhibit substantial performance degradation even when driven by commercially available narrow-linewidth (kHz-range) lasers. Furthermore, it is demonstrated that average RF power gain gets downscaled and fluctuations dominate in large-aperture beamforming networks due to phase noise, imposing an upper limit on the maximum achievable RF antenna array-size. The reported results highlight the quantitative performance and scalability limits imposed by laser phase noise in photonics-assisted RF beamforming, offering key design guidelines for implementing large-scale beamforming networks in next-generation wireless communications, sensing, radar, and radio astronomy applications.

</details>


### [392] [Computational imaging through turbid, bubbly and flowing water near the telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32646)
*Alexander Ingold,Sabina D. Menon,Aadhi Umamageswaran,Alec Ikei,May H. Hlaing,Syed N. Qadri,Rajesh Menon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文构建了透过近镜头浑浊、含气泡、流动水层的望远镜实测数据集（7种流动/气泡/浑浊组合，清水记录作第二参考），发现浑浊主要降低对比度（西门子星上4.4–4.8倍），8帧平均比单帧提升SSIM 0.032、学习式融合收益甚微；原始单帧即可达到96.8%五分类识别；加入合成训练对虽不改变显示图像评分，但将复原西门子星与清水记录的相关性从<0.5提升至>0.9，并可零重训迁移到普通水下照片。据此提出"帧平均—原始帧直接分类—实测+合成对训练复原网络"的简洁流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景（冲浪区、船尾迹后方）相机需透过贴近镜头的浑浊、含气泡、流动水体成像，但现有复原网络通常只在显示图像上评分，缺乏对"透过水层成像"这一物理条件的受控测量与正确评价基准，且评价目标（数字源图像 vs 清水记录）未加区分。

Method: 搭建望远镜透过距物镜45 mm、厚25 mm水层的成像装置，对显示图像与实物成像，覆盖7种流动、气泡与浑浊组合，并以清水记录作为第二参考；量化浑浊对对比度的影响（西门子星），比较单帧、8帧平均与学习式多帧融合；评估原始帧与复原图像在五分类识别任务上的表现；对比仅用实测配对与实测+合成配对训练的网络，并用数字源与清水记录两种参考进行评分。

Result: 悬浮颗粒在1.3°视场外大量散射，浑浊主要造成对比度损失，西门子星上达4.4–4.8倍；8帧平均较单帧提升SSIM 0.032，学习式融合增益很小；原始单帧五分类准确率96.8%，与复原图像相当；加入合成训练对不改变显示图像评分，但使复原西门子星与清水记录的相关性由<0.5升至>0.9；网络可零重训改善普通水下照片，而仅用实测配对训练则失败；以数字源或清水记录评分会反转原始与复原图像的排序。

Conclusion: 评价指标的选择会实质改变结论，应以清水记录与数字源双重参考评估复原；实用流程为帧平均、原始帧直接分类、以及用实测与合成配对共同训练的复原网络，后者具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Cameras in the surf zone or behind ship wakes look through turbid, bubbly, moving water close to the lens, yet restoration networks are usually scored only on displayed images. We introduce a measured dataset of a telescope viewing displayed images and physical objects through a 25~mm water layer 45~mm in front of its objective, under seven combinations of flow, bubbles and turbidity, with clear-water recordings as a second reference. Because the suspension scatters light far outside the 1.3\textdegree{} field of view, turbidity mainly removes contrast, 4.4- to 4.8-fold on a Siemens star. Restoring the mean of eight frames improves structural similarity (SSIM) by 0.032 over a single frame, and learned fusion of the frames adds little. One raw frame supports five-category recognition (96.8\%) as well as restored images. Adding synthetic training pairs leaves display-image scores unchanged but raises the correlation of a restored Siemens star with its clear-water recording from below 0.5 to above 0.9. These networks also improve ordinary underwater photographs without retraining, where training on measured pairs alone fails. Scoring against the digital source or a clear-water recording reverses the ranking of raw and restored images. These results support a simple pipeline: average the frames, classify raw frames directly, and restore with networks trained on measured and synthetic pairs.

</details>


### [393] [Supercontinuum-driven broadband Fourier-transform photothermal microspectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32718)
*Senyu Wang,Zicong Xu,Kazuki Hashimoto,Jianfeng Li,Takuro Ideguchi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出超连续谱驱动的傅里叶变换光热(SC-FT-PT)显微光谱平台，结合宽带近红外激发与快速扫描干涉采集，实现约1秒/谱的宽带光热光谱测量，并可对水/DMSO混合物及单个6 μm聚苯乙烯颗粒进行局域振动表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光热显微光谱依赖逐波长调谐的序列采集，宽带光谱获取速度慢；而显微样品化学表征常需同时解析多个相隔较远、位置与线型各异的振动吸收带。因此亟需一种兼具宽带覆盖与快速采集能力的局域光热光谱方法。

Method: 采用超连续谱(SC)作为宽带近红外泵浦，经干涉仪调制后在样品中诱导波长相关的光热响应；以共传播的可见探测光检测该热响应，并通过傅里叶变换重建光谱。工作波段为1.65–2.35 μm，配合可见探测与暗场检测构型实现局域(微区)测量。

Result: 在水溶液中实现约1谱/秒的宽带光热光谱采集；可同时分辨水–二甲基亚砜混合物的多个分子吸收特征，并以高光谱保真度重现液态水的近红外吸收特征；进一步在暗场构型下获得水环境中单个6 μm聚苯乙烯颗粒的宽带光热光谱。

Conclusion: SC-FT-PT显微光谱为液体与微米级样品的局域振动表征提供了快速、宽带的平台，可实现具微尺度空间选择性的化学分析。

Abstract: Chemical characterization of microscopic samples often requires resolving multiple, widely separated vibrational absorption bands, including their positions and spectral shapes. Photothermal microspectroscopy provides a route to localized vibrational measurements by detecting absorption-induced thermal responses with a separate optical probe. However, rapid acquisition of broadband photothermal spectra remains challenging when spectral information is collected through sequential wavelength tuning. Here, we develop a supercontinuum (SC)-driven Fourier-transform photothermal (FT-PT) microspectroscopy platform that combines broadband near-infrared excitation with rapid-scan interferometric acquisition for localized vibrational measurements. In this approach, an interferometrically modulated SC pump induces wavelength-dependent photothermal responses in the sample, which are detected through a co-propagating visible probe beam and reconstructed by Fourier transformation. Using a 1.65-2.35 um spectral window, we demonstrate broadband photothermal spectra of aqueous samples with acquisition rates of approximately one spectrum per second. The system simultaneously resolves multiple molecular absorption features in a water-dimethyl sulfoxide mixture and reproduces the near-infrared absorption characteristics of liquid water with high spectral fidelity. Extending beyond homogeneous liquids, a dark-field detection configuration enables broadband photothermal spectroscopy of an individual 6 um polystyrene particle in an aqueous environment. This SC-based FT-PT microspectroscopy approach provides a rapid and broadband platform for localized vibrational characterization of liquid and microscopic samples, enabling chemical analysis with microscale spatial selectivity.

</details>


### [394] [Video-rate quantitative phase and polarimetric imaging (QP2I) for label-free imaging of contracting muscle cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32756)
*Lucas Kreiss Roohian,Nachiket Pathak,David Ripp,Mitchell Harling,Nils Kronenberg,Marcel Schubert,Malte C. Gather*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出QP2I计算成像框架，将标准DIC显微镜与偏振相机结合，实现单帧、视频帧率的定量相位与偏振双对比成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量相位成像（QPI）对透明样品无标记成像已成为标准手段，但各向同性相位无法反映分子有序性所对应的光学各向异性；现有可定量获取双折射信息的方案依赖多帧照明、偏振态扫描或专用干涉硬件，牺牲了采集速度或标准显微镜的简便性。

Method: 基于标准DIC显微镜加装偏振相机，构建单帧双对比计算成像系统；提出新的全局正则化相位重建方法以消除以往DIC-to-QPI方法的伪影，获得定量相位；并采用逐像素Stokes重建获得定量各向异性延迟量。通过仿真、静态参考靶标、固定肌肉组织及活体动态运动细胞进行验证。

Result: 在仿真与实验中均验证有效；可全光学、无标记地研究活体心肌细胞的收缩动力学，帧率足以时间分辨快速收缩过程；可逐帧获取肌节长度等结构读参，并获得以往难以测量的肌肉收缩过程中延迟量变化的定量分析，作为肌肉光弹性的直接光学特征。

Conclusion: QP2I在保持标准显微镜简便性的前提下，实现长时间、无标记、定量对比的动态细胞过程观测，克服荧光标记的漂白、生理扰动与难以长期监测等局限。

Abstract: Quantitative phase imaging (QPI) has become a standard approach for label-free microscopy of transparent specimens, mapping the optical path length. However, the isotropic phase is blind to the molecular order underlying optical anisotropy. Implementations that also add quantitative contrast to birefringence, rely on multi-frame illumination or polarization-state scanning, or on dedicated interferometric hardware, that trade away acquisition speed or the simplicity of a standard microscope. We present Quantitative Phase and Polarimetric Imaging (QP2I), a computational imaging framework that converts a standard differential interference contrast (DIC) microscope with a polarization camera into a single-shot, video-rate, dual-contrast imaging system. We developed a new globally regularized phase reconstruction that eliminates artifacts of prior DIC-to-QPI approaches, providing quantitative phase information and used per-pixel Stokes-based reconstruction to obtain quantitative anisotropic retardance. We validate the approach across simulations, static reference targets, fixed muscle tissue and living dynamically moving cells. Thereby, QP2I enables to study contractile dynamics in live cardiomyocytes all-optically and label-free. It provides both structural and functional readouts at frame rates that are sufficient to temporally resolve fast cardiomyocyte contractions. While conventional fluorescent labels bleach out over time, perturb physiology, and preclude long-term monitoring, QP2I provides similar established structural readouts, such as frame-by-frame sarcomere length, as well as previously inaccessible measurements, like quantitative analysis of retardance changes during muscle contraction, as a direct optical signature of muscle photoelasticity. QP2I thus enables long-term, label-free interrogation of dynamic cellular processes at quantitative contrast.

</details>


### [395] [Polarization-encoded photon statistics for counting dipole emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32885)
*Oumeng Zhang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在脉冲激发下，偏振不仅携带偶极取向信息，其单脉冲内的光子统计也可用于计数发射体：三角度线偏振激发使亚泊松亏缺提升约1.5倍，四通道偏振探测可进一步利用多光子脉冲的通道分布读出数目，模拟显示脉冲需求分别降至约1/2.3与最高1/4.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衍射极限区域内发射体数目是定位重叠分子的前提。脉冲激发下每个分子每脉冲最多发一个光子，故光子数分辨探测可在不空间分辨的情况下计数发射体，但所需脉冲数随发射体数目急剧增长，需要提升单脉冲的信息量。

Method: 指出平均偏振分辨信号在"每个发射体被多个更暗副本替代"时不变，故数目信息存在于单脉冲内光子统计中；偏振从两方面重塑该统计：采用三个角度的线偏振激发以放大光子计数的亚泊松亏缺（理论上对任意取向均约1.5倍）；采用四个偏振通道探测，通过多光子脉冲在通道间的分布揭示发射体数目。并以2–5个未知取向与亮度的发射体做最大似然模拟验证。

Result: 三角度激发使所需脉冲数减少约2.3倍，接近预测的1.5²=2.25；叠加四通道探测后减幅最高达4.8倍。

Conclusion: 偏振是光子数分辨探测中未被利用的计数资源，仅需在现有光子数分辨装置上增加偏振光学元件即可获得显著的脉冲数节省，实现无需空间分辨的发射体计数增强。

Abstract: Counting the emitters within a diffraction-limited region is a prerequisite for localizing overlapping molecules. Under pulsed excitation, each molecule emits at most one photon per pulse, so photon-number-resolved detection can count emitters without resolving them. But the required number of pulses grows steeply with the emitter number. Here we show that polarization, conventionally used to measure the orientations of dipole emitters, also carries information on their number. Because mean polarization-resolved signals are unchanged when every emitter is replaced by multiple dimmer copies, this information resides in the photon statistics within each pulse, which polarization reshapes in two ways. Linearly polarized excitation at three angles increases the sub-Poissonian deficit of the photon count by a factor of 1.5 for any emitter orientation, and detection in four polarization channels reveals the emitter number through how the photons of multiphoton pulses are distributed among the channels. Maximum-likelihood simulations for two to five emitters of unknown orientations and brightnesses show that three-angle excitation reduces the required number of pulses by about 2.3-fold, close to the predicted $1.5^2=2.25$, and that adding four-channel detection raises the reduction to as much as 4.8-fold. These gains require only polarization optics added to photon-number-resolved detection.

</details>
